### [1] [Pretraining Latent Information Feedback Transformers with Teacher Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38149)
*Dor Tirosh,Ido Amos,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 LIFT 架构，在预训练中通过教师强制监督让 Transformer 预测并反馈信息密集的隐状态，实现深层到浅层的跨步骤信息传播。实验表明在 135M 到 1B 参数规模上，LIFT 在语言建模、推理和过程任务上一致优于标准 Transformer，推理开销小且随规模增大而减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 语言模型的前馈特性导致深层表示无法反馈到浅层，信息只能通过解码后的 token 向下传递，迫使模型重复计算并丢弃可能的续写，阻碍了跨生成步骤的状态传播。

Method: LIFT 将标准 Transformer 扩展为具有反馈回路的架构。输入 token 序列首先与从现成预训练语言模型（教师）的下一 token 分布中提取的信息密集状态配对，这些状态在训练前预先计算。训练时，模型被要求同时预测下一个 token 和下一个状态（教师强制）。由于输入状态是预计算的，预训练完全并行化，不增加序列长度上的依赖。模型在标准 Transformer 基础上增加少量参数以处理状态预测和反馈。推理时，模型使用自身预测的状态作为下一步的输入状态，实现跨生成步骤的状态传播。这种反馈机制允许深层表示影响浅层计算，减少中间结果的重复计算。推理开销主要来自状态预测和反馈操作，但实验表明该开销较小且随模型规模增大而相对减小。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "语言建模、下游推理任务和过程任务性能", "value": "一致优于标准 Transformer 和基线", "setting": "在 token 匹配预算下，预训练模型规模 135M 到 1B 参数"}, {"metric": "与计算匹配 Transformer 对比", "value": "持平或领先", "setting": "计算匹配预算下，模型规模 135M 到 1B 参数"}, {"metric": "状态跟踪任务性能", "value": "小型 LIFT 优于训练数据量多 8 倍的同等规模 Transformer", "setting": "控制实验，状态跟踪任务；即使使用失败 Transformer 的状态进行训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: LIFT 架构：通过少量额外参数使模型能够预测下一个 token 的同时预测下一个信息密集状态，实现深层到浅层的反馈。；教师强制状态学习：将循环状态学习转化为教师强制预测问题，每个输入 token 与来自预训练 LM 下一 token 分布的信息密集状态配对。；并行预训练：由于输入状态预计算，预训练在所有位置上保持完全并行，不影响训练效率。；推理时自反馈：推理时使用模型自己预测的状态进行反馈，计算开销小且随模型规模增大而减小。

Abstract: Transformer language models (LMs) are feed-forward: deep-layer representations are never fed back to shallower layers, and the only pathway for information to flow downward across generation steps is the decoded token. This narrow channel forces models to recompute intermediate results and to discard alternative continuations. In this work, we remove this bottleneck during pretraining, introducing the LIFT (Latent Information Feedback Transformer) architecture and training method which enable LMs to propagate state across generation. We achieve this by turning recurrent-state learning into a teacher-forced prediction problem: each input token is paired with an information-dense state, derived from the next-token distribution of an off-the-shelf pretrained LM. The model, extended with a small number of additional parameters, is then trained to predict both the next token and the next state. As the input states are precomputed, pretraining remains fully parallel across positions. At inference, the model's own predicted states are fed back, with a minor computational overhead that decreases with model size. Experiments with pretrained models ranging from 135M to 1B parameters show that LIFT consistently outperforms standard Transformers and baselines on language modeling, downstream reasoning tasks, and procedural tasks under token-matched budget, while being on par with or ahead of compute-matched Transformers. Moreover, a controlled study on a state-tracking task shows that a tiny LIFT outperforms same-size Transformers trained on 8x more data, even when trained with the states of a Transformer that fails the task. Overall, we show that LMs can learn to exploit deep-to-shallow feedback during pretraining via scalable teacher supervision.

</details>


### [2] [STEPQuant: When and Where Errors Matter in Delta-Rule Recurrent State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38169)
*Bingchen Yao,Haobo Xu,Haokun Lin,Yichen Wu,Ziyu Guo,Renrui Zhang,Zhichao Lu,Zhenan Sun,Ying Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STEPQuant 发现 Delta-rule 循环状态量化误差在时间（长寿命记忆持续传播）和空间（不同 key 行影响不同、幅度分布不均）两个维度上影响不均，据此提出空间-时间后训练量化，按误差幅度与记忆寿命分配精度并联合拟合 key 行/value 列缩放。在 Qwen3.8-27B 与 Kimi-Linear-48B-A3B 上，6-bit 接近 FP32 精度并优于 4-bit 均匀 INT8；集成 SGLang 后实现 5 倍以上状态压缩与最多 68.7% 服务内存下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Delta-rule 循环状态（固定大小但随并发累积）成为 LLM 服务的内存瓶颈；直接低比特量化循环状态会因误差在连续状态更新中传播而导致严重精度退化。

Method: STEPQuant 是一种面向 Delta-rule 循环状态的空间-时间后训练量化框架。输入是线性注意力层在解码过程中维护的固定大小循环状态矩阵（由 key 行与 value 列构成），以及对应的 Delta-rule 状态更新规则。作者首先分析量化误差的传播特性：时间维度上，写入长寿命记忆的状态误差会跨多个解码步持续影响后续输出；空间维度上，不同 key 行对模型输出的影响不同，且状态数值幅度沿行与列分布差异显著。基于此，STEPQuant 为状态的不同位置分配不同精度：根据误差幅度与记忆寿命决定比特分配，使长寿命、高影响位置获得更高精度。随后，方法联合拟合 key 行缩放因子与 value 列缩放因子，拟合目标同时考虑状态分布以及 key 行对输出误差的影响，从而在量化后最小化对输出误差的贡献。量化在推理前以训练后方式完成，不改变 Delta-rule 更新公式。推理阶段集成到 SGLang，通过定制 GPU 内核执行分精度量化状态的读取与更新，在 6-bit 名义预算下维持接近 FP32 状态的精度，同时显著压缩循环状态占用并降低总服务内存。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "精度（对标 FP32 状态）", "value": "在名义 6-bit 预算下接近匹配 FP32 状态精度", "setting": "Qwen3.8-27B 与 Kimi-Linear-48B-A3B-Instruct，长生成与短生成基准"}, {"metric": "精度（4-bit 配置对比均匀 INT8）", "value": "STEPQuant 4-bit 配置优于均匀 INT8", "setting": "Qwen3.8-27B 与 Kimi-Linear-48B-A3B-Instruct，长/短生成基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现量化误差影响的两个互补维度：时间上长寿命记忆误差跨多解码步持续；空间上不同 key 行对输出影响不同，且状态幅度沿行和列均变化显著。；提出空间-时间后训练量化框架 STEPQuant，按误差幅度与记忆寿命分配精度。；联合拟合 key 行与 value 列的量化尺度，基于状态分布以及 key 行对输出误差的影响进行优化。；在 SGLang 中集成并优化 GPU 内核，实现 6-bit 下超过 5 倍循环状态压缩与最多 68.7% 总服务内存降低。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces growing KV caches with fixed-size recurrent states, yet these persistent states can become a substantial memory bottleneck under concurrent serving. Directly quantizing recurrent states to low precision often leads to severe accuracy degradation, as quantization errors propagate through successive state updates. We discover that the impact of these errors depends on two complementary dimensions: temporally, errors in long-lived memory can persist across many decoding steps; spatially, errors in different key rows affect model outputs differently, while state magnitudes vary substantially along both rows and columns. Motivated by these observations, we propose STEPQuant, a spatial-temporal post-training quantization framework for Delta-rule recurrent states. STEPQuant allocates precision according to error magnitude and memory lifetime, and jointly fits key-row and value-column scales based on state distributions and key-row impact on output error. Experiments on Qwen3.8-27B and Kimi-Linear-48B-A3B-Instruct across both long- and short-generation benchmarks show that STEPQuant closely matches FP32-state accuracy under a nominal 6-bit budget and outperforms uniform INT8 in its 4-bit configuration. Integrated into SGLang with optimized GPU kernels, 6-bit STEPQuant achieves over 5x recurrent-state compression and reduces total serving memory by up to 68.7%. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [3] [LongHarness Bench: Stress-Testing Language Model Harnesses for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38137)
*Quang Hieu Pham,Thuy Duong Nguyen,Jocelyn Qiaochu Chen,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 LongHarness Bench，用多套需要词法检索与语义匹配、并需策略性自适应推理的任务，同时评估长上下文 LM harness 的效果与效率；评测多个前沿模型与四种先进 harness，发现最佳组合仅达 68% 宏平均准确率，且同一模型在不同 harness 下效率差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文评测无法区分现代 LM harness 的优劣，表现为不同 harness 准确率饱和且评测成本相似；需要同时评估长上下文 harness 的有效性和效率，并考察其是否能有策略地处理上下文而非穷举。

Method: 论文定位为评测基准而非单一模型架构。其核心流程为：给定长上下文任务输入，由 LM harness 决定如何对上下文执行检索与推理。harness 可采用的策略包括词法搜索与语义匹配等多样化检索方式，并在全局与局部上下文之间进行策略性、自适应推理；每个任务仅有一小部分上下文在特定步骤真正有用，因此 harness 需要先判断最具选择性的条件或线索以缩小搜索范围，再逐步验证剩余条件。评测在多个前沿语言模型与四种先进 harness 组合上进行，指标兼顾准确率与评测/处理成本，从而暴露同一底层模型在不同 harness 下的效率差异。原文未披露具体模块实现、训练目标或推理硬件细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro-average accuracy", "value": "68%", "setting": "四个评测套件上最佳模型—harness 组合"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出面向长上下文 harness 的基准，同时衡量效果与效率；设计需要多样化检索策略（词法搜索与语义匹配）以及全局/局部自适应推理的任务；构造大量语义相关但每步仅有小部分有用的上下文，制造困难搜索问题与不同准确率—成本权衡；系统评测多个前沿 LM 与四种先进 harness 组合，揭示同一底层模型在不同 harness 下效率差异显著

Abstract: Language-model (LM) harnesses enable LMs to operate effectively over long contexts using additional compute. However, existing long-context evaluations are insufficient for distinguishing modern harnesses, reflected by saturated accuracy across harnesses and largely similar evaluation costs. In this paper, we introduce a benchmark for evaluating both the effectiveness and efficiency of long-context harnesses. Our tasks require diverse retrieval strategies, including lexical search and semantic matching, together with strategic and adaptive reasoning over global and local context. Much of the context is semantically relevant but only a small subset is useful at each step, creating both a challenging search problem and different accuracy--cost tradeoffs across processing strategies. For example, one task requires identifying every person satisfying several conditions using evidence scattered across documents; strategically checking the most selective condition first can narrow the search before verifying the remaining conditions. We evaluate multiple families of frontier language models with four state-of-the-art harnesses. Our benchmarks remain challenging even for strong model--harness combinations: the best reaches 68\% macro-average accuracy across four evaluation suites. More importantly, we find that the same underlying model can exhibit markedly different efficiency under different harnesses. Our results establish efficiency as an important axis for long-context evaluation and provide a testbed for developing harnesses that process context strategically rather than exhaustively.

</details>


### [4] [How Local Mixing Encodes Relative Position in Global NoPE Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38109)
*Cutter Dawes,Nick Alonso,Tom Figliolia,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文从理论与实证上解释混合架构（SWA/门控线性注意力 + 全局 NoPE 注意力）如何隐式编码位置：局部混合层在残差流中诱导 recency bias 并被全局注意力 logits 选择，且该偏置可跨长序列保持，为长上下文 LLM 架构设计提供机制性理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合模型（滑动窗口注意力 SWA / 门控线性注意力等局部混合层 + 全局 NoPE 注意力）在没有显式位置编码的情况下，如何以及为何能够在全局注意力层隐式编码相对位置信息，并相比纯全局 NoPE 注意力在长序列上保持该信息。

Method: 论文分析的对象是“局部混合层 + 全局 NoPE 注意力”的混合 Transformer 架构：输入 token 序列进入残差流；局部混合层（如滑动窗口注意力 SWA 或门控线性注意力）在局部窗口/递归状态上做混合，使残差流中较早的位置产生衰减效应，即 recency bias；该 recency bias 沿残差流传播到全局注意力层。全局注意力层本身不带显式位置编码（NoPE），其 query/key 由残差流投影得到，因此残差流中由局部层注入的、位置相关的分量会被全局注意力的 logits 选择性放大，从而让全局注意力能够区分相对位置。与仅有全局 NoPE 注意力的模型相比，后者的位置信息只能通过因果掩码隐式产生、随序列增长而失效；混合架构则依靠局部层持续刷新残差流中的 recency 信号，使位置信息在长序列上得以维持。论文通过理论推导与实证分析验证上述机制，并据此讨论可外推到更长序列的位置编码设计方向。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出并论证局部混合层（SWA、门控线性注意力）会在残差流中诱导 recency bias，该偏置传播到全局注意力 logits 并被其选择，从而隐式编码位置；对比纯全局 NoPE 注意力（位置信息仅来自因果掩码、无法跨长序列保持）与混合模型，指出混合模型可将 recency bias 维持到长序列；给出该隐式位置编码机制的理论与实证证据，并讨论其对可无限外推到更长序列长度的位置编码设计的启示

Abstract: The attention operation is naively position invariant. However, positional information is fundamental to natural language, and therefore a variety of explicit position encodings have been developed in transformer-based models, such as rotary position encoding (RoPE). Although explicit position encodings have long been assumed to be required, recent methods that interleave local mixing layers, such as sliding window attention (SWA) and gated linear attention, while not encoding position (NoPE) in global attention layers has recently been shown to be successful at scale. How and why this approach works is not well-understood. In this paper, we develop an explanation of how hybrid models of this sort can implicitly encode position at global NoPE layers. Supported by both theoretical and empirical evidence, our central argument is that SWA and gated linear attention induce a recency bias in the residual stream that propagates to, and is selected by, the global attention logits. Moreover, in contrast to the implicit position encodings found in models with only global NoPE attention, in which positional information arises solely from the causal mask, the recency bias in hybrid models can be maintained across long sequences. In addition to deepening our understanding of how hybrid models encode position, these findings may provide insights for how to encode position in a way that can extrapolate to longer sequence lengths indefinitely.

</details>


### [5] [Learning What to Remember: Long-horizon Counterfactual Memory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37930)
*Jiaming Tang,Mingyan Liu,Armin Sarabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MGPO，通过将单次记忆重写的边际贡献作为奖励信号，解决长期记忆中的延迟信用分配问题；在文档级信息抽取上提升效果并将平均记忆长度减少近80%，且记忆策略可跨域复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化文本记忆中的信用分配问题：一次记忆重写可能很久以后才体现价值，且其效用可能继承自已有信息，难以判断哪些记忆更新真正带来长期增量价值。

Method: 系统包含一个记忆策略（memory policy）与下游语言模型。输入为文档级信息抽取任务中的交互流；记忆策略决定每步写入或重写持久化文本记忆的内容。记忆状态随交互累积，下游模型基于当前记忆执行信息抽取并产生效用反馈。MGPO 将一次记忆重写所带来的效用变化定义为边际贡献：扣除改写前已存信息本可获得的继承效用，得到该次重写的净增量，并沿时间向未来步骤传播该增量作为奖励/优势信号，从而对记忆策略进行强化学习优化。训练目标是最大化当前与未来下游效用的边际增益之和，同时使记忆内容更精简。推理时，优化后的记忆策略生成更短且信息密度更高的记忆，可直接用于文档级抽取，并能跨域复用、迁移到未见下游模型而无需再训练。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均记忆长度", "value": "相对初始记忆策略减少近80%", "setting": "优化后的记忆策略在文档级信息抽取任务上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Memory Gain Policy Optimization (MGPO)：以记忆重写对当前及未来下游效用的边际贡献作为奖励信号，隔离单次重写的增量价值；将延迟记忆效用转化为直接学习信号，优化持久化信息的选择；在文档级信息抽取任务上利用结构化监督使单次记忆更新效果可度量；学习到的记忆策略可跨域复用与迁移，无需进一步训练

Abstract: Persistent textual memory allows language models to carry information across long interactions, but learning what to remember is fundamentally a credit-assignment problem. A memory rewrite may only become useful many steps later, while much of the observed utility may be inherited from information already stored before the rewrite. We introduce Memory Gain Policy Optimization (MGPO), which isolates the incremental value of each memory rewrite by crediting it for its marginal contribution to current and future downstream utility. This turns delayed memory utility into a direct learning signal for optimizing what information should persist. We study MGPO on document-level information extraction, where structured supervision makes the effects of individual memory updates directly measurable. MGPO improves extraction while reducing average memory length by nearly 80% relative to the initial memory policy before optimization. The learned memory policy also supports reuse and transfer across domains, downstream models without further training. These results show that effective memory learning depends not only on preserving useful information, but on identifying which memory updates create lasting incremental value.

</details>


### [6] [It's Not What the Image Shows: Irrelevant Context Destabilises VLM Judges Without Informing Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37863)
*Nagham Omar,Mahmoud Jabarin,Kinan Ibraheem,Lotem Peled-Cohen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIST用200条可字面/比喻解读的句子与对齐、误导、无图三种条件，对13个VLM裁判做受控压力测试。结果显示图像存在本身即改变约20%标签，且仅37%的变化朝向图像语义，与人类标注一致性不因图像变化，说明可替代性结论更多反映评测配置而非模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM被用于替代人工标注者，但可替代性测试可能受无关评测条件（如图像是否出现）干扰，而非反映模型本身能力；需要验证图像上下文是否会改变本应仅依赖文本的判断。

Method: 论文不提出新模型架构，而是构建评测框架MIST。输入为200条英文句子，每条围绕一个可作字面或比喻解读的短语；每条句子分别配三种图像条件：对齐图像（描绘其某一读法）、误导图像（描绘相反读法）、无图。标注指南规定标签必须仅由句子决定，因此图像不应改变任何答案。评测对象为13个VLM裁判（judge），对这些条件组合输出标签。分析流程：统计不同图像条件相对于无图条件对标签的改变率；在标签发生变化的样本中，计算其变化方向是否朝向图像所示含义；对比‘删除忽略图像指令但保留图像’这一控制条件；将13个裁判按是否通过alt-test分为7个通过组与6个未通过组，比较效应大小；最后与作者的人类标注者比较一致性。核心结论：图像存在本身而非图像内容使标签改变约19%–20%，高于11.6%的指令删除条件；仅37%的变化朝向图像所示语义；人类一致性不因图像条件改变。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "标签改变比例（对齐图像）", "value": "20.5%", "setting": "13个VLM裁判在MIST上的评测"}, {"metric": "标签改变比例（误导图像）", "value": "19.4%", "setting": "13个VLM裁判在MIST上的评测"}, {"metric": "标签改变比例（删除忽略图像指令但保留图像）", "value": "11.6%", "setting": "同上评测配置"}, {"metric": "标签变化中朝图像所示含义移动的比例", "value": "37%", "setting": "对齐与误导图像之间标签不同的样本"}, {"metric": "与人类标注者一致率无变化", "value": "无图、对齐、误导三种条件一致", "setting": "作者人工标注对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出MIST（Misleading-Image Stress Test）：200条英文句子，每条围绕可作字面或比喻理解的短语，配以对齐图像、误导图像或无图三种条件；标注指南要求仅凭句子判断，图像不应改变任何答案，由此构建受控反事实评测；系统评估13个VLM裁判，比较对齐图像与误导图像对标签改变的比例，并对照删除‘忽略图像’指令后的效果；发现图像存在本身而非图像内容驱动标签变化，且与人类标注者一致性不因图像条件而改变，质疑可替代性结论的稳定性

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in place of human annotators, making it important that substitutability tests reflect the model rather than incidental evaluation conditions. We introduce MIST, the Misleading-Image Stress Test: 200 English sentences, each built around a phrase readable either figuratively or literally and shown with an aligned image depicting its reading, a misleading image depicting the opposite, or no image at all. The guidelines require the label to be decided from the sentence alone, so no image should change any answer. We expected each image to pull a judge's labels toward the sense it depicts, and neither kind did. Across thirteen VLM judges, an aligned image changed 20.5% of labels and a misleading one 19.4%, close for every judge and both above the 11.6% produced by deleting the ignore-the-image instruction with the image left in place. Yet only 37% of the labels that differ between the two images moved toward the sense shown, and agreement with our human annotators is unchanged whether the image is absent, aligned or misleading. The effect is smaller in the seven judges that pass the alt-test than in the six that never do, but present in all of them: what moves a judge is that an image is there, not which of the two it is, so a substitutability verdict describes a configuration as much as a model.

</details>

*Timur Mudarisov,Mikhail Burtsev,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文通过六个预训练语言模型，分析推理时残差流表示如何逐步变得对最终输出特异，揭示方向对齐、竞争端点数量与欧氏距离的独立变化，并提供高维理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 语言模型在推理过程中，残差流表示如何逐步变得对最终输出具有特异性，以及方向对齐、竞争端点数量、欧氏距离等几何指标如何独立变化。

Method: 论文研究 Transformer 语言模型推理时的残差流演化，不涉及新的模型架构或训练/推理系统设计。具体而言，对六个预训练语言模型，在生成过程中提取中间残差状态，将其与自身最终残差状态（own endpoint）以及一个包含其他上下文最终状态的银行（empirical bank）进行比较。通过计算欧氏距离和余弦距离，追踪自身端点何时开始优于平均替代端点，以及有多少个体替代端点仍然更近。引入高维理论模型，将范数、方向对齐和端点几何的作用分离，解释为何渐进的方向改变可以导致竞争集大小急剧下降。理论证明指出，在直线趋向自身端点的路径下，不会出现新的竞争者；因此观察到的竞争者进入表明实际推理路径偏离直线。最后分析最终端点与输出令牌排名之间的关系，发现排名越低的输出令牌对应的端点余弦距离越远。该研究是分析性的，不提出新的模型结构、训练目标或服务系统。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将中间残差状态与自身最终状态及其他上下文的最终状态库进行比较，跟踪‘自身端点的偏好’和竞争端点集的变化。；提出一个简单高维模型分离范数、方向对齐和端点几何的作用，解释渐进方向变化如何导致竞争急剧减少。；证明在欧氏距离或余弦距离下，沿直线趋向自身端点不会引入新的竞争者，观察到的进入意味着对直线收敛的偏离。；发现输出令牌排名较低的端点倾向于具有更大的余弦距离，将残差几何与输出组织联系起来。

Abstract: Transformer language models build predictions through successive residual updates, but how their representations become specific to an eventual outcome remains unclear. We study this process by comparing intermediate residual states with their own final states and an empirical bank of final states from other contexts. Across six pretrained language models, the own endpoint becomes preferable to the average alternative early, while many individual endpoints remain closer. These competing sets generally shrink with depth, but their membership changes and their surviving endpoints need not become more similar to one another. Directional alignment and endpoint rank can therefore improve while Euclidean distance to the final state changes little. We develop a simple high-dimensional model that separates the roles of norm, alignment, and endpoint geometry, showing how gradual directional changes can produce sharp reductions in competition. We also prove that a straight path toward the own endpoint cannot introduce new competitors under either Euclidean or cosine distance; observed entries thus establish departures from straight-line convergence. Finally, endpoints associated with lower-ranked output tokens tend to lie farther away in cosine distance across all studied models, connecting residual geometry to output organization. Together, these findings characterize increasing geometric specificity during transformer inference and explain why distance, competitor count, and concentration of the surviving endpoints provide distinct views of that process.

</details>


### [8] [Thinking in Depth, Speaking Directly: Recurrent Latent Reasoning for Paralinguistically Grounded Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37818)
*Shengbo Cai,Yuxiang Wang,Jingran Xie,Zhisheng Zhang,Shun Lei,Di Cao,Teddy Sun,Zhiyong Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoopSLM 用循环 Transformer 的潜空间推理替代显式 CoT 做情感化语音对话：复用 decoder block 多轮精炼隐状态并反复注入声学 grounding，配合“先学推理、再学回应”的两阶段训练，在 EchoMind 上 reasoning accuracy 提升 20+ 点、token 减少 64.5%、延迟减半，且比 Qwen3-Omni-Thinking 延迟低 34 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式 Chain-of-Thought 虽能提升副语言感知，却无法保证声学线索在回复规划中被有效使用（perception-reasoning gap），且显式 CoT 难以用词语完整表达声学线索并带来额外推理延迟。

Method: LoopSLM 以 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B 为基座，核心是将 looped Transformer 结构用于潜空间推理：不再显式生成 CoT token，而是复用同一个 decoder block，对隐状态进行多轮（多次 pass）迭代精炼。每一轮迭代都重新注入声学 grounding，使语音中的副语言线索（如语调、情绪）持续参与隐状态更新，而不是仅在输入阶段编码一次。训练分为两个阶段：第一阶段学习“如何推理”，让模型在潜空间中完成副语言感知与推理；第二阶段学习“如何回应”，把推理结果转化为共情回复。两阶段解耦的设计目的是缩小 perception-reasoning gap——即模型能感知副语言线索但在回复规划中未有效使用的问题。由于推理被内化到循环隐状态中，推理阶段无需生成 CoT 文本，可直接输出回复，从而避免显式 CoT 的 token 开销与额外延迟。整体数据流为：语音输入 → 多模态编码获得语言与声学表示 → 循环 decoder block 多次精炼隐状态（每轮注入声学 grounding）→ 输出共情回复。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "reasoning accuracy", "value": "相比 CoT-SFT 基线提升超过 20 点", "setting": "EchoMind 基准"}, {"metric": "生成 token 数", "value": "减少 64.5%", "setting": "相比 CoT-SFT 基线"}, {"metric": "推理延迟", "value": "降低至基线的一半（half the latency）", "setting": "相比 CoT-SFT 基线"}, {"metric": "共情回复指标", "value": "在多数共情回复指标上优于 Qwen3-Omni-Thinking", "setting": "EchoMind 基准"}, {"metric": "推理延迟", "value": "相比 Qwen3-Omni-Thinking 低 34 倍", "setting": "EchoMind 基准"}, {"metric": "paralinguistic understanding / reasoning / reply quality", "value": "优于 Qwen2.5-Omni-7B", "setting": "EchoMind 基准"}, {"metric": "general audio benchmark accuracy", "value": "仅用对话数据训练却获得提升", "setting": "通用音频基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于 looped Transformer 的循环潜空间推理，复用同一 decoder block 多轮精炼隐状态，并在每轮注入声学 grounding；两阶段训练策略：将“学习推理”与“学习回应”解耦，缩小感知-推理鸿沟，并支持无 CoT 的直接推理；在保持副语言理解与回复质量的同时，显著降低生成 token 数与推理延迟

Abstract: Empathetic spoken dialogue requires models to use both what is said and how it is said to decide how to respond. Explicit CoT can improve paralinguistic perception and make acoustic cues more explicit in replies, yet does not ensure their effective use in response planning. We call this mismatch the perception-reasoning gap. In addition, CoT may not fully capture acoustic cues in words, and generating it adds inference latency. To address these limitations, we introduce LoopSLM, which builds on looped Transformers for latent reasoning, reusing a decoder block to refine hidden states with acoustic grounding at every pass. Its two-stage training further narrows the perception-reasoning gap by separating learning to reason from learning to respond, enabling direct inference without CoT. On EchoMind, LoopSLM improves paralinguistic understanding, reasoning, and reply quality over Qwen2.5-Omni-7B. Against the CoT-SFT baseline, LoopSLM gains over 20 points in reasoning accuracy while generating 64.5% fewer tokens at half the latency. It also outperforms Qwen3-Omni-Thinking on most empathetic reply metrics with 34x lower latency. Despite training only on dialogue data, LoopSLM improves accuracy on general audio benchmarks.

</details>


### [9] [Selecting What Matters: Semantic Compression-Guided Selective Pooling for Long-Context Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37782)
*Zifeng Cheng,Jie Zheng,Zhiwei Jiang,Shuwen Wang,Fei Shen,Shiping Ge,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SCSP 是一种无训练的长上下文嵌入框架，通过句感知分块、语义压缩提示与提示隔离注意力掩码估计 token 重要性，选择信息 token 并聚合中间层表示，缓解均值池化对显著语义的稀释，可即插即用地提升零样本与微调模型的长上下文嵌入性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档嵌入中，现有方法通常对所有 token 表示做均匀均值池化，导致显著语义信息被大量冗余或弱信息内容稀释。

Method: SCSP 是一个无训练的长上下文嵌入框架。输入为长文档，首先将文档划分为句感知的块（sentence-aware chunks），并在每个块后附加一个语义压缩提示。为了在保留文档 token 之间完整信息流的同时避免不同提示相互干扰，采用提示隔离注意力掩码（prompt-isolated attention mask）：文档 token 之间可正常注意力交互，而每个提示只被允许关注其对应的局部上下文。随后利用这些提示所激发的注意力模式来估计每个 token 的重要性，据此选择信息量高的 token，并聚合这些被选 token 的中间层表示，最终形成文档嵌入。该方法无需训练即可使用，也可即插即用地应用于零样本与微调模型，在长上下文嵌入基准上一致提升性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出无训练框架 SCSP，将长文档切分为句感知块并为每块附加语义压缩提示；设计提示隔离注意力掩码，在保留文档 token 间信息流的同时限制每个提示只关注其对应局部上下文；利用提示激发的注意力模式估计 token 重要性，选择信息 token 并聚合其中间层表示形成最终嵌入；可即插即用地集成到零样本与微调模型中，一致提升长上下文嵌入性能

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential as training-free text encoders for long-context embeddings. Existing approaches primarily improve information flow under causal attention and typically construct embeddings by uniformly averaging all token representations. However, for long documents, such mean pooling can dilute salient semantic information with abundant redundant or weakly informative content. To this end, we propose SCSP, a training-free framework that leverages semantic compression for informative token selection in long-context embedding. Specifically, SCSP first partitions a document into sentence-aware chunks and appends a semantic compression prompt to each chunk. A prompt-isolated attention mask preserves information flow among document tokens while restricting each prompt to its corresponding local context. We then use the attention patterns elicited by these prompts to estimate token importance, select informative tokens, and aggregate their intermediate-layer representations into the final embedding. Extensive experiments on long-context embedding benchmarks demonstrate that SCSP can be integrated into both zero-shot and fine-tuned models in a plug-and-play manner, consistently improving their performance.

</details>

Abstract: Existing product matching benchmarks primarily contain English-language product data and are often dominated by a single product category, such as electronics. This paper introduces this http URL Products, a bilingual German and English entity matching benchmark covering thirteen consumer product categories, including difficult-to-handle categories such as clothing and furniture. The benchmark data originates from the German price comparison platform this http URL . Following the design of WDC Products, the benchmark offers multiple variants that differ in the fraction of corner cases, the size of the development set, and the fraction of entities unseen during training. An aligned English translation of every offer keeps all pairs, splits, and labels fixed, while cross-language test sets combine German and English records within individual pairs. We validate the benchmark using six supervised matchers and zero-shot GPT-5.2 on both language versions and the cross-language test sets. The validation shows the difficulty of the benchmark. The comparison of the results on the English version of the benchmark to the results on the German version shows that most matchers score on average higher on the English version. The difference is largest for RoBERTa and HierGAT, while the zero-shot LLM runs are largely insensitive to the language. Comparing the F1 scores achieved by PLM-based matchers on the English version of this http URL Products with their performance on existing English-language benchmarks, such as WDC Products and Abt-Buy, shows that this http URL Products is more difficult than these benchmarks.

</details>


### [11] [Corpus-Guided Dual-Path Propagation for Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37661)
*Baoxian Liu,Tong Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NexusRAG针对图RAG中因仅依赖查询-句子相似度而遗漏桥接证据的问题，提出语料级实体邻域结构引导的双路径传播，并在多跳QA基准上取得一致提升，GraphRAG-Bench证据召回提高4.2-8.1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无关系图检索方法主要依赖查询-句子相似度搜索证据，可能遗漏低相似度但有价值的桥接证据，并激活与推理链无关的偶然实体。

Method: NexusRAG以无关系Tri-Graph为基础，首先通过实体共现和语义相似度联合构建语料级实体邻域结构。该结构指导两个传播路径：1）邻域约束的语义传播沿句子进行，识别出与查询相关的实体前沿；2）直接结构传播在相邻实体间进行，将前沿扩展至结构相关实体。传播后的实体权重进一步用于邻域感知的段落初始化，作为Personalized PageRank的初始分布，从而提升多跳证据检索的召回。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "证据召回率", "value": "比基线高4.2-8.1个百分点", "setting": "GraphRAG-Bench领域特定子集，所有问题类别"}, {"metric": "多跳QA性能", "value": "一致优于现有方法", "setting": "三个多跳QA基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在无关系Tri-Graph上增加由实体共现和语义相似度联合导出的语料级实体邻域结构；利用该结构引导两种互补传播路径：邻域约束的语义传播识别查询相关实体前沿，直接结构传播扩展至结构相关实体；将传播得到的实体权重用于邻域感知的段落初始化，以改进Personalized PageRank

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation supports multi-hop retrieval by organizing corpus information into graphs. However, existing relation-free graph retrieval methods rely primarily on query-sentence similarity to search for evidence. This can exclude useful bridging evidence with low query similarity and activate incidental entities unrelated to the reasoning chain. In this paper, we propose a simple and effective approach called NexusRAG, which augments the relation-free Tri-Graph with a corpus-level entity neighborhood structure derived from joint entity co-occurrence and semantic similarity. NexusRAG employs this structure to guide two complementary propagation paths: neighborhood-constrained semantic propagation through sentences identifies the query-relevant entity frontier, while direct structural propagation between neighboring entities expands that frontier to structurally related entities. The propagated entity weights also inform neighborhood-aware passage initialization for Personalized PageRank. Experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks and a domain-specific subset of GraphRAG-Bench show that NexusRAG consistently outperforms existing approaches. On the GraphRAG-Bench subset, NexusRAG achieves the highest evidence recall in all question categories, exceeding baselines by 4.2-8.1 points. The implementation code is available at this https URL .

</details>

*Yu Zhang,Pingrui Zhang,Xuefeng Bai,Pengfei Zhang,Yang Xiang,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了音视频大语言模型中的源混淆接地幻觉，通过路径干预与表示分析揭示了 question-relay 机制，并提出免训练方法 SECRET，利用对比问题表示将问题状态引导至所需模态证据。在 CMM 和 AVHBench 上跨三个 AVLLM 取得最高 +18.0 和 +7.1 个百分点的提升，并展现向开放式生成的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频大语言模型中的源混淆接地幻觉：未使用模态的线索干扰所需模态的证据，导致模型回答不受所需模态支持，降低实际应用可靠性。

Method: SECRET 是一种免训练的推理时方法。给定音频-视觉大语言模型（AVLLM），输入包含音频、视觉和文本问题。首先对模型内部进行路径干预和表示分析，识别出 question-relay 机制：问题状态既携带干扰模态的线索，也携带所需模态的证据，从而削弱所需模态证据的接地。基于此，SECRET 通过不同模态路径干预（例如切断干扰模态到问题状态的路径）诱发对比问题表示，然后利用这些对比表示将原始问题状态向所需源证据方向进行引导（steering）。该过程不更新模型参数，仅在推理时对问题状态进行表示层面的干预，以抑制跨模态干扰。最终生成解码基于被引导后的问题状态，从而提升对所需模态证据的接地能力，减少源混淆接地幻觉。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "最高 +18.0 个百分点", "setting": "相比基础模型，CMM 和 AVHBench 基准，三个 AVLLM"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "最高 +7.1 个百分点", "setting": "相比基础模型，CMM 和 AVHBench 基准，三个 AVLLM"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过路径干预与表示分析揭示了 question-relay 机制：问题状态携带干扰线索与所需源证据，干扰所需模态证据的接地；提出 SECRET（Source-Conditioned Relay Steering）：一种免训练方法，利用不同模态路径干预产生的对比问题表示，将原始问题状态引导至所需源证据；在 CMM 和 AVHBench 两个基准上跨三个 AVLLM 验证，显著缓解源混淆接地幻觉，并展示向开放式生成的泛化能力

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (AVLLMs) have made remarkable progress in multimodal understanding and reasoning through interactions among visual, auditory, and linguistic information. However, recent studies show that AVLLMs face a critical challenge: $\textbf{source-confused grounding hallucination}$, where cues from the unused modality induce responses that the required modality does not support, undermining reliability in real-world applications. Existing methods have made progress in mitigating this failure, yet how it arises from internal cross-modal interactions remains insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we conduct path-intervention and representation analyses, revealing a $\textbf{question-relay}$ mechanism: question states carry interfering cues alongside required-source evidence, undermining grounding in required-modality evidence. Cutting pathways from interfering modality to question states yields greater correct-answer logit recovery than cutting those to the generation position. Motivated by these findings, we propose $\textbf{SECRET}$ ($\textbf{S}$ourc$\textbf{E}$-$\textbf{C}$onditioned $\textbf{RE}$lay s$\textbf{T}$eering), a training-free method that mitigates cross-modal interference at the question relay. Using contrasting question representations elicited through different modality-pathway interventions, SECRET steers the original question states toward required-source evidence. Experiments on two widely adopted benchmarks CMM and AVHBench across three AVLLMs show that SECRET consistently outperforms prior training-free methods, substantially mitigating source-confused grounding hallucinations (e.g., up to +18.0 and +7.1 percentage points over base models). Modality-specific captioning further demonstrates its generalizability to open-ended generation.

</details>


### [13] [E-MoE: Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts for Non-Factorized Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37533)
*Arseny Ivanov,Alexander Kolesov,Alexander Korotin,Ivan Oseledets,Mikhail Goncharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: E-MoE通过MoE专家路由产生离散共享隐变量，实现非因子化扩散语言模型的反向过程，在不增加活跃参数的情况下提升少步生成质量，并在合成基准、二值化MNIST和LM1B上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型（MDM）的反向过程通常按位置因子化，限制了少步生成质量，而连续高斯隐变量方法易出现后验塌缩。

Method: E-MoE将掩码扩散模型（MDM）的反向过程设计为混合专家（MoE）骨干网络专家路由决策所定义的离散共享隐变量上的因子分布混合。输入序列经过MoE骨干的前向计算，每个位置的token根据专家路由分配到不同专家；这些专家的路由决策构成一个离散的共享隐变量，捕获跨位置相关性。在去噪步骤中，反向过程基于该共享隐变量对各个位置的条件分布进行混合，从而在不增加活跃参数数量的前提下实现非因子化建模。模型通过掩码扩散目标进行训练，优化反向过程的参数。推理时，模型逐步去掩码多个token，利用专家路由的离散隐变量增强少步生成的质量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "少步生成质量", "value": "提升（未提供具体数值）", "setting": "合成多模态基准、二值化MNIST和LM1B数据集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将反向过程构建为基于MoE骨干专家路由决策的离散共享隐变量的因子分布混合，不增加活跃参数。；避免连续隐变量方法中的后验塌缩问题。；在少步生成场景下提升非因子化扩散语言模型的生成质量。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate sequences by progressively unmasking several tokens per denoising step, but their reverse process is typically factorized over positions, limiting sample quality in the few-step regime where diffusion's speed advantage over autoregressive decoding matters most. A recent line of work introduces a continuous Gaussian latent, trained as a variational autoencoder, to capture correlations across positions, but such approaches are prone to posterior collapse, where the latent is silently ignored. We propose Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts (E-MoE), which builds the reverse process as a mixture of factorized distributions over a discrete shared latent given by the expert-routing decisions of a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) backbone, without increasing active parameters over the factorized baseline. Across synthetic multi-modal benchmarks, binarized MNIST, and LM1B, E-MoE improves few-step generation over factorized baselines.

</details>


### [14] [From Dissonance to Orchestration: Teacher Intervention in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37510)
*Yuhao Wang,Ruiyang Ren,Yinan Zhang,Ruiqing Zhang,Jing Liu,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究 on-policy distillation 中教师干预的调度问题，指出仅看 rollout 质量不够，更深的干预会带来收益递减和 off-policy 负荷上升。提出 MAESTRO，用局部策略不一致性分数联合自适应教师接管的时机与生成时长，在 8 个数学推理基准上对 0.6B/1.7B Qwen3 学生取得最高 macro-average 准确率，并将平均训练响应长度减少 67.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 on-policy distillation 中，教师干预会改变学生学习所用的分布；仅用 rollout 质量作为分配教师指导的标准不完整，需要联合自适应决定教师何时接管以及生成多久。

Method: MAESTRO 面向 on-policy distillation 训练过程。输入是学生模型在自身策略下生成的推理轨迹；教师模型对轨迹提供反馈或干预。MAESTRO 的核心是 policy disagreement score，它由两部分组成：教师加权候选覆盖（衡量教师对候选 token 的偏好覆盖程度）与局部 token 分布相似度（衡量学生局部分布与教师分布的接近程度），并在推理段落（paragraph）内聚合，得到局部策略不一致性信号。该分数用于联合自适应两个决策：教师何时接管（干预位置）以及教师生成多长时间（干预深度）。训练目标仍是在学生自身轨迹上利用教师反馈进行蒸馏，但干预调度由 disagreement 驱动，以控制 off-policy 负荷。推理或服务路径与标准学生模型一致，无额外运行时开销；实验在 8 个数学推理基准上对 0.6B 与 1.7B Qwen3 学生模型进行验证。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro-average accuracy", "value": "最高（具体数值未在摘要给出）", "setting": "8 个数学推理基准，0.6B 与 1.7B Qwen3 学生模型；1.7B 学生在每个基准上领先"}, {"metric": "average training response length", "value": "相对标准 OPD 减少 67.3%", "setting": "标准 OPD 基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过受控实验揭示更深教师干预在 rollout 准确率上收益递减，同时增加 off-policy 负荷；在受限 rollout horizon 探针中，峰值学生准确率与性能保持偏好不同的干预强度，且首选干预深度和位置因基准而异。；提出 MAESTRO，使用局部策略不一致性联合自适应教师接管的时机与生成时长。；设计 policy disagreement score，结合教师加权候选覆盖与局部分布相似度，并在推理段落内聚合。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own reasoning trajectories using feedback from a stronger teacher. Teacher interventions can improve these trajectories, but also change the distribution on which the student learns. Our controlled studies show that rollout quality alone is an incomplete criterion for allocating teacher guidance. Deeper intervention yields diminishing gains in rollout accuracy while increasing off-policy load. In a training probe with a restricted rollout horizon, peak student accuracy and performance retention favor different intervention strengths. The preferred intervention depth and placement also vary across benchmarks. These findings motivate MAESTRO, which uses local policy disagreement to jointly adapt when the teacher takes over and how long it generates. Its {policy disagreement score} combines teacher-weighted candidate coverage with local distribution similarity and is aggregated within reasoning paragraphs. Across eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, MAESTRO achieves the highest macro-average accuracy among the compared methods for both 0.6B and 1.7B Qwen3 students, with the 1.7B student leading on every benchmark. MAESTRO also reduces average training response length by 67.3\% relative to standard OPD. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [15] [Learning to Retrieve Missing Evidence for Long-Term Memory QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37443)
*Yi-Xuan Deng,Yi Zhang,Wei Liu,Chao Xue,Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MERA 针对长时记忆问答中证据分散且问题缺乏定位线索的问题，将全局记忆与证据状态分离，用强化学习训练 0.6B 轻量规划器根据已核实证据迭代生成检索查询以补全缺失证据。在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 上，0.6B 规划器配合 Qwen3-30B 分别达到 77.40% 和 71.29% 准确率，超过未经过检索接地训练的 30B 规划器，且多轮检索将累积证据召回率从 55.5% 提升至 80.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时记忆问答中，回答问题所需的证据分散在相距较远的对话轮次，且问题本身缺少定位这些证据的线索；需要让检索过程能够基于已找到的证据动态决定下一步检索什么，从而补全缺失证据。

Method: MERA 将记忆划分为两部分：全局可搜索记忆和问题特定证据状态。全局记忆不受证据状态限制，可被任意检索访问；证据状态则记录已经过核实的证据，并作为后续检索的条件。系统包含一个轻量级规划器，输入为当前问题、已核实的证据状态以及全局记忆的检索接口，输出下一轮检索查询。规划器通过强化学习训练，奖励与是否找回此前缺失的证据挂钩，从而学习生成能补全缺失证据的查询。检索到的证据经过验证后加入证据状态，形成迭代循环：证据状态引导查询生成，查询从全局记忆取回新证据，新证据再次更新证据状态。最终，累积的证据被送入答案生成模块产生回答。该架构不限制对全局记忆的访问，因此避免了证据状态可能造成的过早收敛，同时利用已有证据线索动态调整检索方向。实验使用 Qwen3-30B 或 GPT-4o-mini 作为证据处理与答案生成的骨干，规划器为 0.6B 参数，在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 上评估答案准确率和累积证据召回率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "answer accuracy", "value": "77.40%", "setting": "LoCoMo 数据集，Qwen3-30B 用于证据处理与答案生成，0.6B 规划器"}, {"metric": "answer accuracy", "value": "71.29%", "setting": "LongMemEval-S 数据集，Qwen3-30B 用于证据处理与答案生成，0.6B 规划器"}, {"metric": "answer accuracy 提升", "value": "+4.10%", "setting": "LoCoMo，相较于未经过检索接地训练的 30B 规划器"}, {"metric": "answer accuracy 提升", "value": "+3.96%", "setting": "LongMemEval-S，相较于未经过检索接地训练的 30B 规划器"}, {"metric": "cumulative evidence recall", "value": "55.5% -> 80.5%", "setting": "LoCoMo，后续检索轮次累积证据召回率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: MERA 框架将全局可搜索记忆与问题相关的证据状态分离，已核实证据仅用于引导后续检索而不限制对全局记忆的访问；通过强化学习训练轻量级规划器，以找回此前缺失证据作为奖励信号来优化查询生成；在多轮检索中累积证据召回率从 55.5% 提升到 80.5%，验证了证据状态引导检索的有效性

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language models to use past interactions in future conversations. However, evidence needed to answer a question may be scattered across distant turns, while the question itself omits clues needed to locate it. Retrieved facts can reveal these clues, motivating retrieval decisions conditioned on evidence already found. We introduce MERA (Missing-Evidence Retrieval Augmentation), which separates globally searchable memory from a question-specific evidence state. Verified evidence guides subsequent retrieval without restricting access to the global memory. We train a lightweight planner through reinforcement learning, rewarding queries that recover previously missing evidence. MERA achieves strong answer accuracy across Qwen3-30B and GPT-4o-mini backbones. With Qwen3-30B for evidence processing and answer generation, the trained 0.6B planner achieves 77.40% accuracy on LoCoMo and 71.29% on LongMemEval-S, exceeding a 30B planner without retrieval-grounded training by 4.10% and 3.96%, respectively. On LoCoMo, later retrieval rounds increase cumulative evidence recall from 55.5% to 80.5%.

</details>


### [16] [Compiling Learning Problems into Adaptation Programs for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37371)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文将模型适配重构为“适配编译”：先学习预测候选更新程序在多目标行为（获取、迁移、边界性、保留性）上的反事实响应曲面，再在适配前选择程序，避免逐次穷举搜索。Llama-3.1-8B 上选择结果接近穷举搜索且优于默认配方，Gemma-2-9B 复现出可预测的程序异质性与选择空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型适配通常依赖固定配方，而不同更新程序会产生显著不同的行为结果；论文要解决的问题是如何在不做穷举搜索/反复试错的前提下，为每个学习问题预测并选择最优适配程序（在获取、迁移、边界性、保留性等多目标之间权衡），并让这份经验可跨任务复用。

Method: 论文提出 adaptation compilation 框架：输入是适配前可获得的信息（pre-adaptation information）与具体学习 episode 的特征；编译器不直接反复搜索程序，而是从先前适配经验中学习一个预测器，输出候选适配程序上的向量值反事实响应曲面，每个候选程序对应一个关于 acquisition、transfer、boundedness、preservation 的多维预期效果向量。该曲面捕获多个行为后果而非单一赢家或标量分数，因此可在下游优先级改变时复用。流程为：1）输入学习 episode 与候选适配程序集合；2）编译器预测各候选程序的响应向量；3）在适配开始前依据当前决策目标从预测曲面中选择程序；4）仅在选中程序上执行实际模型适配。训练目标是从历史适配结果学习预测候选程序的多维反事实结果，使编译器能泛化到新 episode。推理/使用路径是先预测与选择，再执行一次适配，从而把搜索成本摊销到多个相关学习问题上。实验在 Llama-3.1-8B 上进行，覆盖五类学习任务，并与穷举搜索、全局默认、目标特定默认比较；在 Gemma-2-9B 上做复现，发现程序异质性和选择空间存在，但离开强默认时需显式建模不确定性。摘要未披露具体网络结构、优化器、超参数、数据规模、硬件或延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "compiler-selected programs 相对穷举搜索的效果", "value": "接近 exhaustive search（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "Llama-3.1-8B，五种学习类型"}, {"metric": "相对全局默认与目标特定默认的效果", "value": "优于 global defaults 与 objective-specific defaults（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "Llama-3.1-8B，五种学习类型"}, {"metric": "程序异质性与选择空间", "value": "在 Gemma-2-9B 上保持，但利用该空间需考虑不确定性", "setting": "Gemma-2-9B 复现实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 adaptation compilation：把“模型应在哪里、如何、以多大程度适配”重新表述为联合预测与决策问题。；训练一个编译器，从先前的适配经验中预测候选程序的反事实响应曲面，输出向量值（对 acquisition、transfer、boundedness、preservation 的预期影响），而非单一标量分数或单一胜者。；在适配开始前选择程序，而不是为每个学习 episode 重新搜索候选程序，从而摊销适配搜索成本。；预测的几何结构可复用：在改变下游优先级时无需重新训练即可重新选择程序。；在五种学习类型上验证首选程序随 episode 显著变化且可从适配前信息预测；在 Llama-3.1-8B 上接近穷举搜索并优于全局/目标特定默认；在 Gemma-2-9B 上复现程序异质性与选择空间，并指出离开强默认时需要建模不确定性。

Abstract: Model adaptation is typically governed by a fixed recipe, even though different update programs can produce substantially different behavioral outcomes. We introduce adaptation compilation, which reframes where, how, and to what extent a model should adapt as a joint prediction and decision problem. Rather than searching over candidate programs anew for each learning episode, a compiler learns from prior adaptations to predict a vector-valued counterfactual response surface over candidate programs---their expected effects on acquisition, transfer, boundedness, and preservation---and selects a program before adaptation begins. Because this predicted geometry captures multiple behavioral consequences rather than a single winner or scalar score, it can be reused under different downstream priorities without retraining. Across five learning types, preferred programs vary meaningfully across episodes, and this variation is predictable from pre-adaptation information. On Llama-3.1-8B, compiler-selected programs approach exhaustive search while outperforming global and objective-specific defaults. Replication on Gemma-2-9B preserves program heterogeneity and selection headroom, but shows that exploiting this headroom requires accounting for uncertainty when departing from strong defaults. Together, these results show that adaptation search can be amortized across related learning problems, turning prior adaptation experience into a basis for deciding how future learning should occur.

</details>


### [17] [LLM unbranding: Erasing Commercial Identity while Preserving Generic Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37127)
*Kajetan Ożóg,Alicja Wojciechowska,Dawid Malarz,Paweł Batorski,Artur Kasymov,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文定义 LLM Unbranding 任务并构建品牌评测数据集，系统评测现有机器遗忘方法，提出推理时方法 MUTE，通过迭代优化系统指令在无需参数更新的情况下中和文本品牌特征并保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在生成文本中频繁输出品牌特征性的 trade dress（口号、风格标记、特征语言），可能导致商标稀释、错误归属和品牌诽谤；现有机器遗忘方法难以选择性地从文本中消除品牌身份同时保持模型通用能力。

Method: MUTE 是推理时方法，不修改模型参数。其核心流程为：给定输入提示与 LLM 原始输出，系统在推理阶段维护可优化的系统指令（system instructions）；通过迭代精炼循环，评估当前系统指令下模型输出中品牌 trade dress（如口号、特征语言、风格标记）的残留程度，并据此优化系统指令，使模型在不进行参数更新的情况下减少品牌泄漏。该方法将去品牌化问题转化为对系统级提示的优化问题，避免了对模型权重进行脆弱且可能损害通用能力的遗忘更新。论文同时构建了多领域品牌评测数据集，用于评估现有机器遗忘方法与 MUTE 在选择性文本去品牌化及通用能力保持方面的表现。具体迭代优化算法、损失函数、提示搜索空间及评测指标细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化定义 LLM Unbranding 新任务，聚焦文本输出中的 trade dress 管理。；构建包含多商业领域知名品牌的 LLM Unbranding 评测数据集。；系统评测现有最先进机器遗忘模型在该基准上的选择性地文本去品牌化局限。；提出 MUTE：一种推理时方法，通过迭代优化系统指令来中和文本 trade dress，无需脆弱参数更新。

Abstract: Establishing unbranding as a critical practice to prevent visual logos from acquiring negative connotations is standard in image generation. Large Language Models (LLMs) now face a parallel and emerging challenge. These models frequently generate brand descriptions within diverse contexts. This frequency introduces significant risks, such as trademark dilution, false attribution, and brand defamation. In response, we formally define the novel task of LLM Unbranding. We specifically address the complex challenge of managing trade dress within textual outputs. This involves neutralizing characteristic language, slogans, and stylistic markers that define brand identity. Crucially, these elements are less evident than explicit visual logos. To benchmark this task, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation dataset incorporating prominent brands from multiple commercial domains. We rigorously evaluate existing state-of-the-art machine unlearning models using this benchmark. This evaluation identifies their limitations in selective textual unbranding. Finally, we propose MUTE, a novel inference-time method that effectively neutralizes textual trade dress while preserving the LLM's general capabilities and utility. By leveraging an iterative refinement loop, MUTE systematically optimizes system instructions to safely eliminate brand leakage without requiring fragile parameter updates. Code and dataset: The evaluation dataset and code for LLM Unbranding are available at this https URL . The implementation of MUTE is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [18] [LatCom: Cross-Agent Latent Compression for Efficient Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37017)
*Shinan Zhang,Tao Zhang,Qihui Zhu,Mengjie Zhang,Dong Jin,Yunpeng Hou,Shuangwu Chen,Xiaobin Tan,Quan Zheng,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatCom提出跨智能体隐空间压缩框架，将多发送方latent压缩为固定数量、接收方可读且任务相关的slot，通过单发送方可读性学习与多发送方融合学习两阶段训练去除跨智能体冗余。基于Qwen3-4B的实验显示相较LatentMAS平均2.46倍推理加速、输出token减少70.3%且准确率相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体隐空间协作中直接转发所有发送方latent导致接收端上下文随智能体数量和推理长度增长，计算、内存与协作延迟上升；而现有latent压缩方法对每个发送方独立压缩后再拼接，未能消除跨智能体冗余。

Method: LatCom面向基于LLM的多智能体隐空间协作：多个发送方智能体各自产生隐状态latent，直接转发会使接收端上下文规模随智能体数量与推理长度增长。LatCom引入压缩器，将多个发送方latent映射到固定数量的、接收方可读且任务相关的slot，从而控制接收端上下文规模。压缩器训练分两阶段：第一阶段为单发送方可读性学习，使压缩后的latent能被冻结的接收方模型解释，建立latent接口；第二阶段为多发送方融合学习，训练压缩器融合来自不同智能体的互补证据并去除跨智能体冗余。优化目标不是重建全部发送方隐状态，而是最大化接收端任务效用。推理时接收方仅消费固定数量的压缩slot，从而降低计算、内存与协作延迟。实验基于Qwen3-4B在多个基准上验证。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理速度", "value": "平均2.46x加速", "setting": "与LatentMAS对比，Qwen3-4B，多基准测试"}, {"metric": "输出token使用量", "value": "减少70.3%", "setting": "与LatentMAS对比，Qwen3-4B，多基准测试"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "保持相当水平", "setting": "与LatentMAS对比，Qwen3-4B，多基准测试"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出LatCom跨智能体隐空间压缩框架，将多个发送方latent映射为固定数量、接收方可读且任务相关的slot；以接收端任务效用为优化目标，而非重建全部发送方隐状态；两阶段训练：先进行单发送方可读性学习建立冻结接收方可解释的latent接口，再进行多发送方融合学习以合并互补证据并去除冗余

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) increasingly use latent collaboration to avoid the information loss and repeated encoding-decoding overhead of natural-language communication. However, directly forwarding all sender latents makes the receiver-side context scale with both the number of agents and the reasoning length, increasing computation, memory usage, and collaboration latency. A natural solution is latent compression. But we find that cross-agent redundancy remains unresolved in existing latent compression approaches, which typically compress each sender independently and then concatenate the results. We propose LatCom, a cross-agent latent compression framework for efficient multi-agent latent collaboration. LatCom maps multiple sender latents into a fixed number of receiver-readable and task-relevant slots. Rather than reconstructing all sender hidden states, it optimizes the compressed latents for receiver-side task utility. LatCom trains the compressor in two stages: single-sender readability learning first establishes a latent interface interpretable by the frozen receiver, and multi-sender fusion learning then trains the compressor to fuse complementary evidence and remove redundancy across agents. Experiments on multiple benchmarks with Qwen3-4B show that LatCom achieves an average 2.46x inference speed-up over LatentMAS and reduces output token usage by 70.3% while maintaining comparable average accuracy.

</details>


### [19] [Selecting The Most Informative Tokens in Natural Language Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37040)
*Federico Torrielli,Gianluca Barmina,Andrea Blasi Núñez,Amon Rapp,Luigi Di Caro,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何高效选择自然语言自编码器中最具信息量的 token 位置进行解释，用于 LLM 安全审计。基于 470 万条解释的实验表明，仅使用聊天结构的排序器在多数任务上优于计算信号，解释 5% 的位置即可保留几乎全部威胁检测成功率，且预训练 verbalizer 可跨模型恢复隐藏词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自然语言自编码器中，如何在不执行昂贵模型前向计算的情况下，选择最具信息量的 token 位置进行解释，从而高效审计 LLM 的潜在威胁（如提示注入、意图隐藏）。

Method: 论文使用自然语言自编码器（NLAE）将语言模型内部激活翻译成可读解释。系统面临的核心问题是解释所有 token 位置成本过高。为此，论文提出用排序器对 token 位置打分，选择 top-k 比例（如 5%）进行解释。排序器仅基于聊天结构特征（如角色标记、轮次边界、消息长度等），不依赖模型前向计算。对比基线包括模型计算信号（如激活范数、梯度等）。评估在提示注入和意图隐藏数据集上进行，成功定义为获得关于威胁的文本解释。此外，论文测试预训练 verbalizer 跨模型恢复隐藏词汇的能力，无需额外训练。整体推理流程：输入对话和模型激活 -> 轻量级排序器选位置 -> 仅对这些位置运行 verbalizer 生成解释 -> 审计员阅读。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "威胁检测成功率保留比例", "value": "约 95% 以上（解释 5% 的位置 vs 解释 100% 的位置）", "setting": "在 4 个数据集中 3 个上，评估指标为获得关于威胁的解释的成功率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出使用仅基于聊天结构的排序器来选择解释位置，无需模型前向计算。；在 470 万条解释上系统比较聊天结构信号与模型计算信号（如激活幅度、梯度）在威胁检测中的有效性。；发现解释 5% 的位置即可保留几乎全部（解释所有位置）的威胁检测成功率，并展示收益随审计任务变化。；证明预训练 verbalizer 无需额外训练即可恢复微调后模型学会隐藏的词汇，呈跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Natural language autoencoders translate a language model's internal activations into readable explanations. Explaining every token position is costly. Which positions should an auditor inspect to understand a potential threat? We study this question across $4.7$ million explanations on prompt injection and concealment. We compare signals from model computation with a ranker trained only on chat structure. Chat structure usually selects more relevant explanations than the computational signals, without requiring a model forward pass for position selection. On three of four datasets, explaining just $5\%$ of positions retains nearly all of the success rate from explaining every position, where success means obtaining an explanation about the threat. The benefit varies with the audit task. We also show that pretrained verbalizers recover words that models have learned to conceal through fine-tuning, without additional verbalizer training. These results identify where auditors can concentrate explanation generation and show that useful explanations can extend beyond the model a verbalizer was trained to describe.

</details>


### [20] [SRJudge: Empowering Large Language Models with Selective Reasoning for Fine-Grained Knowledge Concept Tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36982)
*Zhiwei Yang,Jiahua Yang,Huiru Lin,Xing Chen,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SRJudge 提出 Selector-Reasoner-Judge 三阶段框架，用 BERT 类小模型筛出 top-K 候选、轻量 LLM 强化学习推理、更大 LLM 评判，解决大规模候选集中细粒度知识概念标注的决策空间过大问题，并发布了生物与物理两个数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在面对大规模候选概念集合时，因决策空间维度高而难以选中正确的细粒度知识概念标签。

Method: SRJudge 由三阶段级联构成。Stage 1 Selector：使用小语言模型（如 BERT）对给定教育内容进行微调，输出候选概念集合的 top-K 短名单，因为 top-K 预测在多数情况覆盖正确概念，从而缩小后续推理的决策空间。Stage 2 Reasoner：使用轻量 LLM 对短名单中的候选进行细化推理，并集成改进的强化学习策略，包含动态任务特定奖励函数与剪枝机制，以对齐人类推理偏好并避免冗余候选。Stage 3 Judge：使用更大的 LLM 评估 Reasoner 产生的推理过程与解释的整体合理性，并据此确定最终的知识概念标签。整体数据流为：输入教育内容 → Selector 生成 top-K 候选 → Reasoner 在短名单上推理 → Judge 评估并输出最终概念。训练目标包括对 Selector 的监督微调、对 Reasoner 的强化学习优化（带动态奖励和剪枝），以及 Judge 的推理合理性评估；推理时按阶段顺序执行，逐级过滤候选并提升决策质量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "一致优于 state-of-the-art baselines", "setting": "在基准数据集及 S_Bio、S_Phy 数据集上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 三阶段 Select-Reason-Judge (SRJudge) 框架：先筛选 top-K 候选，再用轻量 LLM 推理，最后由更大 LLM 评判。；Selector 使用微调的小语言模型如 BERT 将候选概念压缩为 top-K 短名单，从而缩小决策空间。；Reasoner 采用轻量 LLM，并结合改进的强化学习策略、动态任务特定奖励函数和剪枝机制，以对齐人类推理偏好。；Judge 模块使用更大 LLM 评估推理过程与解释的合理性并决定最终输出。；构建两个高质量数据集 S_Bio 和 S_Phy 用于验证。

Abstract: Knowledge concept tagging aims to assign specific concept or topic labels to educational content, which is essential for both educators and learners in traditional and online teaching practices. Recent work has explored large language models (LLMs) for this task, achieving promising performance. However, LLMs still struggle to select the correct concept from a large-scale candidate set due to the high dimensionality of the decision space. In this paper, we propose a novel three-stage Select-Reason-Judge (SRJudge) framework, which empowers LLMs with selective reasoning capability for fine-grained knowledge concept tagging. Specifically, the Selector in Stage 1 first narrows the candidate concepts to a top-K shortlist by fine-tuning a small language model (SLM), e.g., BERT, since the top-$K$ predictions hit the correct concept in most cases, thereby reducing the decision space of correct candidates. Next, the Stage 2 Reasoner employs a lightweight LLM for refined reasoning over the shortlisted candidates. It further integrates an improved reinforcement learning strategy with a dynamic task-specific reward function and a pruning mechanism to better align with human reasoning preferences. Finally, a larger LLM acts as a judger that evaluates the overall rationality of the reasoning process and its explanations to determine the final output. In addition, we construct two high-quality datasets for further validation, i.e., the biology dataset S_Bio and the physics dataset S_Phy. Experimental results demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across benchmark datasets, verifying its effectiveness and superiority. Resources are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [21] [AMU:Admission and Memory Update for Personalized Conversations---Structured Memory with SLM Guided Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36976)
*Tao Hwang,Yishi Diao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AMU 提出一种面向个性化对话的写入时记忆控制框架：先用结构化过滤决定什么能进入长期记忆，再以 SLM 引导的存储管理决定记录是单独保存、作为重复丢弃还是作为更新融合，从而减少瞬时请求、重复与过期状态对记忆的污染；在受控的写入与检索实验中，记忆更干净且更可检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统侧重存储、检索与整合，写入阶段缺乏控制，导致瞬时请求、重复表述与过期用户状态进入记忆并在后续被检索，污染个性化效果

Method: AMU 是一个面向个性化对话的写入时记忆控制框架，由两部分组成。第一部分是结构化记忆过滤：在对话内容进入记忆之前先经过结构化过滤模块，由该模块决定哪些候选内容具备被写入记忆的资格（admission），从而在源头拦截瞬时请求与不适合长期保留的信息。第二部分是 SLM 向导的存储管理：对已经通过准入的记录，使用小语言模型（SLM）驱动的存储管理决策，判断该记录应当被单独存储、因重复而被丢弃，还是作为对已有记忆的更新进行融合。经过这两级写入控制后，记忆库保持更干净一致的状态，后续检索时用于个性化回复的记忆条目质量更高。论文在受控的记忆写入与检索实验设置下评测了该框架，结果显示其维护的个性化记忆更干净、更可检索。摘要未披露具体输入表示、模型结构细节、训练目标、推理/服务流程以及规模与延迟等信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 以写入时控制为核心的记忆管理框架：结构化记忆过滤决定哪些内容可进入记忆；SLM向导的存储管理决策，对已准入记录在单独存储、判为重复丢弃、或作为更新融合三种操作间进行选择；在受控的记忆写入与检索实验设置下验证记忆更干净且更可检索

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become the foundation of personalized assistants, but maintaining persistent user memory across long-term interactions remains challenging. Existing memory systems often focus on storage, retrieval, or consolidation, while memory writing remains less controlled: transient requests, duplicate statements, and outdated user states may enter memory and later be retrieved for personalization. In this paper, we present AMU: Admission and Memory Update for Personalized Conversations, an SLM-guided (Small language model guided) structured framework for writing-time memory control. AMU uses structured memory filtering to decide what should enter memory and SLM-guided storage management to determine whether an admitted record should be stored separately, discarded as a duplicate, or fused as an update. We evaluate AMU in a controlled memory writing and retrieval setting. Experimental results show that AMU maintains cleaner and more retrievable personalized memories.

</details>


### [22] [Chinese-Jev: Bringing System One Model to Chinese-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36965)
*Zexiao Wang,Zihao Zhang,Xudong Wang,Pan Wang,Ziyi Ye,Haoyu Zhao,Zuxuan Wu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Chinese-Jev 是面向中文决策任务的 System One 模型，用统一数据协议将异构标注转成候选概率目标，采用轻量编码器打分与两阶段训练。第一阶段预训练在通用任务上超过 Jev 1.24% 准确率并加速 20.3x；领域微调后在医学上超过 Jev 4.0%，专门领域平均达到 Jev 92% 准确率，加速 17x、平均延迟 15 ms，并展示 INT8 移动端约 1 秒/决策部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 System One 模型如 Jev 在中文决策任务上准确率有限，且预训练分布与中文下游场景存在错位，难以在通用和专门领域提供高效准确的中文决策能力。

Method: Chinese-Jev 采用编码器-only 轻量骨干网络处理中文文本输入，输出用于候选选项评分的表示。数据侧，统一数据处理协议把来自不同领域、不同题型的异构中文标注转换为候选选项上的概率目标，使得模型可以用共享的决策导向训练目标进行训练。训练分两阶段：先在 1000 万条通用示例上预训练，以对齐中文下游场景，再分别针对医疗、法律、金融进行领域微调。推理时模型对每个候选答案打分并选择最高分选项，无需自回归生成。为支持端侧高效推理，模型可进行 INT8 量化并部署在移动设备上。论文通过 CJ-Bench 评估通用与领域特定设置下的决策准确率和校准。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "超过闭源 Jev 模型 1.24%", "setting": "通用领域任务，第一阶段预训练后"}, {"metric": "速度提升", "value": "20.3x", "setting": "相对 Jev 模型，第一阶段预训练后"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "相对 Jev 提升 4.0%", "setting": "医学领域，领域微调后"}, {"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "达到 Jev 平均准确率的 92%", "setting": "专门领域，领域微调后"}, {"metric": "速度提升", "value": "17x", "setting": "专门领域，领域微调后"}, {"metric": "平均延迟", "value": "15 ms/example", "setting": "专门领域，领域微调后"}, {"metric": "移动端推理延迟", "value": "约 1.0 秒/决策", "setting": "INT8 量化模型，移动设备部署"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 统一数据处理协议，将异构中文标注转换为候选选项上的概率目标，支持跨领域与跨题型的共享训练目标。；轻量编码器骨干网络结合决策导向训练，学习对候选答案进行打分，替代生成式语言模型。；两阶段训练：先在 1000 万条通用语料上预训练，再分别针对医疗、法律、金融领域微调。；提出 Chinese-Jev Bench（CJ-Bench），评估通用与领域特定中文决策准确率和校准。；INT8 量化模型实现移动端部署，单次决策延迟约 1.0 秒。

Abstract: System One models such as Jev offer an efficient alternative to generative language models for tasks that require decisions rather than open-ended responses. However, existing Jev models exhibit limited Chinese-language decision accuracy, restricting their utility in both general and specialized settings. In this paper, we introduce Chinese-Jev, a System One model that addresses this gap through a unified data processing and training pipeline. Our data processing protocol converts heterogeneous Chinese-language annotations into probability targets over candidate options, enabling a shared training formulation across domains and question formats. To enable efficient inference, Chinese-Jev adopts a lightweight encoder-only backbone for text encoding and learns to score candidate answers through decision-oriented training. To address the misalignment between the pre-training distribution and downstream Chinese-language scenarios, we first train the model on a general-purpose corpus of 10 million examples, then fine-tune it separately for the medical, legal, and financial domains. To evaluate decision accuracy and calibration in both general and domain-specific Chinese-language settings, we introduce Chinese-Jev Bench (CJ-Bench). After first-stage pre-training, Chinese-Jev exceeds the accuracy of the closed-source Jev model by 1.24% on general-domain tasks while achieving a 20.3x speedup. Subsequent domain-specific fine-tuning yields a 4.0% accuracy improvement over Jev in medicine and achieves 92% of Jev's average accuracy across specialized domains, with a 17x speedup and an average latency of only 15 ms per example. We further demonstrate on-device deployment of an INT8-quantized model on mobile devices, achieving an inference latency of approximately 1.0 second per decision. The project is available at this https URL .

</details>

Abstract: Reproducibility is essential for scientific research, yet prior work shows that LLM outputs vary with hardware and batching. We identify an overlooked factor: the hidden injection of the current date into system prompts, which users cannot control and which changes every day. Across 9 recent LLMs and 6 datasets spanning multiple-choice QA (MCQA), math reasoning, code generation, and machine translation, performance varies solely with the current date, with deltas of up to 6% on MCQA, 14% on math reasoning, 7% on code generation, and 2.84 BLEU on machine translation. Model rankings also shift, affecting leaderboards. This date effect exceeds other sources of non-determinism, such as batch size and numerical precision. Standard prompting techniques -- chain-of-thought and few-shot prompting -- do not reduce the sensitivity; chain-of-thought even amplifies it. Our findings underscore the need for careful evaluation protocols to ensure reproducibility and fair comparisons in LLM research.

</details>

*Ayan Sengupta,Vaibhav Seth,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CaRE-KD提出置信度门控的知识蒸馏框架，用token级自适应KL切换与batch级认知拒绝机制解决教师模型不可靠问题，在八组师生模型、十一个基准上一致优于强基线，最高提升指令遵循ROUGE-L +3.2、MBPP pass@1 +2.1、GSM8k +1.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏默认教师模型为可靠预言机，但LLM教师预测常呈现高熵与幻觉，导致蒸馏后学生模型校准先验退化。

Method: CaRE-KD在标准知识蒸馏流程上引入双粒度置信度感知机制。输入为教师与学生模型在同一token序列上的输出分布；token级模块CaRE-Divergence根据教师-学生置信度动态选择Forward KL或Reverse KL：当教师置信度高于学生时使用Forward KL以学习教师分布，当学生置信度更高时使用Reverse KL以保留学生已校准的分布，从而在token粒度上自适应优化。batch级模块Revival在批次维度比较教师与学生的认知不确定性，若教师比学生更不确定，则抑制该批次更新，过滤不可靠监督。训练目标由上述动态损失构成，采用梯度级分析说明其诱导条件校准。推理阶段与普通蒸馏学生模型一致，不引入额外组件。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均ROUGE-L", "value": "+3.2", "setting": "指令遵循任务，相对最强基线（Skewed-KL, α–β divergence）"}, {"metric": "pass@1", "value": "+2.1", "setting": "MBPP，相对最强基线"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "+1.7", "setting": "GSM8k，相对最强基线"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "+1.8", "setting": "CollegeMath，相对最强基线"}, {"metric": "LLM-as-a-judge factuality", "value": "每个任务最高+2.5", "setting": "相对Skewed-RKL"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: token级损失CaRE-Divergence：根据教师-学生置信度自适应切换Forward与Reverse KL散度。；batch级认知拒绝机制Revival：当教师比学生更不确定时抑制更新。；梯度级分析证明了双粒度设计诱导出条件校准机制，是静态散度无法复现的。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) trains a smaller-capacity student model to imitate a larger-capacity teacher model by matching output distributions, implicitly assuming the teacher to be a reliable oracle. In large language models (LLMs), this assumption often fails: teacher predictions can exhibit high entropy and hallucinations, causing standard KD to degrade well-calibrated student priors. We propose CaRE-KD, a confidence-gated distillation framework that replaces static objectives with uncertainty-adaptive optimization. CaRE-KD has two components: a token-level loss (CaRE-Divergence) that adaptively switches between Forward and Reverse KL divergence based on teacher--student confidence, and a batch-level epistemic rejection mechanism (Revival) that suppresses updates when the teacher is more uncertain than the student. We provide a gradient-level analysis showing how this dual-granularity design induces a conditional calibration mechanism that prior static divergences cannot reproduce. Empirically, across eight teacher--student pairs and eleven benchmarks spanning instruction following, chat alignment, code generation, and mathematical reasoning, CaRE-KD delivers consistent gains over strong baselines (Skewed-KL, $\alpha$--$\beta$ divergence). Highlights include up to $+3.2$ average ROUGE-L on instruction-following tasks, $+2.1$ pass@1 on MBPP, $+1.7$ accuracy on GSM8k, and $+1.8$ accuracy on CollegeMath over the strongest baseline, with consistent gains in LLM-as-a-judge factuality (up to $+2.5$ per task over Skewed-RKL). Revival further acts as a principled, loss-agnostic plug-in that systematically strengthens existing distillation objectives by filtering epistemically unreliable teacher supervision.

</details>


### [25] [ATTUNER: Recomputation-Free KV Cache Reuse via Query-Side Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36722)
*Xinghao Chen,Junnan Dong,Cai Ke,Chak Tou Leong,Haocheng Sun,Keyu Chen,Siyu An,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ATTUNER 通过分析发现位置无关缓存的性能损失源于注意力分数而非 KV 表示，并提出在 query 投影中插入低秩适配器，通过蒸馏全上下文分布训练，从而无需重计算即可复用 KV 缓存，在 Qwen3-4B/8B 上达到全上下文质量并实现最高 3.73 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置无关缓存（PIC）在复用 KV 状态时会带来质量损失，现有方法依赖重新计算或恢复全局位置 ID，开销大。论文旨在定位损失来源并无需重计算地修复该损失。

Method: Attuner 针对位置无关缓存（PIC）的推理流程：首先独立编码每个可复用内容（技能、文档、记忆条目等）并缓存其 KV 状态。在推理时，将缓存的 KV 状态在任意位置复用，而不重新计算。Attuner 在查询投影层插入低秩适配器（LoRA），并在训练阶段使用全上下文 prefill 的注意力分布作为教师信号，对学生模型（使用缓存 KV 和适配后的 query）进行蒸馏。训练仅更新少量参数（<0.05%）。推理阶段，模型读取冻结的 KV 缓存，利用适配后的 query 计算注意力分数，从而恢复与全上下文 prefill 相当的质量，且无需重新计算 KV 或访问全上下文参考。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现 PIC 质量损失主要源于注意力分数而非 KV 表示或位置不匹配；提出 Attuner：在 query 投影中插入低秩适配器，通过蒸馏全上下文分布训练学生模型；训练参数少于 0.05%，推理时无需 KV 重计算或全上下文参考；在 Qwen3-4B/8B 上跨七个基准（技能、文档、记忆、代码）达到与全上下文 prefill 相当的质量，并实现最高 3.73 倍加速

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents repeatedly load reusable content, such as skills, documents, and memory entries, into the current context. Re-encoding this content for every request wastes computation. Position-independent caching (PIC) alleviates this by encoding each artifact independently and reusing its key-value (KV) states at arbitrary positions, but it incurs a quality loss relative to full-context prefill. Existing methods repair this loss by restoring global position IDs or recomputing selected tokens. In this work, we isolate the source of the loss, finding that the positional mismatch has minor effect, and independently cached artifacts retain faithful representations: reading a provided artifact stays largely accurate, and performance degrades only when the model must select among multiple artifacts. Moreover, replacing PIC's attention scores with full-prefill scores recovers performance with the cached KV unchanged, localizing the failure to the attention rather than KV recomputation. Motivated by this, we propose \textsc{Attuner}, a query-side adaptation method that learns to read a frozen artifact cache. \textsc{Attuner} inserts low-rank adapters into the query projections and is trained by distilling full-prefill distribution into the student. It trains fewer than 0.05\% of the model parameters and, at inference, requires neither cache recomputation nor a full-context reference. On Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B across seven benchmarks covering skills, documents, memory, and code, \textsc{Attuner} substantially outperforms prior PIC baselines in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings, matches full-context prefill quality while providing up to $3.73\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [26] [SEED: Self-Speculative Decoding via Implicit Encoder-Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36590)
*Hankun Lin,Patrick Pynadath,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEED提出一种自推测解码方法，将decoder-only transformer重构为隐式编码器-解码器，通过复用验证阶段已计算的深度上下文表示，在两次验证间由轻量解码器快速草拟多个token。在4B规模模型上实现最高2.7倍加速，比EAGLE-3快28%，同时保持或提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自推测解码在草稿质量与草稿成本之间存在尖锐权衡：早退法廉价但草稿质量差，多token预测质量好但每步草稿需完整前向传播。

Method: SEED将标准decoder-only transformer重新解释为隐式编码器-解码器架构。输入为待生成的token序列，前若干层（编码器）负责构建深度上下文表示，最后几层（解码器）从这些表示生成token。在验证阶段，完整的编码器-解码器模型对草稿token进行验证，同时将已验证前缀的上下文表示缓存起来。在两次验证之间，轻量级解码器自回归地草拟多个token，每个草稿token以缓存的上下文表示和之前已草拟的token为条件。验证通过后，已验证前缀的表示被更新缓存，供下一轮草稿复用。这种设计将编码与验证合并为单一步骤，避免了多token预测方法中每次草稿都需完整前向传播的高昂成本，同时利用深层表示保证了草稿质量。训练目标与标准自回归微调一致，未引入额外训练目标。推理流程为：草稿（解码器快速生成多个token）→验证（完整编码器-解码器并行验证）→缓存更新（已验证前缀表示缓存）→循环。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均加速比", "value": "最高2.7倍", "setting": "4B规模模型，多个基准测试"}, {"metric": "相对EAGLE-3的运行速度", "value": "快28%", "setting": "4B规模模型"}, {"metric": "生成质量", "value": "保持或提升标准自回归微调的质量", "setting": "多个基准测试"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将标准decoder-only transformer重新解释为隐式编码器-解码器：前若干层作为编码器构建深度上下文表示，最后几层作为解码器从这些表示生成token。；将编码与验证合并为单一步骤：验证由完整编码器-解码器执行，并将已验证前缀的上下文表示缓存以供后续草稿复用。；在两次验证之间，轻量级解码器自回归地草拟多个token，每个token以缓存表示和先前草稿为条件，实现低成本高质量草稿。

Abstract: Self-speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by drafting tokens from the target model itself, but faces a sharp tradeoff between the quality and cost of the draft. Early-exit methods produce drafts cheaply by terminating computation at intermediate layers, but forgo the deeper representations that later layers provide and thus suffer in draft quality. Multi-token prediction preserves draft quality by emitting from the model's final hidden states, but pays for a full forward pass to produce those states at every drafting step. We propose self-speculative encoder-decoder (SEED), a self-speculative method that obtains high-quality drafts cheaply by reusing the deep contextual representations already computed during verification. We reinterpret the standard decoder-only transformer as an implicit encoder-decoder: the first layers (encoder) build deep contextual representations, and the last few layers (decoder) emit tokens from them. Encoding and verification are merged into a single step: verification is performed by the full encoder-decoder, and the contextual representations of the verified prefix are cached for reuse during drafting. Drafting is therefore very fast: between verifications, the lightweight decoder drafts multiple tokens autoregressively, each conditioned on the cached representations and on preceding drafts. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that SEED achieves up to 2.7$\times$ average speedup on 4B-scale models, outperforming both early-exit and MTP-style self-speculative baselines and running 28% faster than the state-of-the-art EAGLE-3, while preserving or even improving the generation quality of standard autoregressive fine-tuning. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [27] [Memory Consolidation Flattens the Temporal Shape of User Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36457)
*Sugam Panthi,Muhaiminul Yeamin,Siyan Luo,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文揭示长期记忆写入会把进行体事实压缩为简单现在时，产生“时体扁平化”，在3个写入模型、11种配置及mem0/Graphiti/Letta中均出现，并初步影响读取端对事实是否仍成立的判断与是否先询问用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆写入在将用户语句压缩为短笔记时会丢失时体（aspect）线索，导致后续读取模型无法判断事实是否仍然成立。

Method: 论文将长期记忆系统拆为两个阶段：写入端（memory writer）把对话历史压缩成短存储笔记，读取端（reader）基于存储笔记判断事实是否仍然成立并决定是否行动。LAPSE基准构造仅时态/体形式不同的匹配用户陈述对（如进行体与简单现在时），用于检测写入端在压缩时是否保留时体线索。实验在3个写入模型、11种模型配置上进行，并接入mem0、Graphiti、Letta已安装管线，比较写入输出中体线索的保留/丢失。读取端实验只改变存储笔记中的动词形式，观察三个读取模型对事实仍成立概率的估计变化；另设读取端可先向用户提问再行动的条件，统计扁平化笔记下不提问就行动的比例。计划中的记忆使用任务因读取端几乎对所有存储事实（包括已过期事实）都采取行动，未能检测到该效应。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "扁平化不对称对数（进行体被扁平化而简单现在时保留）", "value": "244/381 pairs", "setting": "3个写入模型，只出现进行体被扁平化、反向从未出现"}, {"metric": "不对称性一致性", "value": "全部11种模型配置均成立", "setting": "模型配置实验"}, {"metric": "生产记忆管线复现", "value": "mem0、Graphiti、Letta均出现", "setting": "安装管线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出aspectual flattening（时体扁平化）概念，刻画记忆写入选择性丢失进行体线索的现象；构建LAPSE基准，由仅时态形式不同的匹配用户陈述对组成；在3个写入模型、11种模型配置以及mem0、Graphiti、Letta等安装管线中验证不对称性；初步显示改变存储动词会改变全部三个读取模型对事实是否仍成立的估计

Abstract: Long-term memory systems turn conversations into short stored notes. A note can keep a user fact while losing evidence about whether the fact still holds. For example, "I am driving a Peugeot" can become "The user drives a Peugeot," which drops the cue that the activity is ongoing. We call this aspectual flattening and measure it with LAPSE, a benchmark of matched user statements that differ only in temporal form. We find that memory writers flatten aspect selectively. Three writer models flattened the progressive statement but kept its simple-present match in 244 of 381 pairs, never the reverse. The asymmetry holds in all 11 model configurations tested and in the installed pipelines mem0, Graphiti, and Letta. The lost cue matters to later readers. In exploratory tests, changing only the stored verb shifted all three readers' estimates that a fact still holds. When readers could ask the user before acting, two of three acted without asking more often on flattened notes. Our planned memory-use task could not detect this, because readers there acted on almost every stored fact, even expired ones. Memory writing can thus remove evidence that later models use to decide whether to act.

</details>


### [28] [Reliable Parallel Decoding in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36452)
*Zhenghao He,Bohan Liu,Guangzhi Xiong,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对掩码扩散语言模型并行解码中同一次前向的多个预测并非都可靠的问题，论文诊断出置信度不足以决定提交顺序、且跨层稳定的预测更可靠，提出训练免费的 Reliable Parallel Decoding (RPD)：依据逐层预测稳定性与最终置信度筛选候选，并用前置掩码位置的累积熵预算推迟上游不确定的预测，实现无固定块调度的并行提交。在 LLaDA 与 Dream 上的数学推理和代码生成基准中取得评测方法中最高解码吞吐，同时保持或改善准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型(MDLM)单次前向可并行预测多个掩码token，但并行提交的预测并非都可靠；仅凭置信度无法确定可靠的提交顺序，早期高置信预测可能过早固定答案，导致下游预测因上游上下文不确定而变差。

Method: RPD 作用于预训练的掩码扩散语言模型（LLaDA、Dream），不修改模型权重。输入为部分掩码的 token 序列，解码过程迭代进行：每一解码步中，模型对全部掩码位置做一次前向传播，输出各位置的预测分布。RPD 首先收集每个掩码位置在最终若干网络层中的预测结果，计算其逐层预测稳定性（跨层预测一致的候选更可能正确），并结合最终层的置信度作为筛选依据。随后，对每个候选 token，考察其前置掩码位置的上游上下文不确定性，计算累积熵预算；若某候选的前置上下文累积熵超过预算，则该候选被推迟到后续步提交，以避免在支撑计算尚未建立时固定答案。满足稳定性、置信度且累积熵在预算内的候选 token 在同一并行步中被同时提交（取消掩码）。该过程不依赖固定的块大小或块调度，可自适应地根据每步的稳定性与上游不确定性决定并行提交的集合。整个流程是训练无关的推理时算法，复用单次前向传播的层间信息，旨在提高并行解码的吞吐量同时保持或提升生成准确率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 诊断分析：置信度不足以决定可靠提交顺序，且跨最终层保持稳定的预测更可能正确；RPD：一种训练免费方法，结合逐层预测稳定性与最终置信度选择候选token；在候选token前置掩码位置上施加累积熵预算，延迟上游上下文不确定的预测，并行提交其余候选，无需固定块调度

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) can generate text efficiently by predicting multiple masked tokens in parallel, but predictions from the same forward pass are not necessarily reliable when committed together. We study when parallel commitment is reliable. Our diagnostics show that confidence alone does not determine a reliable commitment order: confident predictions near the end of the sequence can fix an answer before its supporting computations are established, and downstream predictions become less reliable as the uncertainty of their upstream context grows. At the same time, a single forward pass can already resolve several masked tokens, and predictions that remain stable across the final layers are more likely to be correct. Based on these findings, we propose Reliable Parallel Decoding (RPD), a training-free method that selects candidates by layerwise prediction stability and final confidence, and commits them under a cumulative entropy budget over their preceding masked positions. RPD defers predictions with uncertain upstream context while committing the remaining candidates in parallel, without relying on a fixed block schedule. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks on LLaDA and Dream, RPD achieves the highest decoding throughput among the evaluated methods while maintaining or improving accuracy.

</details>


### [29] [MemFold: Learning Compact Soft Memory for Long-Context Personalization via On-Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36435)
*Jingxuan Wu,Yuzhe Yang,Yiqiao Huang,Chengzhi Liu,Qingni Wang,Chengxuan Qian,Shutong Wu,Jiawei Zhang,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemFold将长期用户历史压缩为固定预算的K个连续软记忆向量，并以任务奖励加置信门控on-policy蒸馏训练读者LLM，在PersonaMem-32K/128K取得最高准确率，长历史优势扩大，且零样本迁移到PrefEval与LongMemEval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将长期用户历史压缩为固定预算的连续软记忆，并让记忆按下游读者实际需要的行为来优化，而不是按文本重建或参考答案模仿来优化。

Method: MemFold包含文本记忆构造、软记忆压缩器、读者LLM与冻结的文本记忆教师。输入为长期用户历史与当前查询，先构造查询条件化的文本记忆。该文本记忆被压缩为K个连续向量，作为读者LLM的记忆接口，使读者输入长度不随保留历史增长。训练时读者在自身rollout上优化两个互补信号：一是任务结果的组相对奖励，二是置信门控的on-policy蒸馏，其中冻结的文本记忆教师对学生在文本记忆条件下采样的token重新打分，监督保持在学生当前分布上，且教师从不被采样，因而不会引入额外自回归解码。推理时教师被完全移除，仅保留压缩后的K个软向量与读者LLM。消融表明奖励项贡献主要任务增益，教师信号提供较小额外增益；记忆干预显示读者依赖软记忆的实例特定内容。

Conclusion: 用下游行为直接优化固定预算的软记忆：查询条件化文本记忆压缩为K个连续向量作为读者记忆接口。；结合组相对奖励与置信门控on-policy蒸馏的双信号训练：冻结的文本记忆教师在学生自身采样token上重打分，监督保持在学生分布上，推理时移除教师。；记忆干预实验证明读者依赖软记忆中实例特定内容，而非仅依赖通用格式或位置。；在PersonaMem-32K/128K取得最高准确率，并跨域迁移到PrefEval与LongMemEval。

Abstract: An assistant that serves the same user over a long horizon has to answer from what that user has revealed: which preferences still hold, which were revised, and which constraints apply now. Retaining that information is not the same as acting on it, and the two are usually optimized as if they were. Keeping the information as text makes the reader's input grow with the retained history, while compressing it into a fixed number of latent vectors bounds the interface but is typically trained to reconstruct text or imitate reference answers, both of which are scored on sequences the reader never produced. We present MemFold, which optimizes a fixed-budget soft memory by the behavior it supports. A query-conditioned textual memory is compressed into K continuous vectors that form the reader's memory interface, and the reader is then trained on its own rollouts under two complementary signals: group-relative rewards for task outcomes, and confidence-gated on-policy distillation in which a frozen textual-memory teacher re-scores the student's sampled tokens under the textual memory. The teacher is never sampled from, so supervision stays on the student's current distribution and adds no autoregressive decoding; at inference it is removed entirely. Across three Qwen backbones, MemFold attains the highest accuracy we measure on PersonaMem-32K and PersonaMem-128K, with margins that widen at the longer history length, and transfers to PrefEval and LongMemEval without target-domain training. Ablations attribute most of the task gain to the reward term and a smaller additional gain to the teacher signal, and memory interventions show that the reader depends on the instance-specific content of its soft memory.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化LLM在用户请求删除信息时无法真正遗忘，即使声称已删除或限制上下文，信息仍因对话依赖而残留。

Method: DeLLM框架针对持久化LLM的外部存储记忆系统。输入为用户与LLM的交互历史及当前查询。系统首先动态构建与当前查询相关的上下文，仅检索与查询相关的记忆消息，而非固定窗口内的全部消息。同时，维护一个消息溯源图，记录消息间的依赖关系（如后续消息引用了先前消息的内容）。当收到删除请求时，系统基于溯源图确定所有直接或间接受影响的消息，并从存储中移除这些消息及其依赖。推理时，LLM仅使用动态构建且已删除敏感信息的上下文进行响应。目标是在保证删除率的同时维持LLM的效用。
