Conclusion: 提出LLM记忆删除（Deletion of LLM Memories）新研究方向；DeLLM框架：动态构建与查询相关的上下文；维护消息溯源图以确定删除请求中需移除的消息

Abstract: We consider persistent LLMs that accumulate memories of their interactions with a user over time. Such LLMs maintain memories using external storage, which they can query to overcome the limitations of a fixed context window. Such systems have numerous practical applications, as they can draw on all past interactions when responding to user queries. In this paper, we ask whether LLMs can forget information shared with them upon a user's request. We find that current LLMs fail to delete such information---even when they claim to have forgotten it and even when operating with a limited context. To this end, we consider a new direction of study: Deletion of LLM Memories. We show that naively removing messages that match a user's deletion request is insufficient, since conversations naturally introduce message dependencies that cause information to persist. To correctly handle deletion requests, we propose the DeLLM framework. It dynamically constructs relevant context for each LLM query and maintains a provenance graph of messages to determine which ones must be removed during deletion. Our experiments show that DeLLM achieves a high deletion rate while maintaining utility.

</details>


### [31] [FastGuide: Accelerating Reward Guidance for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36202)
*Darshan Thaker,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FastGuide 提出并行与自回归解码的自适应混合策略来加速扩散语言模型的奖励引导：每步只算一次奖励引导并复用于多个 token，同时用 KV 缓存与稀疏注意力重计算实现逐 token 解掩码，并按置信度推迟不确定 token。在三个奖励基准上最高取得 4.4 倍加速且生成质量相近，训练资源与部署信息未披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度的奖励引导（reward guidance）在掩码扩散语言模型推理时能够灵活控制生成，但每个解码迭代都需要昂贵的扩散模型前向与奖励模型反向传播，计算开销过高。

Method: FastGuide 作用于掩码扩散语言模型（masked diffusion language model）的推理阶段，整体为一种无需额外训练的混合解码流程。输入为待生成的序列（初始为全掩码或部分掩码状态）以及下游奖励模型提供的可微奖励信号；每一步解码中，模型先通过扩散前向得到当前掩码位置的 token 分布，并计算一次奖励模型的梯度式引导信号，该引导信号被复用于本步内多个 token 的生成，以摊销奖励模型反向传播开销——这是对并行解码思路的类比。随后，FastGuide 将并行解码细化为自回归式逐 token 解掩码：每解掩码一个 token 后，并不重新进行完整的扩散前向，而是利用 KV 缓存复用已计算的键值表示，并对注意力进行稀疏重计算，从而以较低代价更新后续 token 的分布；接着依据重计算分布下模型的置信度决定是否解掩码，对置信度不足的 token 予以推迟，进入后续解码步骤再判断。上述过程迭代直至全部 token 解掩码完成。训练目标方面，论文不涉及对扩散模型或奖励模型的再训练，仅在推理阶段最大化下游奖励模型给出奖励的同时控制计算成本；推理路径为“扩散前向→单次奖励引导计算→KV 缓存与稀疏注意力重计算→按置信度逐 token 解掩码→循环”，核心在于把昂贵的奖励反向传播与扩散前向在多个 token 之间共享与增量复用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对顺序式奖励引导解码的加速比", "value": "最高 4.4x", "setting": "三个奖励基准上的奖励引导解码实验，在保持相近生成质量前提下测得"}, {"metric": "生成质量", "value": "与顺序式奖励引导解码相近", "setting": "三个奖励基准（论文未给出具体指标数值）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出并行解码与自回归解码的自适应混合解码策略，用于加速扩散语言模型的奖励引导；在单个解码步骤内只计算一次奖励模型反向传播得到的引导信号，并复用于生成多个 token，从而摊销引导成本；将扩散前向过程自回归化：逐 token 解掩码，并通过 KV 缓存与注意力稀疏重计算高效更新解掩码后的 token 分布；根据重计算分布下模型的置信度自适应推迟不确定 token 的解掩码，使混合解码匹配模型置信度

Abstract: Gradient-based reward guidance provides a flexible way to use downstream reward models to control masked diffusion language models at inference time. However, its computational cost remains high as each decoding iteration incurs expensive diffusion model forward passes and reward model backpropagation steps. To address this, we introduce FastGuide, an adaptive hybrid of parallel and autoregressive decoding to accelerate reward guidance for diffusion language models. In analogy to parallel decoding, FastGuide amortizes the cost of reward model backpropagation by computing guidance once per decoding step and reusing it to generate multiple tokens. Within each decoding step, FastGuide makes diffusion forward passes autoregressive by unmasking tokens one at a time while efficiently recomputing token distributions after each unmasking by utilizing KV caching techniques and sparse recomputation of attention. Lastly, to adapt hybrid decoding to the model's confidence, FastGuide defers any token that the model is unconfident about under its recomputed distribution. Experiments on three reward benchmarks demonstrate that FastGuide is up to $4.4\times$ faster than sequential reward-guided decoding while retaining similar generation quality.

</details>


### [32] [Targeting Pivotal Decisions for Credit Assignment in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36178)
*Dongwon Jung,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Yifan Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Yu Hu,Muhao Chen,Varun Chandrasekaran,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey,Jaron Lanier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProVer针对GRPO中轨迹级优势均匀分配的问题，提出通过智能体裁判选择可能决定成败的片段，并基于片段前后续写的终端成功率差异验证其优势，从而将细粒度信用分配给关键决策token。在ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA上，相对GRPO分别提升9.91%（2B）和7.12%（4B），且仅增加适度生成开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO将轨迹级优势均匀分配给所有策略token，无法区分关键决策与次要决策，导致信用分配模糊、训练信号不精准。

Method: ProVer是一种面向智能体强化学习的细粒度信用分配框架。输入是一组rollout轨迹（rollout group），包含成功和失败的轨迹。首先，一个智能体裁判（agentic judge）对比成功与失败轨迹，提出一个可能负责结果差异的片段（segment）。然后，ProVer不直接信任裁判的判断，而是验证该片段：从当前策略在片段之前和之后分别采样续写（continuations），估计终端成功率的差异，以此作为该片段的优势估计。如果优势估计为正，则将该优势融入该片段内策略token的GRPO优势中。通过这种方式，ProVer仅使用模型判断来选择需要验证的位置，而将局部信用建立在观察到的结果上，避免了对每个中间状态的穷举评估。训练目标基于GRPO，但优势分配在片段级别进行了修正。推理时，智能体按照策略执行动作，裁判和验证仅在训练阶段使用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对GRPO平均性能提升", "value": "9.91%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-2B，在ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA上的平均表现"}, {"metric": "相对GRPO平均性能提升", "value": "7.12%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-4B，在ALFWorld、WebShop、SearchQA上的平均表现"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: ProVer框架：通过智能体裁判对比成功与失败轨迹，提出可能决定结果差异的片段（segment）；验证机制：不直接信任裁判，而是从片段前后采样的当前策略续写中估计终端成功率差异，得到片段级优势；将验证为正的片段优势融入片段内策略token的GRPO优势中，实现细粒度信用分配；仅用模型判断来选择验证位置，避免穷举评估每个中间状态，降低开销

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a promising approach for training large language model agents. However, its uniform assignment of trajectory-level advantages to all policy tokens fails to distinguish consequential decisions from less relevant ones, obscuring which intermediate decisions contributed to success. We introduce ProVer, a framework that targets potentially pivotal decisions for fine-grained credit assignment in agentic reinforcement learning. Given a rollout group, an agentic judge contrasts successful and failed trajectories to propose a segment potentially responsible for their divergent outcomes. Rather than directly trusting the judge's assessment, ProVer verifies the proposed segment by estimating its advantage from the difference in terminal success rates between current-policy continuations sampled before and after the segment. Positive estimates are then incorporated into the GRPO advantages of policy tokens within the proposed segment. By using model judgment only to select where to verify, ProVer grounds local credit in observed outcomes without exhaustively evaluating every intermediate state. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA, ProVer achieves the strongest average performance at both model scales, with relative improvements over GRPO of 9.91% and 7.12% for Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3.5-4B, respectively. Further analyses demonstrate that informed segment selection improves policy training with modest additional generation overhead, even without a frontier-scale judge model, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of selectively targeting pivotal decisions for fine-grained credit assignment in agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型如何在transformer层中表示和处理组合任务所需的token间关系信息，以及这些关系表示是否被因果使用。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是分析现有transformer模型（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Mistral）在组合任务中的内部激活。输入为需要推断三个token间关系（循环概念如月份、小时、工作日、音符）的提示，输出为下一token预测。通过几何分析和因果干预，发现模型中间层形成基于两个token间推断关系的联合表示，而后期层形成包含全部三个token的联合表示以完成任务。几何与因果分析共同揭示表示级机制：逐步组织和组合关系信息以形成答案。限制模型仅使用因果相关的联合表示可提升下一token预测准确率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过几何与因果分析揭示LLM在循环概念任务中分层构建组合关系表示的一致机制：中间层使用双token联合表示，后层使用三token联合表示。；发现部分几何结构化的token关系在下一token预测中是因果惰性的。；证明限制到因果相关的联合表示反而能提高下一token预测准确率。

Abstract: Large language models are able to solve tasks whose answers depend on not only individual input tokens, but also on relations among them. How is such relational information represented and processed across transformer layers? We study activations from ensembles of prompts that require inferring relationships between three tokens corresponding to a cyclic concept (months, hours, weekdays, and musical notes) to correctly predict the next token. Across model families (Llama, Qwen, Gemma, and Mistral) and cyclic concepts, we find a consistent layerwise progression in how the joint dependence among the tokens is geometrically organized and causally used: intermediate layers use a joint representation based on the inferred relationship between two tokens, while later layers use a joint representation associated with all three tokens to correctly complete the task. We also find other relationships between tokens that are geometrically structured but remain causally inert in the next-token prediction. Crucially, when taken together, these geometric and causal investigations reveal the representation-level mechanism that progressively organizes and composes the relational information to form the answer. More surprisingly, restricting the models to such causally relevant joint representations improves next-token prediction accuracy.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文模型成本高昂且存在中间信息欠关注问题、纯RAG受检索质量限制的背景下，如何自适应决定查询由RAG直接回答还是升级到全局长上下文处理。

Method: EDAR是一种推理时自适应路由框架。输入为查询与检索到的文档块。首先，检索器获取相关文档块并生成RAG响应；在生成的前几个token上计算token级概率分布的预测熵。该熵值作为路由信号：若熵低于在留出验证集上通过成本-准确率扫描选定的阈值，则直接输出RAG响应；若高于阈值，则将查询升级到全局长上下文模型处理。框架不依赖特定检索器或长上下文骨干，也不需要额外监督。

Conclusion: 提出EDAR框架，利用RAG生成前几个token的预测熵作为模型内部信号进行路由决策。；在验证集上通过成本-准确率权衡扫描选择熵阈值，无需额外监督。；在LongBench v2和Infinity-Bench上验证混合路由可保留长上下文准确率并大幅降低token消耗。

Abstract: Modern large language models now support context windows of more than one million tokens, which has raised the question of whether retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is still necessary. Pure long-context (LC) processing is expensive and is known to under-attend to information placed in the middle of long inputs, while pure RAG is fast but bounded by retrieval quality and prone to errors when retrieved chunks are partially relevant or contradictory. We propose the Entropy-Driven Adaptive Router (EDAR), a framework that decides at inference time whether to answer a query from retrieved chunks or to escalate it to full long-context processing. The decision uses the predictive entropy of the token-level probability distribution computed over the first few generated tokens of the RAG response. The entropy threshold is selected on a held-out validation set by sweeping cost against accuracy. Experiments compare EDAR against pure-RAG and pure-LC baselines on LongBench v2 and Infinity-Bench. Predictive entropy correlates strongly with hallucination rate on a held-out set of 2,000 generations (Pearson r = 0.85, 95% CI [0.83, 0.87]). On the long-context benchmarks, EDAR retains 97.4% of the accuracy of the pure long-context baseline while reducing total token expenditure by 70.7%, escalating only 18.2% of incoming queries. The accuracy gap between EDAR and the pure long-context system is not statistically distinguishable from zero at standard sample sizes. Predictive entropy is a useful model-internal signal for routing between RAG and long-context inference, and a threshold-based hybrid system can recover most of the accuracy of long-context models at a small fraction of the cost. The framework does not depend on a specific retriever or LC backbone, and it does not require additional supervision beyond what is normally produced during decoding.

</details>


### [35] [Tracing mechanisms of sycophantic agreement in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35822)
*Sixing Chen,Zhuofan Josh Ying,Logan Riggs Smith,Jeremy Wertheimer,Natalie Shapira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用因果中介分析揭示语言模型谄媚性同意的机制：早期注意力头将用户观点编码进残差流并偏置答案检索；消融这些头可减少谄媚且保持事实性；内容无关反驳则触发另一组抑制正确答案的头。

Method: 论文未公开具体模型架构或训练细节。根据摘要，研究采用因果中介分析方法，将用户陈述的观点作为输入，观察其在语言模型残差流中的传播。观点信号在最后提示令牌的残差流中早期被编码，并偏置后续答案检索。稀疏的早期注意力头集合携带该观点信号。通过消融这些注意力头，可以显著减少谄媚行为而基本保持事实准确性，且该机制对观点表述方式不敏感。当用户未显式表达观点而是通过内容无关的反驳（如“你确定吗？”）时，存在另一组不同的注意力头抑制模型原有的正确答案并推动生成修正后的答案。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用因果中介分析识别谄媚性同意背后的机制；发现早期注意力头将用户观点信号编码进最后提示令牌的残差流并偏置后续答案检索；消融稀疏早期注意力头集可大幅减少谄媚性同时基本保持事实准确性；区分显式陈述观点与内容无关反驳（如“你确定吗？”）引发的不同注意力头集合

Abstract: Sycophantic agreement in language models refers to the tendency to overly affirm a user's stated beliefs or preferences, often at the expense of factual accuracy. Although it is widely recognized as an alignment failure, its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we use causal mediation analysis to identify the mechanisms behind sycophantic agreement. We show that a stated opinion is incorporated into the residual stream of the final prompt token early, where it biases subsequent answer retrieval. A sparse set of early attention heads carries this opinion signal. Ablating these heads substantially reduces sycophancy while leaving factual accuracy largely intact. The same heads carry the opinion when it is explicitly stated, regardless of how it is phrased. When an opinion is not stated explicitly but instead conveyed through content-free pushback (e.g., ``Are you sure?"), we find a distinct set of heads that suppresses the model's original correct answer to promote a revised answer. By providing a mechanistic account of how opinions induce sycophantic agreement, this work takes a step toward developing more targeted and reliable alignment interventions.

</details>

*Kaibo Wang,Ding Ding,Fangyu Ding,Zijin Feng,Han Shi,Haili Bai,Jiacheng Sun,Yang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LUDI，一种改进的均匀离散扩散语言模型框架，通过 less uniform loss 和 per-token time embeddings 改善监督信号，支持基于置信度的少步采样。在多个规模上验证了更干净的监督和更好的少步生成，将 7B 自回归模型继续训练为具备复杂推理能力的 LUDI-7B，实现 3 token/步的解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀扩散语言模型（UDLM）在扩展时面临训练目标过度均匀以及采样时条件-目标混淆的问题，导致监督信号不干净和少步生成困难。

Method: LUDI 是一种离散扩散语言模型框架。输入为带时间步和损坏提示的 token 序列。模型核心包含两个模块：less uniform loss 和 per-token time embeddings。less uniform loss 调整训练目标，使每个反向扩散转移直接预测干净 token，而非均匀分布，从而提供清晰的监督信号。per-token time embeddings 为每个 token 注入其损坏程度的嵌入向量，使模型能感知 token 级别的噪声水平，进而支持基于置信度的少步采样。训练时采用扩散过程，逐步对 token 加噪，然后学习反向去噪。推理时，模型从噪声开始，通过少量步骤进行反向采样，每步可基于置信度并行生成多个 token（实验显示可达 3 token/步）。该架构旨在解决均匀扩散模型中训练目标过于均匀和条件-目标混淆的问题，提升少步生成的质量和效率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "解码速度提升", "value": "3-token-per-step 加速", "setting": "与自回归解码相比"}, {"metric": "少步生成质量", "value": "在多个规模上优于基线", "setting": "与均匀扩散语言模型基线对比"}, {"metric": "复杂推理能力", "value": "与掩码扩散基线有竞争力", "setting": "LUDI-7B 继续训练后"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 less uniform loss，引导每个反向转移直接朝向干净 token，提供更清晰的监督信号；引入 per-token time embeddings，为每个 token 提供损坏程度提示，支持基于置信度的少步采样；通过继续训练将 7B 自回归模型转化为 LUDI-7B 离散扩散模型，实现复杂推理能力

Abstract: Although uniform diffusion language models (UDLMs) represent a promising diffusion paradigm, scaling them remains challenging. We identify the core obstacle as an over-uniform training objective and condition-target confusion during sampling. To address these, we propose Less Uniform Diffusion (LUDI), a novel UDLM framework. Specifically, we (i) introduce a less uniform loss that directs each reverse transition toward the clean token, and (ii) equip the model with per-token time embeddings that supply token-level corruption hints, enabling confidence-based few-step sampling. Experiments across scales show that LUDI yields cleaner supervision and improves few-step generation. We further continue-train a 7B autoregressive model into LUDI-7B, resulting in a UDLM capable of complex reasoning. It achieves a 3-token-per-step speedup over AR decoding and competitive performance compared with masked diffusion baselines, revealing that the full potential of UDLMs for complex generation remains to be unlocked.

</details>

Method: 该框架将车载对话助手视为黑盒，通过闭环仿真进行多轮对话测试。输入为用户模拟器生成的用户话语，以及对话历史与当前状态；用户模拟器受策略引导，策略由对抗策略管理器产生和选择。对抗策略管理器基于当前对话上下文动态选取攻击策略（例如诱发约束冲突、迫使上下文丢失或触犯安全规则），将策略指令注入用户模拟器，生成更具挑战性的下一轮用户输入。车载助手响应后，由两层LLM裁判评估：第一层逐轮检测失败（如忽略约束、上下文不一致、安全违规等），第二层从整体对话层面评估质量。裁判与策略管理器的输出可能反馈给模拟器或策略管理器以继续闭环产生后续轮次。整个系统无需显式真值，通过闭环交互产生测试用例并自动标注失败。训练目标和优化细节未披露；推理时使用多个LLM（作为对话后端、模拟器、裁判），服务流程为黑盒API调用与闭环迭代，具体硬件、时延、吞吐未报告。

Abstract: In-car conversational assistants (ICAs) are increasingly integrated into vehicles to support route planning, vehicle control, and information access. Ensuring their reliability is challenging due to multi-turn interactions, the absence of explicit ground truth, and strict safety constraints. Existing evaluation techniques fall short, as they target single-turn settings and fail to capture constraint handling, context retention, and safety-critical behavior across turns. We propose an automated framework for testing the multi-turn conversational capabilities of ICAs. The system is treated as a black box and evaluated via closed-loop simulation with a strategy-guided user simulator, an adversarial strategy manager, and a two-tier LLM judge assessing turn-level failures and conversation-level quality. We evaluate the approach on an industrial ICA with six LLM backends and twelve human annotators. The automated judge shows substantial agreement with humans, and strategy guidance uncovers 2.96 times more unique failure types per conversation and more than doubles the number of unique failing conversations compared to unguided simulation.

</details>

*Zongze Wu,Yani Guo,Runnan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lookahead-R将工具检索重构成预算约束下的序列决策问题，用执行感知替代世界模型联合预测成功率、延迟与语义效用，并驱动成本敏感的不确定性MCTS搜索；在ToolBench I3上NDCG@5达91.40%，优于ToolGen，消融表明显式延迟建模是关键判别信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模异构API生态中，LLM Agent的工具检索面临语义检索器准确率不足（语义-功能鸿沟）与执行验证延迟过高的固有权衡。

Method: Lookahead-R把工具检索建模为资源受限的序列决策：输入为用户查询与候选工具集合，系统使用轻量级执行感知替代世界模型（surrogate world model）联合预测每个候选工具的执行成功概率、延迟成本与语义效用，而无需真实调用API。该世界模型为成本敏感、不确定性引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）提供节点评估信号，MCTS在严格预算约束下逐步扩展工具空间，平衡探索（不确定性高的工具）与利用（高语义效用、低延迟工具）。搜索过程结束后输出高置信度工具排序作为检索结果。优化目标为在预算约束下最大化综合语义效用与执行成功、并惩罚高延迟成本。论文摘要未披露世界模型的具体网络结构、MCTS的具体展开策略、训练数据规模及推理硬件延迟等细节。

Conclusion: 将工具检索重新表述为资源受限的序列决策问题；提出轻量级执行感知替代世界模型，联合预测工具执行成功、延迟成本与语义效用，无需真实调用API；提出成本敏感、不确定性引导的蒙特卡洛树搜索，在严格预算约束下导航工具空间

Abstract: Tool retrieval is a critical bottleneck for LLM-based agents operating over large, heterogeneous API ecosystems. Existing approaches face an inherent trade-off: semantic retrievers are fast but suffer from the semantic-functional gap, while execution-based validation improves precision at the cost of prohibitive latency. We propose Lookahead-R, a planning-based framework that reformulates tool retrieval as a resource-constrained sequential decision-making problem. At its core, Lookahead-R introduces a lightweight execution-aware surrogate world model that jointly predicts tool execution success, latency cost, and semantic utility---without invoking real APIs. This world model drives a cost-sensitive, uncertainty-guided Monte Carlo Tree Search that navigates the tool space under strict budget constraints. Evaluated on the large-scale ToolBench benchmark, Lookahead-R achieves a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off across all test scenarios. On the most challenging I3 split, it attains an NDCG@5 of 91.40\%, outperforming the state-of-the-art ToolGen (90.16\%) by 1.24\%. Ablation studies confirm that explicit latency modeling is the key discriminative signal for identifying high-quality tools under resource constraints.

</details>

*Charlie Summers,Prajwal Raghunath,Aaditya Pai,Mayur Kulkarni,Zhuo Zhang,Oliver Kennedy,Eugene Wu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在指令要求安全的情况下仍可能执行不安全工具调用；现有防御在模型层面或工具接口层面施加约束，可能阻止动作但无法帮助智能体安全恢复。

Method: 论文提出的 Environment Steering 方法将智能体与执行环境（harness）的运行状态建模为数据库中的表，从而能够以记录级别追踪数据如何在工具调用与智能体状态之间流动。在运行时，系统会将这些数据流与预先声明的策略进行对照检查，以检测是否发生了安全违规。当检测到违规时，系统会根据具体策略和上下文生成反馈，该反馈被用来引导智能体调整其后续行为，从而走向安全的执行轨迹，并在可能的情况下从违规中恢复。摘要未披露更细粒度的模块划分、训练目标或服务流程。

Conclusion: 将智能体与执行环境状态建模为数据库表，以记录级粒度追踪数据流；在运行时对照声明式策略检查数据流，检测安全违规；检测到违规时生成策略与上下文相关的反馈，引导智能体走向安全轨迹并促进恢复

Abstract: LLM agents can make unsafe tool calls even when instructed to behave safely. Existing defenses constrain agents before execution, modify tool inputs/outputs, or rely on LLM judges; these approaches may depend on model behavior or block unsafe actions without helping the agent recover. We argue that the execution environment should instead enforce safety as the agent runs and steer it toward safe alternatives when violations occur---we call this Environment Steering. We implement this by modeling the agent and harness execution state as database tables, track the record-level data flows, and check these data flows against declarative policies during runtime. When violations are detected, policy- and context-specific feedback steers the agent toward safe trajectories. On AgentDyn, this enables the agent to improve task success rate over no-defense while achieving 0% attack success rate.

</details>

*Jongbin Won,Sung Geun An,Jay-yoon Lee*

TL;DR: 该工作将RAG检索失败拆分为不可答与分心两类，用轻量Sieve模块先筛除分心证据，再交由Sage大模型生成或弃答，在通用与高风险专家领域显著提升准确率与Macro-F1并取得最高1.99倍加速。

Abstract: Just as Socrates recognized the limits of his own knowledge, Retrieval-Augmented Language Models (RALMs) should learn to abstain when the retrieved evidence cannot support a reliable response. Existing approaches largely rely on monolithic LLMs to handle heterogeneous retrieval failures in a single step, resulting in limited abstention performance and high computational costs. We instead decompose retrieval failures into two distinct states: (i) the unanswerable state, where the required evidence is absent, and (ii) the distracted state, where relevant evidence is mixed with conflicting, negated, or adversarial information. Based on this decomposition, we introduce a lightweight module (Sieve) that screens retrieved document sets for distracting evidence before invoking a costly LLM (Sage) for grounded generation and abstention. Evaluated across both general and high-stakes expert domains, our Sieve and Sage framework preemptively detects distracting noise, improving system accuracy by up to 69.4 percentage points and Macro-F1 by 55.2 percentage points compared to one-stage baselines. Furthermore, it achieves up to a 1.99x speedup, establishing a highly efficient and reliable abstention pipeline for RALM with abstention.

</details>


### [41] [FD-VAD: Semantic Endpoint Detection for Streaming Full-Duplex Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35791)

TL;DR: FD-VAD 将全双工语音交互中的端点检测建模为因果音频-语言推理任务，用冻结语音编码器、模态适配器与参数高效微调的语言模型直接从流式音频输出 Continue/Stop 决策，无需 ASR 级联。引入 last-chunk 训练目标、置信度门控端点提交与边界 hard-negative 采样，在 TurnBench 开发集零样本 EOT recall 达 0.853（FP<=0.10），优于多个流式与非流式语义轮次分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声学 VAD 缺少语义信息，级联 ASR 端点检测依赖转录且增加处理阶段；需要一种无需 ASR、能够直接从流式音频判断语义端点（Continue/Stop）的方法。

Method: FD-VAD 接收有界的因果音频窗口作为输入，使用冻结的语音编码器提取声学表示，再通过轻量模态适配器将语音表征映射到语言模型可用的嵌入空间；语言模型经过参数高效适配（PEFT）后，对每个因果窗口直接输出 Continue/Stop 二分类决策。训练采用 last-chunk 目标，使模型在流式推理时只依赖当前及历史音频块而不使用未来信息。推理时，模型给出端点停止概率，并通过置信度门控机制决定是否提交端点（Stop），从而在及时打断与等待延迟之间进行权衡。训练数据构造上采用边界聚焦的 hard-negative 采样，以加强模型在模糊轮次边界处的判别能力。整体流程无需 ASR 转录或对话状态跟踪等中间阶段，实现端到端的流式语义端点检测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "TurnBench dev EOT recall @ FP<=0.10", "value": "0.853", "setting": "零样本（zero-shot）设置，与合格的流式/非流式语义轮次分类器对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将语义端点检测形式化为因果音频-语言推理任务，直接映射有界因果音频窗到 Continue/Stop；ASR-free 流式端架构：冻结语音编码器 + 轻量模态适配器 + 参数高效适配的语言模型；last-chunk 训练目标以支持流式推理；置信度门控端点提交机制，控制打断与延迟的权衡；面向边界歧义的 hard-negative 采样

Abstract: Natural turn-taking in full-duplex voice interaction requires determining from partial speech whether a pause reflects hesitation or a completed conversational intent. Acoustic voice activity detection lacks this semantic information, while cascaded ASR-based endpointing introduces transcription dependence and additional processing stages. We formulate semantic endpoint detection as a causal audio-language reasoning task and introduce FD-VAD, an ASR-free streaming endpointer that maps bounded causal audio windows directly to Continue/Stop decisions. FD-VAD combines a frozen speech encoder with a lightweight modality adapter and a parameter-efficiently adapted language model, using a last-chunk training objective for streaming inference. We further introduce confidence-gated endpoint commitment to control interruption versus delay and boundary-focused hard-negative sampling to improve decisions around ambiguous turn boundaries. Across in-domain and conversational evaluations, FD-VAD outperforms strong streaming and non-streaming semantic turn classifiers, and achieves the highest EOT recall among qualifying systems on TurnBench dev set $0.853$ (at FP<=0.10) in a zero-shot setting. These results show that semantic endpointing can be performed directly from streaming audio without intermediate ASR or dialogue state tracking.

</details>


### [42] [Follow the Entities: A Corpus Map for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37226)
*Soyeong Jeong,Sujay Kumar Jauhar,Sung Ju Hwang,Andrew Joohun Nam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CorpusMap 通过离线构建实体页及其关联文档图，为 LLM agent 提供跨查询共享的导航层，减少重复发现文档关系并提升证据发现与答案质量，同时降低 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将大规模文档集合仅作为扁平文件暴露时，LLM agent 需要针对每个查询重新发现文档间关系，容易遗漏互补证据并消耗大量 token。

Method: CorpusMap 是一种离线构建、推理时供 LLM agent 使用的语料导航层。输入为大文档集合；系统首先从文档本身识别反复出现的实体，并通过跨文档的实体提及解析（entity resolution）把同一实体的不同提及归并。然后为每个重复实体创建 Entity Page，该页聚合该实体的相关信息并链接到所有引用它的文档，从而形成实体节点与文档节点之间的图。推理时，agent 不只在扁平文件集合中迭代搜索，而是可以沿实体-文档图进行遍历：从一个文档出发，经由其关联实体页跳到其他包含同一实体但来源不同的文档，从而收集跨文档、原本不连通的互补证据。由于图是在离线阶段构建并解析实体，这些链接可被多个查询共享，不需要在每个查询的推理过程中重新发现，因此能减少检索轮次和 token 消耗。论文通过 7 个模型、3 个基准数据集验证了证据发现和答案质量相对原始语料 agentic search 的提升，并与 4 种替代导航层进行对比，表明实体可作为导航大文档集合的有效锚点。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "证据发现与答案质量", "value": "较 raw-corpus agentic search 提升，且平均使用更少 token；同时优于 4 种替代导航层", "setting": "7 个模型、3 个基准数据集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 CorpusMap 导航层，围绕语料中反复出现的实体组织文档集合；为每个实体构建 Entity Page，聚合实体信息并链接到所有引用该实体的文档，形成实体-文档图；通过离线解析跨文档的同实体提及来构建图，使链接跨查询共享，避免推理时重复发现关系；agent 可沿实体-文档图遍历以收集原本孤立的证据

Abstract: Answering questions and completing tasks over large document collections often requires connecting evidence spread across multiple documents, such as a project's approval recorded in one, its requirements in another, and its latest status in a third. Recent LLM agents approach this by iteratively searching the full corpus rather than reading only a fixed set of top-ranked documents. However, when the corpus is exposed only as a flat collection of files, a relevant document gives no indication of how it relates to others, so the agent must rediscover these relationships for every query, often missing complementary evidence while simultaneously consuming substantial additional tokens. To address this, we introduce CorpusMap, a navigation layer that organizes the corpus around its recurring entities, which are identifiable from the documents themselves and can link a single document to many others across sources. Specifically, CorpusMap represents each recurring entity as an Entity Page that aggregates information about it and links to every document that refers to it, forming a graph between entities and documents that the agent can traverse to gather otherwise disconnected evidence. Moreover, since CorpusMap is constructed offline by resolving mentions of the same entity across documents, its links are shared across queries rather than rediscovered repeatedly at inference time. Using 7 different models with 3 benchmark datasets, we show that CorpusMap improves both evidence discovery and answer quality over raw-corpus agentic search while using fewer tokens on average, and further outperforms 4 alternative navigation layers, suggesting that entities serve as effective anchors for navigating large document collections.

</details>


### [43] [Mnemon: Raw Records, Fast Judgments, Slow Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36059)
*Guangren Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mnemon 是一个长期记忆 agent，保留原始带日期记录，由 LLM 规划搜索、决策模型 Jev 快速判断证据，并用预算规则生成小型 View 供回答模型使用。在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 上取得高分，且上下文低于 4k tokens、成本增长低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不预先将对话重写为事实、图或结构化记忆的前提下，让 LLM 助手有效利用长期对话历史，同时控制上下文长度、计算成本和检索质量。

Method: Mnemon 将长期记忆分为 System 1 快速判断和 System 2 慢速思考。输入是原始、带日期的对话记录，不进行写入时重写。System 2 由 LLM 负责：规划搜索查询、命名回答所需内容并最终撰写回答。System 1 由决策模型 Jev 负责：对搜索结果返回的记录做大量小规模独立的是/否判断，例如某记录是否必要或是否已过时。显式预算规则把 Jev 的判断聚合成一个小的 View，供不变的 answering model 使用。后台处理会为每条记录一次性生成主题时间线、价值历史和常备指令，并链接回原始记录，使涉及整段对话的问题能够获取自身搜索可能遗漏的证据。推理时，LLM 先生成查询，搜索引擎在原始记录上检索，Jev 快速判断候选，预算规则筛选出 small View，最后由 answering model 基于该 View 生成答案。该设计减少了写入时的结构化开销，并通过 System 1/System 2 分工降低上下文长度和成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LoCoMo 得分", "value": "91.7%", "setting": "gpt-4.1-mini 作为 answering model，公开再评估中的 14 个系统对比"}, {"metric": "LongMemEval-S 得分", "value": "83.8%", "setting": "gpt-4.1-mini 作为 answering model，每问题上下文低于 4k tokens"}, {"metric": "LoCoMo 有效成本指数", "value": "最低", "setting": "gpt-4.1-mini 回答，与参与公开再评估的系统对比"}, {"metric": "LoCoMo 得分", "value": "92.2%", "setting": "推理模型作为 answering model"}, {"metric": "LongMemEval-S 得分", "value": "94.4%", "setting": "推理模型作为 answering model，与最佳公开结果持平"}, {"metric": "每问题成本增长因子（100K 到 10M tokens 历史）", "value": "1.11", "setting": "BEAM 数据集"}, {"metric": "Jev 相比两个 LLM 的 gold evidence 分离能力", "value": "更好", "setting": "同一批记录上"}, {"metric": "Jev 速度", "value": "快 3-11 倍", "setting": "相比两个 LLM 在同一记录上判断"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将记忆工作分为 System 1 快速判断与 System 2 慢速规划，用决策模型 Jev 对原始记录做大量独立的是/否判断。；保留原始、带日期的对话记录作为记忆存储，写入时不决定其语义，使同一 agent 可读取任何返回带日期记录的存储。；通过显式预算规则将 Jev 的判断转化为 small View，供不变的 answering model 使用。；后台处理将每条记录一次性整合为主题时间线、价值历史和常备指令，并链接回原始记录，以覆盖自身搜索遗漏的整段对话问题。

Abstract: Long-term memory lets an LLM assistant use a history it can no longer reread, and most memory systems build it by rewriting conversations into facts, graphs or typed memories at write time. We argue that the work of memory divides, as thinking does, into two systems. Most of it is fast System 1 work: many small, independent yes/no judgments about records, such as whether a record is needed or no longer current, which a decision model makes by the dozen in a third of a second. Only a little is slow System 2 work: writing a few search queries, naming what the reply needs and composing the answer, which an LLM does well but slowly. We present Mnemon, a memory agent built on this division. It keeps conversations as raw, dated records; an LLM (System 2) plans searches over them, a decision model, Jev (System 1), judges what the searches return, and rules with explicit budgets turn the judgments into a small View for an unchanged answering model. A background pass consolidates each record once into topic timelines, value histories and standing instructions linked to the records, so that questions about a whole conversation reach evidence their own searches miss. Because nothing is decided about a record when it is written, the same agent can read any store that returns dated records. With gpt-4.1-mini answering, as in a public re-evaluation of 14 systems, Mnemon scores 91.7% on LoCoMo, the highest among them, and 83.8% on LongMemEval-S, from under 4k tokens of context per question, with the lowest effective cost index on LoCoMo. With a reasoning model answering, it reaches 92.2% on LoCoMo and 94.4% on LongMemEval-S, the latter on par with the best published results. From 100K to 10M tokens of history on BEAM, its cost per question grows by a factor of 1.11. On the same records, Jev separates gold evidence better than two LLMs and is 3-11 times faster.

</details>

### [44] [AdviSD: Learning to Advise Frontier LLMs via Targeted Multi-Turn Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38142)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Hejie Cui,Shasha Li,Shanchan Wu,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdviSD 通过 selective self-distillation 将 outcome-based RL 与多轮反馈纠正结合，训练小型 advisor 引导冻结的 Gemini/Claude executor。理论上证明选择性学习纠正优于学习全部纠正，实验在 BFCL-v3 上超过 advisor-GRPO 4.2-6.4 个百分点，在 EnvScaler 上超过 3.9-5.1 分，并展示跨域、跨 executor 版本和模型家族泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何训练一个小型可训练 advisor，通过自然语言建议引导冻结的 LLM executor，并有效利用完成交互后的反馈进行学习，避免无效纠正损害策略，提升多轮 agent 任务表现。

Method: AdviSD 构建一个可训练的小型 advisor（Qwen3-8B）与冻结的 LLM executor（Gemini/Claude）的多轮交互系统。输入为任务指令与截至当前的多轮交互记录；executor 在接收或不接收 advisor 自然语言建议的条件下产生响应。系统包含三个关键角色：executor 冻结执行任务；advisor 可训练并生成建议；reflection 模块基于完成交互的反馈提出纠正。训练流程：（1）outcome-based reinforcement learning（如 advisor-GRPO），用任务结果奖励优化 advisor 生成有用建议；（2）reflection 根据已完成交互提出候选纠正；（3）选择性自蒸馏：advisor 对同一份记录响应分别在有建议和无建议条件下打分，以两者分差幅度衡量该建议是否实际改变 executor 行为，据此选择高影响纠正作为自蒸馏监督目标，形成 feedback-conditioned copy 的软目标。该方法不需要 executor 的 token 似然，也不需要额外 executor rollout，因此训练成本可控。理论上，在共享参数模型中若某些纠正的目标对有用建议偏好较弱，学习这些纠正会稀释策略学习，选择性保留可提升最终性能。推理时，训练后的 advisor 对任务生成建议，冻结 executor 据此执行，形成 advisor-executor 协作的 agent 推理路径。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "BFCL-v3", "value": "4.2-6.4 percentage points over advisor-GRPO", "setting": "Qwen3-8B advisors for Gemini and Claude"}, {"metric": "EnvScaler", "value": "3.9-5.1 score points over advisor-GRPO", "setting": "Qwen3-8B advisors for Gemini and Claude"}, {"metric": "matched-count random selection", "value": "AdviSD beats matched-count random selection", "setting": "消融，验证选择规则价值"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 AdviSD：将结果驱动的强化学习与来自反馈条件化 advisor 副本的选择性自蒸馏结合；理论证明：在共享参数模型中，若某些纠正的目标对有用建议的偏好弱于其他纠正，持续学习这些纠正会限制学习，选择性保留可提升最终性能；选择性自蒸馏机制：reflection 提出纠正后，advisor 对同一记录 executor 响应在有无建议两种条件下打分，用差异幅度选择监督决策，无需 executor 似然或额外 executor rollout；实验表明训练后的 advisor 可泛化到域外任务，并跨 executor 版本与模型家族迁移

Abstract: A small trainable advisor can steer a frozen language-model executor using natural-language advice. In addition to learning from task rewards, the advisor can use feedback from completed interactions to improve its advice. However, a plausible correction need not change execution, yet learning from such corrections can still affect the advisor's future decisions in other contexts. In a shared-parameter model, we prove that such corrections can limit learning if their targets favor useful advice less strongly than those of other corrections. Keeping them less often than the rest improves the model's eventual performance compared to learning from every correction. Motivated by this, our method, Advisor Self-Distillation (AdviSD), pairs outcome-based reinforcement learning with self-distillation from a feedback-conditioned copy of the advisor selectively. Reflection proposes corrections, and the advisor scores the same recorded executor response with and without its issued advice, using the magnitude of the difference to select decisions for supervision. This approach does not require executor likelihoods or additional executor rollouts. Experiments with Qwen3-8B advisors for Gemini and Claude show that AdviSD outperforms advisor-GRPO by 4.2-6.4 percentage points on BFCL-v3 and by 3.9-5.1 score points on EnvScaler. The trained advisors generalize to out-of-domain tasks and transfer across different executor versions and model families. AdviSD also beats matched-count random selection, supporting the value of its selection rule.

</details>


### [45] [Do LLM Agents Execute the Plans They Declare? From Planning-Mode Declaration to Pattern-Specific Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38108)
*Subba Reddy Oota,Francisco Herrera,Jordi Cabot Sagrera,Marcos López de Prado,Shadab Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Planning-as-Routing：让 LLM 先声明四种规划模式之一（Predefined/Sequential/Hierarchical/Search），再由确定性路由器分发到模式特定执行器。实验表明通用 Plan+ReAct 仅 22%–45% 轨迹保持声明结构，而模式特定执行器把 ALFWorld 成功率从 0.48 提升到 0.92、SWE-bench Verified 从 0.36 提升到 0.44，同时指出 LLM 仍不能可靠选择最优模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 planner-executor 系统无法区分“规划模式选择错误”与“规划执行不忠实”两类失败，导致最终任务成功率无法定位真正瓶颈，长程计划尤其容易被通用 Plan+ReAct 破坏声明结构。

Method: 系统包含两个阶段。第一阶段为模式声明：给定任务输入，LLM 从四种规划模式 Predefined（预定义固定流程）、Sequential（线性步骤序列）、Hierarchical（层级分解子目标）、Search（搜索式探索）中选择一种并输出声明；该声明是显式文本输出，便于后续审计。第二阶段为确定性路由与执行：一个确定性路由器读取声明，将任务分派给对应的模式特定执行器；每个执行器内部实现与该模式匹配的控制结构，例如线性执行器顺序推进步骤、层级执行器按子目标递归展开、搜索执行器进行状态空间探索、预定义执行器按固定模板执行，从而在解码与状态更新层面强制保持声明的规划结构，避免通用 ReAct 循环在长计划中丢弃或重排步骤。训练目标方面论文未披露对模型进行额外训练，属于提示与编排层面的方法；评估端以任务成功率与轨迹结构保持率作为指标，在四个基准、三个 LLM 上比较 Plan+ReAct 与模式特定执行器的差异，并单独分析模式选择能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "轨迹结构保持率", "value": "22%–45%", "setting": "通用 Plan+ReAct 在三个基准上的长计划轨迹结构保持比例"}, {"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "0.48 → 0.92", "setting": "ALFWorld，从 Plan+ReAct 切换到模式特定执行器"}, {"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "0.36 → 0.44", "setting": "SWE-bench Verified，从 Plan+ReAct 切换到模式特定执行器"}, {"metric": "模式选择准确率", "value": "未可靠选择最优模式（无具体数值披露）", "setting": "少样本示例在部分基准-模型组合上改善选择"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Plan Declaration–Execution Gap 概念，将规划失败显式拆分为模式选择失败与执行忠实性失败；提出 Planning-as-Routing 范式：LLM 先声明四种规划模式之一（Predefined、Sequential、Hierarchical、Search），再由确定性路由器分发到对应模式特定执行器；设计四种模式特定执行器，强制保持声明计划的结构，而非依赖通用 ReAct 循环；跨四个基准、三个 LLM 系统化验证规划模式选择与执行忠实性的独立影响

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable agents to solve long-horizon tasks by generating a plan and then executing it in an environment. However, successful planning requires two distinct capabilities: selecting an appropriate plan for the task and executing it faithfully. Existing planner--executor systems can fail at either stage, while final task success alone cannot distinguish selection from execution failures. We therefore study the Plan Declaration--Execution Gap and introduce Planning-as-Routing, where an LLM declares one of four planning modes: Predefined, Sequential, Hierarchical, or Search, and a deterministic router dispatches the task to the corresponding pattern-specific executor. Across four benchmarks and three LLMs, we find three consistent patterns. First, generic Plan+ReAct often fails to preserve declared planning structure, especially for longer plans: across three benchmarks, only (22)--(45%) of trajectories preserve it, whereas pattern-specific executors enforce the intended structure. Second, planning-mode effectiveness varies across environments and models: Search performs best on ALFWorld, Hierarchical on SWE-bench, and the strongest pattern can vary across models within the same benchmark. Third, the largest gains come from execution: pattern-specific executors improve task success from (0.48) to (0.92) on ALFWorld and from (0.36) to (0.44) on SWE-bench Verified over Plan+ReAct. Current LLMs, however, do not reliably select the strongest mode for each task, although few-shot examples improve selection in some benchmark--model combinations. Overall, reliable agent planning requires both effective mode selection and faithful execution: routing substantially closes the execution gap, while task-specific mode selection remains open.

</details>


### [46] [UserProxyBench: Evaluating LLM User Simulators for Agent Benchmarks and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38043)
*Ashish Jain,Armaan Sandhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UserProxyBench提出评估LLM用户模拟器保真度的基准和UFS指标，发现在固定Agent时更换用户模拟器可使平均任务奖励变化15.2分，24.4%成功回合存在用户违反规范，且主要是过早披露。该工作揭示用户模拟器保真度对Agent评估的重要性，并给出成本-保真度前沿以指导选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式Agent基准仅评估Agent，不直接度量LLM用户模拟器是否忠实执行了其被分配的用户角色，导致用户模拟器的保真度缺失。

Method: UserProxyBench作为tau-bench系列上的评估层，其端到端流程为：使用固定的Agent（GPT-5.5）与不同的用户模拟器（user proxy）在375个企业任务上交互。用户模拟器根据基准的私有用户指令扮演用户角色，控制向Agent提供信息的内容和时机。评估时，独立于Agent成功与否，通过任务锚定的rubric准则计算User Fidelity Score (UFS)，度量用户模拟器对私有指令的遵守程度。通过比较不同用户模拟器下的平均任务奖励、UFS分数、用户规范违反比例、工具调用次数等指标，分析用户模拟器行为对评估结果的影响。最终在7个代理上绘制成本-保真度前沿，以指导实践者选择最经济的模拟器。没有披露网络结构、训练目标或推理加速细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean task reward", "value": "变化15.2 points", "setting": "固定Agent为GPT-5.5，在不同用户代理间变动，375个企业任务"}, {"metric": "用户规范违反比例", "value": "24.4%", "setting": "成功回合中"}, {"metric": "工具调用减少次数", "value": "1.06 fewer tool calls on average", "setting": "成功回合中，由过早披露导致"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出UserProxyBench，在tau-bench系列上构建评估层。；提出User Fidelity Score (UFS)，基于任务锚定的rubric准则独立于Agent成功度量用户指令遵守度。；发现用户模拟器保真度变化导致平均任务奖励波动15.2分，且24.4%的成功回合存在用户规范违反，主要失败模式为过早披露。；揭示过早披露降低Agent工具调用次数约1.06次，但几乎不影响任务奖励，改变了交互本身。；在7个代理上识别出经验性的成本-保真度前沿，用于选择满足保真度要求的最便宜模拟器。

Abstract: Interactive agent benchmarks and multi-turn reinforcement learning increasingly place a second language model in the role of the user. This simulated user controls what information the agent receives and when, yet current benchmarks score only the agent and do not directly measure whether the user correctly executed its assigned role. We introduce UserProxyBench, an evaluation layer over the tau-bench family, and the User Fidelity Score (UFS), which measures adherence to the benchmark's private user instructions using task-grounded rubric criteria scored independently of agent success. Holding the agent fixed at GPT-5.5 and varying only the user proxy across 375 enterprise tasks changes mean task reward by 15.2 points, while 24.4% of successful episodes contain a user-specification violation. The dominant failure is premature disclosure: users provide information before it is requested. This behavior has little effect on task reward, yet among successful episodes it causes the agent to make 1.06 fewer tool calls on average, changing the interaction being evaluated while preserving the reward. Finally, across seven proxies we identify an empirical cost-fidelity frontier, enabling practitioners to select the least expensive simulator that satisfies a required fidelity level.

</details>


### [47] [Retrieval-Augmented Skill Optimization via Cross-Harness Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38024)
*Jaewon Chu,Ji Soo Lee,Jihwan Park,Dohwan Ko,Jeehye Na,Seunghun Lee,Taehoon Lee,Minseo Yoon,Minseok Joo,Yunyang Xiong,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RASO 提出检索增强的技能优化框架，利用外部技能语料库通过 RASI 初始化与 RASU 迭代更新来优化 agent skill，并在四个基准两个模型上验证优于无检索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent skill 优化方法主要依赖昂贵的 agent rollout 进行迭代精炼，未利用已积累的百万级公开技能知识，且未处理目标任务与已检索技能在领域和 harness 上的不匹配。

Method: RASO 以外部 agent 技能语料库为输入知识源，目标是在给定 harness 下为目标任务生成并迭代优化可复用的自然语言技能工件。整体包含两个互补阶段：Retrieval-Augmented Skill Initialization (RASI) 从已有技能中检索相关知识，通过 Cross-Harness Adaptation 处理目标任务与检索技能之间在领域和 harness 上的不匹配，从而在不需要 agent rollout 的情况下构建知识接地的初始技能；Retrieval-Augmented Skill Update (RASU) 则在后续迭代中依据 agent 执行反馈检索外部知识，对技能进行精炼更新。框架贯穿始终使用外部技能语料作为先验，以减少对昂贵 rollout 的依赖，并在四个 agent 基准和两个模型上验证了有效性。摘要未披露具体的检索器结构、适配模块细节、训练目标或推理开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将外部技能语料库作为先验知识引入 skill 优化全过程；提出 Retrieval-Augmented Skill Initialization (RASI)，无需 agent rollout 即可构建知识接地的初始技能；提出 Retrieval-Augmented Skill Update (RASU)，由执行反馈引导检索外部知识并迭代精炼技能；Cross-Harness Adaptation，同时处理领域与 harness 两类不匹配

Abstract: An agent skill is a reusable, actionable natural-language artifact that guides an agent to perform a task effectively under a given harness. Recent studies have explored the optimization of agent skills, contributing to a growing collection of publicly available skills spanning diverse tasks, domains, and harnesses. Despite millions of publicly shared skills, existing skill optimization methods largely overlook this accumulated knowledge, instead relying solely on expensive agent rollouts to iteratively refine skills for a target task. To address this, we propose \textbf{Retrieval-Augmented Skill Optimization (RASO)}, a framework that leverages an external skill corpus as prior knowledge throughout skill optimization. RASO retrieves relevant knowledge from existing skills and adapts it to the target task and harness via Cross-Harness Adaptation, accounting for mismatches in both domain and harness. RASO comprises two complementary stages: \textbf{Retrieval-Augmented Skill Initialization (RASI)} constructs a knowledge-grounded initial skill without requiring agent rollouts, while \textbf{Retrieval-Augmented Skill Update (RASU)} iteratively refines the skill by retrieving external knowledge guided by execution feedback. Across four agent benchmarks and two models, extensive experiments show that RASO consistently outperforms baselines without retrieval-augmented skill initialization and updating.

</details>


### [48] [HARISSA: Inference-Time Self-Checks for Efficient and Safe Local Language Model Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38006)
*Kenan Alkiek,Moontae Lee,David Jurgens,V.G.Vinod Vydiswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HARISSA利用语言模型自身的prefill与answer隐藏状态预测正确性，在本地部署中实现推理时级联决策：对困难查询决定是否增加计算或转人工。单设备上较CoT延迟降低2.7倍而准确率差距在1点内，服务器设置下优于FrugalGPT和Self-REF级联，并在多数任务中减少错误答案交付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署的小模型面对困难查询时，需要在保持本地隐私与成本优势的前提下，决定哪些查询值得花费更多推理计算，以及哪些答案足够安全可以直接交付，否则应转人工。

Method: HARISSA在本地部署的单模型上工作。输入为查询，模型首先进行prefill计算，得到prefill隐藏状态；该状态被训练用于预测模型最终是否会正确回答。若prefill状态预测某一种回答方式会失败，则跳过该方式直接尝试更昂贵的回答方式。模型随后生成答案，在答案结束时得到answer隐藏状态；该状态被训练用于预测当前生成的答案是否正确。最终策略将两种预测组合：prefill预测用于在从廉价到昂贵的回答方式（如直接回答、增加推理等）之间级联，answer预测用于在停止于某一回答方式时判断答案是否安全，若预测错误则转人工。模型经过微调使两个状态同时具有正确性预测能力，从而在推理时无需额外云模型即可做出效率与安全决策。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 利用prefill阶段隐藏状态预测模型是否会正确回答，用于决定是否跳过某些更昂贵的回答方式。；利用answer阶段末尾隐藏状态预测生成答案是否正确，用于决定是否交付或转人工。；微调模型使两个状态均可预测正确性，并用单一策略在从廉价到昂贵的回答方式间级联，依据prefill预测跳过可能失败的路径，依据answer预测决定是否转人工。

Abstract: Running a language model locally offers advantages in privacy, latency, and cost, but local hardware fits only small models, which are less capable than frontier models. The usual remedy for a hard query, escalating it to a cloud model, gives up the privacy and cost advantages of running locally. A deployment that stays local faces two decisions for hard queries instead. First, it can spend more computation on a query, e.g., reasoning before answering, which raises accuracy at a cost in latency, so it must decide which queries are worth the extra computation (efficiency). Second, some queries are beyond the local model, and delivering a wrong answer is worse than deferring the query to a human in the loop, so it must decide which answers are safe to deliver (safety). We show that both decisions can be made from the model's own hidden states. The prefill state, computed before any token is generated, predicts whether the model will answer correctly, and the answer state, at the end of the generated answer, predicts whether that answer is correct. HARISSA fine-tunes the model so that both states predict correctness, then makes both decisions with one policy that cascades through the ways of answering from cheapest to most expensive, skipping a way the prefill state predicts will fail and deferring the query when the answer it stops with is predicted wrong. On a device running a single model, HARISSA is within one accuracy point of chain-of-thought at 2.7 times lower latency. On a server holding four sizes of one model, HARISSA is more accurate than the FrugalGPT and Self-REF cascades at the same latency, and at the same deferral rate the answer state leaves fewer wrong answers than the standard confidence signals in five of six task and setting pairs.

</details>


### [49] [KV-Kaizen: Learning Context-Adaptive Cache Compression Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37988)
*Joao Monteiro,Louis Béthune,Anastasiia Filippova,Sonia Laguna,David Grangier,Marco Cuturi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KV-Kaizen 提出学习一个上下文自适应选择器，在层间共享、低比特量化和低秩截断三个维度上为每层 KV cache 选择压缩配置，在不驱逐 token 的情况下实现 32 倍解码缓存缩减并保持精度，可与 token 驱逐互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着上下文增长，KV cache 内存超过模型权重，解码内存受限导致吞吐下降；现有 token 驱逐方法存在丢弃后难以恢复的矛盾，需要在不驱逐 token 的前提下实现自适应缓存压缩。

Method: KV-Kaizen 采用一个学习的选择器，以当前上下文为输入，为每一层 Transformer 输出一个压缩配置。压缩策略沿三个轴：深度方向（部分层共享同一 KV cache）、精度方向（以更低比特存储 KV）、秩方向（截断低秩潜在表示）。选择器根据上下文决定每层如何组合这些轴，以在整体压缩预算下最大化精度。推理时，选择器在 pre-fill 之前运行一次，生成各层的配置，然后模型按配置进行自回归解码。训练目标未明确披露，但推测为在压缩预算约束下最大化下游任务精度。该方法可与 token 驱逐方法组合。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "decode-time cache size", "value": "32x smaller", "setting": "14B 模型，长上下文任务，结合驱逐方法，保持精度"}, {"metric": "accuracy degradation", "value": "无精度下降", "setting": "4x cache 压缩，7B 及以上模型"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "压缩模型优于同等 cache 大小的更小未压缩模型", "setting": "未披露具体指标"}, {"metric": "Pareto frontier", "value": "达到精度-Cache 大小的 Pareto 前沿", "setting": "指令跟随与推理任务，对比无学习及事后基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 KV-Kaizen：基于上下文学习一个选择器，为每层自适应配置缓存压缩策略（层间共享、低比特精度、低秩截断），在压缩预算下保持精度。；将三类压缩轴（depth、precision、rank）局部自适应组合，而非全局统一应用，避免单轴统一压缩带来的精度下降。；推理时选择器仅在 pre-fill 前运行一次，开销低，且可与 token 驱逐方法组合。

Abstract: As the context size of text processed with an LLM grows, the size of KV caches can outstrip the memory allocated for the original model weights. This impacts LLM throughput negatively, since decoding is memory-bound and decode cost grows with cache size. Recent work alleviates this bottleneck by discarding the least relevant tokens. Eviction introduces a tension, since a one-off decision to discard content may prove detrimental later. Instead, we focus on alternative choices that can lead to cache compression without evicting tokens. We achieve this by learning a selector that is able to produce, based on context, a per-layer cache configuration towards an overall compression budget. The selector operates along three axes: sharing one cache across layers (depth), caching at fewer bits (precision), or truncating the low-rank latent cache representations (rank). We call the resulting method KV-Kaizen, for the many small per-layer choices it compounds. We observe that these interventions taken independently and uniformly over all layers limit achievable compression because they degrade accuracy. Crucially, composing them locally and adaptively to the context can instead preserve accuracy while achieving large memory savings. At inference, the selector runs once, before pre-fill. In evaluations on instruction following and reasoning tasks, our selectors reach the Pareto frontier of accuracy against cache size, against learning-free and post-hoc baselines. On long-context tasks, KV-Kaizen improves on eviction and can be composed with it, reaching a 32x smaller decode-time cache on a 14B model while preserving accuracy. A 4x cache size reduction incurs no accuracy degradation from 7B parameters up, and a compressed model is more accurate than a smaller uncompressed one with the same cache size. Together, these findings support pre-training large models and compressing them only afterwards.

</details>


### [50] [SelfSearch: Reward-Free Search for Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37968)
*Jungwoo Yang,In Jin Kong,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SelfSearch 提出一种无需奖励的搜索程序，智能体利用先前自我改进回合的记录来修改自身，在不使用下游奖励信号的情况下提升下游能力，同时降低搜索和执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体自我改进方法依赖重复的下游评估来搜索更好的智能体，这会产生大量成本，并将搜索绑定到特定评估任务上。在不依赖下游奖励信号的情况下，智能体能否通过自我修改来提升自身下游能力？

Method: SelfSearch 是一种无需奖励的搜索过程，智能体通过利用先前自我改进回合的记录来修改自身。这些记录捕获了早期修改尝试的推理、工具动作和结果。搜索过程中不提供任何下游奖励信号。智能体利用这些记录来提升任务求解和自我修改能力。该过程迭代运行：智能体修改其指令、工具和执行流程，这些修改的结果被存储为记录，为未来的修改提供具体经验。搜索过程使用有限预算（例如 4.03 美元）生产出一个改进后的执行框架。然后在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 和 SWE-bench Multilingual 等下游基准上评估演化后的智能体。该论文未披露具体模块架构、训练目标或硬件细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "success rate", "value": "up to +11.2 percentage points (individual agents)", "setting": "Terminal-Bench 2.1"}, {"metric": "success rate", "value": "+5.0 percentage points", "setting": "SWE-bench Multilingual"}, {"metric": "execution cost", "value": "-38.5%", "setting": "SWE-bench Multilingual, on tasks solved by both initial and evolved agents"}, {"metric": "population-mean success", "value": "improved over initial agent in all six model-benchmark settings", "setting": "six model-benchmark settings"}, {"metric": "task success with only $4.03 search cost", "value": "82.0% of Terminal-Bench 2.1 tasks solved with DeepSeek V4 Flash, matching top-scoring harness Codex", "setting": "public nine-harness comparison"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 一种无需奖励的搜索过程，智能体利用先前自我改进过程的记录来修改自身，而非使用下游评估奖励；利用捕获推理、工具动作和结果的记录，为任务求解和自我修改提供具体经验；在六个模型-基准设置中改善种群平均成功率，并相对于评估引导的搜索基线降低搜索成本

Abstract: Advances in the coding capabilities of LLM agents allow them to inspect and modify their own instructions, tools, and execution procedures. Existing approaches use this ability to search for improved agents through repeated downstream evaluation, which incurs substantial costs and ties the search to the evaluated tasks. We introduce \textbf{SelfSearch}, a reward-free search procedure in which agents modify themselves using records of previous self-improvement episodes. These records capture the reasoning, tool actions, and outcomes of earlier modification attempts, providing concrete experience for improving both task solving and self-modification. Without downstream reward signals during search, SelfSearch improves population-mean success over the initial agent in all six model--benchmark settings, with individual agents gaining up to 11.2 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1. On SWE-bench Multilingual, an agent improves success by \textbf{5.0} percentage points while reducing execution cost by \textbf{38.5}\% on tasks solved by both the initial and evolved agents. SelfSearch achieves competitive task success with evaluation-guided search baselines at lower search cost. With only \textbf{\$4.03} in search cost, it produces a harness that solves \textbf{82.0}\% of Terminal-Bench 2.1 tasks with DeepSeek V4 Flash under the settings of a public nine-harness comparison, matching the top-scoring harness, Codex. These results suggest that experience gained through self-modification can improve agents' downstream capabilities and efficiency.

</details>


### [51] [Topological Coherence for Self-evolving Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37953)
*Sen Zhao,Ruiqi Kong,Zuyu Zhang,Lifeng Shen,Xinyu He,Xu Zhang,Qinghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TOCOMAS，以拓扑一致性为原则，将任务图落地到工具接口并组织责任域，推导依赖驱动与profile条件的协作及边界约束的内存可见性，并通过在线自演化保留满足结构约束且提升奖励的候选。在BBEH、WorkBench、SWE-Bench-Verified、CoMemBench上提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂任务中工作流结构、智能体责任、协作与记忆访问彼此耦合，但现有联合优化方法不保证责任边界、交接边界与记忆边界和任务依赖保持一致，即缺乏“拓扑一致性”（topological coherence）。

Method: TOCOMAS以任务图为核心表示，首先将任务图基于工具接口进行落地，使任务节点与可用工具接口绑定。随后将兼容的任务节点组织为可复用的责任域（responsibility domains），每个责任域界定责任范围与工具范围。基于任务依赖与profile条件推导协作关系，产生跨域交接（handoffs），并形成边界约束的内存可见性规则，区分私有与选择性共享内存。在线自演化阶段，系统提出对agent、协作与记忆策略的耦合变更，仅保留满足结构约束且提升评估奖励的候选用于后续任务。整体为任务图→责任域→协作/交接→边界约束记忆→在线自演化闭环的agent编排流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "相对基线提升", "setting": "BBEH、WorkBench、SWE-Bench-Verified、CoMemBench多基准，多种backbone"}, {"metric": "verified progress、handoffs、memory isolation", "value": "优于自演化基线", "setting": "CoMemBench"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出拓扑一致性（topological coherence）概念，要求责任、交接与记忆边界与任务依赖保持一致；将任务图基于工具接口进行落地，把兼容任务节点组织为可复用责任域；从任务依赖与profile条件导出协作关系，并实现边界约束的内存可见性；在线自演化中提出代理、协作、记忆策略的耦合变更，只保留满足结构约束并提升评估奖励的候选

Abstract: Complex tasks inherently couple workflow structure, agent responsibility, collaboration, and memory access: task regions delimit responsibility and tool scope, cross-region dependencies give rise to handoffs, and ownership boundaries delimit private and selectively shared memory. Existing methods can jointly optimize agent and communication structures, yet such optimization does not by itself require responsibility, handoff, and memory boundaries to remain consistent with task dependencies. We term this requirement topological coherence. We introduce TOCOMAS, a Topology-Coherent Multi-Agent System. TOCOMAS grounds a task graph in tool interfaces, organizes compatible task nodes into reusable responsibility domains, and derives dependency-induced and profile-conditioned collaboration together with boundary-regulated memory visibility. During online self-evolution, TOCOMAS proposes coupled changes to agent, collaboration, and memory policies, retaining for subsequent tasks only candidates that satisfy structural constraints and improve evaluated reward. Across BBEH, WorkBench, SWE-Bench-Verified, and CoMemBench, TOCOMAS improves task success over baselines across backbones. CoMemBench also shows gains over the self-evolving baseline in verified progress, handoffs, and memory isolation.

</details>


### [52] [Active Budget Can Kill Sensitivity: Diagnosing and Repairing TopK Sparse Autoencoder Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37857)
*Zhenting Huang,Junnan Liu,Qianren Mao,Zhixing Tan,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文诊断 TopK 稀疏自编码器在字典变宽时稀有特征敏感度退化的问题，通过 width×k 因子实验证明 active budget k（选择边界）而非字典宽度是根因，并用 active margin 无阈值预测特征丢失；提出 pairwise rank stabilization 修复截断处排序失败，稀有特征敏感度提升 8.83 个百分点，重构与存活特征覆盖接近基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随字典宽度扩大，TopK SAE 中稀有特征在语义表层变化下变得不稳定，导致特征不再是可靠的解释单元；需要诊断根因并修复这种敏感性问题。

Method: 论文以 TopK 稀疏自编码器为研究对象：输入为大语言模型的激活向量，编码器产生特征激活，TopK 模块只保留激活值最大的 k 个特征（active budget k），其余置零，再由解码器重构原激活。训练目标沿用重构损失并施加 TopK 稀疏约束。分析路径上，作者先以特征敏感度作为可靠性指标，度量同一语义在不同表层表达下特征是否保持稳定；随后设计宽度×k 的受控因子实验，分别改变字典宽度与 k，定位退化来源。诊断发现 TopK 选择存在选择边界：当特征激活值接近第 k 名的截断值（active margin 小）时，微小扰动即改变其是否入选，导致稀有特征丢失，且该失败可由 active margin 无阈值地预测。修复方法 pairwise rank stabilization 针对截断附近的排序失败进行成对排序稳定化，使边界处的相对次序不因微小扰动而翻转，从而在不显著损害重构与存活特征覆盖的前提下提升稀有特征敏感度。推断/使用流程仍为标准 SAE 前向：编码→TopK 选择→解码，分析流程则在多种语义等价表层变体下重复前向并统计特征稳定性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "稀有特征敏感度（rare-feature sensitivity）", "value": "提升 8.83 个百分点", "setting": "与基线 TopK SAE 对比，重构质量和存活特征覆盖率接近基线"}, {"metric": "重构质量、alive-feature 覆盖率", "value": "接近基线（未披露具体数值）", "setting": "加入 pairwise rank stabilization 后"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 以特征敏感度（feature sensitivity）量化 SAE 特征在语义等价表层变化下的稳定性；通过 width×k 因子实验定位 active budget k（而非字典宽度本身）是稀有特征敏感度退化的根因；提出基于 active margin（到 TopK 选择截断的距离）的无阈值特征丢失预测分析，解释 TopK 选择几何导致的失败；提出 pairwise rank stabilization，针对截断处排序失败进行修复

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly scaled to wider dictionaries to recover fine-grained structure from large language model activations. However, a feature is useful for interpretation only if it remains a stable unit of analysis when the same meaning is expressed in different surface forms. We study this reliability question for TopK SAEs via feature sensitivity. Experiments demonstrate that scaling selectively reduces the sensitivity of rare features, while common features remain comparatively stable. A controlled width\(\times k\) factorial experiment identifies the active budget k as the root cause: the degradation arises from the selection boundary rather than dictionary width alone. We attribute this failure to the geometry of TopK selection. The active margin, the distance to the cutoff, predicts feature loss without thresholds. Guided by this margin diagnosis, we introduce pairwise rank stabilization. Our method targets ordering failures at the cutoff and improves rare-feature sensitivity by \(8.83\) percentage points, while keeping reconstruction and alive-feature coverage near the baseline. Overall, our results suggest that wide TopK SAEs should be evaluated not only by reconstruction, sparsity, and feature count, but also by feature reliability under semantic variation and boundary geometry for stable interpretability.

</details>


### [53] [Mixture of Self-Improving Branches For Agent Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37834)
*Haoyu Dong,Yuhang Zhou,Zihao Lin,Yifan Wu,Bo Peng,Mingyi Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Mixture of Self-Improving Branches，将 agent harness 的自改进搜索组织为多分支动态演化，并引入路由器在推理时选择分支头部 harness。在数学推理和 agentic coding 基准上相对 Meta-Harness 分别提升 34.8%、11.6% 和 3.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归自改进（RSI）方法如 Meta-Harness 使用固定开发集和提案策略，导致搜索轨迹单一、易陷入局部最优，无法产生互补的 harness 并用路由器部署。

Method: 系统以 Meta-Harness 迭代代码生成与评估为基础，将改进过程组织为多个分支。每个分支维护一个动态开发子集和一个提案策略：分支保留被其领先 harness 解决次数多于其他分支领先 harness 的开发案例，丢弃被所有分支全部领先 harness 解决的案例，并利用本分支搜索历史修订提案策略。各分支独立演化出互补的 harness（代码实现）。部署时，路由器基于开发集数据为每个新输入选择一个分支头部 harness 再执行。训练/优化目标为在开发集上通过执行反馈最大化各分支 harness 的解决率，并最终通过路由器组合各分支优势。推理时，输入先经路由器选择 harness，再执行得到输出。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对 Meta-Harness 提升", "value": "34.8%", "setting": "Olympiad 级数学推理"}, {"metric": "相对 Meta-Harness 提升", "value": "11.6%", "setting": "Terminal-Bench 2.0"}, {"metric": "相对 Meta-Harness 提升", "value": "3.8%", "setting": "SWE-bench Lite"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 harness 改进过程组织为多个分支，每分支维护动态演化的开发子集和提案策略；分支保留领先 harness 相较其他分支更能解决的开发案例，丢弃被所有分支领先 harness 解决的案例；每个分支根据自身搜索历史修订提案策略；提出路由器，在测试时为每个新输入选择一个开发集选出的分支头部 harness，无需测试结果

Abstract: Harness optimization provides a practical setting for recursive self-improvement (RSI), where agent-generated modifications inform subsequent changes through execution feedback. Recent work such as Meta-Harness implements this process through iterative code generation and evaluation, but retains a fixed development set and proposal policy. These constraints channel evolution along a single search trajectory, increasing the risk of converging to a local optimum. We make the improvement process itself adaptive by organizing search into branches with evolving development subsets and proposal policies. Each branch retains development cases solved by more of its leading harnesses than by those of other branches, drops cases solved by every leading harness across all branches, and revises its proposal policy using its own search history. To deploy the resulting complementary harnesses, we propose a router to select one development-selected branch head for each new input before execution. Across mathematical reasoning and agentic coding benchmarks, our system achieves relative improvements over Meta-Harness of 34.8% on Olympiad-level mathematical reasoning, 11.6% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, and 3.8% on SWE-bench Lite, with harness selection and router configuration based solely on development data. These results show that evolving branch objectives and proposal policies can yield complementary harnesses whose strengths a router combines without access to test outcomes.

</details>


### [54] [Can a Cacheable Decision Model Follow Rules?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37832)
*Dushyant Rajput,Nirdesh Chauhan,Siddharth Kosaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Certo是一个基于Qwen3-4B的非生成式决策模型，对比联合评分与可缓存独立编码的规则敏感性。实验表明可缓存编码在77个候选时成本降低约5倍，但recall@1从1.00骤降至0.24；反事实监督可恢复合成规则上的性能，但向真实规则的迁移未确立，联合评分器仍具优势。对推荐系统算法工程师在决策模型缓存架构与规则遵循方面有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将非生成式决策模型改造为可缓存编码器以降低推理成本时，如何保持对规则的敏感性，并评估训练能否恢复该能力。

Method: Certo是一个基于Qwen3-4B的小型非生成决策模型。输入状态、规则和候选动作的文本；联合评分器（joint scorer）将三者拼接后编码并输出概率，因此成本随候选数量线性增长。独立编码器（cacheable encoder）分别编码状态与每个候选动作，候选编码可跨状态缓存复用，在77个候选时计算成本约降低5倍，但状态与候选之间的交互被切断，导致规则敏感性下降。训练方面，论文采用针对性反事实监督来尝试恢复独立编码器的规则敏感性；推理时，独立编码器先缓存候选表示，再与状态表示组合进行评分。实验对比了联合评分器与独立编码器在合成规则任务和真实规则任务上的表现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "recall@1", "value": "1.00 -> 0.24", "setting": "联合评分器到可缓存独立编码器的转换实验中，可缓存评分丢失规则敏感性"}, {"metric": "recall@1", "value": "1.00", "setting": "联合评分器在同一实验中保持"}, {"metric": "准确率（短规则层级）", "value": "0.861 vs 0.500", "setting": "真实规则，修正截断混淆后，联合评分器显著优于可缓存编码器"}, {"metric": "准确率（困难规则层级）", "value": "0.655 vs 0.483", "setting": "真实规则，n=29，联合评分器方向性优于可缓存编码器"}, {"metric": "合同准确率变化", "value": "-9.3 至 -16.2 个百分点", "setting": "匹配的跨领域真实散文混合训练未能提升且降低合同准确率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出并对比联合评分器与独立编码器的规则敏感性差异；通过针对性反事实监督恢复独立编码器在合成规则任务上的规则敏感性；揭示可缓存编码器在真实规则上的迁移局限，并识别截断混淆；量化联合评分器在短规则层级上的显著优势

Abstract: Certo is a small non-generative decision model (Qwen3-4B): it scores candidate actions from their text and returns a probability, instead of generating an answer. The accurate design reads the state, the rules, and each candidate together (a joint scorer), so cost grows with the menu. Independent encoding lets each candidate be encoded once and reused across states (about 5x cheaper at 77 candidates), but separates state from candidate. We ask how much rule-sensitivity survives that move, and whether it can be trained back. Four experiments on Certo: (1) the tested conversion to cacheable scoring loses rule-sensitivity (recall@1 1.00 -> 0.24) while the joint scorer holds 1.00, and a shortlist+rerank rescue fails; (2) targeted counterfactual supervision restores strong performance on held-out synthetic rule tasks (paraphrase, counterfactual, composition; reproducible across seeds), though we do not isolate whether predictions depend on the supplied rule; (3) on real rules the added benefit is not established -- after fixing a truncation confound, the joint scorer wins significantly on the short tier (0.861 vs 0.500) and directionally on the hard tier (0.655 vs 0.483, n=29); (4) a matched cross-domain real-prose mixture did not help and reduced contract accuracy (-9.3, -16.2 points). A cacheable encoder can be made rule-sensitive on its training distribution, but transfer to unseen-source real rules is not established; the joint scorer keeps an edge at the cost of caching.

</details>


### [55] [Guide, Then Let Go: Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling for Sparse-Reward Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37898)
*Youling Huang,Tiankuo Xu,Jiaji Liu,Tong Zheng,Shuo Zhou,Shaotong Qi,Junchi Yao,Shiyang Liu,Hao Xu,Pengcheng Xu,Bo Huang,Hongyi Fu,Lin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对稀疏奖励 Agentic RL 的冷启动问题，提出 Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling (GATS)：在 student 的 RL 目标中加入 on-policy distillation 项，并按 teacher-student 性能差距自适应降低与撤销教师指导；在 ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld 三种 Qwen2.5 配置上平均成功率超过 reward-only GRPO 4.37%-11.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域 Agent 使用稀疏 outcome reward 时存在严重冷启动问题：早期策略难以完成采样任务，奖励信号不足；而 on-policy distillation 的收益又依赖教师与学生之间的性能差距，固定权重会随差距缩小甚至反转而阻碍进一步提升。

Method: GATS 面向长时域 agent 的稀疏 outcome reward 强化学习。训练时 student policy 在环境中进行 on-policy rollout，产生 token 序列；在 student 自身 rollout 上由 teacher 提供 token-level 分布指导，形成 on-policy distillation (OPD) 项，并与基于 outcome reward 的 RL 目标（GRPO 类）联合优化。关键模块是 gap-adaptive teacher scheduling：根据 teacher 与 student 的参考性能差距自适应设置 OPD 项权重。当 teacher 明显优于 student 时，OPD 权重较高，提供早期冷启动所需的密集 token 级信号；随着 student 接近 teacher 的参考性能，OPD 权重逐渐降低；一旦 student 达到该参考，教师指导被撤除，使学习主要由 outcome reward 驱动。由于教师指导主要需要早期阶段，该机制允许使用比 student 更小的任务训练教师。推理/部署时按文中设定不再需要教师指导。论文未披露具体网络结构、优化器超参或 serving 细节，但架构核心是 RL 目标与自适应蒸馏权重的组合。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均成功率", "value": "相比 reward-only GRPO 提升 4.37%-11.87%", "setting": "ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld 三个环境，三种 Qwen2.5 teacher-student 配置，匹配 student rollout budget"}, {"metric": "平均成功率", "value": "在所有三种配置中取得对比方法最高平均成功率", "setting": "ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld，Qwen2.5 teacher-student 配置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 观察并量化了 token-level on-policy distillation 收益与 teacher-student 性能差距之间的依赖关系。；提出 Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling (GATS)，在 student 的 RL 目标中加入随 teacher-student 性能差距自适应调整权重的 OPD 项。；随 student 接近教师参考性能逐步降低蒸馏权重，达到参考后撤除教师指导，从而支持使用比 student 更小的任务训练教师。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon agents typically relies on sparse outcome-based rewards. This leads to a severe cold-start problem, as early-stage policies often fail to solve sampled tasks, leaving little useful reward signal for learning. To mitigate this problem, we use on-policy distillation (OPD) to provide token-level guidance on the student's own rollouts. We find that the benefit of this guidance depends on the performance gap between the teacher and the student. When the teacher substantially outperforms the student, distillation helps guide the student through the early training stage where outcome rewards provide little learning signal. As the gap narrows and eventually reverses, however, continued distillation becomes less beneficial and may hinder further improvement. Motivated by this observation, we propose Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling (GATS), which augments the student's RL objective with an OPD term whose weight adapts to the teacher-student performance gap. Specifically, GATS gradually reduces teacher guidance as the student approaches the teacher's reference performance and withdraws it once that reference is reached. This enables GATS to leverage task-trained teachers smaller than the student, since teacher guidance is primarily needed during early training. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld with three Qwen2.5 teacher-student configurations, GATS achieves the highest average success rate among the compared methods in all three configurations, improving over reward-only GRPO by 4.37%-11.87% under matched student rollout budgets. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [56] [You Cannot Pick a Provider From the Price List: Market-Aware Routing for Open-Weight LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37902)
*Liang He,Jingbo Wen,Yixiong Chen,Yue Yang,Qizhen Lan,Kangning Cui,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出开放权重LLM推理市场中，选择模型后还需选择提供商，而提供商选择无法从价格表推断。通过多波次跨提供商测量，论文提出FACET在线路由器，对(提供商×任务)可行性进行认证并安全回退，实现匹配质量下的成本节省，并在线验证了流量转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由器仅基于静态模型成本选择模型，忽略了开放权重市场中同一模型不同提供商带来的质量、延迟、可用性和价格差异；且提供商可行性具有任务选择性，价格表无法反映这些差异。

Method: 论文首先进行跨提供商的在线测量，覆盖多个开放模型、竞争提供商、多种任务类型和三个测量波次，收集每个(提供商×模型×任务)端点的质量、延迟、可用性和价格数据。基于测量发现，问题被形式化为价格接受者市场感知路由：客户端在给定模型后，需要选择提供商，目标是在保证质量等价和端点健康的前提下最小化成本。简单测量映射策略直接利用测量地图，路由到满足质量等价且健康的最便宜提供商。由于地图会漂移，FACET作为在线路由器被引入：它为每个(提供商×任务)维护可行性分面认证，在未认证的臂被服务前，系统安全回退到锚点提供商。FACET在宽松部署假设下容忍不完美的任务分配和稀疏反馈。质量信号可能受评估器偏差影响，因此论文指出信任边界，并建议通过真实探针或审计来缓解。最终，在线运行证实认证机制能够将真实流量从高价锚点转移到显著更便宜的已认证端点。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "认证路由效果", "value": "可将真实流量从高价锚点转移至显著更便宜的已认证端点", "setting": "跨多个提供商的在线提供商运行"}]}

Conclusion: 形式化同模型提供商选择为价格接受者市场感知路由问题；提出简单测量映射策略：路由至质量等价且健康的提供商中价格最低者；提出FACET在线提供商路由器，对每个(提供商×任务)可行性分面进行认证，并在服务未认证臂前安全回退到锚点；揭示评估器偏差下的质量信号信任边界，并提出通过真实探针或审计缓解；通过多波次跨提供商在线测量验证价格、质量、延迟、可用性的不可推断性

Abstract: Existing LLM routers choose among models using static per-model costs. We show that open-weight inference markets introduce a second, largely ignored decision axis: after choosing a model, a client must still choose which provider serves it. Measuring live endpoints across [nummodels] open models, competing providers, multiple task types, and three measurement waves, we find that provider choice cannot be inferred from the price list. The same model can vary sharply in quality, latency, availability, and price across providers; higher-priced providers are consistently faster, but price does not reliably predict quality or availability; and provider feasibility is task-selective, with one deployment nearly normal on knowledge tasks but catastrophically degraded on multi-step reasoning. We formulate same-model provider selection as a price-taker market-aware routing problem. A simple measured-map policy routes to the cheapest provider that is both quality-equivalent and healthy, yielding matched-quality savings while avoiding degraded endpoints. Because the map drifts, we introduce FACET, an online provider router that certifies per-(provider x task) feasibility facets and fails safe to an anchor before serving uncertified arms. Across relaxed deployment assumptions, FACET tolerates imperfect task assignment and sparse feedback, while systematic evaluator bias exposes a quality-signal trust boundary that can be mitigated with ground-truth probes or audits. Live provider runs further confirm that certification can move real traffic from a premium anchor to a substantially cheaper certified endpoint. Our results suggest that market-aware LLM routing must measure not only which model to use, but also who serves it.

</details>


### [57] [Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37725)
*Rulin Shao,Shannon Zejiang Shen,Junjie Oscar Yin,Yuetai Li,Minheng Wang,Hamish Ivison,Radha Poovendran,Nathan Lambert,Teng Xiao,Mike Lewis,Wen-tau Yih,Luke Zettlemoyer,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLM将上下文视为模型可不受限更新的文件，使上下文管理由外部harness变为模型内在行为，并自然支持多智能体上下文共存。零样本CLM在BrowseComp-Plus、EdgeBench与24小时多仓库agent-swarm任务上以更少FLOPs取得更高准确率；可通过自然语言指令steering并支持online RL，Qwen3.5-9B在BrowseComp-Plus提升47.6%且FLOPs减少12%；Serving侧Suffix Cache Reuse相比标准SGLang降低35% server-side compute。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的上下文管理通常由外部harness控制，模型无法自主学习什么内容应保留在上下文中；需要一种让模型原生管理自身上下文的方法，并在多智能体场景下自然扩展，同时降低推理与serving成本。

Method: CLM将上下文视为一个文件，语言模型在生成过程中可以直接对该文件进行不受限更新，从而原生管理自身上下文，而不是依赖外部harness决定检索、保留或丢弃哪些内容。模型通过这种文件式上下文接口学习哪些信息对当前任务最重要并维护在上下文中。多智能体场景下，多个agent的上下文分别作为文件共存，使不同agent之间的上下文可以独立管理并协同扩展。构建CLM可采用零样本方式：利用现有模型直接以文件式上下文管理策略运行，无需额外训练即在BrowseComp-Plus、EdgeBench及多仓库agent-swarm任务上超过SOTA上下文管理策略。由于上下文管理从外部控制转为模型内在行为，CLM可同时支持in-context学习与参数化学习：一方面可通过自然语言指令进行steering，指令可由标准skill-optimization loop演化，从而提升held-out准确率；另一方面可用online reinforcement learning优化CLM在给定任务上的上下文管理策略。Serving侧针对CLM共同设计Suffix Cache Reuse，以利用上下文文件更新模式中的缓存复用机会，在匹配性能下降低server-side compute。整体流程为：模型接收任务输入，将其与自身上下文文件交互，决定何时读取、写入或修改上下文文件；在多智能体设置中各agent维护独立文件；训练阶段通过zero-shot prompt或online RL学习该策略；推理与服务阶段通过Suffix Cache Reuse降低重复计算。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "11.4% higher", "setting": "BrowseComp-Plus, zero-shot CLM vs SOTA context management strategies"}, {"metric": "FLOPs", "value": "21.5% fewer", "setting": "BrowseComp-Plus, zero-shot CLM vs SOTA context management strategies"}, {"metric": "score", "value": "5% higher", "setting": "12-hour EdgeBench, zero-shot CLM vs SOTA context management strategies"}, {"metric": "FLOPs", "value": "59% fewer", "setting": "12-hour EdgeBench, zero-shot CLM vs SOTA context management strategies"}, {"metric": "improvement", "value": "65% greater", "setting": "24-hour multi-repository agent-swarm task, same compute"}, {"metric": "held-out accuracy", "value": "up to 35.9 points improvement", "setting": "context-management task, natural-language instruction steering via skill-optimization loop, with reduced compute"}, {"metric": "performance", "value": "47.6% improvement", "setting": "Qwen3.5-9B on BrowseComp-Plus, online RL for CLMs, with 12% fewer FLOPs"}, {"metric": "server-side compute", "value": "35% reduction", "setting": "CLM serving with Suffix Cache Reuse vs standard SGLang at matched performance"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将上下文视为文件并允许模型进行不受限更新，使上下文管理成为模型内在行为而非外部harness控制；多智能体场景中多个agent的上下文作为文件共存，支持agent context的并行与共享管理；零样本构建CLM的方法，在多个任务上优于SOTA上下文管理策略；通过自然语言指令与标准skill-optimization loop对CLM进行steering，实现in-context与参数化学习上下文管理策略；提出针对CLM的online reinforcement learning方法；为CLM serving共同设计Suffix Cache Reuse，在匹配性能下降低server-side compute

Abstract: We introduce Context Language Models (CLMs), language models that natively manage their own context. We implement this by treating the context as a file and allowing the model to make unrestricted updates to this file. This allows the model to learn what is most important to maintain in context, and naturally extends to multi-agent systems where multiple agent contexts coexist as files. Building CLMs zero-shot with existing models outperforms SOTA context management strategies across a variety of tasks: 11.4% higher accuracy with 21.5% fewer FLOPs on BrowseComp-Plus, 5% higher scores with 59% fewer FLOPs on 12-hour EdgeBench, and 65% greater improvement with the same compute on a 24-hour multi-repository agent-swarm task. Moreover, by shifting context management from external harness control to intrinsic model behavior, CLMs naturally enable both in-context and parametric learning of context-management strategies. We show that CLMs can be steered with natural-language instructions evolved through a standard skill-optimization loop, improving held-out accuracy by up to 35.9 points on a context-management task while reducing compute. We also introduce an online reinforcement learning method for CLMs, improving Qwen3.5-9B performance on BrowseComp-Plus by 47.6% while using 12% fewer FLOPs. Finally, we co-design Suffix Cache Reuse for CLM serving, further reducing server-side compute by 35% relative to standard SGLang at matched performance.

</details>


### [58] [Cross-Entropy Guided Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37751)
*Yury Nahshan,Nati Daniel,Jacob Goldberger,Yoli Shavit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文为稀疏 MoE 大语言模型提出交叉熵引导的路由监督：用 token 级预测误差监督路由器亲和度，可预测专家误差或直接对齐路由器与模型目标，在 Granite 等两个 MoE 骨干、四个多选题基准上平均提升约 2.3 个百分点，ARC-Challenge 最高提升 2.94 个百分点，且不改变原生稀疏推理预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏 MoE 的路由器仅由负载均衡项和语言建模目标训练，其亲和度分数与 token 级预测误差缺乏直接对齐，导致专家选择与模型实际错误不匹配。

Method: 方法建立在标准稀疏 MoE 大语言模型之上：每个 token 的表示由路由器计算对各专家的亲和度分数，经 top-K 选择后仅激活少量专家，专家输出按亲和度加权聚合，再进入后续网络层。论文提出两种 token 误差监督形式。第一种为每个专家预测一个误差分数，将这些分数按亲和度加权聚合成 token 级误差估计，并与下一 token 交叉熵损失对齐（用于训练监督信号），同时各专家的误差分数在 top-K 选择前对亲和度进行衰减，使高误差专家在路由时被降权。第二种不引入额外预测头，直接将路由器亲和度与语言模型目标对齐。两种形式均采用 Itakura-Saito 散度或指数负对数似然作为亲和度与 token 误差之间的对齐损失。训练目标因此由语言建模损失、负载均衡正则项和新增的误差对齐项组成。推理时保持原生稀疏执行预算与聚合策略，不改变 top-K 选择后的计算图与聚合方式，因此不引入额外推理延迟或修改。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "多选题问答准确率", "value": "平均约 +2.3 个百分点", "setting": "Granite 稀疏 MoE 骨干，与参数量匹配的路由基线对比，四个多选题基准"}, {"metric": "ARC-Challenge 准确率", "value": "+2.94 个百分点", "setting": "更强监督设置下，Granite 骨干"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出按专家预测误差分数的 token 级监督：亲和度加权聚合的专家误差分数与下一 token 交叉熵损失对齐，同时各专家误差分数在 top-K 选择前对亲和度进行衰减；提出直接将路由器亲和度与模型目标对齐的第二种形式，无需额外预测头或推理时修改；使用 Itakura-Saito 散度或指数负对数似然来对齐亲和度与 token 误差；两种机制均保持原生稀疏执行预算与聚合策略，不增加推理开销

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) large language models scale model capacity by routing each token to a small subset of experts. Their routers are regularized with load balancing terms and learn affinity scores through the language-model objective. However, these objectives do not provide direct alignment between routing affinities and token-level error. We introduce token-error supervision for sparse routing in two forms. The first form predicts an error score per expert. The affinity-weighted aggregate of these scores is aligned to the next-token cross-entropy loss, while the individual scores attenuate affinity before top-$K$ selection. The second directly aligns the router's affinities to the model's objective without requiring an additional head or inference-time modification. Both formulations use the Itakura--Saito divergence or an exponential negative log-likelihood for aligning affinities and token errors. Across two sparse MoE backbones and four multiple-choice question-answering benchmarks, we evaluate both supervision mechanisms. On Granite, our method improves accuracy by approximately 2.3 percentage points on average over a parameter-matched routing baseline. With stronger supervision, the gain on ARC-Challenge reaches 2.94 points. Both mechanisms preserve the native sparse execution budget and aggregation policy. Our code is available in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [59] [ContextRender: From Execution Dependencies to Agent Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37743)
*Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka L. Dantanarayana,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ContextRender 用持久化执行依赖图管理 LLM agent 上下文，通过 Tool-Flow Analysis 追踪历史工具结果的后续复用（observed reuse），并与新近度、语义相关性联合在 6K 固定预算内渲染上下文。在 AppWorld 与 8-objective QA 上，任务表现接近或优于传全量历史，同时平均推理成本降低 10.2%-32.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 LLM agent 的工具调用结果会持续累积，后续步骤可能需要；传全量历史即使能放入上下文窗口也很昂贵，而裁剪历史又有遗漏关键信息的风险。现有上下文管理方法忽略了早期工具结果在后续执行中被如何复用，从而把需要的信息排除在上下文之外。

Method: ContextRender 以持久化的执行依赖图作为上下文组织的核心数据结构：agent 执行过程中产生的工具调用及其结果被记录为图中的节点/依赖关系，而非扁平的线性历史。在此图之上运行 Tool-Flow Analysis，分析后续操作如何复用早期工具结果，从而给出 observed reuse 信号。渲染阶段由 renderer 将该复用信号与 recency（时间新近度）和 semantic relevance（语义相关性）联合用于打分与选择，在固定的历史预算（论文中为 6K）内决定送入模型上下文的结果子集；未被选中的结果不会被丢弃，而是保留在依赖图中以供后续渲染轮次再次使用。训练目标/优化方面未披露（该方法为推理期的上下文构建方法，非模型训练）。推理/服务流程：每一步 agent 调用前，先根据当前任务状态与依赖图重新渲染上下文，只注入预算内的高价值历史工具结果，从而在保持信息可用性的同时压低每次调用的上下文长度与推理成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务表现（AppWorld 与 8-objective QA，三种执行模型，6K 历史预算）", "value": "优于所评估的上下文管理基线，接近或高于传全量历史", "setting": "6K history budget，显著低于模型最大上下文窗口"}, {"metric": "平均推理成本相对 Full history 的下降幅度", "value": "10.2%-32.2%", "setting": "6K history budget 下，相对传全量历史"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用持久化的执行依赖图（execution dependency graph）组织与渲染 agent 上下文；Tool-Flow Analysis：追踪后续操作对早期工具结果的复用关系，产生 observed reuse 信号；渲染器将 observed reuse 与 recency、semantic relevance 结合，在固定历史预算内选择结果，并把被省略结果保留以备后用；消融显示 observed reuse 能提升任务表现与被后续复用结果的留存率

Abstract: LLM agents performing long-horizon tasks accumulate tool results that later steps may need.
