Passing the full history to every invocation is costly even when it fits within the context window, while reducing it risks omitting needed information. Existing context management methods can overlook how earlier tool results are used in subsequent execution, leaving needed information out of context. We introduce ContextRender, which manages context through a persistent graph of execution dependencies. We develop Tool-Flow Analysis to track how later operations reuse information from earlier tool results, providing a signal called observed reuse. A renderer combines this signal with recency and semantic relevance to select results within a fixed history budget, retaining omitted results for later use. Across AppWorld and 8-objective QA with three execution models, ContextRender outperforms the evaluated context management baselines using a 6K history budget, well below the models' maximum context windows. Within this budget, it achieves task performance close to or above that of passing the full history while reducing mean inference cost by 10.2%-32.2% relative to Full history. Ablations show that observed reuse improves task performance and retention of results reused later.

</details>


### [60] [How Can Recommendation Feedback Evolve Agent Memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37544)
*Shanwen Mao,Mingming Li,Hao Zhang,Zhiheng Li,Yige Wang,Penghua Yu,Junxiong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TIDE 提出以延迟推荐反馈驱动智能体外部记忆进化的框架，将记忆视为容量受限经验种群，通过时序/语义信用分配与责任信用完成归因，并以强化、交叉、变异、淘汰更新记忆；提出 MEG 度量。在电商会员营销内容生成智能体上，离线时序回放 MEG 提升 7.75 个百分点，线上 A/B 测试显著提升 UCTR 与激活率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统反馈（曝光、点击、转化、负反馈）具有延迟性、噪声性，且受受众构成、投放位置与推荐策略混淆，同时一次结果可能由多个记忆共同作用，导致难以将推荐结果可靠地转化为记忆适应度；现有方法依赖即时反馈或语义检索，无法可靠完成该归因。

Method: TIDE 将智能体记忆建模为一个容量受限的经验种群。输入为内容生成智能体在推荐系统中获得的延迟结果信号（曝光、点击、转化、负反馈）以及生成时被引用的记忆轨迹（trajectory）。流程上分为三步：第一步，时序与语义信用分配模块依据结果发生的时间远近与语义相关性，估计每条记忆在特定上下文中的适应度（contextual fitness）；第二步，责任信用模块按照生成过程中实际被引用的记忆，将结果信号按责任比例分配给相应记忆，以缓解一次结果由多个记忆共同作用造成的归因混淆；第三步，用上述分配到的信号作为进化算子（reinforce 强化、crossover 交叉、mutate 变异、evict 淘汰）的驱动力，在容量约束下更新记忆种群。评估上提出 MEG，用严格未来任务上相对无记忆基线的效用提升衡量进化后记忆的收益。推理/服务时，内容生成智能体检索演化后的记忆作为外部条件完成内容生成，其产生的真实推荐反馈再回流至上述归因与进化流程，形成闭环。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MEG", "value": "+7.75 percentage points", "setting": "电商会员营销内容生成智能体，离线时序回放（offline temporal replay）"}, {"metric": "MAE", "value": "最低（具体数值未披露）", "setting": "延迟标签基准（delayed-label benchmark）对比方法中"}, {"metric": "RMSE", "value": "最低（具体数值未披露）", "setting": "延迟标签基准（delayed-label benchmark）对比方法中"}, {"metric": "MEG", "value": "最高（具体数值未披露）", "setting": "延迟标签基准（delayed-label benchmark）对比方法中"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "UCTR（独特点击率）", "value": "显著提升（具体数值未披露）", "setting": "电商会员营销内容生成智能体线上A/B测试"}, {"metric": "activation rate（激活率）", "value": "显著提升（具体数值未披露）", "setting": "电商会员营销内容生成智能体线上A/B测试"}]}

Conclusion: TIDE（Trajectory-Informed Directed Memory Evolution）：由延迟推荐反馈驱动、将记忆视为容量受限经验种群的外部记忆进化框架；时序与语义信用分配估计上下文适应度，责任信用（responsibility credit）按生成时被引用的记忆分配结果信号；基于信号对记忆执行强化、交叉、变异或淘汰的进化操作；Memory Evolution Gain (MEG)：以严格未来任务上相对无记忆基线的效用提升度量记忆进化效果

Abstract: Content-generation agents continuously receive impressions, clicks, conversions, and negative feedback from recommendation systems, providing real-world outcome signals for memory evolution. However, these signals are delayed and noisy, confounded by audience composition, placement, and recommendation policies, and may result from the combined influence of multiple memories, making accurate attribution difficult. Existing methods rely primarily on immediate feedback or semantic retrieval and therefore struggle to reliably translate recommendation outcomes into memory fitness. To address this challenge, we propose TIDE (Trajectory-Informed Directed Memory Evolution), an external memory evolution framework driven by delayed recommendation feedback. We further introduce Memory Evolution Gain (MEG), which measures the utility improvement of evolved memory over a no memory baseline on strictly future tasks. TIDE treats memory as a capacity-constrained population of experiences: temporal and semantic credit assignment estimates contextual fitness, while responsibility credit distributes outcome signals according to the memories referenced during generation. These signals are then used to reinforce, crossover, mutate, or evict memories. On an e-commerce membership marketing content-generation agent, TIDE achieves a +7.75-percentage-point MEG in offline temporal replay and significantly improves both unique click-through rate (UCTR) and activation rate in an online A/B test. On a delayed-label benchmark, TIDE achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) and root mean squared error (RMSE) and the highest MEG among the compared methods, demonstrating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [61] [Boundary-State Control for Tool-Using Language-Model Agents: Commit-Time Consistency under State Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37475)
*Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BSC-R，一种确定性效果边界机制，用于解决工具调用型LLM智能体在提议到提交之间安全状态漂移的问题。实验显示其在AgentDojo和CONTINUITY等数据集上能有效保持效用并阻止多种攻击，但对未表示的无效效果提交率较高，并非通用安全方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用型LLM智能体在决定执行某个操作后到实际提交前，安全相关状态可能已发生变化，导致原本许可的操作在提交时不安全（提议-提交鸿沟）。

Method: BSC-R是一种确定性效果边界机制，设计用于工具调用型语言模型智能体的提交时一致性控制。其核心思想是在智能体提议动作时，不仅绑定动作本身，还绑定授权该动作的授权状态的语义投影，形成单次使用的提交授权。当智能体试图提交时，系统重新计算当前状态的语义投影并与绑定的投影比较，若状态发生改变或提交被重放，则拒绝提交。该机制在动作-状态边界处作为内核运行，不改变智能体本身的决策策略，仅对提交阶段进行拦截。实验在多个数据集上评估：在AgentDojo攻击情节上，内核保持未保护智能体的效用和攻击成功率不变；在冻结提案集上，它能接受所有未变更提交并拒绝预定义的变更/重放类；在独立边界漂移实验中，完全联合绑定拒绝所有无效上下文同时保留所有有效上下文；在CONTINUITY套件上，它保留良性案例，阻止表示的非重放无效效果，处理重放生命周期，并拒绝模糊无释放案例。然而，在更广泛套件上，对未表示的无效效果提交率高达25%，表明该方法具有范围限制。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "效用（utility）", "value": "80.576%", "setting": "未保护的智能体行为保持率，在2,847个受攻击的AgentDojo情节上"}, {"metric": "攻击成功率（attack success）", "value": "1.616%", "setting": "在2,847个受攻击的AgentDojo情节上"}, {"metric": "无效上下文提交数", "value": "0/4,403", "setting": "完全联合绑定下，独立生成的边界漂移实验，同时保留5,899/5,899有效上下文"}, {"metric": "良性案例保留数", "value": "700/700", "setting": "在3,460个场景的前瞻性外部评估CONTINUITY套件上"}, {"metric": "表示的非重放无效效果阻止数", "value": "1,200/1,200", "setting": "CONTINUITY套件"}, {"metric": "重放生命周期正确处理数", "value": "160/160", "setting": "CONTINUITY套件"}, {"metric": "模糊无释放案例拒绝数", "value": "200/200", "setting": "CONTINUITY套件"}, {"metric": "无效效果提交率", "value": "25%", "setting": "在2,560次攻击的更广泛外部套件上，对比CONTINUITY的0%"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出BSC-R确定性效果边界机制，将单次使用的提交授权绑定到精确动作与授权状态的语义投影；在多个数据集上验证边界内核能在不改变原有行为的前提下接受所有未变更提交，拒绝变更或重放类；揭示该方法在更广泛攻击集上的局限性：对未在边界漂移中表示的无效效果提交率为25%

Abstract: Tool-using language-model agents can decide that an action is permissible and execute it only after security-relevant state has changed. We study this proposal-to-commit gap and introduce BSC-R, a deterministic effect-boundary mechanism that binds a single-use commit authorization to the exact action and to a semantic projection of the authorization state that justified it. On 2,847 attacked AgentDojo episodes, the boundary kernel preserves the unprotected agent's behavior exactly (80.576% utility; 1.616% attack success). On 10,302 frozen proposals, it accepts every unchanged commit and rejects every instance of ten prospectively specified changed or replayed classes. In an independently generated boundary-drift experiment, full joint binding commits 0/4,403 invalid contexts while retaining 5,899/5,899 valid contexts. A prospective external evaluation on the 3,460-scenario CONTINUITY suite retains all 700 benign cases, prevents 1,200/1,200 represented non-replay invalid effects, handles 160/160 replay lifecycles correctly, and withholds 200/200 ambiguous no-release cases. The broader external suite also exposes the method's limit: across all 2,560 attacks, BSC-R has a 25% invalid-effect commit rate versus 0% for CONTINUITY. The result is therefore a scoped commit-time consistency mechanism, not a universal agent-safety claim.

</details>


### [62] [Pretrain Once, Route Anywhere: Towards a Foundation Model for LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37362)
*Guannan Lai,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出RouteFM，将LLM路由从局部拟合转向“预训练一次、随处路由”的基础模型范式。通过跨异构路由环境的情景式预训练，学习从行为上下文表征匿名候选模型并推断目标任务能力，使冻结路由器仅靠上下文即可适配新领域、模态、候选池和上下文预算。在未参与预训练的MMR-Bench上，每候选模型仅8次观测即以2.23质量分优势超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由器通常针对特定查询负载和候选模型池进行局部拟合，当路由环境变化时需要额外监督或重新训练。论文试图回答：能否从基础模型视角学习一种可复用、可泛化到不同任务、候选模型和部署条件的路由能力。

Method: RouteFM采用基础模型式路由架构。输入为查询及其对匿名候选模型的行为上下文（如历史交互/观测结果），不依赖固定模型标识。主要模块包括：候选模型行为表征模块，从行为上下文中提取匿名候选模型的特征；目标能力推断模块，基于行为证据推断候选模型面向当前目标任务的能力。训练采用跨异构路由环境的情景式预训练（episodic pretraining），使路由能力可在多个环境间复用。推理时使用冻结的路由器，仅通过上下文适配新环境，无需额外监督或重新训练。论文未披露更细的模块连接、损失函数、优化细节及服务路径。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "quality points", "value": "比最强基线高2.23", "setting": "MMR-Bench（未参与预训练），每个候选模型仅8次观测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出RouteFM，从行为上下文表征匿名候选模型并推断其面向目标任务的能力，而非将路由决策绑定到固定模型标识或单一环境。；通过跨异构路由环境的情景式预训练（episodic pretraining）学习可复用的路由能力，使冻结的路由器能通过上下文适配新环境。；实现跨领域、跨模态、跨候选池和跨上下文预算的迁移，并在行为证据有限时获得最大增益。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing aims to assign each query to the most suitable model from a heterogeneous candidate pool, improving the quality--efficiency trade-off of LLM inference. Existing routers are typically learned through local fitting: a router is optimized for a particular query workload and candidate pool, and often requires additional supervision or retraining as the routing environment changes. We ask whether LLM routing can instead be approached from a foundation-model perspective, learning a reusable routing capability that generalizes across tasks, candidate models, and deployment conditions. To this end, we introduce RouteFM, which learns to characterize anonymous candidate models from behavioral context and infer their target-specific capabilities, rather than binding routing decisions to fixed model identities or a single environment. Through episodic pretraining across heterogeneous routing environments, this capability can be reused by a frozen router and adapted to new environments through context alone. Experiments demonstrate transfer across changes in domains, modalities, candidate pools, and context budgets, with the largest gains when behavioral evidence is limited. On MMR-Bench, which is excluded from pretraining, RouteFM outperforms the strongest baseline by 2.23 quality points with only eight observations per candidate. These results support moving LLM routing from repeated local fitting toward a pretrain once, route anywhere paradigm. Our code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [63] [Solving Without Stopping: On-Policy Distillation at Small Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37326)
*Hongyang Li,Yiming Zhu,Xiao Li,Caesar Wu,Said Mammar,Pascal Bouvry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文在 Qwen3 系列 4B/1.7B/0.6B 学生上系统分析 on-policy distillation 从 8B 教师迁移了哪些推理能力。发现蒸馏能迁移解题能力（但受单次尝试上限约束），却在 thinking 模式下无法迁移“适时停止推理”的能力：教师停止信号只出现在学生现有停止处，导致小模型推理结束后不停止或写过头。作者提出区分答案标记、正确性与停止行为的诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 on-policy distillation 下，小模型（学生）能从强教师迁移哪些推理能力，尤其是长链推理中的“解题”与“知道何时停止推理”两种能力是否都能被迁移。

Method: 论文采用 on-policy distillation 范式：教师为 Qwen3-8B，学生在自身生成输出上接收教师反馈进行学习，学生规模分别为 Qwen3 4B、1.7B、0.6B。训练在两种模式下进行：thinking 模式要求学生先生成长推理过程再结束推理并给出答案；非 thinking 模式无独立推理阶段，用于对照。评估将学生行为分解为三个可分离维度：答案标记（是否标记出答案）、答案正确性、停止（是否结束推理）。论文通过在训练前后测量学生单次尝试与多次尝试的表现，量化“解题”与“停止”两类能力的迁移情况，并分析教师停止信号的位置分布如何影响学生在 thinking 模式下的停止行为。具体网络结构、损失函数、优化器、数据规模与训练硬件未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "解题能力（单次尝试）", "value": "在所有学生规模上均提升，但受两个上限约束：不超过训练前多次尝试可达的最好结果，且学生越小距教师越远", "setting": "on-policy distillation，Qwen3-8B 教师蒸馏至 Qwen3 4B/1.7B/0.6B，thinking 与非 thinking 模式"}, {"metric": "停止能力（thinking 模式）", "value": "训练中学生会减少结束推理，学生越小该能力保留越少；教师几乎只对学生已停止的位置发出停止信号，因此未教出新的停止", "setting": "thinking 模式 on-policy distillation"}, {"metric": "停止能力（非 thinking 模式）", "value": "每个学生都保持停止行为", "setting": "非 thinking 模式 on-policy distillation"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 以 Qwen3-8B 为教师，系统抽取 Qwen3 4B/1.7B/0.6B 学生，在 thinking 与非 thinking 两种模式下对比 on-policy distillation 的迁移效果。；提出诊断框架，将“答案标记（marking）”“正确性（correctness）”“停止（stopping）”三种行为分离评估。；发现蒸馏迁移“解题能力”但 thinking 模式下不迁移“停止能力”：教师几乎只在其自身推理结束的位置发出停止信号，因此蒸馏只保留学生原有且落在正确答案上的停止。；测量并报告学生单次尝试的上限：训练后单次尝试不超过训练前多次尝试所达到的最好结果（两个天花板），且学生越小距教师越远。

Abstract: On-policy distillation, where a student learns from a stronger teacher's feedback on its own outputs, is a common way to pass reasoning to smaller models. We analyze what it transfers at small scale, distilling Qwen3-8B into Qwen3 4B, 1.7B and 0.6B students, in thinking mode (reason at length, then end the reasoning and answer) and, for comparison, in non-thinking mode (no separate reasoning phase). Long reasoning needs two abilities, solving a problem and knowing when it is solved, and we find that distillation transfers the first, but in thinking mode not the second. Solving improves at every size, up to two ceilings, which we measure comprehensively across both modes and all student sizes: a student's single attempt never exceeds what it could already reach in many attempts before training, and the smaller the student, the further it stays below the teacher. Stopping is where the modes part. In non-thinking mode every student keeps stopping; in thinking mode students stop ending their reasoning early in training, and the smaller the student, the less of this ability survives: the teacher signals a stop almost only where a student already ends its reasoning, so distillation teaches no new stops; it only keeps the student's existing stops that land on a right answer, and a weak student has few such stops. The smallest students often reach the right value but do not commit to it: they either rarely mark it or mark it and write past it. Together, these results describe how small students behave under on-policy distillation, and a diagnostic that separates answer marking, correctness and stopping.

</details>


### [64] [Mubric: Mutation Testing-Guided Rubric Generation for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37322)
*Jiayuxuan Yang,Jie M. Zhang,Yiling Lou,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mubric提出一种变异测试引导的评分标准生成方法，通过从真实回答对中挖掘缺陷并抽象为变异算子，再将其注入参考回答以检验评分标准能否惩罚缺陷，从而迭代精炼评分标准。在703个任务、四个领域上对比六种方法，整体评估准确率超过最强基线7.48个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动生成能够可靠捕捉任务特定质量要求的评分标准（rubric）仍然困难。

Method: Mubric采用变异测试引导的评分标准生成流程。首先，从真实偏好与非偏好回答对中挖掘常见缺陷，并将这些缺陷抽象为可复用的变异算子，每个算子具体说明如何向回答中注入某一类缺陷。对于新任务，系统选择相关变异算子并将其应用于一个参考回答，从而生成带有已知缺陷的回答变体。然后，利用当前评分标准对这些变体进行评分，检查注入缺陷是否导致得分下降。如果某个缺陷没有被充分惩罚（即得分未显著降低），则表明当前评分标准未能捕捉该质量要求，系统据此精炼评分标准。该迭代过程持续进行，直到评分标准能够可靠区分高质量回答与各类缺陷回答。最终，生成的评分标准用于评估LLM-based系统的回答质量。训练目标与优化过程未在摘要中详细披露；推理或服务流程未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "整体评估准确率", "value": "比最强基线高7.48个百分点", "setting": "703个任务，四个代表性领域，与六种先进评分标准生成方法对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将变异测试思想类比到评分标准评估：如果评分标准捕捉了重要质量要求，则向高质量回答注入对应缺陷应降低其得分。；从真实偏好/非偏好回答对中挖掘常见缺陷，并抽象为可复用的变异算子，每个算子指定如何注入特定类型的回答缺陷。；对新任务，将相关变异算子应用于参考回答，检查注入的缺陷是否降低回答质量，并利用惩罚不足的缺陷来精炼评分标准。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is widely used to assess LLM-based systems by decomposing response quality into task-specific scoring criteria. However, automatically generating rubrics that reliably capture task-specific quality requirements remains challenging. We introduce Mubric, a mutation testing-guided approach to rubric generation. Mutation testing, a classic software testing methodology, evaluates a test suite by injecting faults into programs and checking whether the tests detect them. We draw an analogy between test suites and rubrics: if a rubric captures an important quality requirement, introducing a corresponding defect into an otherwise high-quality response should reduce its score. Mubric first mines common defects from real pairs of preferred and dispreferred responses and abstracts these defects into reusable mutation operators, each specifying how to introduce a particular type of response defect. For a new task, it applies relevant operators to a reference response, checks whether the injected defects reduce response quality, and uses insufficiently penalized defects to refine the rubric. We evaluate Mubric on 703 tasks across four representative domains against six advanced rubric generation methods. Mubric achieves the highest overall evaluation accuracy, outperforming the strongest baseline by 7.48 percentage points.

</details>


### [65] [SkillCome: Group Contrast Skill Optimization with Dual Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37128)
*Haolin Li,Feng Hong,Ang Li,Chilin Fu,Weichang Wu,Ya Zhang,Yanfeng Wang,Xiaolu Zhang,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillCome提出基于组对比优化与双记忆的技能演化框架，通过多轨迹组对比识别关键行为分歧并借助双记忆积累跨组证据，在六个基准、五种模型上实现最高+5.69分提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能演化方法对每个问题仅生成单条轨迹，优化信号不足，难以定位失败原因或识别成功轨迹中应纳入技能的关键动作；同时依赖局部批次轨迹分析，优化方向易受噪声证据影响。

Method: SkillCome是一个基于组对比优化与双记忆的技能演化方法。输入为问题及当前技能；对每个问题生成多条轨迹；组对比分析模块通过比较成功与失败轨迹识别关键行为分歧，产出可靠优化信号；双记忆系统分别累积历史步骤证据并追踪跨组共享模式，形成更泛化的优化方向。整体流程将成功轨迹中的经验转化为可复用技能，迭代优化技能。推理时，模型使用演化后的技能执行任务。训练目标或优化细节未在摘要中具体说明。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "总体得分提升", "value": "+5.69 points", "setting": "六个基准（问答、推理、智能体任务）上跨五种不同家族和规模模型，与基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 组对比优化：对每个问题生成多条轨迹，通过成功与失败轨迹的组对比分析精确识别关键行为分歧，提供可靠优化信号；双记忆系统：积累历史步骤中的证据，追踪跨组共享模式，得到更泛化的优化方向；将观测到的成功轨迹经验系统性转化为可复用技能，形成端到端优化流程

Abstract: Skill evolution improves the capabilities of large language models by analyzing trajectories generated under a given skill and modifying the skill accordingly. Existing approaches typically generate a single trajectory per question. However, this provides insufficient optimization signals since it requires inferring effective skill edits from a solitary path. It is difficult to pinpoint which actions caused the failure in a failed trajectory, or to determine which actions in a successful one should be incorporated into the skill. Furthermore, they rely on a local batch of trajectories for analysis, making the optimization direction susceptible to noisy evidence. To address these, we propose SkillCome, a Skill-evolution method based on group Contrast optimization with dual memory. For each question, SkillCome generates trajectories and performs group contrast analysis to precisely identify key behavioral divergences between successful and failed trajectories, offering reliable optimization signals. The dual memory system further accumulates evidence from historical steps to track patterns shared across different groups, leading to more generalized optimization directions. Together, SkillCome builds a systematic optimization process that transforms experience from observed successful trajectories into reusable skills. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks spanning question answering, reasoning, and agentic tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. SkillCome consistently outperforms baselines across five models of varying families and scales, with gains up to +5.69 points.

</details>


### [66] [From Judgment Quality to Downstream Utility: Rethinking LLM-as-a-Judge for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37145)
*Zheng Zhang,Lufei Li,Xinyue Tan,Yuanhao Zeng,Ziwei Shan,Yexin Li,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统研究 LLM-as-a-Judge 的判定协议与使用方式，发现判定质量与下游效用不一定一致，并将 Judge 扩展到测试时推理策略，为开放式任务的评估与训练提供多面视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏标准答案的开放式任务中，如何通过设计 LLM-as-a-Judge 的判定方式与使用方式，同时提升判定质量与下游策略效用。

Method: 论文构建了一个围绕 LLM-as-a-Judge 的实验框架。输入为开放式任务下需要评估的策略响应，以及可选的参考答案或评价标准。判定阶段（judgment elicitation）沿三个维度配置 Judge：verdict granularity 控制判定输出的粒度；critique usage 控制是否要求 Judge 生成批评文本；evaluation batching 控制是逐条评估还是批量评估。判定结果随后可被用于两个方向：一是作为奖励信号用于策略训练（policy training），二是用于测试时推理，包括 Best-of-N selection、Judge-guided revision 与 beam search。系统通过改变 Judge 协议和下游使用方式，测量内在判定质量与下游任务效用，以分析两者之间的关系。论文未披露具体模型规模、训练资源或推理资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 沿 verdict granularity、critique usage、evaluation batching 三个维度系统变化 Judge 协议；将 Judge 从训练时监督扩展到测试时推理，包括 Best-of-N selection、Judge-guided revision 和 beam search；发现判定质量与下游效用并不总是一致，主张对 LLM Judge 进行包含内在判定质量与下游效用的多面评估

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge is increasingly used to evaluate policy responses on open-ended tasks that lack ground-truth answers. Existing work often directly converts the resulting judgments into reward signals for policy training, paying limited attention to intrinsic judgment quality and largely restricting the use of Judges to training-time supervision. We systematically investigate judgment quality and downstream utility by examining both how judgments are elicited and how they are used. For judgment elicitation, we vary the Judge protocol along three dimensions: verdict granularity, critique usage, and evaluation batching. For judgment usage, beyond policy training, we extend Judge to test-time inference through Best-of-N selection, Judge-guided revision, and beam search. We find that, (i) Surprisingly, judgment quality and downstream utility do not always align. (ii) Judge protocol design substantially affects both intrinsic judgment quality and downstream utility. (iii) Judge guidance effectively converts test-time compute into performance gains, with benefits varying across inference strategies. Our results call for a multifaceted evaluation of LLM Judges on open-ended tasks, encompassing intrinsic judgment quality, and downstream utility.

</details>


### [67] [When Should Agents Check External State? Budgeting Observations for Stored Intentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37125)
*Zhengkun Di,Bin Shi,Kai Sun,Yiming Xu,Bo Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BudgetPM首次把存储意图所需的外部状态检查建模为共享episode预算下的资源分配问题，用Logistic门控与hindsight蒸馏策略在硬预算内决定何时检查，在PM-Bench上以更少观察维持质量并优于Mem0等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存储的意图无法揭示其未来条件当前是否成立，而检查条件需要web访问、多步工具和付费调用；现有系统只决定何时关注意图，却没有在共享预算下分配由此产生的观察开销。

Method: BudgetPM围绕一个共享硬预算执行器组织。部署时，存储意图由前置查询信息表示；Static变体使用轻量Logistic评分器判断一次外部检查是否可能改善当前决策，从而决定是否消耗预算调用web或多步工具。Sequential变体则利用完整episode的事后hindsight调度进行训练，将其蒸馏为只依赖预查询信息的轻量策略，在部署时决定花费或保留容量，以应对当前与未来观察机会之间的竞争。两个变体共享同一硬预算执行器，保证观察总数不超过episode预算。评价覆盖PM-Bench等两个基准与三种backbone，对照两个公开memory-agent系统（Mem0、PMA）、五个匹配控制与四个手工设计的监控或预算自适应规则，并通过匹配归因、精确成本分析和固定预算负载干预验证收益来自现在与未来机会的竞争。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "观察次数减少", "value": "42–54%", "setting": "PM-Bench上BudgetPM-Static相比无约束质量，保留99.9–100%质量"}, {"metric": "Set F1", "value": "1.92–2.58", "setting": "严重资源稀缺且相同硬上限下BudgetPM-Sequential相对最强自然监控调度"}, {"metric": "观察次数减少", "value": "16–33%", "setting": "达到相同Set F1与on-time recall"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次将存储意图所需的外部观察建模为共享episode预算下的资源分配问题；BudgetPM-Static：用轻量Logistic评分器学习一次检查是否改善当前决策；BudgetPM-Sequential：将完整episode事后调度蒸馏为仅依赖预查询信息的轻量策略，决定何时消费或保留预算；统一的硬预算执行器；提出需求-容量设计规则：容量覆盖需求时局部门控有效，观察跨时间竞争时未来感知监督才带来价值

Abstract: Prospective memory allows an agent to retain an intention tied to a future condition, but the stored intention does not reveal whether that condition currently holds. Checking it may require web access, multi-step tool use, and paid calls. Existing systems decide when intentions require attention, but do not allocate the resulting observations under a shared budget. We introduce the first resource-allocation formulation for the external observations required by stored intentions under a shared episode budget. BudgetPM offers two policy variants that share a hard-budget executor. BudgetPM-Static uses a lightweight Logistic scorer to learn whether a check improves the current decision. BudgetPM-Sequential distills full-episode hindsight schedules into a lightweight policy that decides when to spend or reserve capacity using only pre-query information at deployment. We evaluate BudgetPM against two public memory-agent systems, five matched controls, and four hand-designed monitoring or budget-adaptation rules. Across two benchmarks and three backbones, BudgetPM-Static outperforms adapted Mem0 and PMA workflows. On PM-Bench, its Logistic scorer reaches competitive quality--cost operating points alongside higher-capacity scorers and retains 99.9--100\% of unconstrained quality with 42--54\% fewer observations. Under severe scarcity and the same hard caps, BudgetPM-Sequential exceeds the strongest tested natural monitoring schedule by 1.92--2.58 Set F1 points. It reaches the same Set F1 and on-time recall with 16--33\% fewer observations. Matched attribution, exact-cost analysis, and a fixed-budget load intervention link this gain to competition between present and future opportunities. These results yield a demand--capacity design rule: local gating works when capacity covers demand, while future-aware supervision adds value when observations compete across time.

</details>


### [68] [Learning from Viable Failure Prefixes: Milestone Viability Potential Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37111)
*Qi Zhou,Yuanfan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MVPO 针对长时程 LLM 智能体稀疏奖励下的零信用失败问题，利用 Union-Find 可行性区域估计前缀势能，修复零信用组的优势并动态衰减势能分支，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上以极低开销（0.16%-0.20%）超越 GRPO/GiGPO 基线，成功率分别提升 4.4 和 5.3 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 LLM 智能体在稀疏且延迟奖励下，组内优势估计方法（如 GRPO、GiGPO）在早期训练中大量失败轨迹包含有效前缀，但因组内回报无变化而无法提供任务判别性优势，即零信用失败（zero-credit failure）。

Method: MVPO 是一种势能路由策略优化算法，用于长时程 LLM 智能体的强化学习后训练。输入为 LLM 智能体与环境的交互轨迹（rollout），每条轨迹由状态、动作、奖励组成，最终奖励稀疏且延迟。算法首先将轨迹中的前缀状态映射到 Union-Find 维护的可行性区域（viability regions），区域表示可共享成功潜力的状态集合。然后估计每个前缀的势能（prefix potential），即从该前缀出发到达成功的预估可能性。在优势计算阶段，对传统组内优势估计（如 GRPO/GiGPO）中奖励零方差的组（零信用组），用势能差（potential-difference）构造替代优势，使包含有用失败前缀的轨迹获得任务判别性梯度信号。同时，根据组内相对性能进展动态衰减势能分支的权重：当组内奖励已产生足够方差时，降低对势能信号的依赖，避免势能估计误差干扰学习。训练目标为策略梯度/PPO 类目标，使用修复后的优势更新策略。推理时仅使用训练好的策略模型，无额外开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "success rate", "value": "+4.4 percentage points vs GiGPO", "setting": "ALFWorld, 相同训练长度, Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct"}, {"metric": "success rate", "value": "+5.3 percentage points vs GiGPO", "setting": "WebShop, 相同训练长度, Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct"}, {"metric": "advantage-construction overhead", "value": "+0.16%-0.20%", "setting": "训练时额外开销"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别并定义零信用失败模式：组内奖励零方差导致无法计算有效优势。；提出基于 Union-Find 的可行性区域（viability regions）估计前缀势能。；用势能差优势修复零信用组（repairs zero-credit groups）。；按相对性能进展衰减势能分支（attenuates potential branch according to relative performance progress）。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require reinforcement learning methods that can assign credit to intermediate decisions under sparse and delayed rewards. Existing group-based methods such as GRPO and GiGPO alleviate this issue by comparing rollout returns or repeated anchor states, but they still fail when the compared returns have no variation. We identify this failure mode as zero-credit failure: during early training, many failed rollouts contain useful prefixes, yet existing methods assign them no task-discriminative advantage. To address this issue, we propose Milestone Viability Potential Policy Optimization (MVPO), a potential-routed policy optimization algorithm that learns from viable failure prefixes. MVPO estimates prefix potential over Union-Find viability regions, repairs zero-credit groups with potential-difference advantages, and attenuates the potential branch according to relative performance progress. Experiments with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct show that MVPO outperforms eight strong baselines, including GRPO and GiGPO. Under the same training length, MVPO improves over the GiGPO baseline by +4.4 success points on ALFWorld and +5.3 on WebShop, while adding only 0.16%-0.20% advantage-construction overhead.

</details>


### [69] [Beyond Low-Rank Parameterization: Narrowing the Gap Between LoRA and Full Fine-Tuning via Gradient Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37027)
*Yihao Ouyang,Shiwei Li,Haozhao Wang,Xiandi Luo,Zhuoqi Hu,Jinglun Yu,Yichen Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GDLoRA 通过正交分解全权重梯度并利用法向分量直接更新基座权重，在不增加标准 LoRA 优化器状态内存的前提下缩小与全量微调的差距，在自然语言理解、数学推理、常识推理和图像分类上一致优于 LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 等低秩适配方法在微调时，其一阶权重空间更新方向被当前低秩参数化所限制，导致与全量微调（FFT）存在性能差距。

Method: GDLoRA 在标准 LoRA 微调框架上增加一条梯度分解路径。输入为训练样本，基座模型权重冻结，旁路 LoRA 低秩适配器参与前向计算并产生输出；反向传播时，一方面按常规计算 LoRA 因子梯度并交由 AdamW 更新；另一方面利用前向激活与反向信号重建当前参数处的全权重梯度。随后将该全权重梯度正交分解，得到属于 LoRA 参数化切空间的分量与垂直于该空间的法向分量（normal gradient）。法向分量被直接用于更新基座权重，从而补充 LoRA 无法表达的一阶更新方向；LoRA 因子仍由 AdamW 更新。该设计在匹配适配器和优化器配置下不增加标准 LoRA 的优化器状态内存开销。论文摘要未披露具体模型、数据规模、训练硬件、推理开销或逐项实验数值。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 刻画 LoRA 可访问梯度空间并证明其等于当前 LoRA 参数化诱导的切空间；对当前参数处的全权重梯度进行正交分解，定义垂直于该空间的分量（normal gradient）；提出 GDLoRA：从前向激活与反向信号重建全权重梯度，提取法向分量直接更新基座权重，同时保留 LoRA 因子的标准 AdamW 优化；在匹配适配器与优化器配置下，不增加标准 LoRA 的优化器状态内存预算

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used approach to parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), yet a performance gap can remain relative to full fine-tuning (FFT). Many LoRA variants improve the initialization or optimization of low-rank factors. At each training step, however, their first-order weight-space directions are constrained by the current parameterization. We characterize the corresponding LoRA-accessible gradient space and show that it coincides with the tangent space induced by the current LoRA parameterization. This characterization yields an orthogonal decomposition of the full weight gradient at the current model parameters. We term the component orthogonal to this space the normal gradient. Based on this decomposition, we propose GDLoRA (Gradient-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation). GDLoRA reconstructs the full weight gradient from forward activations and backward signals, extracts its normal component, and directly updates the base weights with this component, while retaining standard AdamW optimization for the LoRA factors. GDLoRA incorporates complementary normal gradients without increasing standard LoRA's optimizer-state memory budget under matched adapter and optimizer configurations. Experiments on natural language understanding, mathematical reasoning, commonsense reasoning, and image classification show that GDLoRA consistently improves over LoRA and narrows the performance gap to FFT. The code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [70] [AnyAct: Universal Action for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37025)
*Lingrui Xu,Yangqin Jiang,Jiachang Zhang,Xubin Ren,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnyAct 提出面向 LLM Agent 的通用动作层：通过分层渐进式检索压缩超大规模工具生态、通过测试时可靠性演化剪枝不可靠动作、通过异构观测接地统一多模态反馈，并在原语+语义混合动作空间上平衡成功率与执行成本；在 LiveMCPBench 与自建 OSMCP 上取得 SOTA，OSMCP 以 50 步达到 77.27% 成功率，约为多数竞争方法步数的一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放世界的大规模动态工具生态中，LLM Agent 面临工具总量超出上下文窗口的“规模困境”、工具质量随更新/宕机而变化的“非平稳性”，以及像素/文本/结构化数据等异构反馈造成的“信息孤岛”；需要一个统一动作层来高效且可靠地完成复杂顺序任务。

Method: AnyAct 构建一个统一的“通用动作层”，输入端是可用的工具/能力集合（从 GUI 操作到语义 API）以及任务指令与执行过程中产生的多模态观测。核心数据流为：(1) 动作空间构建阶段，采用分层渐进式检索，从海量工具生态中逐层过滤出与当前任务相关的动作，缓解工具总量超过 LLM 上下文窗口的规模困境；(2) 测试时可靠性演化阶段，在推理过程中持续剪枝不可靠动作，以应对工具更新或宕机导致的非平稳性；(3) 可靠性感知的动作编排模块负责在混合动作空间（原语动作 + 语义动作）中选择并调度动作，并通过异构观测接地模块将像素、文本、结构化数据等不同格式的反馈统一到同一表示，从而打通信息孤岛。优化目标是任务成功率与执行成本之间的平衡。摘要未披露具体的网络模块结构、训练目标函数、参数规模、推理硬件与延迟，故不进一步推断。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "OSMCP overall success rate", "value": "77.27%", "setting": "OSMCP 基准（作者构建的多粒度动作协作基准），使用 50 步完成"}, {"metric": "OSMCP 所需步数", "value": "50 steps", "setting": "对比多数竞争方法所需步数的一半"}, {"metric": "LiveMCPBench 性能", "value": "state-of-the-art（相对基线方法有显著提升，且对原生能力较弱的 LLM 基座提升尤为明显）", "setting": "LiveMCPBench，多种 LLM 基座模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出通用动作层 AnyAct，将可用能力统一为可自我演化的动作空间；分层渐进式检索：逐步过滤任务相关动作以应对工具规模超出上下文窗口的问题；测试时可靠性演化：在推理阶段剪枝不可靠动作以应对工具质量非平稳性；异构观测接地模块：统一像素、文本、结构化数据等多模态反馈，避免信息孤岛；定义原语动作 + 语义动作的混合动作空间，并针对任务成功率与执行成本的平衡进行优化；构建多粒度动作协作基准 OSMCP

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) advance, AI agents are increasingly deployed in open-world environments to tackle complex sequential tasks (e.g., document processing, cross-application collaboration), relying heavily on actions ranging from GUI operations to semantic APIs. However, three core challenges persist: the "scale dilemma" of massive tool ecosystems exceeding LLM context windows, the "non-stationarity" of tool quality due to updates or outages, and the "heterogeneity" of feedback formats (pixels, text, structured data) creating information silos. To address these, we propose AnyAct, a universal action layer that unifies available capabilities into a self-evolving action space, enabling agents to operate efficiently and reliably in large-scale, dynamic tool ecosystems. AnyAct's core design focuses on two objectives: constructing this action space via hierarchical progressive retrieval (filtering task-relevant actions) and test-time reliability evolution (pruning unreliable actions), and enabling reliability-aware action orchestration through a heterogeneous observation grounding module that unifies multi-modal feedback. Additionally, it defines a hybrid action space (primitive + semantic actions) and optimizes for a balance between task success rate and execution cost. Evaluations on LiveMCPBench and OSMCP (a new benchmark we developed for multi-granularity action collaboration) demonstrate state-of-the-art performance. AnyAct delivers substantial performance gains over baseline methods across various LLM base models on LiveMCPBench and improvements are particularly notable for models with constrained native capabilities. On OSMCP, it achieves 77.27% overall success with only 50 steps, which is half the steps required by most competitors.

</details>


### [71] [CADOC: Cache-Aware Dynamic Object Context for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37012)
*Junjie Yao,Zhangchen Zhou,Zhi-Qin John Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CADOC is a cache-aware dynamic context management method for long-horizon LLM agents. It replaces structured objects with compact retrieval Cards while preserving exact original retrieval, and schedules replacements in batches to avoid breaking prefix-cache reuse. The batching rule is derived from an economic order quantity trade-off and recovers the optimal integer batch under stationary assumptions. Evaluation shows roughly 40% average input cost reduction with task performance close to full context.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon agents suffer from growing context history, where resending full structured objects shortens usable task length and degrades reasoning. Existing recoverable context compression methods that replace objects with compact retrieval Cards can break prefix-cache reuse, and prior edit scheduling relies on inaccurate future-reuse forecasts or fixed intervals.

Method: CADOC operates as an online context-management algorithm for long-horizon agents. Inputs are the agent's accumulated history containing structured objects. The system replaces those structured objects with compact retrieval Cards that summarize the object but preserve an exact, on-demand retrieval path to the original contents. Scheduling of replacements is done in batches rather than per-object, since individual edits would invalidate prefix-cache reuse. CADOC balances the accumulated waiting cost incurred by delaying replacement against the shared cache-reconstruction cost incurred when a batch edit invalidates cached prefixes. This trade-off follows an economic order quantity formulation, and under stationary assumptions the rule recovers the optimal integer batch size. At inference time, the agent continues with a shortened prompt while exact originals remain retrievable, preserving task performance close to full-context execution.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "aggregate input cost reduction", "value": "approximately 40% average reduction", "setting": "evaluation across compared configurations, maintaining task performance close to full context (no online A/B test disclosed)"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Cache-aware dynamic object context (CADOC): replaces structured objects with compact retrieval Cards while preserving on-demand exact original retrieval.；Batch scheduling of replacements that balances accumulated waiting cost against shared cache-reconstruction cost.；An economic order quantity (EOQ) style scheduling rule that recovers the optimal integer batch under stationary assumptions.

Abstract: For a long-horizon agent, context is the bottleneck: the history is resent with every request, the window caps task length, and reasoning degrades as the history grows. Replacing structured objects with compact retrieval Cards shortens the prompt and keeps the exact originals retrievable, but editing the history can break prefix-cache reuse, and prior recoverable methods time their edits by forecasts of future reuse or by preset intervals. We propose CADOC (Cache-Aware Dynamic Object Context), an online algorithm that replaces structured objects with compact Cards while preserving exact, on-demand retrieval of their original contents. CADOC schedules replacements in batches by balancing accumulated waiting cost against shared cache-reconstruction cost. Its scheduling rule follows from an economic order quantity trade-off, recovers the optimal integer batch under stationary assumptions. Across evaluation, CADOC consistently achieves the lowest aggregate input cost among the compared configurations, which reduces input cost by approximately 40\% on average while maintaining task performance close to full context. CADOC thus provides a cost-derived approach to compressible context management, demonstrating that efficient compression depends not only on shortening prompts but also on scheduling edits to preserve cache reuse.

</details>


### [72] [ImbalancE: Inference-Time Latent Search Against Degree Imbalance in Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36996)
*Alberto Bernardi,Luca Costabello,Christophe Gueret*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了知识图谱链接预测中的度不平衡偏差问题，并提出一种推理时潜在搜索优化方法ImbalancE，无需重新训练模型即可显著提升不平衡三元组上的预测性能，对推荐系统召回阶段的链接预测有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱嵌入模型在预测链接时，当目标实体度数远小于锚点实体度数时（度不平衡三元组），预测性能显著下降，影响推荐系统等场景中的关键角落案例。

Method: 方法基于预训练的知识图谱嵌入模型。在推理阶段，针对每个测试三元组，若目标实体与锚点实体存在度不平衡，则执行潜在搜索优化：在预训练嵌入空间中探索，通过融合已知的嵌入信息和带外信息（out-of-band information）来调整目标实体的表示，从而减轻度不平衡带来的偏差。具体搜索策略、优化目标及数据流未在摘要中详细披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "链接预测性能", "value": "显著提升（具体数值未披露）", "setting": "在常见基准数据集的度不平衡三元组上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现并量化了度不平衡偏差：目标实体度数远低于锚点实体时预测性能急剧下降，且该问题比以往认为的更普遍。；提出推理时潜在搜索优化方法（ImbalancE），在评估时探索嵌入空间，融合已知信息和带外信息以缓解度不平衡偏差。；方法建立在预训练模型之上，无需重新训练，即可显著提升最不平衡三元组上的预测性能。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Embedding models have been extensively used to learn representations of entities and relations in Knowledge Graphs for predicting missing links. However, the quality of the learned representations varies a lot across different areas of the graph. If previous research has loosely linked the problem to relation types or degree bias, we show that it is more widespread and it correlates with the degree imbalance of the entities in test triples. In particular, the prediction of a target entity that has a degree much smaller than the degree of the anchor entity is extremely problematic. This is critical in recommender systems and other use cases, where these triples represent important corner cases. To address this issue, we propose an inference-time latent search optimization method capable of significantly improving model predictions on the most imbalanced triples. Built on top of a pre-trained model, it explores the embedding space at evaluation time, blending known and out-of-band information to mitigate the degree imbalance bias. We show the value of our approach on imbalanced triples from common benchmark datasets, where we outperform conventional methods, opening the door to the successful adoption of Knowledge Graph Embedding models on these critical corner cases.

</details>


### [73] [CoEM: Empowering Long-Context Reasoning with Commit-on-Evidence Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36935)
*Jingguang Li,Yebo Wu,Zuyi Guo,Kailang Ma,Xianjie Dai,Han Zheng,Benwang Chen,Li Li,Can Rong,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Commit-on-Evidence Memory（CoEM），通过延迟压缩、学习型策略和冻结验证器，在固定上下文-记忆预算下提升LLM长上下文推理性能，在Qwen3.5-9B上6400文档输入上F1提升10.4-11.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长上下文推理时，随着上下文长度增加性能下降；现有分块处理并维护有界文本记忆的方法会因过早压缩而丢失后续推理所需的关键细节。

Method: CoEM是一个用于长上下文推理的记忆管理框架。输入为长文档（如6400篇文档）分块序列。系统维护一个固定预算的待处理集合（pending set），逐字保留潜在有用的源摘录，不立即压缩。随着新上下文到达，一个学习型策略（policy）对每个待处理摘录进行决策：提升为已提交记忆（committed memory）、保留待考虑（retain）或丢弃（discard）。已提交记忆构成紧凑的记忆事实，供后续推理使用。一个冻结验证器（frozen verifier）确保提议的事实只有在被保留摘录和当前上下文支持时才被接受。策略通过强化学习训练，奖励由细粒度步骤级证据奖励和最终答案奖励组合而成。推理时，模型基于当前上下文和已提交记忆进行答案生成，整个过程在有限的上下文-记忆预算下运行。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "F1", "value": "10.4-11.4 points improvement over strongest memory baseline", "setting": "Qwen3.5-9B, 6400 documents long-context input"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Commit-on-Evidence Memory机制：在固定上下文-记忆预算下，将潜在有用的源摘录逐字保留在待处理集合中，等待后续上下文澄清其相关性后再决定压缩。；学习型策略：当新上下文到达时，策略重新审视每个待处理摘录，决定将其提升为已提交记忆、保留待考虑或丢弃。；冻结验证器：确保提议的事实只有在被保留摘录和当前上下文支持时才被接受。；使用强化学习训练策略：结合细粒度的步骤级证据奖励与最终答案奖励。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning is essential for complex and long-horizon tasks, yet the performance of large language models (LLMs) degrades as context length increases. Recent approaches address this by processing input chunk by chunk while maintaining a bounded textual memory in model context. However, premature information compression can discard critical details essential for subsequent reasoning. In this paper, we introduce Commit-on-Evidence Memory (CoEM), which learns when to convert source evidence into compact memory facts. Specifically, under a fixed context-memory budget, CoEM preserves potentially useful source excerpts verbatim in a pending set, allowing subsequent context to clarify their relevance before irreversible compression. As new context arrives, a learned policy revisits each pending excerpt and decides whether to promote it to the committed memory, retain it for further consideration, or discard it. A frozen verifier ensures proposed facts are accepted only if supported by retained excerpts and current context. To further guide effective memory management, we train this policy using reinforcement learning by combining fine-grained, step-level evidence rewards with final answer rewards. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoEM consistently improves long-context reasoning. When evaluated on 6,400 documents long-context input, CoEM outperforms the strongest memory baseline by 10.4-11.4 F1 points on Qwen3.5-9B. Code repository: this https URL .

</details>


### [74] [Neuro-Symbolic Computer Use: Learning Reusable Policies for Reliable and Efficient Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36927)
*Hyewon Suh,Thanh Minh Nguyen,Chih-Lun Lee,Darrow Hartman,Lizhao Liu,Xin Eric Wang,Ang Li,Jiachen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出神经符号计算机使用范式：把重复工作流中稳定的决策固化为可执行代码策略，仅将 grounding、状态检查等观测依赖决策交给神经模型；通过神经符号策略迭代、变体生成和部署前动作验证器，在 OSWorld-Verified 和 ScienceBoard 上取得最高 Pass^3，同时将单次运行成本降低 15-217 倍、延迟降低 3.4-5.1 倍，并证明策略可迁移至原始留出任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 computer-use agent 每次运行都逐步重新规划，导致重复工作流成本高、可靠性差；需要学习可复用的神经符号策略，将跨运行稳定的决策固化为可执行代码，仅把依赖观测的决策交给神经网络模型。

Method: 系统输入端为一条 agent 轨迹与目标工作流。策略以可执行代码形式表示，代码中固化跨运行稳定的决策：操作顺序、变量、循环和分支；对 grounding、状态检查等依赖当前观测的决策，则调用神经模型（如视觉语言模型）动态完成。训练采用 neuro-symbolic policy iteration 循环：先从一条 agent 轨迹初始化代码策略；执行策略后，由任务完成 judge 和步骤级 judge 诊断失败；coding model 根据诊断信息以及 agent 在失败点处的 continuation 来修订代码，该过程不访问 benchmark evaluator，以避免评测器泄漏。为增强可复用性，在生成的参数变体和初始状态变体上迭代执行与修订，使策略适应输入变化和不同起始状态。部署时，每一步会改变系统状态的动作都先经过 pre-action verifier 校验，以拦截不安全或错误的状态变更。推理时直接执行学习到的代码策略，仅在需要观测依赖判断处调用神经模型，从而避免每次运行都完整重新规划，降低单次运行成本与延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Pass^3", "value": "3.6-15.8 points above the base agent", "setting": "OSWorld-Verified 与 ScienceBoard 全部四种设置中取得最高 Pass^3"}, {"metric": "per-run cost reduction", "value": "15-217×", "setting": "重复工作流执行"}, {"metric": "latency reduction", "value": "3.4-5.1×", "setting": "重复工作流执行"}, {"metric": "Pass^3 on held-out original tasks", "value": "exceeding AutoRPA by 8.6-17.5 points", "setting": "OSWorld-Verified，仅在变体上构建的策略迁移至原始留出任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 neuro-symbolic computer use 范式：将稳定决策（顺序、变量、循环、分支）固化为可执行代码，观测依赖决策（grounding、状态检查）交给神经模型。；提出 neuro-symbolic policy iteration：从单条 agent 轨迹出发，执行策略、用任务完成与步骤级 judge 诊断失败，再由 coding model 结合失败点处 agent continuation 修订代码，且不使用 benchmark evaluator。；通过在生成的参数和初始状态变体上迭代，提升策略可复用性，并验证可迁移至原始留出任务。；部署时使用 pre-action verifier 守护每一步状态变更操作。

Abstract: Many computer tasks recur: the same workflow runs many times, with new inputs and from different starting states. Current computer-use agents re-plan every step of every run, which makes them costly and unreliable on such tasks. We introduce neuro-symbolic computer use, in which a recurring workflow is executed by a learned policy rather than re-derived by an agent on each run. The policy fixes the decisions that are stable across runs (ordering, variables, loops, and branches) in executable code, and delegates observation-dependent decisions, such as grounding and state checks, to neural models. We learn these policies with neuro-symbolic policy iteration: starting from one agent trajectory, it executes the policy, diagnoses failures with task-completion and step-level judges, and revises the code with a coding model informed by an agent's continuation from the point of failure, without access to the benchmark evaluator. Iterating on generated parameter and initial-state variants makes the policy reusable, and a pre-action verifier guards each state-mutating step at deployment. On OSWorld-Verified and ScienceBoard, the learned policies achieve the highest Pass^3 of all methods in all four settings, 3.6-15.8 points above the base agent, while cutting per-run cost by 15-217$\times$ and latency by 3.4-5.1$\times$. On OSWorld-Verified, policies built only on variants transfer to the held-out original tasks, exceeding AutoRPA by 8.6-17.5 points in Pass^3.

</details>


### [75] [Harness Evolution as Learning: Approximation, Generalization, and Optimization Limits of Self-Improving Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36892)
*Zeyu Gan,Zixuan Gong,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究个人智能体的外壳演化，将其形式化为学习问题，从近似、泛化与优化误差三方面分析外壳架构、规模与自演化算法的能力与局限，并提出偏好导向基准。对关注智能体记忆、上下文工程与自演化系统的读者有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在底层模型固定的条件下，如何通过对外壳（管理上下文、记忆、工具与执行的外围层）进行工程化设计与演化，使个人智能体持续适应用户偏好；以及外壳演化的架构、规模与自演化算法受到哪些近似、泛化与优化限制。

Method: 论文以外壳演化（harness evolution）为核心对象：在底层 LLM 固定不变的前提下，智能体通过外围外壳层管理上下文、记忆、工具与执行流程，并持续演化以适应用户偏好。方法上从三个维度展开：外壳架构（如何组织上下文、记忆、工具与执行模块）、外壳规模（有限交互证据下的容量）、自演化算法（更新动态是否有偏）。经验部分引入偏好导向的基准，系统评估个人智能体在这三个维度上的能力与限制；理论部分将外壳演化形式化为学习问题，分别用近似误差、泛化误差与优化误差解释所观察到的现象：可达策略刻画外壳能表达的行为空间，有限交互证据下的容量分析刻画泛化限制，有偏更新动态分析刻画优化过程中的偏差。三层误差共同构成对通过外壳演化实现个性化的统一限制视角。摘要未披露具体的模块连接、训练目标、推理路径或在线服务流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出面向偏好的基准，用于系统刻画个人智能体在外壳架构、外壳规模与自演化算法三个维度的能力与局限；将外壳演化形式化为学习问题，并用近似误差、泛化误差与优化误差解释经验现象；对可达策略、有限交互证据下的容量、以及有偏更新动态进行理论分析

Abstract: As the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) continue to advance, increasing attention is turning to how to translate their abilities into useful behavior. Personal agents bring this question into everyday settings, where models are expected to serve individual users and continually adapt to their preferences. With the underlying model held fixed, such adaptation relies on harness engineering: designing and evolving the surrounding layer that manages context, memory, tools, and execution. Despite rapid progress, the factors governing effective harness evolution remain insufficiently understood. To narrow this gap, we investigate three central questions concerning harness architecture, harness scale, and self-evolution algorithms through complementary empirical and theoretical analyses. Empirically, we introduce a preference-oriented benchmark and systematically characterize the capabilities and limitations of personal agents associated with these three dimensions. Theoretically, we formulate harness evolution as a learning problem and explain these phenomena through approximation, generalization, and optimization errors. Analyses of reachable policies, capacity under finite interaction evidence, and biased update dynamics provide theoretical accounts of the observed phenomena. Together, these results offer a unified perspective on the limits of personalization through harness evolution and inform future harness design.

</details>


### [76] [Learn from the Gap: Differential-Aware Advantage Pruning with Adaptive Rollout Sampling for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36932)
*Jiahua Yang,Zhiwei Yang,Xianpeng Zhang,Dongyu Chen,Xing Chen,Tianhuang Su,Haonan Lu,Quanlong Guan,Kai Tang,Chuangchuang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FastRL 是一个即插即用的强化学习后训练框架，通过优势感知剪枝和自适应 rollout 采样，在 GRPO、DAPO、GSPO 上实现平均 2.07 倍训练加速和约 1.64% 的视觉推理准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 及其变体在 RL 后训练中因每题多次 rollout 采样和重复计算每个 token 概率而导致计算开销巨大；同时低信息或高度同质的轨迹会降低学习信号效率，拖慢优化并限制最终性能。

Method: FastRL 是一个用于强化学习后训练的框架，作为即插即用模块集成到现有 GRPO、DAPO、GSPO 等算法中。其核心流程如下：首先，在标准的多 rollout 采样阶段，模型对每个问题生成多个候选轨迹；然后，优势感知剪枝模块评估每条轨迹的优势值，并根据优势大小和轨迹间梯度多样性，选择性地保留高优势且多样的轨迹，丢弃低信息或高度同质的轨迹；接着，自适应 rollout 采样机制根据历史上剪枝分布动态调整每个训练阶段的采样数量，在探索充分性和计算效率之间取得平衡；最后，使用保留的轨迹计算梯度并更新策略。该设计减少了冗余的前向和反向传播计算，同时通过保持梯度多样性提高学习信号质量，从而在视觉推理任务上实现了训练加速和性能提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "训练速度", "value": "平均 2.07 倍加速", "setting": "Geometry3K 和 GeoQA8K-R1V 数据集"}, {"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "提升约 1.64%", "setting": "视觉推理基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 优势感知剪枝策略：选择性保留高优势轨迹，同时最大化轨迹间梯度多样性。；自适应 rollout 采样机制：根据历史剪枝分布动态调整不同训练阶段的采样规模，平衡探索充分性与计算效率。；即插即用框架 FastRL，可无缝集成到 GRPO、DAPO、GSPO 等变体中。

Abstract: Recently, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and its variants have been developed for policy optimization and demonstrated notable performance gains. However, these methods usually incur substantial computational overhead due to per-question multi-rollout sampling and repeated per-token probability evaluation across rollouts. Furthermore, low-information or highly homogeneous trajectories can degrade downstream learning signal efficiency, hindering model optimization and limiting final performance. To address these issues, we propose FastRL, a novel plug-and-play reinforcement learning framework that simultaneously improves training efficiency and the effectiveness of policy learning. Specifically, 1) We introduce an advantage-aware pruning strategy to selectively preserve high-advantage trajectories while maximizing inter-trajectory gradient diversity. 2) Then, we design an adaptive rollout sampling mechanism to dynamically adjust the sampling scale across different training stages based on historical pruning distributions, balancing exploration adequacy and computational efficiency. Experiments demonstrate that FastRL can be seamlessly integrated into GRPO, DAPO, and GSPO variants, achieving an average 2.07$\times$ training speedup on Geometry3K and GeoQA8K-R1V, along with an approximately 1.64\% improvement in average accuracy on visual reasoning benchmarks. Source codes will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [77] [IronLLM: Forging Compact Edge-Native Language Models for Real-Time Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36860)
*Changdi Yang,Fengquan Jiao,Haochih Lin,Haoran Yang,Jing Xiao,Liangyu Huo,Suxin Lu,Tiance Chen,Wei Liu,Yinggan Xu,Yunxiang Lu,Zai Zheng,Zhirui Xie,Zhongyang Che,Ziyan Tang,Zuoxiang Zhao,Jian Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IronLLM-0.6B是一个654M参数的端侧LLM，采用混合注意力与X-MTP共享KV多token预测实现1.48x解码加速，6.2T token预训练加多域on-policy蒸馏，并有量化友好的Light版本，主打资源受限场景的性能-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限端侧设备上实现低延迟、低成本的LLM实时推理，同时尽量保持与更大模型可比的生成质量。

Method: IronLLM-0.6B是一个654M参数的端侧语言模型。输入为文本token序列，主干采用混合注意力架构以在端侧推理中平衡表达能力与计算效率。在解码阶段引入X-MTP：一种轻量共享KV的多token预测设计，它通过共享KV避免每个预测深度重复进行KV-cache重放，并配合轻量验证头完成无回滚（rollback-free）的drafting，从而在不牺牲生成正确性的前提下实现1.48x解码加速。训练流程先在大约6.2万亿tokens上以质量导向的数据管道进行预训练，随后通过Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation进行后训练，从多个领域专用教师模型中集成能力。为满足端侧低延迟需求，模型采用Instruct-Only设计，减少不必要的通用生成模式开销。此外提供IronLLM-0.6B-Light变体，用Dynamic Tanh替换RMSNorm并简化若干高计算量组件，以提升推理与量化效率。整体推理路径为：端侧加载模型后，文本输入经混合注意力主干前向计算，X-MTP共享KV多token预测并行生成候选token并由轻量验证头验证，减少逐token解码串行开销，输出简洁回答。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "decoding speedup", "value": "1.48x", "setting": "X-MTP 共享KV多token预测与轻量验证头带来的解码加速"}, {"metric": "competitive performance vs larger models", "value": "competitive relative to Qwen3.5-0.8B and MiniCPM5-1B", "setting": "评测显示在多个任务上具有竞争力并产生更简洁回答，具体基准与数值未在摘要中披露"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: X-MTP轻量共享KV多token预测设计，消除逐深度KV-cache重放，并用轻量验证头实现无回滚drafting，带来1.48倍解码加速；混合注意力架构适配端侧高效推理；Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation，从领域专用教师模型集成多域能力；Instruct-Only设计以减少端侧低延迟场景的开销；IronLLM-0.6B-Light：用Dynamic Tanh替代RMSNorm并简化高开销组件，提升推理与量化效率

Abstract: We present IronLLM-0.6B, a 654M-parameter language model designed for efficient on-device inference. IronLLM-0.6B combines a hybrid attention architecture with X-MTP, a lightweight shared-KV multi-token prediction design that eliminates per-depth KV-cache replay and employs a lightweight verification head for rollback-free drafting, achieving a 1.48x decoding speedup. The model is pretrained on approximately 6.2 trillion tokens using a quality-oriented data pipeline and is further post-trained with Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation to integrate capabilities from domain-specialized teachers. To better meet the low-latency requirements of on-device scenarios, IronLLM-0.6B adopts an Instruct-Only design. Evaluations show that IronLLM-0.6B achieves competitive performance relative to larger models such as Qwen3.5-0.8B and MiniCPM5-1B, while producing more concise responses on many tasks. We further present IronLLM-0.6B-Light, which replaces RMSNorm with Dynamic Tanh and simplifies several computationally expensive components to improve inference and quantization efficiency. Together, the IronLLM models provide an effective performance-efficiency trade-off for resource-constrained deployment.

</details>


### [78] [Where Does Staleness Accumulate? Pool Aware Effective Staleness Control for Asynchronous RL in LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36830)
*Chenliang Li,Neiwen Ling,Zijun Wei,Alfredo Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE将异步RL轨迹陈旧度分解为生成与等待两部分，通过池感知拒绝预算和前缀感知有效陈旧度评分控制轨迹质量，在单轮数学推理中六基准平均验证准确率提升18.7%，匹配同步RL性能只需47.1% GPU时间，并适用于多轮工具集成推理、MoE模型与不同RL算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全异步强化学习在提升资源利用率的同时，因轨迹生成与策略优化重叠而产生策略滞后，如何在不牺牲异步墙钟收益的前提下控制轨迹陈旧度。

Method: PACE针对完全异步RL的轨迹池进行控制。异步RL中，rollout生成与策略优化并行，轨迹在生成和排队过程中经历策略版本变化。PACE首先将每条轨迹的陈旧度分解为生成陈旧度（从开始生成到生成完成期间策略更新次数累积）和等待陈旧度（生成完成后在池中等待到被采样期间继续累积）。然后，PACE根据池占用情况动态计算自适应拒绝预算：当池占用超过期望水平时，将过剩转化为拒绝预算。对池中已完成轨迹，计算有效陈旧度评分：结合等待陈旧度和前缀感知的生成陈旧度，其中前缀感知生成陈旧度考虑轨迹前缀在不同策略版本下的生成情况，避免长轨迹因跨越多个版本而被过度惩罚。最后依据有效陈旧度评分对轨迹排序和筛选，丢弃超出拒绝预算的最陈旧轨迹。该机制在训练循环中在线运行，不影响rollout与训练的重叠，保持异步执行。训练目标仍为策略优化（未指明具体算法，但提到替代RL算法验证），通过控制送入训练器的轨迹陈旧度来稳定优化并提升验证性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "六基准平均验证准确率", "value": "相对无过滤异步RL提升18.7%", "setting": "单轮数学推理，相同墙钟预算"}, {"metric": "匹配同步RL性能所需GPU时间", "value": "减少47.1%", "setting": "单轮数学推理"}, {"metric": "验证性能", "value": "优于同步RL和无过滤异步RL", "setting": "多轮工具集成推理"}, {"metric": "适用性验证", "value": "在混合专家模型和替代RL算法上均有效", "setting": "其他模型架构和训练算法"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将轨迹陈旧度分解为生成陈旧度（生成完成前累积）与等待陈旧度（完成入池后累积）；提出PACE框架，将池占用过剩转化为自适应拒绝预算，并基于有效陈旧度评分对完成轨迹排序；有效陈旧度评分结合等待陈旧度与前缀感知生成陈旧度，避免仅因跨越多个策略版本而惩罚长或中断的rollout

Abstract: Fully asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) improves resource utilization in large language model post-training by overlapping rollout generation with policy optimization, but it also introduces policy lag as trajectories are generated and queued while the trainer continues to update. We study how this lag accumulates over a trajectory's lifetime and how it can be controlled without sacrificing the wall-clock benefits of asynchronous execution. We decompose trajectory staleness into Generation Staleness, accumulated before rollout completion, and Waiting Staleness, accumulated after a completed trajectory enters the pool. Motivated by this decomposition, we introduce PACE (Pool-Aware Control of Effective Staleness). PACE converts excess pool occupancy into an adaptive rejection budget and ranks completed trajectories using an effective-staleness score that combines Waiting Staleness with prefix-aware Generation Staleness. This avoids penalizing long or interrupted rollouts solely because they span multiple policy versions. In single-turn mathematical reasoning, PACE improves the six-benchmark average validation accuracy by 18.7\% over unfiltered asynchronous RL at the same wall-clock budget and matches synchronous RL performance with 47.1\% less GPU time. PACE also improves validation performance in multi-turn tool-integrated reasoning, outperforming both synchronous and unfiltered asynchronous RL. Further experiments with the mixture-of-experts model and an alternative RL algorithm support its applicability across model architectures and training algorithms.

</details>


### [79] [UpliftMem: Learning Set-Level Uplift for Agent Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36805)
*Mengkun Liang,Haoran Qiang,Guannan Liu,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UpliftMem从集合级执行提升中学习LLM Agent的记忆集检索，通过EVSI准则定向探测替代记忆集以克服反馈覆盖不足，在ALFWorld、WebShop和BigCodeBench上取得最佳成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何从集合级别的执行提升（uplift）中学习LLM Agent的memory检索，避免传统检索只能观测已执行记忆集而缺乏反事实反馈的问题。

Method: UpliftMem由共享scorer和冻结的LLM executor组成。输入是候选记忆集合，scorer对每个集合进行评分并选择用于exeuctor执行的集合。训练时，为了获得反事实反馈，系统对替代记忆集进行定向探测（probing），探测选择基于期望样本信息价值（EVSI）准则，该准则在相关高斯模型下具有闭式解，用于分配有限的训练rollout以最大化局部检索决策的期望改进。scorer使用冻结的executor执行所选记忆集产生的集合级uplift作为监督信号进行训练。推理时，scorer直接选择记忆集，无需测试时探测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "success rate", "value": "最佳", "setting": "ALFWorld、WebShop、BigCodeBench主评估集，与所评估基线相比"}, {"metric": "memory-use decisions and execution feedback usage", "value": "改进", "setting": "固定存储和匹配探测预算的受控评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出set-level uplift建模，以同一executor无记忆时的表现为基线来学习记忆集检索。；理论分析检索偏好如何限制反馈覆盖，并据此进行有目标的替代记忆集探测（probing）。；基于相关高斯模型推导EVSI闭式准则，按对局部检索决策的期望改进分配有限的训练rollout。；共享scorer使用冻结executor训练，推理时无需额外探测即可选择记忆集。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents reuse external memory to guide new tasks, but effective retrieval requires learning which memory sets improve execution. Such learning relies on costly outcome feedback: ordinary retrieval observes only executed sets, while evaluating alternatives requires additional rollouts. We introduce \textsc{UpliftMem}, which learns memory retrieval from set-level execution uplift relative to the same executor without memory. A theoretical analysis of how retrieval preferences restrict feedback coverage motivates targeted probing of alternative memory sets. Probe selection follows an expected value of sample information (EVSI) criterion, derived in closed form under a correlated Gaussian model, to allocate limited training rollouts according to their expected improvement in local retrieval decisions. The shared scorer is trained with a frozen executor and selects memory sets without test-time probes. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and BigCodeBench, \textsc{UpliftMem} achieves the best success rates among evaluated baselines on the main evaluation sets. Controlled fixed-store and matched probe budget evaluations further demonstrate improved memory-use decisions and more effective use of execution feedback.

</details>


### [80] [Calibrate the Decisions That Change the Future: On-Policy Post-Training Quantization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36828)
*Wenxiao Fan,Jingling Fu,Lichen Ma,Yu He,Luohang Liu,Jinbao Xue,Ke Zhang,Junshi Huang,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OnPTQ 提出一种面向多模态大语言模型的后训练量化校准框架，通过在量化策略访问的轨迹上进行 on-policy 校准，利用反事实 rollout 评估决策后果，并引入 Decision-Consequence 风险优先处理关键状态。在多个 Qwen 模型低比特设置下提升了下游性能并减少正确性翻转，且不改变推理图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PTQ 校准方法使用固定序列和局部目标重建，忽略了自回归反馈：量化导致的 token 变化会改变前缀并影响未来状态，从而造成决策偏差累积。

Method: OnPTQ 框架利用当前量化策略生成的轨迹进行校准。首先，在共享前缀上识别量化侵蚀边界，即量化导致 token 决策发生显著变化的节点。然后，对这些边界处的竞争 token 进行短反事实 rollout，评估每个候选 token 对未来生成的影响。结合当前策略差异（当前 token 与参考 token 的差异）和分支后果（未来价值跨度），构造 Decision-Consequence 风险。该风险用于优先处理关键状态。校准过程中，采用上下文锚定和轨迹刷新机制：上下文锚定保持多模态输入的信息，轨迹刷新使校准数据与更新后的量化策略保持一致。训练目标是最小化 Decision-Consequence 风险，从而减少量化带来的决策偏差。推理时，部署的推理图不变，仅量化参数被优化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "下游性能与正确性翻转次数", "value": "相比基线有所提升，正确性翻转更少", "setting": "视觉-语言及全模态 Qwen 模型，多种低比特设置，与 Dense/FP16 参考对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 OnPTQ，在量化策略实际访问的轨迹上进行 on-policy 校准，捕捉自回归反馈。；在共享前缀上识别量化侵蚀边界，通过短反事实 rollout 评估候选 token，并结合当前差异与分支后果构造 Decision-Consequence 风险。；利用风险优先处理关键状态，同时通过上下文锚定和轨迹刷新保持多模态行为与校准对齐。；推导 Decision-Consequence 界，将行为偏差与当前策略差异和动作条件未来价值跨度联系起来。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) lowers deployment cost for multimodal large language models, but calibration typically reconstructs fixed sequences with local objectives. This overlooks autoregressive feedback: a quantization-induced token change redirects the prefix and changes future states. Yet on-policy coverage alone is insufficient because many decision mismatches barely affect future generation. We propose OnPTQ, an on-policy framework that calibrates on trajectories visited by the current quantized policy. On shared prefixes, OnPTQ identifies quantization-eroded boundaries, evaluates competing tokens through short counterfactual rollouts, and combines current discrepancy with branch consequence into a Decision--Consequence risk. The risk prioritizes critical states, while context anchoring and trajectory refresh preserve multimodal behavior and keep calibration aligned with the updated policy. We further derive a Decision--Consequence bound linking behavioral deviation to current policy discrepancy and action-conditioned future-value span. Across vision--language and omni-modal Qwen models under multiple low-bit settings, OnPTQ improves downstream performance and yields fewer correctness flips against the corresponding Dense/FP16 references, without changing the deployed inference graph.

</details>


### [81] [AI as a Compiler: Compiling Triton kernels without the Triton compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36800)
*François Costa,Charly Castes,Thomas Bourgeat,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出用 LLM 智能体直接将 Triton 内核编译为 NVIDIA PTX，绕过传统编译器降低管线，并在 12 个常见内核和 10 个 ML 论文内核上取得 autotuned Triton 的 0.83x-3.34x 性能。同时扩展 PTX 验证器以支持 Blackwell 架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统编译器后端随编程模型和硬件演进难以维护；能否用 LLM 替代优化和降低（lowering）阶段，直接将 Triton 内核翻译为 NVIDIA PTX。

Method: 输入：Triton 内核源代码。系统包含两个主要部分：(1) 评估环境：用于编译和测试智能体生成的 PTX 代码，包含对候选 PTX 的评估和验证。验证建立在 Volta（现有 PTX 验证器）之上，并扩展支持 Blackwell tcgen05 Tensor Core 接口，为此需要建模三个架构特性：受管张量内存、基于描述符的操作数布局、以及通过 commits、waits、内存屏障和代理栅栏协调的异步执行。(2) 智能体框架：LLM 智能体将 Triton 内核翻译为 PTX，可能涉及多轮迭代，利用评估环境反馈优化生成的 PTX。训练目标/优化：文中未披露具体训练目标；智能体可能通过提示或反馈循环进行优化。推理/服务流程：LLM 生成 PTX，评估环境验证并测试性能，然后智能体可根据结果调整。该架构旨在解决传统编译器后端维护成本高的问题，通过 LLM 的灵活性直接生成针对特定硬件优化的 PTX 代码。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对 autotuned Triton 的性能比", "value": "0.83x-3.34x", "setting": "12 个常见内核，Ada/Hopper/Blackwell GPU"}, {"metric": "BitDelta 加速", "value": "3.34x", "setting": "解码打包二进制权重到 Tensor Core 操作数"}, {"metric": "FlashAttention 加速", "value": "1.37x", "setting": "每个线程在张量内存中处理完整 softmax 行"}, {"metric": "卷积窗口重用加速", "value": "最高 2.23x", "setting": "重用重叠卷积窗口"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 AI lowering 概念：用 LLM 智能体直接将 Triton 内核翻译为 PTX，绕过传统编译器降低流程。；构建自动评估候选 PTX 的环境和智能体框架，包含全面的验证支持。；扩展 Volta PTX 验证器以支持现代 GPU 架构，特别是 Blackwell 的 tcgen05 Tensor Core 接口，建模受管张量内存、基于描述符的操作数布局以及通过 commit/wait/内存屏障/代理栅栏协调的异步执行。；发现 Triton 降低管线未执行的变换可带来显著性能提升：解码打包二进制权重到 Tensor Core 操作数（BitDelta 3.34x）、每个线程在张量内存中处理完整 softmax 行（FlashAttention 1.37x）、重用重叠卷积窗口（最高 2.23x）。

Abstract: Compiler backends are expensive to build and maintain as programming models, workloads, and accelerators evolve. We investigate whether large language models can replace the conventional optimizing and lowering pipeline, a process that we call AI lowering. We study AI lowering from Triton to NVIDIA PTX: an LLM agent translates Triton kernels directly into PTX. We build an environment that evaluates candidate PTX, and an agentic harness in which an LLM translates Triton kernels into PTX. Across twelve common kernels on Ada, Hopper, and Blackwell GPUs and ten kernels from recent ML papers, AI lowering achieves 0.83x-3.34x the performance of autotuned Triton. The largest gains come from transformations that Triton's lowering pipeline does not perform, such as decoding packed binary weights directly into Tensor Core operands (3.34x on BitDelta), assigning each thread a complete softmax row in tensor memory (1.37x on FlashAttention), and reusing overlapping convolution windows (up to 2.23x). These results rely on a robust evaluation harness with comprehensive verification support. We build on Volta, an existing PTX verifier, and substantially extend it to support modern GPU architectures by introducing support for Blackwell's tcgen05 Tensor Core interface. This requires modeling three architectural features: managed tensor memory, descriptor-based operand layouts, and asynchronous execution coordinated through commits, waits, memory barriers, and proxy fences. We discuss the challenges involved in formalizing them, as well as the current limitations. Our results suggest an emerging future in which AI compilers replace custom-written intermediate representations and checkers, reducing the time and engineering effort required to bring up software for new general-purpose and custom chips.

</details>


### [82] [Aperture: Merge-Consistent Rotary States for Compressed Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36781)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aperture 为 token 压缩提出基于傅里叶矩的旋转状态，证明其最小维度可保持合并一致性，在视频问答任务中与部署的合并规则准确率接近（65.63% vs 67.12%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 token 压缩（合并多个位置的内容）时，旋转位置嵌入通常只给合并后的 token 分配一个坐标，导致后续合并中位置信息损失。论文研究必须保留哪些位置信息以保证压缩下旋转交互的一致性。

Method: Aperture 将 token 压缩视为合并问题：输入是序列中多个位置，每个位置有内容表示和旋转频率。它不将合并后的 token 简单赋予单一旋转坐标，而是在模型旋转频率上存储加权支撑的傅里叶矩作为连续状态。这些矩以加权质量（代表被合并位置的总权重）表示，使后续合并的更新具有可加性：多个 token 的矩可以直接相加，从而保持合并一致性。注意力归一化被分离为独立的读出步骤，不包含在状态更新中。对于均匀区间，该状态等价于中心旋转乘以 sinc 增益，即位置信息分解为中心位置和区间宽度两个部分。论文刻画了中心能否确定区间宽度的条件，并构造了中心相同但区间宽度不同的匹配示例。在训练的时间读取器中，压缩迁移效果随增益校准和特征放置而变化。推理时，Aperture 状态与标准旋转位置编码结合使用，通过存储的傅里叶矩恢复合并后的位置交互。整体流程：加权支撑 -> 傅里叶矩存储 -> 可加合并 -> 注意力读出。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "video question answering accuracy", "value": "65.63%", "setting": "prespecified native video question-answering comparison, stored support (Aperture) vs deployed merging rule"}, {"metric": "video question answering accuracy", "value": "67.12%", "setting": "prespecified native video question-answering comparison, deployed merging rule baseline"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Aperture 状态：在模型的旋转频率上存储 token 加权支撑的傅里叶矩。；证明这些矩在连续状态中具有最小实维度，足以表示选定的期望旋转交互。；加权质量使更新可加，注意力归一化作为独立的读出选择。；对均匀区间给出中心旋转乘以 sinc 增益的形式。；刻画中心决定区间宽度的条件，并构造反例。；数值验证加权支撑实现，并在训练的时间读取器和视频问答任务中测试压缩迁移。

Abstract: Token compression combines content from several positions, yet rotary position embeddings usually assign the merged token one coordinate. We ask what positional information must survive later merges. Aperture stores Fourier moments of the token's weighted support at the model's rotary frequencies. We prove that these moments have minimal real dimension among continuous states sufficient for the selected expected rotary interactions. Represented mass makes updates additive; attention normalisation remains a separate readout choice. Uniform intervals give a centre rotation times a sinc gain. We characterise when centres determine interval widths and construct matched examples where they do not. Numerical checks verify the weighted-support implementation. In trained temporal readers, compression transfer varies with gain calibration and feature placement. In a prespecified native video question-answering comparison, stored support reaches $65.63\%$ accuracy versus $67.12\%$ for the deployed merging rule. These results separate exact positional preservation under compression from downstream benefit.

</details>


### [83] [Generalizable Lifelong Model Editing via Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36748)
*Dahyun Jung,Suhyune Son,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GLIME 提出一种终身知识编辑框架，通过偏好优化、重放编辑和梯度约束提升编辑知识的泛化能力，同时保持模型通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在终身/持续知识编辑场景下，现有编辑方法容易过拟合目标提示，导致编辑知识的泛化能力下降，同时损害模型原有的通用能力。

Method: GLIME 在终身知识编辑框架中，通过偏好优化（Preference Optimization）对生成行为进行优化，使得模型在编辑后不仅能够正确回答目标提示，还能泛化到相关提示。同时，采用基于重放（replay-based editing）的策略，在更新新知识时回顾历史编辑样本，以缓解对先前编辑知识的遗忘。此外，引入梯度约束（gradient constraint）限制参数更新幅度，进一步保护通用能力。输入为待编辑的知识三元组及提示，模型依次经过编辑模块、重放缓冲区采样和偏好优化目标训练，推理时直接调用编辑后的模型生成回答。各个模块共同解决终身编辑中的泛化与遗忘问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将知识编辑与偏好优化相结合，优化生成行为以提升泛化；引入基于重放的编辑机制以保留先前编辑的知识；设计梯度约束以缓解灾难性遗忘

Abstract: Knowledge editing enables rapid updates of specific factual knowledge in large language models (LLMs) without full retraining. However, more realistic scenarios call for a lifelong framework that handles continual updates rather than one-off modifications. In such settings, existing editing methods often overfit to target prompts, significantly degrading both the generalization of the edited knowledge and the model's general capabilities. To address this issue, we propose GLIME (Generalizable Lifelong Model Editing), which combines knowledge editing with preference optimization over generation behavior. GLIME further incorporates replay-based editing and a gradient constraint to preserve previously edited knowledge. Experimental results show that GLIME significantly improves knowledge generalization in lifelong editing settings while maintaining both editing performance and general capabilities.

</details>


### [84] [ReMem: Rethinking Perception and Memory in Long-Context Recommendation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37311)
*Haohao Qu,Yongcheng Jing,Chun Hin Chan,Shanru Lin,Wenqi Fan,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReMem提出了一种结合OCR多模态感知与动态记忆的推荐智能体框架，通过分块记忆更新和多记忆GRPO训练，在搜索、排序、判断任务上平均提升5.16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐智能体存在两个关键局限：基于噪声异构HTML页面的脆弱物品感知，以及长上下文推理效率低下。

Method: ReMem是一个推荐智能体框架，其核心架构包含多模态感知模块和动态记忆模块。输入为用户历史交互序列和当前物品页面截图；感知模块通过OCR工具将截图转换为结构化文本信息，替代原始HTML解析，实现平台无关的类人感知。动态记忆模块采用分块顺序更新策略：智能体顺序处理长上下文，维护一个固定大小的记忆缓存，仅保留信息量大的历史交互，从而以线性推理复杂度处理任意长上下文，且不干扰标准自回归生成。训练阶段采用多记忆GRPO变体，将最终答案的奖励优势传播到所有中间对话轮次，以优化动态记忆指令。推理时，智能体根据当前记忆和感知信息生成推荐动作（搜索、排序或判断）。整体架构旨在解决长上下文推荐中的感知脆弱性和推理效率问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均性能提升", "value": "5.16%", "setting": "在三个数据集上，针对搜索、排序、判断三项推荐智能体任务，对比最先进基线模型。"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于OCR的多模态感知：通过截图和OCR提取结构化信息，实现平台无关的类人感知。；分块顺序记忆更新策略：选择性维护固定大小记忆，以线性复杂度处理任意长上下文，不依赖外部记忆模块。；多记忆GRPO变体：将最终答案优势传播至所有中间对话，增强动态记忆指令。

Abstract: Recent Recommendation Agents (RecAgents) offer a promising alternative by shifting recommendation to an active, user-side paradigm, where generative agents autonomously perceive external platforms, reason over user preferences, and execute decisions. However, existing RecAgents still suffer from two critical limitations: brittle item perception based on noisy and heterogeneous item pages, and inefficient long-context reasoning over extended user histories and multi-step interaction traces. To address these challenges, we propose a novel recommendation agent framework, termed as ReMem, that combines OCR-based multimodal perception with time-evolving dynamic memory. Instead of parsing raw HTML, ReMem observes item pages through screenshots and extracts structured multimodal information via an OCR tool, enabling a more humanoid and platform-agnostic perception mechanism. To support long-horizon preference modeling, ReMem further introduces a chunk-wise sequential memory update strategy, where the agent selectively maintains a fixed-size memory of informative historical interactions while processing arbitrarily long contexts with linear inference complexity and bounded context length. This design allows the agent to preserve evolving user preferences without relying on external memory modules or disrupting the standard autoregressive generation process. To enhance the dynamic memory instruction, we further develop a multi-memory GRPO variant, which propagates the final-answer advantage to all intermediate conversations that contribute to the final response. Extensive experiments on three datasets demonstrate that ReMem consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving an average improvement of 5.16\% across three recommendation agent tasks, namely searching, ranking, and judging.

</details>


### [85] [SIPO: Unifying Reinforcement Learning with On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36742)
*Zhenrui Yue,Huimin Zeng,Yueqi Wang,Yaokun Liu,Fengran Mo,Jinghan Zhang,Mung Yao Jia,Gyuseok Lee,Yang Zhang,Na Wei,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIPO 提出一种统一的强化学习与在线自蒸馏方法，通过对比自教师提供 token 级密集信用，在多个推理和代码生成基准上超越 RLVR 和 OPSD 基线，且无需外部教师或额外生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习与可验证奖励稀疏，缺乏 token 级信用分配；已有的在线自蒸馏因自教师过度自信并对长推理轨迹施加过度惩罚而表现不佳。

Method: SIPO 在每次迭代中，对每个 prompt 从当前策略采样多个 rollout，并用环境奖励对它们打分。随后为每个 rollout 构造两种教师上下文：一种将参考答案与组内错误配对，另一种类似；模型在这两种上下文下重新评估自身响应，利用两种上下文下教师对数概率的差值作为 token 级反馈，期望抵消两个上下文共享的偏差。目标函数为每个 rollout 产生 token 级优势：奖励仍设定每次更新的主方向，而自教师将信用重新分配到各个 token 上。即使在所有 rollout 都失败、组相对优势消失的组中，SIPO 仍能提供学习信号。该方法保留了对任务奖励的直接优化，同时提供密集的 token 级反馈，从而桥接了强化学习与在线自蒸馏。推理时无需外部教师或额外生成。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出对比自教师：为每个 rollout 构造参考答案与组内错误配对两种教师上下文，利用两种上下文中教师对数概率之差作为 token 级反馈以抵消共享偏差。；设计自指导策略优化目标：奖励设定更新主方向，自教师重新分配 token 级信用，且在全失败组中仍能提供学习信号。；无需外部教师或额外生成，统一了强化学习与在线自蒸馏。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard paradigm for improving large language models (LLMs) on various tasks, yet its sparse outcome rewards lack token-level credit assignment for intermediate steps. To address this, on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) leverages a self-teacher with privileged context to provide additional dense learning signals. However, because the self-teacher is often overconfident and imposes excessive penalties on long reasoning trajectories, OPSD frequently struggles in practice. To mitigate this, we propose self-instructing policy optimization (SIPO) with a contrastive self-teacher to provide dense credit. At each iteration, SIPO samples multiple rollouts per prompt from the current policy, scores them with environment rewards, and constructs two teacher contexts for each rollout by pairing the reference answer with mistakes made within the group. The model then re-evaluates its own responses under both contexts, using the difference between the two teacher log-probabilities as token-level feedback, so that biases shared by both contexts are expected to largely cancel. The resulting objective yields a token-level advantage for every rollout: the reward still sets the main direction of each update while the self-teacher redistributes credit across tokens. Even in groups where every rollout fails and group-relative advantages vanish, SIPO still provides a learning signal. By preserving direct optimization of the task reward while providing dense, token-level feedback, this approach bridges reinforcement learning and on-policy self-distillation. Extensive experiments across multiple reasoning and code-generation benchmarks demonstrate that SIPO outperforms both RLVR and OPSD baselines without an external teacher or additional generation.

</details>


### [86] [JudgeProfile: Understanding and Steering Subjectivity in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36705)
*Qi Cao,Kangning Liu,Xuan Kan,Shunwen Tan,Yang Pei,Dake Chen,Yatai Ji,Zixuan Ye,Yuanpeng Tu,Daniel Li,Junbiao Tang,Pengtao Xie,Zihao He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JudgeProfile将LLM评判器的主观性解耦为属性感知与优先级权重，构建了包含50,013对、21个评判器、87个属性的SubjectiveSet数据集，发现评判器在感知上存在隐藏共识但优先级不同；通过学习参考标签的属性权重，可将与参考标签的一致率从66.48%提升至71.97%，优于微调和提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判器在进行成对比较时具有主观性，即使两个回答都没有明显错误也会偏好不同；如何理解并调控这种主观性，使评判结果可适配目标评估标准。

Method: JudgeProfile将LLM评判过程分解为感知和优先级两个阶段。感知阶段：给定一对回答和一组具体属性（如清晰度、正确性、细节），每个LLM评判器对每个属性分别判断哪个回答更好，产生属性级比较结果。优先级阶段：每个属性对最终成对选择的贡献由一组权重决定，这些权重可从评判器自身的整体选择中估计，从而刻画该评判器的主观偏好。基于该分解，论文构建SubjectiveSet数据集：从17个公开数据源收集50,013个回答对，由21个LLM评判器在87个属性上进行评估，得到属性级感知与整体选择数据。分析发现，不同评判器在属性感知上经常一致，但整体选择因优先级权重不同而分歧。为解决主观性，论文学习新权重：以参考标签为目标，优化属性权重以使加权后的感知判断与参考标准一致。该方法仅调整优先级权重，不改变评判器对属性的感知。在held-out集合上，重加权后与参考标签的平均一致率从66.48%提升至71.97%，超过微调和rubric提示基线。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "held-out与参考标签的平均一致率", "value": "从66.48%提升至71.97%", "setting": "在SubjectiveSet上，通过学习新属性权重对感知属性重加权，优于微调和rubric提示"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出JudgeProfile框架，将LLM评判解耦为感知（对具体属性的比较判断）和优先级（各属性对最终选择的影响权重）两阶段。；构建SubjectiveSet数据集：来自17个公开数据源的50,013个回答对，由21个LLM评判器在87个属性上评估，用于研究评判器主观性。；发现评判器在属性感知上存在隐藏共识，即使整体选择分歧；证明优先级权重因评判器而异，且可从其自身整体选择中估计。；提出从参考标签学习新属性权重的重加权方法，使评判决策适配目标评估标准，无需微调。

Abstract: LLM judges are inherently subjective, often favoring different responses in pairwise comparison when neither option is objectively wrong. To study this subjectivity, we introduce JudgeProfile, a framework that dissects LLM evaluation into perception (how a judge compares two responses across specific attributes like clarity, correctness, and detail) and prioritization (how much each attribute influences the final choice). We curate SubjectiveSet, a dataset of 50,013 response pairs from 17 public data sources, evaluated by 21 LLM judges across 87 attributes. We find a hidden consensus in perception: judges frequently agree on attribute judgments even when their overall choices diverge. Building on this separation, we first characterize each judge's prioritization using attribute weights estimated from its own overall choices. These weights differ across judges even when estimated from the same attribute judgments. We then learn new weights from reference labels to adapt their decisions to a target evaluation standard. Reweighting perceived attributes improves average held-out agreement with reference labels from 66.48% to 71.97%, outperforming fine-tuning and rubric prompting. Our findings show that understanding and steering the subjectivity of LLM judges requires attention not only to what they perceive, but also to how they prioritize it.

</details>


### [87] [MLToolBench: Learning Tool-Augmented Agents for Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36679)
*Xin Yu,Lizhu Zhang,Jiamu Bai,Yanhong Wu,Zellux Wang,Serena Li,Weiwei Li,Lingzhou Xue,Xiangjun Fan,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ToolMLBench工具套件与SFT+RL训练流水线，并用SPICE过程奖励（基于特权上下文对工具动作似然的变化）解决诊断工具调用奖励稀疏问题。在80个合成任务上训练，25个域内和10个域外任务上评测，Qwen3-8B域内成功率从24.8%提升至52.4%，Qwen3.5-35B-A3B从35.6%提升至69.2%，域外从31%提升至48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML实验成本高，合成环境虽降低代价但数据与实验设置差异需要任务特定诊断；仅提供诊断工具接口不能保证智能体学会何时使用工具以及如何根据诊断结果行动，且由于诊断调用的价值取决于后续决策，最终结果奖励难以有效指导哪些工具调用应被强化。

Method: 论文提出ToolMLBench，一个包含可执行工具的套件，用于机器学习开发中的三类操作：数据检查、代码验证和实验诊断。智能体在合成ML任务环境中运行，可调用这些诊断工具获取证据。训练分为SFT和RL两个阶段：先进行监督微调，再通过强化学习优化工具使用策略。由于诊断调用的价值依赖于后续决策，最终任务结果对单个工具调用的奖励信号稀疏，论文引入SPICE奖励机制。SPICE衡量特权上下文（privileged context）如何改变某个被采样工具动作的似然，并将这种似然差异作为回合级（turn-level）奖励，与最终结果奖励结合，从而强化那些在正确上下文中被正确选择的诊断调用。评测时在25个域内任务和10个域外任务上测试，比较不同规模模型（Qwen3-8B、Qwen3.5-35B-A3B）在仅提供工具接口与描述时的表现，以及经过完整训练流水线后的表现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "域内成功率", "value": "24.8% -> 52.4%", "setting": "Qwen3-8B，使用相同诊断接口，训练流水线（SFT+RL）后，在25个域内任务上评测"}, {"metric": "域内成功率", "value": "35.6% -> 69.2%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，使用相同诊断接口，训练流水线（SFT+RL）后，在25个域内任务上评测"}, {"metric": "域外成功率", "value": "31% -> 48%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，训练流水线后，在10个域外任务上评测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: ToolMLBench：一套可执行工具套件，用于数据检查、代码验证和实验诊断，并配套SFT与RL训练流水线学习工具使用。；SPICE：通过衡量特权上下文如何改变采样工具动作的似然，将这种差异作为回合级奖励，与最终结果奖励一起使用，以强化有价值的诊断调用。
