Abstract: Machine learning engineering (MLE) agents have made substantial progress, but learning through ML experimentation remains costly in time and computation. Synthetic environments reduce these costs while introducing variations in data and experimental settings that require task-specific diagnosis. Access to diagnostic tools alone does not ensure that agents learn when to use them or how to act on their findings. We introduce ToolMLBench, a suite of executable tools for data inspection, code verification, and experiment diagnosis, together with an SFT and RL pipeline for learning their use. Diagnostic calls acquire evidence whose value depends on subsequent decisions, so final outcomes provide limited guidance on which calls to reinforce. We address this challenge with SPICE, which measures how privileged context changes the likelihood of a sampled tool action and uses this difference as a turn-level reward alongside the final outcome. We train on 80 synthetic tasks and evaluate on 25 in-domain and 10 out-of-domain tasks. Providing tool interfaces and descriptions alone yields inconsistent gains across unadapted models. With the same diagnostic interface, our training pipeline raises in-domain success from 24.8% to 52.4% for Qwen3-8B and from 35.6% to 69.2% for Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. The latter also improves from 31% to 48% out-of-domain, supporting learned diagnostic tool use on held-out sources and targets.

</details>


### [88] [FineSID: Scalable and Efficient Semantic Identifier Learning for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36670)
*Song-Li Wu,Weinan Gan,Zhaocheng Du,Xianquan Wang,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FineSID 提出一种软可微的量化框架，向整个码本传播梯度，解决生成式推荐中 Top-1 硬分配导致的梯度稀疏、码本利用不足和 SID 冲突问题，在多个公开基准上提升码本利用率和推荐准确率，且对初始化配置鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Top-1 硬分配的向量量化方法导致梯度稀疏，码本利用率低、SID 冲突严重，且依赖启发式初始化或事后冲突解决，破坏端到端语义对齐。

Method: 论文提出 FineSID 量化框架，用于生成式推荐的语义标识符（SID）学习。输入为物品的语义嵌入表示，经过量化模块生成离散码字，进而组合为 SID。与标准 Top-1 硬分配不同，FineSID 在训练时以软可微的方式将梯度传播至整个码本中的所有码字，而非仅更新被选中的单个码字，从而促进码本的全局平衡优化。该设计旨在严格保持语义一致性，缓解因梯度稀疏导致的码本利用不足和 SID 冲突。推理阶段，物品通过量化模块获得离散表示并映射为语义标识符。摘要未披露具体模块细节、训练目标或推理流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 FineSID 统一量化框架，用软可微方式向整个码本传播梯度，替代 Top-1 硬分配。；实现全局平衡的码本优化，同时严格保持语义一致性，缓解 SID 冲突并稳定大码本训练。；设计对初始化配置鲁棒、无需复杂初始化先验的 SID 学习方案。

Abstract: A critical prerequisite of generative recommendation is designing semantic identifiers (SIDs) that are both scalable to large item sets and efficiently learnable. Existing SID learning methods fundamentally rely on Top-1 hard assignment during vector quantization. While heuristic strategies -- such as clustering-based initialization or forced post-hoc collision resolution -- can artificially inflate codebook coverage, they often disrupt end-to-end semantic alignment and fail to address the underlying optimization bottleneck: sparse gradient propagation. In standard Top-1 assignment, gradients concentrate on a narrow subset of frequently selected codewords, leaving the majority inherently under-trained and causing severe SID collisions. To overcome this limitation natively without relying on complex initialization priors, we propose FineSID, a unified quantization framework that moves beyond Top-1 assignment by enabling fine-grained gradient propagation across the entire codebook. Instead of updating only a single selected codeword, FineSID distributes learning signals to all codewords in a soft, differentiable manner. This design promotes globally balanced codebook optimization while strictly preserving semantic consistency, effectively alleviating SID collisions and stabilizing training in large, high-dimensional codebooks. Extensive experiments on multiple public benchmarks demonstrate that FineSID is robust to initialization configurations and consistently improves both codebook utilization and recommendation accuracy. Our work provides a principled, initialization-agnostic solution for semantic identifier learning, advancing the practicality of generative recommendation.

</details>


### [89] [FairDiff: Mitigating the Self-Reinforcing Matthew Effect in Diffusion Recommender Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36671)
*Song-Li Wu,Xianquan Wang,Zhaocheng Du,Weinan Gan,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FairDiff 揭示了扩散推荐模型通过生成动力学自我强化流行度偏差的机制，并提出即插即用的推理时重加权（PCG）与一步最优传输校准（SC）模块，在保持推荐性能的同时缓解马太效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散推荐模型（DRM）在生成过程中通过两种机制自我强化流行度偏差：优化损失被高频物品主导，以及前向终端分布与标准高斯先验的结构性错配导致反向采样轨迹坍缩至热门物品，抑制长尾物品生成。

Method: FairDiff 是一个即插即用的公平性感知扩散推荐框架，用于缓解扩散推荐模型中的自我强化马太效应。输入为用户-物品交互数据。架构包含两个核心模块：Popularity Condition Guidance (PCG) 和 Semantic Calibration (SC)。PCG 在推理阶段作为分布重加权机制，不改变训练目标，而是通过数学方式重塑基于分数的梯度场，对高流行度区域进行惩罚，引导反向采样轨迹偏向长尾语义。SC 模块则针对前向终端分布与标准高斯先验之间的结构性错配，利用一步最优传输对齐前向和反向分布，从而弥合先验错配。整体推理流程为：从标准高斯先验采样初始噪声，在反向扩散过程中应用 PCG 修正梯度场，同时 SC 模块校准分布，最终生成推荐物品。训练目标与标准扩散推荐模型一致，PCG 和 SC 仅在推理时生效。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Popularity Condition Guidance (PCG)：推理时分布重加权机制，重塑基于分数的梯度场以惩罚高流行度区域并引导轨迹走向长尾语义；Semantic Calibration (SC) 模块：通过一步最优传输对齐前向与反向分布，弥合先验错配

Abstract: While the "Matthew Effect" and filter bubbles are widely recognized outcome-level biases in recommender systems, we reveal that Diffusion Recommender Models (DRMs) uniquely compound this issue through their generative dynamics. Rather than merely inheriting data imbalances, DRMs trigger a self-reinforcing amplification of popularity bias. We identify that this phenomenon is driven by two compounding mechanisms. First, while optimization loss is universally dominated by high-frequency items across recommenders, DRMs suffer from a unique structural prior mismatch during generation. Because the forward terminal distribution of long-tailed data deviates significantly from the standard Gaussian prior, reverse sampling trajectories inherently collapse toward high-density popular items, fundamentally suppressing niche item generation. To dismantle this self-reinforcing loop, we propose FairDiff, a plug-and-play fairness-aware diffusion framework. To overcome the popularity-dominated loss, we introduce Popularity Condition Guidance (PCG). Rather than altering the training objective, PCG acts as an inference-time distributional reweighting mechanism, mathematically reshaping the score-based gradient field to penalize high-popularity regions and guide trajectories toward niche semantics. Furthermore, we design a Semantic Calibration (SC) Module to bridge the prior mismatch, aligning the forward and reverse distributions via one-step optimal transport. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that FairDiff achieves state-of-the-art performance while effectively mitigating the self-reinforcing Matthew Effect, highlighting its value as a general framework for DRMs.

</details>


### [90] [RankBuffer: Efficient Ranking-Based Rewards for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36652)
*Zixuan Yang,Yiqun Chen,Qi Liu,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Liyi Chen,Qimeng Wang,Yan Gao,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RankBuffer 通过维护可重用有序响应对缓冲区，以粗判断插入加局部细排序的方式，高效构建基于排序的奖励，在开放生成任务上优于点式基线，并显著降低判断成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放生成任务缺乏标准答案，点式奖励难以校准；基于排序的奖励虽更合适，但现有方法需大量成对判断，成本高昂。

Method: RankBuffer 维护一个有序的、查询特定的已判断响应对缓冲区，作为可重用的质量标尺。对于新 rollout，首先通过独立的粗粒度判断将其插入锚点区间；随后仅对分配到同一区间的 rollout 进行局部细排序。将局部排序结果与全局锚点结合，得到完整排序，进而转换为有界排名奖励。缓冲区通过边界扩展、局部精炼和无效锚点修剪随策略演化自适应调整。最终排名奖励用于基于组的强化学习训练。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能", "value": "在四个开放生成基准上一致优于所有点式基线，与最强基于排序的奖励基线性能几乎相当但大幅降低判断成本", "setting": "四个开放生成基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 维护一个有序的、查询特定的已判断响应对缓冲区，作为可重用质量标尺；先通过独立粗判断将新响应插入锚点区间，仅同区间内进行局部细排序，大幅减少成对比较；通过边界扩展、局部精炼和无效锚点修剪使缓冲区随策略演化自适应；将完整排序转化为有界排名奖励

Abstract: Open-ended generation lacks canonical answers, making pointwise rewards difficult to calibrate for group-based reinforcement learning. Directly ranking same-query rollouts provides a more suitable relative reward signal, but existing ranking-based reward methods can incur substantial judging cost. We introduce RankBuffer, which maintains an ordered, query-specific buffer of previously judged responses as a reusable quality scale. Each rollout is first inserted into an anchor interval through an independent coarse judgment, after which only rollouts assigned to the same interval undergo local fine ranking. The resulting complete order is converted into bounded rank rewards, while boundary expansion, local refinement, and inactive-anchor pruning adapt the buffer as the policy evolves. Across four open-ended benchmarks, RankBuffer consistently outperforms all pointwise baselines. It also achieves nearly on-par performance with the strongest ranking-based reward baseline while substantially reducing judging cost. Ablations demonstrate the importance of both local fine ranking and anchor response content, while buffer analyses show that rollout-derived anchors progressively extend and refine the covered quality scale. These results establish response reuse as an effective approach to efficient relative reward construction.

</details>


### [91] [Semantic Projection for Continual Self-Evolution of Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36626)
*Ziyu Liu,Jun Chen,Lixu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出语义投影自进化（SSPE），将梯度投影思想从参数空间迁移到行为空间，让语言智能体的共享自然语言技能在非平稳异构任务流中持续演化而不遗忘旧能力；在合成流和真实智能体基准上优于强基线，且技能迁移到不同执行模型后仍保持最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享自然语言技能在非平稳、异构任务流中反复修订时，新任务的改进会覆盖旧任务所需的过程，导致能力遗忘；而自然语言技能修订既无梯度也无规范向量空间，无法直接应用正交梯度下降（OGD）进行投影。

Method: SSPE 的端到端架构如下：输入为共享的自然语言技能定义、一个非平稳且异构的任务流，以及每个任务上的执行反馈（观察到的增益与回退）。首先，对每个新任务，演化模型基于当前技能提出一个无约束的技能修订，作为“提议更新”。然后，SSPE 将该提议更新与已获得的能力进行比对，识别出可能发生干扰（即新修订会损害旧任务表现）的已获能力。与 OGD 在参数空间将新任务梯度投影到局部保持先前预测的子空间不同，自然语言技能没有梯度也没有规范向量空间，因此 SSPE 在行为空间构建兼容修订：它利用在任务流上实际观察到的增益（新任务性能提升）与回退（旧任务性能下降）作为信号，对提议修订进行约束或调整，从而生成一个既能吸收新能力又尽量不破坏旧能力的修订版本。该过程不需要向演化模型暴露语义领域身份，使同一共享技能能够在潜在和反复出现的任务上下文中持续演化。训练/优化目标可理解为在行为空间上最大化跨域最终能力并最小化遗忘，通过增益与回退信号引导修订方向。推理/服务流程中，更新后的技能被直接用于下游执行智能体；论文还报告了将演化后的技能迁移到不同执行器模型后仍保持最强平均性能，表明技能具有可迁移性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Semantic-Scope Projected Evolution (SSPE)，将梯度投影的功能原理从参数空间迁移到行为空间。；将无约束的技能修订视为候选更新，识别可能干扰的已获能力，并利用观察到的增益与回退构造兼容修订，而非简单拒绝更新。；使同一共享技能能在潜在和反复出现的任务上下文中演化，且不向演化模型暴露语义领域身份。

Abstract: Language-model agents increasingly rely on persistent natural-language skills to adapt beyond their frozen model parameters. When a shared skill is repeatedly revised from a non-stationary, heterogeneous task stream, however, improvements for new tasks can overwrite procedures needed for earlier ones. In continual learning, Orthogonal Gradient Descent (OGD) addresses analogous interference by projecting a new-task gradient onto a subspace that locally preserves prior predictions. Natural-language skill revisions, however, have neither gradients nor a canonical vector space in which such a projection can be performed. We introduce \emph{Semantic-Scope Projected Evolution} (SSPE), which transfers the functional principle of gradient projection from parameter space to behavior space. SSPE treats an unconstrained skill revision as a proposed update, identifies acquired capabilities with which it may interfere, and uses the observed gains and regressions to construct a compatible revision rather than merely rejecting the update. This enables one shared skill to evolve across latent and recurring task contexts without exposing semantic domain identities to the evolution model. Across controlled synthetic streams and heterogeneous real-agent benchmarks, SSPE improves final cross-domain competence and mitigates forgetting relative to strong skill-evolution baselines. The evolved skill also retains the strongest average performance after transfer to a different executor model. These results establish semantic projection as a promising principle for stable and adaptive self evolution of language agents.

</details>


### [92] [Transformers Stop Thinking Too Early, and a Tiny LoRA Fixes It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36585)
*Zehao Jin,Ruixuan Deng,Junran Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在单个早期层注入rank-8 LoRA并冻结全部预训练权重，即可让Transformer在上下文引用跟随任务中利用更深计算，Qwen3-8B精确准确率从15.5%升至99%，并揭示中间层"relay"机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer在上下文引用跟随任务中只使用了很浅的深度，额外循环层收益有限；如何用极小的参数编辑（单个早期层的rank-8 LoRA）大幅扩展模型可访问的推理深度。

Method: 方法在冻结的预训练Transformer上工作。输入为包含引用链的上下文（如程序行序列）；推理时，在单个早期层注入一个rank-8 LoRA适配器，基础模型全部权重保持冻结。该LoRA在中间层的短范围内启动一个"relay"：每一程序行通过其链身份将信息传递给后续层。随后冻结的模型head（输出头）逐步读取链上更远位置。训练目标为任务特定的精确准确率（如跟随引用链）。通过消融实验移除parent-line attention，relay机制被破坏，说明该注意力路径是深度扩展的关键。此外提出一种冻结模型测量方法，可在四个held-out模型中的三个中定位最后一个有效干预层。推理时直接使用训练好的LoRA，无需修改基础权重，支持通过额外循环层进一步扩展链长。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "exact accuracy on 24-line chains", "value": "15.5% -> 99%", "setting": "Qwen3-8B base model, task-trained rank-8 LoRA at one early layer, all weights frozen"}, {"metric": "supported chain length", "value": "50 lines", "setting": "longer-trained LoRA on Qwen3-8B"}, {"metric": "supported chain length", "value": "60 lines after four loops, at least 160 after eight", "setting": "Ouro-1.4B"}, {"metric": "last useful intervention layer localization", "value": "within tolerance in 3 of 4 held-out models", "setting": "frozen-model measurement"}, {"metric": "MuSiQue performance", "value": "improved", "setting": "task-specific LoRAs"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在单个早期层注入rank-8 LoRA并冻结全部基础权重，以极低成本扩展计算深度；发现LoRA启动"relay"机制：程序行通过链身份在中间层短范围内传递，冻结的head逐步读取链上更远位置；通过移除parent-line attention验证relay机制，并给出定位最后一个有效干预层的冻结模型测量方法

Abstract: Pretrained transformers use little of their depth to follow references in context. Thirteen base models reliably follow only 1.4-3.6 lines, and extra pretrained loops add little. A task-trained rank-8 LoRA at one early layer extends this computation with all model weights frozen. Qwen3-8B improves from 15.5% to 99% exact accuracy on 24-line chains; a longer-trained LoRA reaches 50 lines. Ouro-1.4B reaches 60 lines after four loops and at least 160 after eight. The LoRA starts a relay: program lines pass on their chain identity through a short range of middle layers. Frozen heads read progressively further up the chain, and removing parent-line attention stops the relay. A frozen-model measurement locates the last useful intervention layer within tolerance in three of four held-out models. Task-specific LoRAs also improve MuSiQue. Default answers therefore understate the computation accessible through a tiny edit. Code and an interactive demo are available at this https URL

</details>


### [93] [SAKI: Maximal-Coupling-Routed Teacher Supervision for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36601)
*Miteto Wei,Xiaohan Wang,Zehao Chen,Jiajun Chai,Sichao Liu,Li Wang,Haoyuan Xu,Zhaoyu Hu,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAKI提出了一种新的on-policy蒸馏方法，通过KL约束的教师引导rollout和最大耦合机制，动态路由token级监督，并实现引擎驻留推测验证器提升吞吐4.22倍，在数学推理基准上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation中弱学生模型可能访问与教师不一致的前缀，导致监督信号不具代表性，需在rollout偏差和干预频率之间取得平衡，并高效实现。

Method: SAKI是一个用于on-policy蒸馏的框架，核心包括三个阶段：(1) 教师引导的rollout生成：学生模型生成轨迹时，引入KL约束使得学生分布向教师分布靠近，并通过最大耦合实现接受/纠正事件。具体地，在每一步t，学生分布p_t和教师分布q_t之间进行最大耦合，以概率min(1, p_t(x)/q_t(x))接受学生采样token x，否则以概率TV(p_t, q_t)纠正为教师最高概率token。该机制保证纠正概率恰好为TV(p_t, q_t)，且信任域半径ε同时控制rollout偏差和干预频率。(2) 监督路由：接受位置使用采样token的reverse-KL监督，纠正位置则直接使用教师最高概率token的交叉熵监督。这种路由根据是否发生纠正事件自适应地分配token级监督信号。(3) 引擎驻留的推测验证器：为了提高rollout效率，实现了一个推测验证器，保持精确的q轨迹分布和耦合语义，在匹配负载下将rollout吞吐量提升4.22倍。整个框架在七个数学推理基准上评估，学生模型规模为1.7B和0.6B。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Mean@8", "value": "improved", "setting": "1.7B student on seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks"}, {"metric": "Pass@8", "value": "improved", "setting": "1.7B student on seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks"}, {"metric": "Mean@8", "value": "improved", "setting": "0.6B student on seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks"}, {"metric": "Pass@8", "value": "improved", "setting": "0.6B student on seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks"}, {"metric": "rollout throughput", "value": "4.22x", "setting": "matched-workload, engine-resident speculative verifier"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: SAKI方法：结合KL约束的教师引导rollout与最大耦合，复用接受/纠正事件路由token级监督。；理论分析：最大耦合下纠正概率恰好等于TV(p_t, q_t)，同一信任域半径控制rollout偏差并上界干预频率。；引擎驻留的推测验证器：保持精确q轨迹分布和耦合语义，实现匹配负载下4.22倍rollout吞吐提升。；放置控制和固定前缀分析支持纠正触发的路由作为冲突自适应监督信号。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) reduces train-test state mismatch by training a student on its own generated trajectories, but weak students may visit teacher-misaligned prefixes where supervision is less representative. We introduce SAKI (Supervision Allocation with KL-constrained Interpolation), which combines a KL-constrained teacher-guided rollout with maximal coupling and reuses realized accept/correction events to route token-level supervision. Accepted positions retain sampled-token reverse-KL supervision, while correction positions receive direct supervision on the teacher's highest-probability token. Under maximal coupling, the correction probability is exactly TV(p_t, q_t), so the same trust-region radius controls rollout deviation and upper-bounds intervention and specialized-supervision frequency. We further implement an engine-resident speculative verifier that preserves the exact-q trajectory distribution and coupling semantics while improving matched-workload rollout throughput by 4.22x. Across seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SAKI improves the matched teacher-guided baseline in Mean@8 and Pass@8 for both 1.7B and 0.6B students. Placement controls and fixed-prefix analysis further support correction-triggered routing as a conflict-adaptive supervision signal.

</details>


### [94] [SafeCoEvo: Co-Evolving Safety Harnesses and Guards for LLM Agents at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36580)
*Yu Cheng,Yongkang Hu,Shuaijie Ma,Zhihang Lin,Weicheng Meng,Jingyang Qiao,Jiuan Zhou,Yushuo Zhang,Yihang Chen,Weilin Luo,Kun Shao,Dong Li,Zhizhong Zhang,Yuan Xie,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeCoEvo 提出测试时 Harness-Guard 协同进化框架，让 LLM Agent 的安全系统能从运行时经验中持续学习。S-Harness 快速外化显式安全知识，GuardVPO 长期内化参数化风险判断，在真实部署设置下将不安全结果率降低 10.05%，任务成功率提升 12.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化方法依赖对固定任务分布的多轮优化，无法应对真实部署中仅能利用过去任务经验来提升未来未见任务安全性的测试时适配需求。

Method: SafeCoEvo 是一个测试时 Harness-Guard 协同进化框架，针对 LLM Agent 在真实部署中的安全自适应问题。其输入是 Agent 执行任务时产生的运行时经验与执行反馈，反馈仅在任务完成后可用。框架包含两个互补的安全能力提升模块：S-Harness 在短时间尺度上工作，将近期运行时经验快速外化为可更新的显式安全知识，以便立即影响后续任务的决策；GuardVPO 在长时间尺度上工作，将累积的运行时安全经验内化为参数化的风险判断能力。两者协同进化，短期快速适应与长期能力固化相结合，使外部安全系统能从累积运行时经验中持续适配未来未见任务。框架在任务执行过程中收集经验，更新显式知识与参数化能力，并用于后续任务的安全判断，从而同时提升安全性与任务效用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "不安全结果率 (unsafe outcome rate)", "value": "降低 10.05%", "setting": "相比最强基线"}, {"metric": "任务成功率 (task success rate)", "value": "提升 12.15%", "setting": "相比最强基线"}]}

Conclusion: 提出 SafeCoEvo 测试时 Harness-Guard 协同进化框架，使外部安全系统能从累积运行时经验持续适配；S-Harness 在短时间尺度上将近期运行时经验外化为可更新的显式安全知识，快速影响后续任务；GuardVPO 在长时间尺度上将累积的运行时安全经验内化为参数化风险判断能力；结合短期快速适应与长期能力固化的协同进化机制

Abstract: LLM agents deployed in real-world environments continually encounter new tasks and safety risks, while execution feedback typically becomes available only after each task is completed. However, existing self-evolving approaches commonly rely on multiple rounds of optimization over fixed and repeatedly accessible task distributions, fundamentally differing from test-time adaptation in real-world deployment, where only experience accumulated from past tasks can be used to improve safety decisions on future unseen tasks. To address this limitation, we propose SafeCoEvo, a test-time Harness-Guard co-evolution framework for LLM agent safety that enables the external safety system to continually adapt from accumulated runtime experience. SafeCoEvo jointly improves two complementary safety capabilities at different timescales: S-Harness rapidly externalizes recent runtime experience into updatable explicit safety knowledge that can promptly influence subsequent tasks, while GuardVPO internalizes accumulated runtime safety experience over a longer timescale into parametric risk-judgment capabilities. By combining short-term rapid adaptation with long-term capability consolidation, SafeCoEvo continually improves the agent's safety capabilities, reducing the unsafe outcome rate by 10.05% while improving the task success rate by 12.15% over the strongest baseline, thereby achieving simultaneous gains in safety and task utility.

</details>


### [95] [MemEvo: Automatic Discovery of Streaming Video Memory Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36581)
*Guohong Liu,Jialei Ye,Shanhui Zhao,Yunxin Liu,Yuanchun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemEvo 将流式视频理解的记忆机制设计转化为对可执行记忆程序的搜索问题，用 DSL 结构化 memory 原语并由 LLM 基于实验反馈迭代发现，最终得到免训练、有界内存机制，在 StreamingBench 与 OVO-Bench 上取得强性能并显著节省上下文与推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在查询未知的流式视频理解场景中，如何自动发现一个在因果与有界内存约束下、兼顾下游理解性能与推理效率的视觉记忆机制。

Method: 问题设定：查询不可知（query-agnostic）的流式视频理解要求视觉语言模型在未来查询出现之前，持续将无限增长的视觉流压缩为有界内存。整体框架分为离线发现阶段与在线推理阶段。

离线发现阶段：首先定义轻量领域特定语言（DSL），用结构化原语表达记忆机制，包括 representation（观测的表示方式）、admission（哪些观测进入内存）、retention（保留策略）、consolidation（记忆合并/压缩）、budgeting（内存预算控制）、retrieval（查询到来时的信息检索），并在程序语义中强制因果与有界内存约束。随后 MemEvo 以预训练 LLM 作为语义感知的提议模型，基于累积的实验反馈迭代生成并细化候选记忆程序；每个候选程序由确定性评估管线进行校验与下游执行评估（在 StreamingBench、OVO-Bench 等基准上），评估结果反馈给提议模型以驱动下一轮搜索。整个发现过程中底层视觉语言模型保持冻结。最终输出一个免训练、有界内存的记忆机制程序。

在线推理阶段：该程序对持续到达的视觉流执行表示、准入、保留、合并与预算裁剪，将历史视觉观测压缩到固定大小的内存中；当查询到达时，通过检索原语从有界内存中取出与查询相关的信息，交由冻结的视觉语言模型生成回答。由于内存有界，上下文长度与推理成本不随视频流无限增长，从而在流式视频理解性能与上下文/推理效率之间取得平衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将记忆机制设计形式化为对可执行记忆程序的搜索问题，并定义轻量领域特定语言（DSL）以结构化表示 representation、admission、retention、consolidation、budgeting、retrieval 等原语，同时强制因果与有界内存约束。；提出 MemEvo：以预训练 LLM 作为语义感知的提议模型，基于累积的实验反馈迭代生成与细化候选记忆程序，底层视觉语言模型在发现过程中保持冻结。；确定性评估管线在执行层面对每个候选程序进行校验与评估，避免不可执行或违反约束的设计。；最终产出一个免训练、有界内存的记忆机制，在 StreamingBench 与 OVO-Bench 上取得强流式视频理解性能，并显著降低上下文与推理开销。

Abstract: Query-agnostic streaming video understanding requires vision-language models to continuously compress an indefinitely growing visual stream into a bounded memory before future queries are known. The performance depends critically on the memory mechanism--what observations to preserve, how to represent and consolidate them, and what information to retrieve when a query eventually arrives. Rather than designing a single memory architecture by hand, we formulate memory design as a search problem over executable memory programs. We introduce a lightweight domain-specific language that expresses memory mechanisms through structured primitives for representation, admission, retention, consolidation, budgeting, and retrieval, while enforcing causal and bounded-memory constraints. Although structured, the derived program space remains large and contains heterogeneous, conditionally dependent design choices whose effects can only be assessed via downstream execution. We therefore propose MemEvo, an LLM-driven auto-research framework that uses pretrained LLM as a semantics-aware proposal model to iteratively generate and refine candidate memory programs based on accumulated experimental feedback. At runtime, a deterministic evaluation pipeline validates and evaluates each candidate, while the underlying vision-language model remains frozen throughout discovery. We finally produce a training-free, bounded-memory mechanism. Extensive experiments on StreamingBench and OVO-Bench demonstrate strong streaming video understanding performance together with substantial context and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [96] [Divide and Inject: Can Agents Reconstruct an Indirect Prompt Injection from Fragments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36576)
*Michael Lee,Zhipeng Wei,Yue Dong,N. Benjamin Erichson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AdaLCPI，把恶意目标拆成不完整片段散布在智能体检索到的外部内容中，并用重建线索与OpenEvolve迭代优化让智能体自行拼接出攻击目标；宏观平均攻击成功率达61.4%，显著高于Trojan Hippo-style（32.8%）与AgentVigil（30.0%），说明安全评测需要覆盖‘碎片重构’场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体通过工具检索外部内容并处理长上下文时，攻击者是否可以通过分散的不完整恶意片段，让智能体自行重构出有害目标，从而绕过依赖完整恶意指令检测的防御。

Method: AdaLCPI以智能体工作流为攻击面：攻击目标首先被切分为多个不完整的片段，这些片段被嵌入智能体通过工具检索到的外部内容中，并随检索结果进入智能体的长上下文。为促使智能体把分散片段拼回完整目标，方法在上下文中加入一个重建线索，利用智能体的长上下文推理能力完成语义组合。片段内容与重建线索随后通过OpenEvolve进行迭代优化：优化过程使用分级评分函数以及对目标智能体执行结果的自然语言反馈，逐步改进碎片表达与线索措辞，以提升目标智能体实际执行恶意目标的比例。整体流程为：目标分解→片段嵌入外部内容→工具检索回填长上下文→重建线索触发重组→目标智能体执行→基于执行反馈的进化式精炼，形成闭环自适应攻击管线。评测以宏观平均攻击成功率与Trojan Hippo-style、AgentVigil等强自适应基线对比。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Macro-average attack success rate (ASR)", "value": "61.4% vs 32.8% (Trojan Hippo-style) vs 30.0% (AgentVigil)", "setting": "针对长上下文智能体间接提示注入的对比实验，使用宏观平均ASR作为指标，与两类强自适应基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出自适应长上下文提示注入（AdaLCPI），将攻击目标拆成不完整片段分散嵌入被检索的外部内容中；设计重建线索（reconstruction cue）引导智能体把碎片组合为完整恶意目标；使用OpenEvolve对片段与线索进行迭代精炼，采用分级评分和来自目标智能体的自然语言执行反馈；将攻击评估从‘完整恶意指令注入’扩展到‘碎片重构’这一新的威胁模型

Abstract: Agentic systems are now being widely used to orchestrate tools and reason over long contexts. However, the improving capabilities of the large language models powering these agents also create new attack surfaces for indirect prompt injection. In particular, an attacker may not need to place a complete malicious instruction in retrieved content if the agent can reconstruct the objective from incomplete fragments distributed across a long context. In this work, we introduce adaptive long-context prompt injection (AdaLCPI), which combines long-context fragmentation with adaptive search. AdaLCPI splits an attack objective into incomplete fragments, embeds them in external content retrieved through the agent's tools, and uses a reconstruction cue to prompt the agent to combine them. It then iteratively refines the fragments and cue with OpenEvolve using graded scoring and natural-language execution feedback from the target agent. Empirically, AdaLCPI achieves higher attack success than strong adaptive baselines, reaching 61.4\% macro-average ASR compared with 32.8\% for Trojan Hippo-style and 30.0\% for AgentVigil. Safety evaluations should therefore test whether agents remain robust when harmful objectives must be reconstructed from incomplete fragments.

</details>


### [97] [Rethinking Reasoning Paths as Phase-Structured Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36461)
*Zhenghao He,Guangzhi Xiong,Sanchit Sinha,Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PAIR，把 LLM 推理路径视为固定问题内的相位结构化轨迹，通过归一化轨迹进度对齐变长路径，并在同问题同相位内比较成功与失败轨迹，提取相位特定的路径质量方向。实验表明标准跨问题正确性探针在同问题评估下预测能力大幅下降，而 PAIR 提升了同问题轨迹排序与 Best-of-N 选择，且相位引导具有因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理路径隐藏状态分析把每个中间状态直接赋予最终答案正确性标签，并跨异构问题训练探针，导致正确性预测可能依赖问题级差异而非路径质量，且按绝对步索引对齐的状态可能对应不同的推理功能阶段。

Method: 论文提出 PAIR（Phase-Aligned Intra-question Reasoning）。输入为 LLM 对同一问题生成的多条变长推理轨迹，以及每条轨迹对应的隐藏状态序列。架构分三步：第一，对每个问题采样多条轨迹，收集各轨迹的中间隐藏状态；第二，按归一化轨迹进度（相对位置而非绝对步索引）将变长路径映射到共享的相位空间，使不同长度轨迹中功能相近的阶段对齐；第三，仅在同一个问题、同一个相位内比较成功与失败轨迹，计算相位特定的路径质量方向，从而把路径质量信号与问题级变异隔离。训练目标并非端到端训练 LLM，而是学习可区分同问题内好坏轨迹的相位级方向（类似探针/线性方向）。推理或使用时，这些方向可用于同问题内轨迹排序与 Best-of-N 选择；相位引导实验进一步将学到的方向注入生成过程以改变输出，验证其因果相关性。论文未披露隐藏状态的具体层、维度或模型参数规模。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "同问题轨迹排序与 Best-of-N 轨迹选择", "value": "PAIR 在多个模型与基准上优于基线（具体数值未在摘要中披露）", "setting": "within-question 评估，多模型多基准"}, {"metric": "标准跨问题正确性探针在同问题评估下的预测能力", "value": "大幅下降", "setting": "within-question 评估对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将推理路径视为固定问题内的相位结构化轨迹，用归一化轨迹进度把变长路径映射到共享的相对相位；PAIR 方法：同一问题、同一相位内比较成功与失败轨迹，提取相位特定的路径质量方向，从而剥离问题级变异；实验证明跨问题正确性探针在同问题评估下预测能力大幅下降，揭示此类探针依赖问题级信息；相位引导（phase-wise steering）改变生成结果，为所学方向捕捉轨迹相关信息提供因果证据

Abstract: Large language models often improve problem-solving performance by generating multi-step reasoning paths, yet how to analyze the hidden states along these paths remains unclear. Existing approaches typically assign each intermediate state the final-answer correctness label and train probes across heterogeneous questions. We argue that this protocol obscures reasoning dynamics in two ways: (1) correctness prediction can exploit question-level variation rather than path quality, and (2) states aligned by absolute step indices may correspond to different functional phases of reasoning. In this work, we propose to view reasoning paths as phase-structured trajectories within fixed questions. We instantiate this view as PAIR, short for Phase-Aligned Intra-question Reasoning. PAIR samples multiple trajectories for each question, maps variable-length paths into shared relative phases based on normalized trajectory progress, and compares successful and unsuccessful trajectories only within the same question and phase. This yields phase-specific path-quality directions that better isolate path-quality signals from question-level variation. Empirically, we find that standard across-question correctness probes lose much of their predictive power under within-question evaluation, suggesting that these probes partly rely on question-level information. PAIR improves within-question trajectory ranking and Best-of-N trajectory selection across models and benchmarks. Phase-wise steering further shows that the learned directions can change generation outcomes, providing causal evidence that they capture trajectory-relevant information.

</details>


### [98] [Bits Under ZK-LLM: Evaluating Zero-Knowledge-Friendly Quantization for Verifiable Private LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36437)
*Taeung Yoon,Yupeng Zhang,Xiaojing Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首个系统研究ZK-LLM中量化友好性的工作，形式化定义ZK友好量化，并在9个LLM（含Qwen2.5-14B和MoE模型）上评估权重、激活及非线性查找表精度对模型效用和ZK证明成本的影响，揭示激活精度更敏感、RMSNorm逆平方根查找是常见瓶颈，并指出低比特量化不直接等价于ZK证明节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在零知识证明（ZK）推理场景下，缺乏对LLM友好量化的系统理解，传统低比特量化启发式方法无法直接转化为ZK证明效率，需要明确量化如何影响ZK推理的算术结构、约束复杂度和证明成本。

Method: 论文未提出新的端到端LLM架构，而是围绕现有LLM（如Qwen2.5-14B、Qwen3-30B-A3B）在零知识证明推理框架下，对量化方案进行系统评估。输入为LLM的权重、激活和用于非线性函数的查找表，输出为量化后的LLM推理结果及其对应的ZK证明。核心流程包括：1）定义ZK友好量化需满足的性质（如有限域算术操作、低约束复杂度等）；2）设计量化空间，涵盖权重精度、激活精度和非线性查找表精度；3）在多个LLM上依次应用不同量化配置，测量模型效用（如困惑度或下游准确率）和ZK证明成本（约束数量、证明时间等）；4）分析精度变化对证明效率的影响，发现激活精度和非线性查找表是主要瓶颈，特别是RMSNorm逆平方根查找；5）提出算子感知的精度选择策略，即仅对瓶颈算子提高精度以恢复效用，而非全局提高量化位宽。训练目标与优化未涉及，推理流程为量化模型在ZK协议下的证明生成，其中证明成本取决于算术操作数量和类型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次形式化定义ZK友好量化，明确其满足ZK证明生成所需的属性；在9个语言模型（包括Qwen2.5-14B和MoE模型Qwen3-30B-A3B）上系统评估权重、激活和非线性查找表精度的设计空间；发现激活精度比权重精度敏感得多，非线性查找表近似是效用退化的主要来源；识别RMSNorm逆平方根查找是多个大模型中的重复瓶颈，并通过选择性提高瓶颈精度恢复接近基线效用；证明降低比特宽度或查找表大小不一定带来成比例的端到端证明节省，表明传统低比特量化启发式不适用于ZK证明效率，并倡导算子感知的精度选择

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proofs are emerging as a promising approach for enabling private, verifiable LLM governance and auditing, where regulators, users, and auditors need to verify claims about training-data usage or LLM inference-time behavior, while model providers must protect proprietary model parameters. However, despite the growing interest in ZK-LLMs, the understanding of ZK-friendly quantization remains limited. This gap matters because in the ZK setting, quantization directly shapes the arithmetic structure, constraint complexity, and proving cost of ZK inference. ZK protocols operate over finite fields and incur costs that depend heavily on the number and type of arithmetic operations, nonlinearities, and lookup constraints. Understanding ZK-friendly quantization is therefore essential for making ZK-LLMs practical. In this work, we present the first systematic study of ZK-friendly quantization for LLMs. We first formalize the definition of ZK-friendly quantization, capturing the properties required for ZK proof generation. We then evaluate nine language models, including Qwen2.5-14B and the mixture-of-experts model Qwen3-30B-A3B, across a broad design space of weight, activation, and nonlinear lookup table precision. Our results show that activation precision is substantially more sensitive than weight precision, while nonlinear lookup approximations can become the dominant source of utility degradation. Also, we identify RMSNorm inverse-square-root lookups as a recurring bottleneck in several large models and recover near-baseline utility by selectively increasing precision only at the bottleneck. Finally, we show that reducing bit-width or lookup-table size does not necessarily yield proportional end-to-end proving savings, showing that conventional low-bit quantization heuristics do not directly translate to ZK proving efficiency and motivating operator-aware precision selection.

</details>


### [99] [Engineering Simplicity: Simple Mechanism Interfaces Steer LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36365)
*Kehang Zhu,Anand Shah,David Parkes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究交互接口与提示脚手架如何影响LLM智能体在拍卖和匹配中的决策，发现顺序拍卖接口减少出价偏离，解释规则和收益有助于决策，而规划/信念提示反而有害，且决策改善与语言化理解指标不同步，对LLM智能体评测有方法学启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何通过交互格式和文本脚手架帮助LLM智能体在拍卖与匹配等有明确规则和已知最优策略的多智能体环境中做出更好的决策，并厘清决策质量与解释质量之间的关系。

Method: 论文未提出端到端的模型架构，而是围绕LLM智能体的交互与提示设计展开实验框架。输入是拍卖或匹配的决策问题，以两种接口呈现：完整接口要求智能体一次性给出完整出价或完整排序；顺序接口则逐步呈现选择，使安全选择更易识别。在此基础上保持交互格式不变，分别加入不同推理脚手架（如规划匹配轮次、形成对对手的信念）以及不同规则描述（如列出收益 contingencies、解释诚实报价为何安全）。输出为智能体的出价或排序决策以及简短陈述计划。评估使用已知最优策略作为基准，比较出价偏离、匹配误差以及言语化的策略理解指标。整个流程没有训练阶段，推理即通过API调用四个模型家族进行提示与收集响应，属于行为实验而非系统架构创新。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将人类启发的简单性理论应用于LLM智能体决策接口设计，对比完整出价/排序接口与顺序接口；在固定交互格式下系统变化推理脚手架和规则描述，隔离不同设计因素的影响；揭示顺序响应需要不同于完整排序的误差计算方式；发现行为收益与语言化策略理解指标之间不相关，脚手架应同时通过实际选择和解释评估

Abstract: Can interaction formats and textual scaffolds help large language model (LLM) agents make better decisions, and do better decisions come with better explanations? We study these questions in auctions and matching, multi-agent environments with explicit rules and known optimal strategies. These settings let us vary how a decision problem is presented while retaining a benchmark for evaluating behavior. Drawing on human-motivated theories of simplicity, we compare interfaces that elicit a complete bid or ranking with sequential interfaces that make safe choices easier to identify. We then hold the interaction format fixed and vary reasoning scaffolds and rule descriptions. Across four model families, the ascending auction interface substantially reduces bid deviations. The matching comparison also shows why sequential responses require different error accounting from complete rankings. Laying out payoff contingencies and explaining why truth-telling is safe also improve choices, whereas prompts to plan through matching rounds or form beliefs about opponents worsen play overall. In auctions, these behavioral gains are not accompanied by corresponding improvements in measured verbal indicators of strategic understanding in the agents' short stated plans. Other prompts change those indicators without improving bids. Our findings suggest that human-motivated theories of simplicity can inform the design of decision environments for artificial agents. They also show why scaffolds should be evaluated through realized choices as well as explanations: improvements in one need not appear in the other.

</details>


### [100] [Better Nearest Neighbor Graph Indices via (Efficient) LLM-Guided Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36359)
*Fangzhou Wu,Haike Xu,Sandeep Silwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有图式ANN索引按几何关系构建、却按语义相关性评估的错配，提出LLM-Guided Graph Pruning (LGP)，用LLM推理识别并替换图中结构低价值邻居，在保留稀疏性与可导航性的同时注入语义信息。实验显示在DiskANN和HNSW等索引上端到端检索优于贪心图搜索与LLM重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图式ANN索引主要按嵌入几何关系构建，但下游检索按语义相关性评价，存在“几何-语义”错配；现有LLM重排只在查询时缓解，未解决图结构层面的问题。

Method: LGP以已有图式ANN索引（如DiskANN、HNSW）为输入，图节点对应数据集的嵌入向量（文档或图像）。框架首先识别每个节点在结构上“低价值”的邻居边，即对贪心图搜索导航贡献有限或语义信息不足的连接；随后调用LLM推理，为这些位置选择能提供有用语义信息且保持原图期望几何结构（稀疏性、可导航性）的替代邻居。被替换后的图仍保持原有图索引的导航特性，可直接用于贪心图搜索。训练/优化目标是使索引结构同时满足几何导航效率与下游查询的语义相关性。推理/服务阶段沿用原索引的贪心图搜索流程，因索引已被精炼而无需查询时额外LLM重排，从而在端到端检索中提升语义相关性。摘要未披露LLM具体类型、提示设计、训练细节、数据规模或延迟开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出LLM-Guided Graph Pruning (LGP)通用框架，用LLM推理直接精炼已有ANN图索引；识别结构上低价值的节点邻居，用LLM选择的替代邻居替换，同时保留原图的稀疏性和可导航性；在DiskANN和HNSW等主流图索引上验证可提升端到端检索，并优于vanilla贪心图搜索和LLM重排

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) is widely used for large-scale semantic search. Its indices are constructed primarily based on geometric relationships among embeddings of an input dataset (e.g., documents or images), rather than explicitly optimizing for semantic relevance. However, when using these indices for downstream query retrieval, performance is evaluated based on the semantic relevance of the retrieved results to the query. This creates a fundamental "geometry-semantic" mismatch between how the indices are constructed and how their retrieval results are evaluated. While existing LLM-based reranking methods can partially mitigate this mismatch at query time, they leave this underlying structural problem in the graph unresolved. We therefore propose LLM-Guided Graph Pruning (LGP), a general framework that addresses this mismatch directly by leveraging LLM reasoning to refine an existing ANN graph index itself. LGP identifies structurally "low-value" neighbors of nodes and replaces them with LLM-selected alternatives that provide useful semantic information while retaining desired geometric structures of the original graph, including sparsity and efficient navigability. Experiments on representative semantic retrieval benchmarks show that LGP consistently improves end-to-end retrieval performance over both vanilla greedy graph search and LLM-based reranking across widely used graph-based ANN indices such as DiskANN and HNSW.

</details>


### [101] [HyperZip: Efficient Data Compression through Personalized Diffusion LLMs with Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36357)
*Thai Nguyen,Khang Tran,NhatHai Phan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HyperZip利用扩散LLM与超网络进行高效无损数据压缩，通过多token预测提升吞吐，并用数据特定参数更新改善压缩率与速度的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM无损数据压缩受自回归解码高计算成本与低吞吐限制，且扩散式压缩中存在解码吞吐提升导致压缩率下降的权衡。

Method: HyperZip是一个基于扩散式LLM与超网络的LLM数据压缩框架。输入为待压缩数据及其上下文表示；主干采用扩散式LLM进行无损压缩解码，并利用多token预测（MTP）在一次前向中预测多个token以提升解码吞吐。为解决扩散压缩中吞吐与压缩率的权衡，框架引入超网络，以上下文表示为条件生成数据特定的参数更新，使dLLM无需微调即可适配目标数据。训练目标与优化细节未在摘要中披露。推理时，超网络根据当前数据上下文快速生成适配参数，dLLM结合MTP并行解码，从而在保持较低压缩率的同时获得高吞吐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 采用扩散式LLM（dLLM）与多token预测（MTP）加速LLM数据压缩；使用超网络从上下文表示生成数据特定的更新，在不进行昂贵微调的情况下适配目标数据；在压缩率与吞吐之间实现更优权衡

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for lossless data compression, but existing approaches are constrained by the high computational cost and low throughput of autoregressive decoding. We propose HyperZip, an efficient and scalable LLM-based compression framework that leverages diffusion-based LLMs (dLLMs) with Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) to accelerate LLM-based data compression processes. We identify a trade-off in diffusion-based compression, where increasing decoding throughput degrades the compression rate. To mitigate this trade-off, HyperZip employs a hypernetwork to generate data-specific updates from a context representation, adapting the dLLM to the target data without costly fine-tuning, resulting in a low compression rate and high throughput. Extensive experiments show that HyperZip achieves a superior trade-off between compression rate and speed compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Towards an AI Software Factory for Data Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36323)
*Anna Pavlenko,Bogdan Crivat,Brandon Haynes,Carlo Curino,Fotis Psallidas,Jaro Slawinski,Johannes Freischuetz,Laura Pereira Sanchez,Markus Weimer,Mathieu Demarne,Matthias Jasny,Mauktik Gandhi,Max Bovykin,Mirco Milletari,Purbasha Ghosh,Qiushi Bai,Raghu Ramakrishnan,Rahul Pandita,Sergiy Matusevich,Shivaram Venkataraman,Subru Krishnan,Md. Tareq Mahmood,Tiemo Bang,Venkatesh Emani,Xuan Zhao,Yiwen Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 微软提出AI SW Factory，覆盖Targeting、Coding、Reviewing、Ops全SDLC，通过开发元数据回流实现模型微调与World Model自改进；在数据系统领域的数十个内部仓库部署中，取得相对agentic coding 3倍工程效率与最高22倍Token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编码只在SDLC局部环节带来加速，受Amdahl定律限制，端到端软件开发效率提升有限；如何构建覆盖全生命周期的AI软件工厂，并利用开发过程产生的元数据实现持续自改进。

Method: AI SW Factory围绕软件开发生命周期的四个阶段构建：Targeting负责确定演化编码任务及其可度量目标；Coding由LLM/智能体执行代码修改与生成；Reviewing对代码变更进行审查；Ops负责部署与运维反馈。各阶段产生的开发元数据（metadata exhaust）被收集并回流，用于微调模型权重以及更新World Model这一丰富数据基质。World Model为任务理解、代码上下文与改进决策提供数据支撑，整体形成‘执行-度量-反馈-自改进’的闭环。论文聚焦数据系统领域，选择具有明确可度量目标的Evolutionary Coding Tasks，以爬山方式优化目标函数。推理与服务路径是在微软内部数十个仓库的规模化部署中运行，验证端到端效率与Token效率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "engineering efficiency", "value": "3x above agentic coding", "setting": "Microsoft internal scaled deployment across tens of repositories"}, {"metric": "token efficiency", "value": "up to 22x", "setting": "Microsoft internal scaled deployment across tens of repositories"}]}

Conclusion: 提出覆盖Targeting、Coding、Reviewing、Ops全SDLC的AI SW Factory框架，而非单点编码助手。；将Data Systems作为目标领域，聚焦带可度量目标的Evolutionary Coding Tasks，通过目标度量进行爬山式优化。；利用SDLC产生的metadata exhaust驱动模型权重微调与World Model更新，实现闭环自改进。；在微软内部数十个仓库进行规模化部署，并报告相对agentic coding的工程效率与Token效率收益。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding tools deliver significant acceleration of coding, but only limited impact across the end-to-end software development lifecycle (SDLC)--an Amdahl's law effect! In this paper, we discuss our progress towards building an AI SW Factory that accelerates all the stages of SDLC-Targeting, Coding, Reviewing, and Ops. The AI SW Factory produces a metadata exhaust that enables self-improvement by fine-tuning model weights and updating our World Model (a rich data substrate). We focus on Data Systems and the important class of Evolutionary Coding Tasks (i.e., those with a measurable objective to hill-climb) and report on 1) scaled deployments at Microsoft (tens of repositories) leading to 3x engineering efficiency above agentic coding and up to 22x token efficiency, and 2) several open challenges.

</details>


### [103] [PILLAR: Private Inverted-Index Lexical Lookup for Augmented Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36326)
*Truong Son Nguyen,Daniel Blackley,Ni Trieu,Evgenios M. Kornaropoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PILLAR提出隐私保护RAG的两阶段私有混合检索：稀疏BM25+PIR过滤候选，再本地稠密重排。PILLAR-Bin以单轮哈希分箱换取低延迟，PILLAR-Tree用不经意树遍历结合cuckoo哈希在更低延迟下取得最高检索质量，避免私有稠密检索的大量PIR轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私保护RAG(PPRAG)仅依赖稠密检索，将近似最近邻搜索转化为大量依赖查询的多轮PIR请求，导致延迟高、检索质量受限；需要在服务器不获知查询词项与访问模式的前提下，实现高质量、低延迟的私有混合检索。

Method: PILLAR在客户端与持有公开语料库的服务器之间工作。输入为用户查询，目标是在服务器不获知查询词项与访问模式的情况下取回与查询最相似的k篇文档。系统分两阶段：稀疏阶段针对预先计算好的BM25分数构建倒排索引，客户端发出少量固定数量的PIR查询，仅筛出与查询共享词项的候选文档，服务器无法得知共享的是哪些词项；稠密阶段则仅获取这些候选文档的嵌入向量，并在本地对候选进行重排序。稠密阶段不再需要私有稠密检索常见的大量PIR查询，从而降低延迟。摘要给出两种协议实例：PILLAR-Bin将posting list分箱进哈希表，为单轮设计；PILLAR-Tree把block-max pruning改造为不经意树遍历并配合cuckoo哈希表，以更低延迟取得最高检索质量。文档嵌入、BM25分数等预计算索引由服务器持有，客户端本地完成重排后得到最终k篇文档。摘要未披露训练目标、优化方法、硬件与吞吐细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "检索质量", "value": "PILLAR-Tree 达到最高检索质量（具体数值摘要未给出）", "setting": "与SOTA私有检索方案对比"}, {"metric": "延迟", "value": "PILLAR-Bin 与 PILLAR-Tree 均低于SOTA私有检索方案（具体数值摘要未给出）", "setting": "与SOTA私有检索方案对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 两阶段私有混合检索：先用固定次数PIR查询在预计算BM25分数的倒排索引上做稀疏过滤，再做稠密重排，避免稠密检索所需的大量PIR轮次；PILLAR-Bin：将posting list分箱到哈希表，单轮交互设计，延迟低于SOTA私有检索方案；PILLAR-Tree：将block-max pruning转化为不经意树遍历，结合cuckoo哈希表，在更低延迟下取得最高检索质量；服务器无法获知查询词项及其访问模式，候选文档仅共享词项而不泄露具体词项

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) hands the user's query to whoever hosts the corpus. We propose PILLAR, a Privacy-Preserving RAG (PPRAG) system based on Private Information Retrieval (PIR) in which a client utilizes the k documents most similar to their query from a server-held and publicly known corpus to respond to their query, while the server learns nothing about the query, either its terms or its access pattern. Prior PPRAG constructions rely on dense retrieval alone, translating approximate nearest-neighbor search into many query-dependent rounds of PIR, and pay for it in both latency and retrieval quality. PILLAR instead performs private hybrid retrieval in two stages. A sparse stage issues a small, fixed number of PIR queries against a carefully designed index of precomputed BM25 scores, filtering the corpus down to candidates that share terms with the query without the server ever seeing which terms these are. A dense stage then fetches only those candidates' document embeddings and re-ranks them locally, avoiding the many costly PIR queries that private dense retrieval typically requires. We instantiate PILLAR with two protocols that trade latency against retrieval quality, each built on a different private rendering of lexical search. PILLAR-Bin bins posting lists into a hash table and is a single-round design that achieves lower latency than state-of-the-art private retrieval schemes. PILLAR-Tree turns block-max pruning into an oblivious tree traversal combined with cuckoo hash tables and achieves the highest retrieval quality at lower latency than state-of-the-art schemes.

</details>


### [104] [OTROPE: Optimal Transport-based Robust Off-policy Evaluation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36264)
*Liner Xiang,Wenbo Zhang,Hengrui Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OTROPE，一种基于最优传输的无似然离策略评估方法，用于在标注有限、分布偏移且无法获取黑盒LLM似然的情况下可靠评估LLM。通过语义空间分布校正，结合残差与代理预测器实现双重鲁棒评估，并在合成与真实任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标注数据稀缺、行为策略与目标策略分布偏移、且无法获取黑盒LLM输出概率（似然）的挑战下，如何对新的目标LLM进行可靠、安全的离线策略评估。

Method: OTROPE 接收两类输入：来自行为策略的有标注样本（包含语义表示）和来自目标策略的无标注样本（仅包含语义表示）。首先，在语义空间中利用最优传输（Optimal Transport, OT）进行分布校正，将行为策略的分布对齐到目标策略分布，从而为有标注样本赋予权重。接着，使用校正后的权重计算人类标注残差（human-labeled residuals），同时训练一个代理预测器（proxy predictor）来预测评分或奖励。最后，将校正后的残差与代理预测器的预测结果结合，形成双重鲁棒风格的评估量。整个流程无需对行为策略建模，也无需估计密度比，仅依赖语义表示，支持黑盒LLM场景。理论分析部分证明了在重加权行为分布或代理预测器至少一个收敛时，该估计量具有一致性和收敛速率。推理时直接利用上述计算得到的评估量作为目标LLM的性能估计。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "评估性能", "value": "OTROPE consistently outperforms baselines", "setting": "合成数据与真实LLM评估任务"}, {"metric": "评估器集成效果", "value": "weaker LLM evaluators 的集成接近并有时超越 stronger evaluators", "setting": "真实LLM评估任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出OTROPE，一种基于最优传输的无似然离策略评估方法，在语义空间中进行分布校正以对齐有标注的行为策略样本与无标注的目标策略样本。；结合校正后的人类标注残差与代理预测器，形成无需行为策略建模和密度比估计的双重鲁棒式评估框架。；理论刻画了基线评估器在LLM分布偏移下失效的原因，并为OTROPE建立了当重加权行为分布或代理预测器收敛时的一致性及收敛速率。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of large language models (LLMs) is essential for their development and deployment, yet is often costly, risky, and difficult to perform safely online. We study off-policy evaluation for LLMs, where limited human-labeled data from a behavior model are used to evaluate a newer target LLM. This setting is challenging because labels are scarce, behavior--target distribution shift is common, and response likelihoods are often unavailable for black-box LLMs. We propose the Optimal Transport-based Robust Off-Policy Evaluation (OTROPE), a likelihood-free evaluation that performs distributional correction in a semantic space via optimal transport to align labeled behavior-policy samples with unlabeled target-policy samples. OTROPE combines corrected human-labeled residuals with proxy predictors, yielding a doubly robust-style evaluation without behavior-policy modeling or density-ratio estimation. We theoretically characterize why baseline evaluators fail under LLM distribution shift, and establish consistency and convergence rates for OTROPE when either the reweighted behavior distribution or the proxy predictor converges. Experiments on synthetic and real LLM evaluation tasks show that OTROPE consistently outperforms baselines while enabling ensembles of weaker LLM evaluators to approach and sometimes surpass stronger evaluators. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [105] [Memory Is a Derivation: The Distributed-Evidence Paradox in Long-Term Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36130)
*Hongjun Liu,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出长时程智能体把历史压缩为持久记忆时存在推导有效性问题：证据散落、压缩可能引入历史未确立的含义。作者提出 DerivAudit 审计框架，将问题拆为证据范围、组合有效性与准入可靠性三条要求，并在两类记忆语料上发现：用更广的写入前历史可恢复近 60% 看似无支持的记忆，但仍有 17-21% 无支持；且扩展证据并不能提升、在某些骨干上反而恶化写入时的准入可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体在写入持久记忆时，如何判断该记忆是否真的被写入时刻可用的交互历史所支持：相关证据可能散落在更早的交互中，压缩过程可能引入历史并未确立的新关系或事件状态，导致合法记忆因引用不全而看似无支持，或由单独成立的事实组合出历史未曾确立的更强陈述。

Method: DerivAudit 是一个针对长时程 LLM 智能体持久记忆的审计框架。其输入是写入时点可用的完整交互历史（作为证据源）以及写入方给出的记忆及其引用。框架将审计分解为三条相互关联的分析流程：(1) 证据范围审计：以写入前完整历史为证据池，判断支持某条记忆的证据是否落在写入方提供的引用之外，从而区分“真正无支持”与“仅引用不全导致看似无支持”；(2) 组合有效性审计：检查被压缩后的记忆是否在由多个已支持事实组合时引入了历史从未确立的关系、事件状态或更强断言，即组合不一定保持有效性；(3) 准入可靠性审计：评估在写入时刻，不同验证模型对无支持记忆的准入决策，并检验扩展证据输入是否改变准入结果。实验在两类自然记忆语料上进行，通过更广的写入前历史重新审计，报告可恢复支持比例与仍无支持比例，并比较不同验证模型骨干以及“扩展证据”条件下准入行为的恶化情况。论文未披露具体模型规模、训练数据、硬件或延迟成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "看似无支持记忆中恢复支持的比例", "value": "近 60%", "setting": "使用更广的写入前历史进行审计，两类自然记忆语料"}, {"metric": "扩展证据后仍无支持的记忆比例", "value": "17-21%", "setting": "使用更广的写入前历史进行审计，两类自然记忆语料"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出记忆推导问题（derivation problem）并形式化为三个耦合要求：证据范围（Evidence scope）、组合有效性（Compositional validity）、准入可靠性（Admission reliability）；提出 DerivAudit 审计框架，将审计拆分为三个问题：支持性证据是否超出写入方提供的引用、组合记忆是否引入未被支持的含义、写入时准入决策如何影响后续记忆使用；在两类自然记忆语料上量化了引用范围不足与证据扩展带来的准入副作用（扩展证据后准入可靠性反而变差）

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents compress past interactions into persistent memories that may be reused as premises for later tasks. This creates a distinct derivation problem: whether the memory actually follows from what the interaction history supports. Relevant evidence may be scattered across earlier interactions, while compression can introduce relations or event status that the history never established. A valid memory may therefore appear unsupported because its citations omit relevant evidence, while individually supported facts may be composed into a stronger statement the history never established. We characterize this problem through three coupled requirements: (1) Evidence scope; (2) Compositional validity; (3) Admission reliability. We therefore ask whether the interaction history available at write time supports what enters persistent memory. We introduce DerivAudit, a framework for auditing whether a memory is actually supported by the history available when it was written. The audit separates three questions: whether supporting evidence lies beyond writer-provided citations, whether the composed memory introduces unsupported meaning, and how write-time admission decisions affect later memory use. Across two natural memory corpora, audits using broader pre-write history recover support for nearly 60% of memories that appear unsupported from citations alone, while 17-21% remain unsupported after expansion. Yet broader evidence does not by itself make admission reliable: unsupported memories are still frequently admitted across verification models, and evidence expansion alone worsens it on two backbones.

</details>


### [106] [Principled Thoughts for Latent Recursive LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36159)
*Fahd Seddik,Fatemeh Fard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REST 提出一种表示监督训练目标，将思维表示的四属性（因果性、最小性、可分离性、稳定性）作为可微损失加入 CE，在单/多智能体潜空间推理中提升准确率与收敛性，并增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用交叉熵（CE）监督最终解码答案，不约束潜在思维表示，会导致思维崩溃、保留无关信息等失败，从而降低正确答案概率。

Method: 论文未提供详细的端到端架构描述。REST 是一种训练目标，将因果性、最小性、可分离性、稳定性四个思维表示属性转化为可微损失，与交叉熵损失相加。该目标可实例化在单智能体和多智能体潜空间系统中，智能体通过在自身隐藏状态上循环或传递隐藏状态进行推理。训练时联合优化 CE 和 REST 损失，不改变模型架构，推理时不增加参数。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "最多提升 7.5 个百分点", "setting": "7 个基准（数学、科学、医学、代码生成），与 CE-only 训练相同训练数据、计算和潜空间预算，跨智能体设置和模型规模"}, {"metric": "最终答案收敛率", "value": "提升 30%", "setting": "同上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别并验证 CE-only 训练的四个失败模式；提出将因果性、最小性、可分离性、稳定性四个思维表示属性转化为可微损失，与 CE 联合训练（REST）；在单智能体和多智能体潜空间系统中实例化 REST，无需架构改动或推理时增加参数；REST 使潜空间通信更可解释，解码潜思维能更好恢复智能体预期输出

Abstract: Large language models can reason in continuous space instead of decoded text, by recurring on their own hidden states or by passing those states between agents, while training supervises only the Cross-Entropy (CE) of the final decoded answer and does not constrain the thought. Theoretical and empirical analyses establish and confirm four failures of CE-only training that lead to a lower probability of the correct answer such as collapsing thoughts across distinct questions and retaining irrelevant information. We introduce REST (REpresentation-Supervised Thoughts), a training objective that turns four properties of a valid thought representation (causality, minimality, separability, and stability) into differentiable losses added to CE. We instantiate it in latent single-agent and multi-agent systems, without architectural changes or added parameters at inference. Across 7 benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, medicine, and code generation, with the same training data, compute, and latent budget, REST increases accuracy over CE-only training across agent settings and model sizes by up to 7.5 percentage points and convergence on a final answer by 30\%. Furthermore, REST thoughts encode more of what is required to achieve the correct answer, and decoding them better recovers the intended output of the agent, which makes latent communication easier to interpret. Project Website: this https URL

</details>


### [107] [An Exact Generate - Transform Decomposition of Small-LLM Team Scaling Across Orchestration Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36104)
*Blaz Bertalanic,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在大规模智能体编排实验（8种架构、5种7-9B模型、5个基准）中提出精确的生成-变换分解，揭示了LLM团队扩展的收益高度依赖于任务与架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体团队规模扩展是否以及如何在不同任务和架构下提升LLM准确性，其收益来源与限制机制是什么。

Method: 论文未详细披露具体模型架构，但描述了智能体编排架构的对比实验框架：输入为短答案问题和可执行代码任务，智能体团队通过多轮调用（最大30次）进行协作。八种编排架构中，Proposer-Critic架构表现出最陡峭的算术任务扩展曲线，并在最大预算下总体超越其他架构。论文提出的生成-变换分解将工作流分为提议覆盖（proposal coverage）和下游变换（downstream transform），准确率变化可精确分解为覆盖红利和变换变化两部分。该分解用于诊断不同任务的表现：算术任务存在覆盖提升空间且批评者引导的变换能有效利用，而多项选择基准覆盖饱和或变换失败，开放域代码生成中生成恢复几乎消失，准确率跟随覆盖变化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "最高17个点（从3次到30次调用）", "setting": "两个算术文字题基准（GSM8K, GSMHard），所有架构"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "最多4个点（从3次到30次调用）", "setting": "ARC, GPQA, MMLU，所有架构"}, {"metric": "token成本变化倍数", "value": "2.1倍", "setting": "同等调用预算下不同架构"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出精确的生成-变换分解（generate-transform decomposition），将任意工作流的准确率变化严格分解为覆盖红利（extensive coverage dividend）和变换变化（intensive transformation change）；在八种智能体编排架构、五种7-9B指令微调模型、五个短答案基准和一个可执行代码基准上进行大规模系统扫描，最多30轮调用；揭示团队扩展回报高度依赖于任务和架构，纠正了任务平均指标掩盖的巨大差异

Abstract: Replacing one LLM agent with a collaborating team can raise accuracy, but whether scaling the team helps, and which architecture to scale, is unclear. Sweeping eight agent orchestration architectures across five instruction-tuned 7-9B models, five short-answer benchmarks, and an executable-code benchmark up to 30 calls, we find that the returns to team scaling are sharply task-dependent: from three to thirty calls accuracy rises by up to 17 points on the two arithmetic word-problem benchmarks (GSM8K, GSMHard) but by at most four on ARC, GPQA, and MMLU, for every architecture, a split the usual task-averaged number conceals. Proposer-Critic captures the arithmetic gains, scaling steepest and, in aggregate, surpassing every other architecture at the largest budget (item-clustered intervals exclude zero), though it ranks among the weakest elsewhere, and no architecture wins across tasks. We explain these trajectories with an exact generate-transform decomposition. Partitioning any workflow into proposal coverage and a downstream transform, any accuracy change splits exactly into an extensive coverage dividend and an intensive transformation change. The decomposition diagnoses each task: arithmetic offers coverage headroom that a critic-guided transform converts, whereas the multiple-choice benchmarks either saturate in coverage or fail to convert it, and on open-ended code generative recovery nearly vanishes so accuracy tracks coverage. At equal call budgets token cost still varies 2.1x. Extra calls therefore create candidate opportunity that only some architectures, on some tasks, convert. Team scaling is a task- and architecture-specific bet, not a uniform lever.

</details>


### [108] [SMat-Attention: Structured Long-Context Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36062)
*Emile Anand,Abdullah Ateyeh,Archer Wang,Marin Soljačić*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMat-Attention通过VC维数d参数化结构化因果掩码，统一softmax与线性注意力范式，给出分块算法与复杂度界，并在检索任务中验证路由表达能力，将结构作为长上下文计算与内存的显式旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在softmax注意力的灵活token级交互与线性注意力的高效压缩之间建立可控的结构化连接，以调节长上下文建模的计算复杂度与表达能力。

Method: SMat-Attention通过一族因果掩码M_d定义，掩码的行支持具有VC维数d。输入序列首先被分块处理，前向与反向传播采用分块算法以利用硬件效率。对于给定的d，行支持决定每个查询可路由的键子集模式：d=1时退化为标准因果掩码（每个查询仅关注自身及之前所有token）；d>1时允许更丰富的子集路由，例如规则辅助的多键检索或可学习的top-k读取。硬路由构造下，计算复杂度为O(T^{2-3/d}+T)，尽管掩码是稠密的。在流式解码中，固定视野下解码远距离前缀后的token只需常数时间，并维护O(T^{1-1/d})个缓存状态。该框架可扩展至Mamba-2与Gated DeltaNet，通过可学习路由替换固定掩码，保持亚二次预填充复杂度。训练目标为标准语言建模损失，未披露具体数据或硬件。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "召回准确率", "value": "在若干设置下优于Mamba-2与Gated DeltaNet基线", "setting": "子集路由与规则辅助多键检索实验"}, {"metric": "小规模语言建模性能", "value": "与基线相当", "setting": "应用扩展至Mamba-2与Gated DeltaNet的预训练实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于VC维数d的结构化矩阵注意力家族，d=1退化为因果掩码，d增大允许更丰富的子集路由模式。；给出分块前向与反向算法实现硬件效率。；证明对于长度T的序列，硬路由构造在稠密掩码下达到O(T^{2-3/d}+T)计算复杂度，固定视野流式解码每token常数时间且需O(T^{1-1/d})缓存状态。；将结构参数作为预填充代价与解码内存的显式旋钮。；将扩展应用于Mamba-2与Gated DeltaNet，采用可学习路由top-k查询读取，保持亚二次预填充并提升召回率。

Abstract: Long-context sequence models face a fundamental tradeoff: softmax attention uses flexible token-level interactions at quadratic cost, whereas linear attention obtains linear-time training and constant-time decoding by compressing history into a fixed-size state. In this work, we ask whether we can connect these regimes through a tunable notion of structure. To this end, we introduce Structured Matrix Attention (SMat-Attention) via a family of causal masks with structured long-range routing whose row supports have VC-dimension $d$. In our construction, $d=1$ recovers the standard causal mask, and increasing $d$ permits richer subset-routing patterns. We give chunkwise forward and backward algorithms to enable hardware-efficiency. For sequences of length $T$, the hard-routing construction takes $O(T^{2-3/d}+T)$ work, despite the mask being dense, for our prescribed family. In fixed-horizon streaming, decoding after the distant prefix takes constant time per token using $O(T^{1-1/d})$ cached states. SMat-Attention therefore makes VC-dimension an explicit knob governing access-pattern complexity, prefill cost, and decoding memory. Empirically, subset-routing and rule-assisted multi-key retrieval experiments illustrate the masks' routing expressiveness. Extensions to Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet using learned routing with top-$k$ query reads retain subquadratic prefill, improve recall accuracy over the backbones in several settings, and achieve comparable small-scale language-modeling performance.

</details>


### [109] [LongCat-DeepResearch Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36071)
*Meituan LongCat Team,He Zhu,Yue Xu,Wanli Wu,Haolin Ren,Yuxin Bian,Jiarui Zhao,Rongzhi Zhang,Quanchi Weng,Jinghao Cui,Yu Fan,Yuhan Liu,Yunhu Ye,Jiyuan Ren,Fengcheng Yuan,Zhao Yang,Jiacheng Zhang,Yuchuan Dai,Ruixuan Xiao,Haozhe Sun,Xiangyuan Liu,Cheng Sun,Yao Du,Yiming Hao,Hongbo Guo,Shuo He,Lei Wang,Xunliang Cai,Yan Chen,Fan Yang,Lingchuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 美团LongCat团队提出LongCat-DeepResearch，一个结合增强LongCat模型与多智能体工作流的深度研究系统，通过规划-并行调查-全局审查-局部修订的流程生成证据grounded报告，并用于通用模型的中训练与后训练，在多个基准上取得有竞争力的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何通过多智能体工作流实现证据grounded的深度研究报告生成，并将该流程用于通用LLM的中训练与后训练任务构建。

Method: LongCat-DeepResearch是一个结合增强LongCat模型与多智能体工作流的深度研究系统。输入为研究主题或问题，工作流首先由多个规划智能体探索外部来源并迭代精炼出可操作的研究计划ResearchSpec。随后研究智能体并行调查各自分配的章节，在独立上下文中持续收集证据并起草内容。各章节草稿组装成报告后，由全局审查模块进行整体评估，指导针对性的局部修订，从而减少对全文反复重写的依赖。该工作流还支持为LongCat通用模型构建研究任务与轨迹，用于中训练和后训练。最终输出为证据grounded的综合报告。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "DeepResearchBench", "value": "55.25", "setting": "LongCat-DeepResearch系统评估"}, {"metric": "DeepResearchBench II", "value": "51.35", "setting": "LongCat-DeepResearch系统评估"}, {"metric": "ResearchRubrics", "value": "79.83", "setting": "LongCat-DeepResearch系统评估"}, {"metric": "in-house benchmark", "value": "76.04", "setting": "四个对比系统中排名第二"}, {"metric": "average automatic readability preference", "value": "提升", "setting": "两个基准上，额外编辑改善平均自动可读性偏好，但各基准趋势不同"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 分离全局规划与详细调查的多智能体工作流；引入ResearchSpec作为可操作研究计划并由多个规划智能体迭代精炼；研究智能体在独立上下文中并行调查并起草指定章节，持续补充证据；章节组装后由全局审查指导针对性局部修订，减少全文重复重写；利用该工作流为LongCat通用模型构建中训练与后训练的研究任务和轨迹

Abstract: We present LongCat-DeepResearch, a deep research system that combines an enhanced LongCat model with a multi-agent workflow for producing comprehensive, evidence-grounded reports. The workflow separates global planning from detailed investigation and coordinates revision at the section level. Multiple planning agents first explore external sources and refine an actionable research plan, termed ResearchSpec. Research agents then investigate and draft their assigned sections in parallel, gathering additional evidence in separate contexts as their analyses develop. Once the sections are assembled, global review guides targeted local revisions, reducing reliance on repeated full-report rewriting. This workflow also supports the construction of research tasks and trajectories for the mid-training and post-training of LongCat's general-purpose models. LongCat-DeepResearch achieves 55.25 on DeepResearchBench, 51.35 on DeepResearchBench II, and 79.83 on ResearchRubrics. On an in-house benchmark, it scores 76.04, ranking second among four compared systems. Development-set analyses show benefits from combining planning perspectives, while further planning refinement has mixed effects. Additional editing improves average automatic readability preference across two benchmarks, with different trends on each.

</details>


### [110] [More Programs or More Rolls? Separating Coverage from Specialization in LLM Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35873)
*Ziyang Xu,Haitian Zhong,Hao Zhou,Hao Qin,Chenhan Jin,Te Qi,Shengze Xu,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出受控评估，分离LLM harness的覆盖增益与任务专精，发现字节相同程序也能带来2.16pp的oracle headroom，生成程序的重复优势主要表现为持续失败而非胜出，冻结选择器增益为0，呼吁更严格的多样性评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在控制推理预算和重复执行效应下，区分生成程序对LLM harness的覆盖增益与可重复的任务专精优势，避免错误归因。

Method: 论文未提出新的LLM架构，而是设计受控评估流程。输入为386个MATH-500任务，每个任务使用八个生成的LLM harness程序和一个基线程序，外加九个字节相同的基础副本，每个成员执行三次，共获得多次推理输出。评估分为三个层面：（1）答案覆盖：聚合所有程序的答案计算oracle覆盖率；（2）重复可重复性：分析同一程序多次执行得分的一致性，识别持续失败与持续胜出；（3）预执行选择增益：使用冻结的选择器基于任务特征选择harness，比较其与随机或单程序基线的性能。同时使用BIRD轨迹分析失败原因，定位在机制实现、激活和输出有效性。实验在匹配推理预算（相同总执行次数）下比较不同策略，最终提出评估标准要求任务优势跨执行持久、指导可用决策并在额外固定程序执行下保持优势。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "重复平均oracle headroom", "value": "2.16 percentage points", "setting": "386个MATH-500任务，9个字节相同基础副本，每个执行3次"}, {"metric": "oracle覆盖率", "value": "98.70%", "setting": "两群体在27次harness执行时"}, {"metric": "冻结选择器增益", "value": "0.00 percentage points", "setting": "预执行选择"}, {"metric": "持续失败任务数", "value": "100", "setting": "相对于基线的损失在三次重复中持续存在"}, {"metric": "持续胜出任务数", "value": "1", "setting": "相对于基线的持续胜利且对答案提取敏感"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出分离答案覆盖、可重复任务优势与预执行选择增益的受控评估框架；使用多个字节相同的基础harness副本作为重复执行对照，量化随机性对覆盖的贡献；揭示生成程序的持续失败与持久胜出模式，并指出选择器无增益；提出harness多样性评估标准：任务优势需跨执行持久、指导可用决策并在匹配预算下优于固定程序

Abstract: Automated generation of LLM harnesses promises to improve inference through task specialization. Yet additional answer coverage can arise from repeated execution of the same program, making specialization difficult to identify. We introduce a controlled evaluation that separates answer coverage, repeatable task advantages, and gains from pre-execution selection. On 386 MATH-500 tasks, we compare eight generated harnesses plus a baseline with nine byte-identical baseline copies, using three executions per member. Identical programs yield 2.16 percentage points of repeat-averaged oracle headroom. Generated programs exhibit substantially more repeatable score patterns, but these chiefly reveal persistent weaknesses: losses relative to the baseline persist across all three repeats on 100 tasks, while persistent wins occur on only one task and are sensitive to answer extraction. The frozen selector gains 0.00 percentage points, and both populations reach 98.70% oracle coverage at 27 harness executions. Stable complementarity remains unresolved at three repeats. Supporting BIRD traces locate failures in mechanism implementation, activation, and output validity. Together, these findings establish why coverage and repeatability alone cannot justify claims of useful specialization. They motivate an evaluation standard for harness diversity: task advantages should persist across executions, guide usable decisions, and improve on additional fixed-program executions under matched inference budgets.

</details>


### [111] [The Price of Token Boundaries: Compression Certificates and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35869)
*Yuhao Du,Shunian Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文量化预分词边界的压缩代价，通过线性规划下界和整数检查器验证，发现英语维基百科上边界使最优 token 数增加 28.3–36.8%；在 85M 参数和匹配 token 预算下，压缩与预测偏好不同词典，无约束拟合在 12 种语言中多数获得更优 bits per byte；提出边界许可证以研究中间策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化预分词边界对压缩成本的影响，并区分压缩效率与预测质量：在相同边界下比较分词器会掩盖边界强加的成本。

Method: 该工作并非提出新的模型架构，而是围绕预分词边界对压缩与预测的影响构建分析框架。输入为文本序列和分词器产生的 token 边界。核心模块包括：1）边界约束下的最小 token 数下界计算：对 token 出现赋予非负价格，通过最短路径和词汇预算选择得到下界，最大化所有价格以恢复线性规划松弛，并由独立整数检查器验证报告值；2）无约束下界的对应计算；3）边界许可证机制：限制允许跨越切分的词汇条目，通过调整许可证比例控制边界严格程度；4）在 85M 非嵌入参数的语言模型上进行配对训练实验，匹配训练 token 预算，在 12 种语言上评估留出集 bits per byte。训练目标为标准自回归语言建模，优化器与具体训练细节未披露。推理路径为使用无约束解码器评估不同分词策略训练出的模型。该框架旨在将压缩成本与预测质量解耦量化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最优 token 数增加", "value": "28.3–36.8%", "setting": "英语维基百科，有边界规则 vs 无边界规则"}, {"metric": "BPE 相对约束下界的差距", "value": "2.1%", "setting": "英语维基百科，有边界约束"}, {"metric": "BPE 相对无约束下界的差距", "value": "10.9%", "setting": "英语维基百科，无边界约束"}, {"metric": "无约束拟合在留出集平均 bits per byte 上更优的语言数", "value": "12/12", "setting": "85M 非嵌入参数，匹配训练 token 预算，共用无约束解码器，配对研究"}, {"metric": "无约束拟合在留出集平均 bits per byte 上更优的语言数", "value": "11/12", "setting": "85M 非嵌入参数，独立调优和评估"}, {"metric": "许可 10% 词汇预算恢复 token 数减少的比例", "value": "英语 85.2%；中文 100.0%", "setting": "单独的英语和中文拟合语料，移除所有切割的 token 数减少量作为基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过最短路径和词汇预算选择，从非负价格得到最小 token 数的下界，并利用线性规划松弛最大化价格以获得紧下界，同时用独立整数检查器验证。；提出边界许可证（boundary licences），限制允许跨越切割的词汇条目，并允许相同形式的证书。；在 85M 非嵌入参数和匹配训练 token 预算下，对 12 种语言进行配对研究，证明压缩和预测偏向不同词典。

Abstract: Pre-tokenisation restricts which text fragments can become prediction units, but its compression cost is obscured when tokenisers are compared only under the same boundaries. We measure this cost by bounding the minimum token count from both sides, with and without a regular-expression boundary rule. Nonnegative prices on token occurrences yield a lower bound through shortest paths and vocabulary-budget selection; maximising over all prices recovers the linear programming relaxation, and an independent integer checker certifies the reported values. On English Wikipedia, boundaries increase the optimal token count by 28.3--36.8\%. Byte pair encoding lies 2.1\% above the constrained lower bound, but 10.9\% above the unrestricted bound. Compression and prediction favour different dictionaries: at 85M non-embedding parameters and matched training-token budgets, unrestricted fitting yields higher mean held-out bits per byte under a common unrestricted decoder in all 12 languages in the paired study and 11 of 12 under independent tuning and evaluation. To study intermediate boundary policies, we introduce boundary licences, which limit the vocabulary entries permitted to cross cuts and admit the same form of certificate. On separate English and Chinese fitting corpora, licensing 10\% of the vocabulary budget recovers 85.2\% and 100.0\% of the achieved token-count reduction from removing all cuts. These results quantify the compression cost of boundaries while separating it from the prediction quality of the resulting token units.

</details>


### [112] [Is Human-Readable Text Necessary for Effective LLM Fine-Tuning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35868)
*Jinhao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Zicheng Yan,Yunquan Zhang,Daning Cheng,Song Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究人类可读文本是否是大语言模型有效微调的必要条件，提出DASA方法，利用激活梯度反馈优化连续合成输入嵌入，在多个模型和基准上实现与自然语言数据相当或更好的性能，并比GRADMM快3.6-4.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可读文本是否是大语言模型有效微调的必要条件？

Method: DASA利用冻结的参考模型，通过激活梯度反馈来优化连续合成输入嵌入。具体地，它不要求合成数据具有人类可读的文本形式，而是直接优化嵌入向量，使其产生的激活梯度能够引导模型进行有用的适应更新。优化目标关注适应效用而非源文本重建或语言流畅性。生成的合成嵌入直接用于下游微调，而离散token投影仅用于定性检查。实验在六种模型（Llama和Qwen家族，1B到32B参数）上进行，覆盖六个基准（知识、数学推理、代码生成、常识推理），在匹配的LoRA适配设置下评估性能，并与源自然语言数据和GRADMM进行比较。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能", "value": "与源自然语言数据相当，在多个配置中超越", "setting": "在匹配的LoRA适配设置下，六种模型（Llama和Qwen家族，1B到32B参数），六个基准（知识、数学推理、代码生成、常识推理）"}, {"metric": "性能", "value": "在大多数比较中超越GRADMM", "setting": "同上实验设置"}, {"metric": "加速比", "value": "3.6-4.9倍", "setting": "相比GRADMM，在评估的合成设置下，峰值GPU内存相当"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Desired-Update-Aligned Synthetic Data (DASA)，使用冻结参考模型的激活梯度反馈引导连续合成输入嵌入的优化；目标为有用适应更新而非源文本重建或语言流畅性；合成嵌入直接用于下游微调，离散token投影仅用于定性检查；在多个模型和基准上验证，相比GRADMM取得3.6-4.9倍加速且峰值GPU内存相当

Abstract: Is human readability necessary for effective fine-tuning of large language models? We investigate whether model-conditioned training representations can preserve or improve adaptation utility without requiring a human-readable textual form. We propose Desired-Update-Aligned Synthetic Data (DASA), which uses activation-gradient feedback from a frozen reference model to guide the optimization of continuous synthetic input embeddings. Inspired by the role of activation gradients in local risk reduction, DASA targets useful adaptation updates rather than source-text reconstruction or linguistic fluency. The resulting embeddings are used directly for downstream fine-tuning; discrete token projections are employed only for qualitative inspection. Experiments on six models from the Llama and Qwen families, ranging from 1B to 32B parameters, cover six benchmarks spanning knowledge, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and commonsense reasoning. Under matched LoRA adaptation settings, DASA achieves performance comparable to the source natural-language data and surpasses it in multiple configurations, while outperforming GRADMM in most comparisons. Further experiments cover general-domain and task-specialized source data. Under the evaluated synthesis settings, DASA provides a $3.6$--$4.9\times$ speedup over GRADMM with comparable peak GPU memory.

</details>


### [113] [Neurosymbolic Routing for Reliable Reasoning on Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35833)
*Avyay Sadhu,Alvaro Velasquez,Lekai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出神经符号路由器，用 L* 学习 DFA 将边缘查询分派给确定性求解器或小语言模型，在 Raspberry Pi 4B 上实现 100% 路由准确率、98.3% 整体准确率，并显著提升速度与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在边缘设备上对算术、代数、形式逻辑等结构化推理任务不可靠，而强制概率模型近似确定性任务会浪费准确率与能耗。

Method: 系统输入为自然语言查询。神经符号路由器首先对查询进行分类，将其分派到最便宜的、能给出正确答案的求解器：结构化确定性任务被送往确定性符号引擎，而开放式应用题则留给小语言模型（SLM）处理。路由逻辑并非手工编码，而是通过 L* 语法推断算法学习一个确定性有限自动机（DFA）：SLM 充当成员查询 oracle，标注数据充当等价查询 oracle，从而自动构建路由自动机。推理时，格式化查询直接由符号求解器处理，不经过 SLM，因此可在 1–11 ms 内返回答案；仅开放式查询交由 SLM 生成。训练目标为学习正确的 DFA 路由自动机，优化路由准确率与整体正确率，并兼顾计算开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "整体准确率", "value": "98.3%", "setting": "100 条未测试提示，来自 DeepMind Mathematics、GSM8K 和 RuleTaker，推理预算 512 token，Raspberry Pi 4B（8 GB RAM，无 GPU）"}, {"metric": "路由准确率", "value": "100%", "setting": "100 条未测试提示，来自 DeepMind Mathematics、GSM8K 和 RuleTaker，推理预算 512 token，Raspberry Pi 4B"}, {"metric": "应用题准确率", "value": "93.3%", "setting": "100 条未测试提示，来自 DeepMind Mathematics、GSM8K 和 RuleTaker，推理预算 512 token，Raspberry Pi 4B"}, {"metric": "最强基线 Program-of-Thought 整体准确率", "value": "72.0%", "setting": "相同测试设置，100 条未测试提示，推理预算 512 token，Raspberry Pi 4B"}, {"metric": "工具调用 agent 整体准确率", "value": "58.7%", "setting": "相同测试设置，相同求解器，100 条未测试提示，推理预算 512 token，Raspberry Pi 4B"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出神经符号路由器，将查询分类并分派到最便宜的正确求解器；使用 L* 语法推断学习确定性有限自动机（DFA）作为路由逻辑，以 SLM 作为成员查询 oracle、标注数据作为等价查询 oracle；在无 GPU 的 Raspberry Pi 4B 上实现 1–11 ms 的格式化查询路由延迟，并在 30 token 配置下相比 Program-of-Thought 获得 8.8 倍加速与 2.8 倍能效提升

Abstract: Running a language model on edge hardware provides private and low-latency reasoning without a network connection, and yet the small models that fit on such devices are unreliable on the tasks computers are expected to handle well, such as arithmetic, algebra, and formal logic problems. We argue that much of this unreliability is avoidable. Many queries appearing to demand reasoning are in fact structurally deterministic and permit fast and exact symbolic solutions. Therefore, forcing a probabilistic model to approximate them sacrifices accuracy and energy for little benefit. We present a neurosymbolic router that classifies each incoming query and dispatches it to the cheapest correct solver, sending structured tasks to deterministic engines and reserving the small language model (SLM) for open-ended word problems. Instead of hand-coding the routing logic, we learn a deterministic finite automaton (DFA) with the L* grammatical inference algorithm, using the SLM as a membership oracle and labeled data as an equivalence oracle. On a Raspberry Pi 4B (8 GB RAM, no GPU), evaluated on 100 untested prompts from DeepMind Mathematics, GSM8K, and RuleTaker, learned routing attains 100% routing accuracy and 98.3% overall accuracy with a 512-token reasoning budget (93.3% on word problems), compared with 72.0% for the strongest agent baseline, Program-of-Thought, and 58.7% for a tool-calling agent given the same solvers. Since formatted queries never reach the model, the router answers them in 1-11 ms and, in its 30-token configuration, runs 8.8x faster and 2.8x more energy-efficient than Program-of-Thought.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [MERGE: Multi-LLM Ensemble for Retrieval via Generative Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37574)
*Tzu-I Ho,Yung-Yu Shih,Shang-Yu Su,Dongzhe Wang,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MERGE提出两阶段多LLM查询扩展：多个7-8B异构模型生成候选扩展，更大LLM合成单一查询，并用下游检索性能驱动的自动提示优化替代人工提示工程；在五个BEIR基准上使BM25 nDCG@10提升2.1至14.9点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个LLM进行查询扩展受训练数据和架构偏差限制，且手写提示需要为每个新模型重新设计，难以规模化；如何用多个异构小模型稳定提升BM25检索效果并自动优化提示。

Method: MERGE是两阶段检索查询扩展框架。输入为用户原始查询，Stage-1由三个异质7-8B开源LLM各自独立生成候选查询扩展；Stage-2使用一个更大的LLM对这些候选扩展进行生成式合成，输出单一查询。为避免为每个模型手工设计提示，框架在两个阶段均集成任务驱动的自动提示优化循环：循环不依赖LLM评估器，而是直接用候选提示产生的扩展查询在目标语料上的下游检索性能打分，并在当前冠军提示与优化器提出的草稿之间进行小型锦标赛，当冠军连续两轮胜出即终止；历史增强变体还会把最近的锦标赛轨迹作为上下文反馈给优化器，以指导下一轮提示生成。优化后的提示分别驱动Stage-1各成员与Stage-2合成器。最终合成查询仅执行一次BM25检索，不使用rank fusion、监督式文档扩展或重建索引，因此与具体检索器解耦。训练目标/优化并非模型参数训练，而是通过检索指标驱动的离散提示搜索来优化框架行为；推理路径为原始查询→多LLM候选扩展→LLM合成→单次BM25检索→返回文档排序。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "nDCG@10 over original BM25 queries", "value": "+2.1 到 +14.9 点", "setting": "五个BEIR基准（NQ, SciFact, FiQA, Touche-2020, DBPedia），BM25检索，仅使用紧凑开源模型，匹配或超过强LLM查询扩展基线"}, {"metric": "Stage-2 ensemble vs 任一Stage-1单LLM", "value": "Stage-2集成优于任何单个Stage-1 LLM", "setting": "消融实验"}, {"metric": "task-grounded APO", "value": "将较大的种子提示性能回退转化为一致增益", "setting": "无手工调参的消融实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 两阶段多LLM生成式查询扩展框架：三个异质7-8B模型独立生成候选扩展，再由更大LLM生成式合成为单一查询；任务驱动的自动提示优化APO循环，候选提示直接以下游检索性能评分，而非用LLM评估器；当前冠军提示与优化器草稿的小型锦标赛机制，冠军连续两轮幸存即终止；历史增强变体将近期锦标赛轨迹反馈给优化器；检索器无关设计：仅单次BM25检索，无需rank fusion、监督式文档扩展或重建索引

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to enrich user queries in information retrieval (IR) so that a standard retriever such as BM25 can bridge vocabulary gaps with the target corpus. Any single LLM, however, is limited by its training data and architectural biases, and its enrichment behavior depends on hand-crafted prompts that must be re-engineered for each new model -- an expensive and poorly scalable process. We present MERGE (Multi-LLM Ensemble for Retrieval via Generative Enrichment), a two-stage framework: three heterogeneous 7-8B open-source LLMs independently produce candidate expansions, and a larger LLM generatively synthesizes them into a single query. To make prompt engineering scalable across the ensemble, we integrate a task-grounded Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) loop into both stages. Unlike APO methods that judge candidates with an LLM evaluator, our loop scores each candidate by its downstream retrieval performance and runs a small tournament between the current champion prompt and optimizer-proposed drafts, terminating once the champion survives two consecutive rounds; a history-augmented variant additionally feeds the recent tournament trajectory back to the optimizer. MERGE is retriever-agnostic and issues a single BM25 pass with no rank fusion, no supervised document expansion, and no re-indexing. On five BEIR benchmarks (NQ, SciFact, FiQA, Touche-2020, DBPedia), MERGE improves BM25 nDCG@10 over the original queries by +2.1 to +14.9 points and matches or outperforms strong LLM-based query-expansion baselines despite using only compact open-source models. Ablations confirm that the Stage-2 ensemble beats any single Stage-1 LLM, and that task-grounded APO converts large seed-prompt regressions into consistent gains without hand-tuning.

</details>


### [115] [Effective Dense Retrieval using Only In-Context Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38099)
*Nour Jedidi,Abdul Basit Ali,Hang Li,Jimmy Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RICE，一种无需训练的LLM稠密检索方法，通过少量上下文示例提示LLM生成高质量稠密表示，显著提升提示式嵌入的检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不进行检索器训练的情况下，仅通过少量上下文示例提示解码器LLM，使其产生高质量的稠密检索表示。

Method: RICE方法将查询和文档编码为稠密向量，不训练任何参数。输入为一组包含查询与相关文档的上下文示例，这些示例为查询端和文档端编码提供共享上下文。给定一个查询或文档，LLM被条件化在该上下文示例上，生成对应的稠密表示。该表示从LLM的隐藏状态中提取（具体提取方式未在摘要中说明），并用于后续的相似度计算与检索。整个流程无需梯度更新，仅依赖LLM的前向推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出RICE（Representations from In-Context Examples）方法，利用共享上下文的查询-文档示例对LLM进行提示，无需训练即可提取稠密表示；证明仅用上下文示例的免训练方法能显著提升提示式LLM嵌入的检索准确率

Abstract: Turning decoder-only large language models (LLMs) into strong dense retrievers typically requires some form of retriever training. In this paper, we ask whether LLMs can instead be prompted to produce effective representations for dense retrieval given only a few in-context examples. To answer this, we introduce RICE (Representations from In-Context Examples), a simple "training-free" approach that extracts high-quality dense representations from LLMs. To do so, RICE conditions the LLM on examples that provide a shared context for query and document encoding. Our results demonstrate that RICE embeddings can substantially improve the accuracy of prompt-based LLM embeddings, establishing it as a simple method to build LLM-based dense retrievers that do not require training. We release our code at this https URL .

</details>


### [116] [Generated Query Expansion Still Helps Strong Sparse Retrieval: A Controlled Study with SPLADE-v3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37911)
*Ryan C. Barron,Cade W. Trotter,Maksim E. Eren,Kim Ø. Rasmussen,Liz D. Miller,Benjamin J. Migliori*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文在冻结 SPLADE-v3 文档索引、统一查询侧集成和 256 维预算下，系统比较四种生成查询扩展格式，证明生成词汇仍能提升强稀疏检索器，但原始查询必须保持高权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强学习稀疏检索器（SPLADE-v3）下，生成式查询扩展是否仍然有效，以及哪种生成格式、如何与原始查询结合能稳定提升科学检索效果。

Method: 系统以 SPLADE-v3 为冻结的稀疏检索器，文档索引在全部实验中保持不变。输入为科学查询，先通过生成式组件产生四种查询扩展格式：1）term lists，即相关术语列表；2）pseudo-document，即伪文档；3）multiple pseudo-references，即多个伪参考文本；4）corpus-steered text，即受语料引导的文本。然后按统一的查询侧集成规则，将生成内容与原始查询结合为 256 维稀疏查询表示，再对冻结文档索引进行检索并计算 nDCG@10。实验在 NFCorpus、TREC-COVID 和 SciDocs 三个科学检索集合上进行，通过插值权重扫描分析原始查询与生成内容的权重比例影响。此外，引入 shuffled-text 和非上下文 lexical-bag 控制来检验增益来源，并构建 corpus-induced typed concept graph 及其关系、深度、验证、随机和门控控制作为负面分析。
