Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "aggregate nDCG@10 relative gain", "value": "4.81%", "setting": "NFCorpus 上最佳方法-集合比较"}, {"metric": "aggregate nDCG@10 relative gain", "value": "8.92%", "setting": "TREC-COVID 上最佳方法-集合比较"}, {"metric": "aggregate nDCG@10 relative gain", "value": "9.47%", "setting": "SciDocs 上最佳方法-集合比较"}, {"metric": "significant improvements after Holm correction", "value": "11/12", "setting": "全部方法-集合比较"}, {"metric": "interpolation settings with persistent gain", "value": "103/114", "setting": "包括所有原始查询权重至少 30% 的设置"}, {"metric": "aggregate comparisons above baseline for shuffled-text and lexical-bag controls", "value": "24/24", "setting": "所有聚合比较"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在冻结文档索引和固定 256 维查询预算下，统一比较四种生成查询扩展格式：term lists、pseudo-document、multiple pseudo-references、corpus-steered text。；采用统一的查询侧集成规则，隔离生成内容本身对 SPLADE-v3 检索效果的影响。；通过插值权重扫描发现，只要原始查询权重不低于 30%，增益在 103/114 个设置中持续存在。；通过 shuffled-text 和非上下文 lexical-bag 控制实验证明，新增词汇而非上下文结构贡献了主要收益。；证明 corpus-induced typed concept graph 不能带来一致增益，其多种控制也无法挽救。

Abstract: Scientific queries are often brief, while relevant papers use specialized vocabulary. Generated query expansion can bridge this mismatch, but earlier work suggests that its value shrinks as the underlying retriever becomes stronger. We test the four generated formats of term lists, a pseudo-document, multiple pseudo-references, and corpus-steered text all together with SPLADE-v3 on NFCorpus, TREC-COVID, and SciDocs. Every condition searches the same frozen document index and follows the same query-side integration rule and 256-dimension budget, isolating the effect of the added content. All twelve method-collection comparisons improve aggregate nDCG@10, with best relative gains of 4.81%, 8.92%, and 9.47%. Eleven remain significant after Holm correction. The gain persists in 103 of 114 interpolation settings, including every setting that assigns at least 30% of the mixture weight to the original query. Shuffled-text and non-contextual lexical-bag controls also remain above baseline in all 24 aggregate comparisons, showing that the added vocabulary carries most of the benefit. A corpus-induced typed concept graph, by contrast, produces no consistent gain, and its relation, depth, validation, random, and gating controls do not rescue it. Generated vocabulary can therefore complement a strong learned sparse retriever, provided that the original query remains strongly represented.

</details>


### [117] [Towards Semi-Automatically Comparing Keyword-Based and Semantic Search Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37749)
*Mohamed Ben Salha,Fiete Lüer,Maik Betka,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个半自动评估框架，用于定量比较关键词搜索与语义对话搜索（如RAG）的准确率。框架使用可互换等价类处理不同输出格式，并在工业案例中验证，显示上下文感知搜索有统计显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何定量、半自动地比较输出格式不同的关键词搜索与语义对话式搜索（如RAG）的检索准确率，尤其是排序准确率与信息完整性。

Method: 该论文提出一个框架，用于比较关键词搜索与语义对话搜索的准确率。框架的核心是定义可互换的等价类（equivalence classes），这些等价类根据领域上下文（如公司或问题）定制，从而将不同格式的输出（列表或消息）映射到统一的评估空间。对于关键词搜索，评估侧重排序准确率；对于语义对话搜索，评估侧重检索信息的完整性。然后进行半自动比较，并在工业案例中使用Mann-Whitney U检验验证差异的统计显著性。具体模块、数据流及训练/推理细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出一个可互换等价类的半自动评估框架，用于比较不同输出格式的搜索系统；针对关键词系统关注排序准确率，针对语义对话系统关注检索信息完整性；通过工业案例研究验证框架，使用Mann-Whitney U检验证明统计显著性

Abstract: The increasing importance of Information Retrieval (IR) in managing large datasets has highlighted significant limitations in traditional keyword-based search systems. Context-aware chat-based search methods, such as Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG), have recently emerged, but their evaluation compared to keyword-based systems often relies on subjective user feedback. A rigorous, quantitative comparison between these paradigms remains lacking. This work introduces a novel, preliminary framework to quantitatively assess IR accuracy of search systems that produce different output formats, such as lists and messages. It focuses on two key aspects: the ranking accuracy for keyword-based systems and the completeness of retrieved information for semantic chat-based systems. Our approach enables semi-automatic comparisons of semantic and keyword-based methods using interchangeable equivalence classes tailored to domain-specific contexts (e.g., companies or problems). We validate the framework through an industrial case study, demonstrating statistically significant improvements in context-aware search over keyword-based methods, supported by analyses including the Mann-Whitney U-Test. With its adaptable design, the proposed framework provides a strong foundation for objectively assessing keyword-based and semantic chat-based search methods.

</details>


### [118] [HELIX: Purified and Unified - Rethinking Feature Interaction and Sequence Modeling for Large-Scale Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37183)
*Yuntao Zheng,Miao Zhang,Yadong Ding,Yanchuan Tang,Lixiyu Chen,Hao Wang,Quan Li,Shiying Cai,Yue Lin,Jiayu Li,Yu Feng,Wentao Yang,Rongkun Xing,Jiekai Wang,Mingge Zhang,Feiling Gong,Xiang Gao,Jinyu Dong,Yajing Zhang,Pengfei Ren,Yinzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HELIX 统一了推荐排序中的特征交互与序列建模，通过交替序列检索与单向信息流设计，在 TikTok 电商推荐中实现离线 CTR/CVR AUC 提升和线上人均 GMV 约 6% 的增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐排序模型通常分别扩展特征交互和长用户行为序列建模，但单独扩展任一轴都存在缩放上限和次优的缩放律斜率；论文试图证明联合扩展两轴可以获得更优的缩放律。

Method: HELIX 是一种用于大规模推荐的净化统一架构，旨在联合缩放特征交互和序列建模两个轴。输入包括异构的用户特征、物品特征、上下文特征以及交叉特征，同时也包含长而多类型的用户行为历史。架构交替执行序列检索和特征交互：序列检索模块从用户行为历史中提取可复用的序列状态；特征交互模块则处理候选物品条件化的 mix-tokens。两者之间强制执行单向信息流，即从可复用的序列状态流向候选条件 mix-tokens，从而保留跨深度通信，同时使用户侧序列计算能够被摊销。这种设计允许序列建模和特征交互进行灵活且非对称的缩放。训练目标未在摘要中明确说明，推理或服务流程未详细披露，仅提及部署于 TikTok 电商推荐系统并经过离线与线上评估。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "CTR AUC", "value": "持续提升", "setting": "TikTok 电商推荐系统离线评估"}, {"metric": "CVR AUC", "value": "持续提升", "setting": "TikTok 电商推荐系统离线评估"}, {"metric": "其他排序指标", "value": "持续提升", "setting": "TikTok 电商推荐系统离线评估"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "电商视频人均GMV", "value": "提升约 6%", "setting": "TikTok 电商推荐系统线上 A/B 测试"}]}

Conclusion: 提出 HELIX 架构，交替进行序列检索与特征交互，统一两个建模轴。；强制从可复用序列状态到候选条件 mix-tokens 的单向信息流，兼顾跨深度通信与用户侧序列计算的可摊销性。；支持序列建模和特征交互的灵活、非对称缩放。

Abstract: Industrial recommendation ranking models typically scale along two modeling axes: feature interaction over heterogeneous user, item, context, and cross features, and sequence modeling over long, informative, and multi-type user behavior histories. We find that scaling either capability in isolation is insufficient, as each exhibits a limited scaling ceiling and a suboptimal scaling-law slope. We conjecture that achieving a more favorable scaling-law slope requires jointly scaling both axes. To support this, we present HELIX, a purified and unified architecture for large-scale recommendation. HELIX interleaves sequence retrieval and feature interaction while enforcing one-way information flow from reusable sequence states to candidate-conditioned mix-tokens. This design preserves cross-depth communication between the two modeling axes while keeping user-side sequence computation amortizable, enabling flexible and asymmetric scaling of sequence modeling and feature interaction. Deployed in TikTok's e-commerce recommendation system, HELIX consistently improves offline CTR AUC, CVR AUC, and other ranking metrics. In online A/B tests, it achieves an approximately 6% increase in e-commerce video GMV per user.

</details>


### [119] [GRP v0.1 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36688)
*Wenfeng Zhuo,Vincent Xue,Charles Wei,Cong Ni,Ruiming Lu,Jiwen Ren,Mo Li,Peng Yang,Xufei Wang,Dongheng Li,Jiacong He,Yi Song,Yufei Fan,Mikhail Obukhov,Yiwen Chen,Yvette Liu,Yin Ye,Chengjie Wu,Mingtao Zhang,Jinchao Ye,Lili Zhang,Chunhui Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GRP v0.1 提出统一编码器-解码器生成式推荐框架，将召回、排序与奖励建模融合，采用多模态 Semantic ID 与联合排序模块，并用冻结排序器为 mGRPO 提供奖励；线上 A/B 显示检索延迟降低 69%，观看时长最高 +0.82%、分享最高 +2.56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统多阶段级联中检索、排序与服务组件难以联合替换，本文探索用单一编码器-解码器模型统一召回、排序与奖励建模，并实现渐进式端到端部署。

Method: GRP 是一个统一的编码器-解码器生成式推荐框架。输入侧，模型生成多模态 Semantic ID 作为物品表示；编码器处理用户历史行为序列（历史编码），解码器生成候选物品的 Semantic ID。框架内联合训练一个排序模块，对生成的候选进行打分，从而在同一模型中耦合召回与排序。训练目标包含生成任务与排序任务；随后，冻结的排序模块为强化学习后训练提供奖励信号。在强化学习阶段，提出 mGRPO，在奖励优化中引入参考锚定的 margin 项，以在对齐生成结果的同时保持对日志目标（logged targets）的预测似然，避免策略退化。推理/服务阶段，采用服务优化（具体细节未披露）将端到端检索延迟降低 69%；在线部署时采用渐进式路径：先作为额外召回源，再尝试早期排序旁路，最后替换较弱的召回源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "观看时长 (view time)", "value": "+0.46%", "setting": "仅作为召回源（retrieval-only）与生产系统对比"}, {"metric": "分享 (shares)", "value": "+0.77%", "setting": "仅作为召回源（retrieval-only）与生产系统对比"}, {"metric": "观看时长 (view time)", "value": "+0.82%", "setting": "早期排序旁路 + 替换较弱召回源的组合方案，平台级护栏指标中性"}, {"metric": "分享 (shares)", "value": "+2.56%", "setting": "早期排序旁路 + 替换较弱召回源的组合方案，平台级护栏指标中性"}, {"metric": "端到端检索延迟", "value": "-69%", "setting": "服务优化后"}]}

Conclusion: GRP：单一编码器-解码器生成式推荐框架，联合实现召回、排序与奖励建模；生成多模态 Semantic ID 作为物品表示，并由联合训练的排序模块对候选打分；利用冻结的排序模块为强化学习后训练提供奖励信号；mGRPO：在奖励优化中加入参考锚定的 margin，以保持对日志目标（logged targets）的似然；服务优化使端到端检索延迟降低 69%，并设计早期排序旁路与召回源替换等渐进部署策略

Abstract: Industrial recommendation systems rely on multi-stage cascades whose retrieval, ranking, and serving components are difficult to replace jointly. We present GRP, a generative recommendation framework that combines retrieval, ranking, and reward modeling in a single encoder-decoder model, and evaluate a progressive path toward end-to-end recommendation. The model generates multimodal Semantic IDs and scores candidates with a jointly trained ranking module. The frozen ranking module then supplies rewards for reinforcement-learning post-training. We introduce mGRPO, which adds a reference-anchored margin to reward optimization to preserve the likelihood of logged targets. Offline experiments examine history encoding, model capacity allocation, event selection, tokenization, and reward discrimination. Serving optimizations reduce end-to-end retrieval latency by 69%. Online experiments evaluate the model as a retrieval source, with early-ranking bypass, and with replacement of weaker sources. In a retrieval-only comparison, view time increases by 0.46% and shares by 0.77% relative to production. A separate comparison combining bypass and source replacement yields increases of 0.82% in view time and 2.56% in shares, with neutral platform-level guardrails. These results support progressive deployment while identifying remaining gaps in ranking quality and performance across recommendation metrics.

</details>


### [120] [Structured Interaction, Visual Localization, and Robust Execution for Complex Web Tasks: A Technical Report on the WebRetriever Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35904)
*Ziqi Zhang,Shaohui Li,Bing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 面向WebRetriever Challenge的网页智能体系统，以结构化交互优先、网格辅助视觉定位、分层上下文管理和容错执行为核心，在官方Protocol 3竞赛中以八个并发浏览器工作者实现59%通过率并排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在复杂网页任务中高效、稳定地执行多浏览器交互，同时最小化对视觉感知的依赖并管理冗余的页面与交互历史。

Method: 系统采用结构化交互优先策略，输入为语义网页信息与可选的视觉截图。执行流程：首先利用语义网页信息（如DOM结构）进行常规浏览器操作；当结构化表示不足以定位控件时，触发视觉感知模块，使用网格辅助视觉定位方法定位难访问控件。分层上下文管理模块负责维护并压缩页面与交互历史，减少冗余上下文。容错执行机制处理多浏览器并发中的异常与故障，保证任务稳定执行。最终输出为任务执行结果与通过率指标。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "pass rate", "value": "up to 79%", "setting": "local evaluation on Protocol 1"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "pass rate", "value": "59%", "setting": "official Protocol 3 competition, 8 concurrent browser workers, ranked first overall"}]}

Conclusion: 结构化交互优先策略：优先使用语义网页信息执行常规浏览器操作，仅在结构化表示不足时调用视觉感知。；网格辅助视觉定位：针对难以访问的控件进行网格辅助的视觉定位。；分层上下文管理：减少冗余页面与交互历史，提升上下文利用效率。；容错执行机制：保障多浏览器任务处理的稳定性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This report presents the web agent system developed for the WebRetriever Challenge. The system follows a structuredinteraction- first strategy, using semantic webpage information for routine browser operations and invoking visual perception only when structured representations are insufficient. Three key designs are introduced: grid-assisted visual localization for difficult-to-access controls, hierarchical context management for reducing redundant page and interaction history, and fault-aware execution mechanisms for stable multi-browser task processing. The system achieved a pass rate of up to 79% in local evaluation on Protocol 1. In the official Protocol 3 competition, it achieved a 59% pass rate with eight concurrent browser workers and ranked first overall, winning the WebRetriever Challenge.

</details>


### [121] [Soft Curriculum Learning for Optimizing Fresh and Generalized Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35783)
*Arnab Bhadury,Siyan Zheng,Anlan Yu,Palaksh Rungta,Jiawei Li,Changping Meng,Dapeng Hong,Chuan He,Onkar Dalal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Google提出了一种可扩展的软课程学习框架，用于工业级短视频推荐系统的连续训练。通过损失退火和图内权重调整替代动态数据过滤，避免硬件加速器饥饿，缓解流行度反馈循环。在序列召回、双塔召回和大规模排序模型上验证，并报告在线A/B测试中用户满意度和新鲜内容消费的显著提升，同时保持吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模短视频推荐系统中，因流行度反馈循环导致训练数据严重偏向头部物品，检索与排序模型过度记忆头部模式而无法泛化到长尾目录；现有课程学习因动态数据拒绝导致CPU瓶颈、硬件加速器利用率低，难以在工业连续训练中落地。

Method: 论文提出一种面向工业级检索与排序模型连续训练的软课程学习框架。输入为连续训练数据流，包含用户-物品交互以及大规模短视频目录。框架核心模块包括：课程调度器，根据训练进度动态调整课程难度；损失退火模块，对样本损失进行加权而非过滤，渐进地增加困难样本（长尾）的权重；图内权重调整模块，在计算图内直接修改样本权重，避免数据丢弃导致的CPU-GPU/TPU数据管道瓶颈。数据流为：原始训练数据进入模型前，课程调度器生成每个样本的权重，损失函数按权重聚合，并随训练步骤逐步退火，从而在不降低系统吞吐的前提下打破流行度反馈循环。训练目标为标准的检索或排序损失，仅增加软权重；推理时与原模型一致，无需额外模块。该设计解决了动态数据拒绝带来的硬件利用率下降问题，并支持连续训练场景下的动态课程节奏。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "整体用户满意度", "value": "显著提升", "setting": "短视频平台在线A/B测试"}, {"metric": "新鲜内容消费", "value": "显著提升", "setting": "短视频平台在线A/B测试"}]}

Conclusion: 提出可扩展的软课程学习框架，专为工业级检索和排序模型的连续训练设计。；采用损失退火和图内权重调整替代刚性数据过滤，避免数据丢弃并提升硬件利用率。；在序列召回模型（如SASRec）、双塔召回模型和大规模连续排序模型上验证有效性，并在短视频平台进行在线A/B测试，展示用户满意度和新鲜内容消费提升。

Abstract: Large-scale recommender systems, particularly short-form video platforms, are often bottlenecked by massive popularity feedback loops. In such environments, as models recommend popular items, they generate an overwhelming amount of skewed training data for "head" items. This creates a self-reinforcing cycle where retrieval and ranking models memorize "head" item patterns at the expense of generalizing across the vast "tail" of the catalogue. While Curriculum Learning (CL) offers a powerful mechanism to break this feedback loop by systematically exposing models to progressively more difficult and less frequent examples, its adoption in industrial recommendation has been hampered by hardware utilization inefficiencies or the needs for complicated pre-processing techniques because dynamic data rejection algorithms tend to starve hardware accelearators (TPUs/GPUs) by becoming largely CPU-bound. In this work, we introduce a scalable Soft Curriculum Learning framework designed specifically for continuous training setups within industry-scale retrieval and ranking models. By utilizing loss annealing and in-graph weight adjustments rather than rigid data filtering, we break the popularity feedback and enable dynamic curriculum pacing without sacrificing system throughput. We demonstrate empirical evidence through applications across sequence-based retrieval models (such as SASRec), two-tower retrieval models, and large-scale continuous ranking models. Online A/B tests on our short-video platform demonstrate substantial lifts in both overall user satisfaction and fresh content consumption, all without degrading model throughput.

</details>


### [122] [Financial Evidence Crowding: Diagnosing and Mitigating Constraint-Induced Displacement in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35782)
*Yixi Zhou,Jiayi Yin,Fan Zhang,Xiangyi He,Haipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文诊断金融RAG中约束不兼容候选挤占top-k上下文导致证据被置换的集合级排序失败，提出FinDeCrowd-Stress控制实验量化该效应，并设计带查询级身份门控的学习式分数校正FinDeCrowd-RAG，在受控候选上提升top-10证据包含率（0.757→0.902），在未做候选插入的索引上提升至0.492，且在固定生成器下改善FinanceBench和FinQA答案准确率与引用召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融问答RAG中，与查询主题匹配但约束不兼容的候选会占用有限的top-k上下文槽位，导致答案支撑证据被挤出（约束诱导位移），即集合级排序失败。

Method: 系统基于RAG检索流程：首先从候选集中检索得到固定相关性分数的候选证据，然后FinDeCrowd-RAG对候选分数进行学习式校正，校正项由类型化兼容性（如期间、分部、指标范围、表格范围等约束的匹配程度）和局部词汇竞争（候选间的词汇重叠/竞争程度）组成，与原始固定相关性分数结合形成新分数。随后查询级身份门控模块预测该校正是否会产生更好的排序，若预测为否则保留原始排序，若为是则应用校正后的排序，最终将有限top-k上下文送入生成器。训练目标为学习分数校正与身份门控，摘要未披露优化细节。推理时先执行第一阶段的固定相关性排序，再按需进行门控重排序，在不改变第一阶段top-100覆盖率的前提下提升top-10证据包含率与生成端答案准确率和引用召回率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Recall@10", "value": "不兼容候选池相对兼容候选池下降0.147", "setting": "FinDER测试集，训练未见的公司，匹配兼容与不兼容候选池，固定查询、相关证据、排序模型、候选数和检索预算"}, {"metric": "top-10证据包含率", "value": "从0.757提升至0.902", "setting": "在相同受控候选上，FinDeCrowd-RAG恢复已存在于候选集中的证据"}, {"metric": "top-10证据包含率", "value": "从0.420提升至0.492", "setting": "FinDER索引未做查询特定候选插入，门控重排序"}, {"metric": "top-100检索覆盖率", "value": "0.743", "setting": "按设计保持不变，FinDER索引未做查询特定候选插入"}, {"metric": "答案准确率与引用召回率", "value": "提升（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "固定生成器，在FinanceBench和FinQA上，仅改变排序"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出并形式化金融证据拥挤/约束诱导位移这一RAG集合级排序失败问题；构建FinDeCrowd-Stress控制实验，固定查询、相关证据、排序模型、候选数和检索预算，匹配兼容与不兼容候选池，隔离该失败；提出FinDeCrowd-RAG，将固定相关性分数与类型化兼容性和局部词汇竞争结合的学习式分数校正；引入查询级身份门控，仅在预测更好排序时应用校正，否则保留原始排序，避免损害原有排序

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) retrieves candidate evidence and sends only a limited top-ranked subset, the top-k context, to a generator. In financial question answering, passages can match a query's topic while conflicting with its period, segment, metric scope, or table scope. We study the resulting set-level ordering failure, which we call financial evidence crowding. FinDeCrowd-Stress isolates this failure through matched compatible and incompatible candidate pools while fixing the query, relevant evidence, ranking model, candidate count, and retrieval budget. On a FinDER test split containing companies unseen during training, incompatible pools reduce top-10 evidence inclusion (Recall@10) by 0.147 relative to equally difficult compatible pools. This gap shows that conflicting candidates consume limited context slots and displace answer-supporting evidence. We then introduce FinDeCrowd-RAG, a learned score correction that combines a fixed relevance score with typed compatibility and local lexical competition. A query-level identity gate applies the correction only when it predicts a better order; otherwise, it preserves the original ranking. On identical controlled candidates, FinDeCrowd-RAG raises top-10 evidence inclusion from 0.757 to 0.902 by recovering evidence already present in the candidate set. On a FinDER index built without query-specific candidate insertion, gated reranking raises this inclusion rate from 0.420 to 0.492, while top-100 retrieval coverage remains 0.743 by design. With a fixed generator, the same ordering change improves answer accuracy and citation recall on FinanceBench and FinQA. These results identify constraint-induced displacement as a measurable RAG evaluation target and show that identity-gated reranking can recover evidence already covered by first-stage retrieval.

</details>


### [123] [TSG Suggester: Tree-Structured Knowledge-Graph Retrieval for Troubleshooting Guide Recommendation in Cloud Incident Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35780)
*Shawn Pan,PavanUttej Ravva,Walt Williams,CJ Barberan,Nutan Sahoo,Ziran Min,David Gross,Irene Shaffer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TSG Suggester是一种基于树结构知识图谱检索的故障排查指南推荐系统，用于云事件管理。在314个真实事件上，Tree+KG方法取得54.78% Top 1和82.48% Top 5准确率，优于所有基线。研究还发现结构对齐对检索至关重要，而多模态图像增强反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何从事件描述中高效准确地推荐最相关的故障排查指南（TSG），解决传统关键词搜索的耗时和低准确率问题。

Method: Tree+KG系统将每篇故障排查指南（TSG）转换为树结构，保留其原生章节层次，并在内部节点附加LLM生成的问题抽象，以桥接指南的解决方案导向语言与事件的问题导向语言。同时，为每篇指南提取实体知识图谱。在查询时，系统接收事件描述，计算嵌入相似度并进行实体级匹配，融合两种信号生成最终排序，推荐相关TSG。具体模块包括：1）树构建模块：将TSG文档解析为树，每个节点对应章节，内部节点生成问题抽象；2）实体知识图谱提取模块：从指南中抽取实体及其关系形成KG；3）检索模块：对事件描述进行嵌入表示，与树节点及KG实体进行相似度计算和匹配，融合分数后返回Top-K TSG。训练目标未明确说明。推理流程为：输入事件描述→嵌入与实体识别→树节点嵌入相似度与KG实体匹配→分数融合→排序输出。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Top 1 准确率", "value": "54.78%", "setting": "Tree+KG方法在314个真实事件、112个TSG、18个服务团队的生产数据集上"}, {"metric": "Top 5 准确率", "value": "82.48%", "setting": "Tree+KG方法在同一数据集上"}, {"metric": "Top 1 提升", "value": "8.58个百分点", "setting": "Tree+KG相比Text Only RAG基线"}, {"metric": "Top 5 下降", "value": "22.64个百分点", "setting": "Image Augmented RAG相比Text Only RAG基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Tree+KG方法：将每篇TSG转换为保留原生章节层次的结构树，在内部节点添加LLM生成的问题抽象以桥接指南的解决方案导向语言与事件的问题导向语言；为每篇指南提取实体知识图谱，在查询时融合嵌入相似度与实体级匹配；发现结构对齐主导：保留或重建文档结构的方法在精确区分场景下优于扁平分块；发现多模态增强（图片标题注入）反而损害检索性能：通用标题稀释嵌入，导致Top 5下降22.64个百分点

Abstract: On call engineers in large scale cloud services work under intense time pressure, yet locating the correct Troubleshooting Guide (TSG) for an incident remains a largely manual, keyword driven process, and prior empirical work finds that guide search consumes a substantial fraction of total mitigation time. We present TSG Suggester, a retrieval system that recommends relevant TSGs directly from an incident description. We evaluate five retrieval strategies: Text Only RAG, Image Augmented RAG, RAPTOR, Tree Structured Retrieval, and our proposed Tree + Knowledge Graph (Tree+KG), on 314 real world incidents spanning 112 unique TSGs drawn from 18 service teams on a production incident management system. Tree+KG converts each guide into a tree that preserves its native section hierarchy, attaches LLM generated problem abstractions to internal nodes to bridge the solution oriented language of guides and the problem oriented language of incidents, extracts a per guide entity knowledge graph, and fuses embedding similarity with entity level matching at query time. Tree+KG attains 54.78% Top 1 and 82.48% Top 5 accuracy, leading every baseline at every cutoff, with an 8.58 point Top 1 gain over text only RAG. Two findings are of independent interest. First, structural alignment dominates: methods that preserve or rebuild document structure outperform flat chunking where precise discrimination matters. Second, and contrary to our initial hypothesis, multimodal enrichment actively hurts. Captioning guide screenshots and injecting the captions costs 22.64 Top 5 points relative to the text only baseline because generic captions dilute embeddings rather than sharpen them. We report error analyses for both results and provide concrete deployment guidance.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Scaling Zero-Order Pretraining through Model Sharding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37899)
*Francois Chaubard,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Chris Ré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SOMA，通过将N个LSTM专家在数据簇上独立零阶优化训练，避免梯度交换，在8.44M参数规模下取得比单体SPSA和EGGROLL更低的测试损失，并通过top-k路由实现吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化（ZO）的梯度方差随扰动维度增大而增大，限制了大型模型的训练；如何通过模型分片提升ZO预训练的计算效率并降低推理成本。

Method: SOMA将训练数据聚类为N个簇，并为每个簇独立训练一个LSTM专家。每个专家使用同时扰动随机逼近（SPSA）进行零阶优化，仅依赖前向传播计算损失，不进行反向传播，专家之间不交换梯度、激活或优化器状态。损失函数为可分离损失：每个专家仅在自己对应的数据簇上计算损失，独立更新，这种设计消除了跨专家的扰动噪声，理论分析表明在等大小块的可分离目标下，独立损失相对于共享损失可将相对梯度方差降低至约1/N。推理时，大集成模型（如N=256）可采用top-k路由（k=4），即对每个输入选择k个专家进行前向计算并聚合输出，从而在模型规模相似的情况下大幅提升吞吐量。训练目标是最小化各专家在其数据簇上的SPSA估计损失，推理目标是低延迟高吞吐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "测试nats/byte", "value": "1.76", "setting": "SOMA N=2，64扰动，8.44M参数，150累计GPU小时"}, {"metric": "测试nats/byte", "value": "2.00-2.11", "setting": "单体SPSA基线，64/256/1024扰动"}, {"metric": "测试nats/byte", "value": "2.21", "setting": "EGGROLL基线"}, {"metric": "WikiText-103测试nats/byte", "value": "2.07", "setting": "SOMA N=2冻结检查点"}, {"metric": "WikiText-103测试nats/byte", "value": "2.25-2.36", "setting": "单体SPSA基线"}, {"metric": "WikiText-103测试nats/byte", "value": "2.49", "setting": "EGGROLL基线"}, {"metric": "测试损失降低", "value": "0.035 nats/byte", "setting": "SOMA N=4，独立损失 vs. 求和损失，1000次更新，三个随机种子，起始权重/数据/扰动/计算固定"}, {"metric": "测试损失", "value": "1.68", "setting": "SOMA N=256，top-k路由(k=4)"}, {"metric": "测试损失", "value": "1.71", "setting": "SOMA N=8，top-k路由(k=4)"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出SOMA（Sharded Optimization Mixture of Assemblies），将N个LSTM专家在N个数据簇上独立训练，使用SPSA优化，不交换梯度、激活或优化器状态；使用可分离损失消除专家间的扰动噪声，代价是跨域表示无法联合学习；理论证明：固定可分离目标下，独立损失估计器的相对梯度方差约为共享损失估计器的1/N；大集成模型配合top-k路由（k=4）提升推理吞吐量

Abstract: Zero-order optimization (ZO) trains without backpropagation, making it relevant to forward-only hardware and non-differentiable loss, but its gradient variance grows with perturbed dimension, inhibiting large-model training. Sharded Optimization Mixture of Assemblies (SOMA) trains LSTM experts independently on $N$ data clusters using simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation (SPSA), without exchanging gradients, activations or optimizer state. Its separable loss removes cross-expert perturbation noise at the cost of jointly learned representations across domains. Using 80,000 estimated RTX 5090 GPU-hours, we show modest sharding improves training compute efficiency over all tested monolithic ZO controls. At 8.44M parameters and 150 aggregate GPU-hours, SOMA $N=2$ with 64 perturbations reaches 1.76 test nats/byte, versus 2.00--2.11 for monolithic SPSA at 64, 256 or 1,024 perturbations and 2.21 for EGGROLL. On WikiText-103, these frozen checkpoints reach 2.07, 2.25--2.36 and 2.49, respectively. On a fixed separable objective with equal-size blocks, we prove independent losses reduce relative gradient variance to approximately $1/N$ of a shared-loss estimator's. Holding starting weights, data, perturbations and compute fixed, independent rather than summed losses lower SOMA $N=4$ test loss by 0.035 nats/byte after 1,000 updates across three seeds. Larger ensembles offer a separate inference benefit: at similar model size with top-$k$ routing ($k=4$), SOMA $N=256$ achieves 2.36M tokens/s versus 257k for SOMA $N=8$ ($9.19\times$, including routing), at lower test loss (1.68 versus 1.71), albeit using $59.9\times$ as much aggregate training compute. We release all training and evaluation code and checkpoints.

</details>


### [125] [Behavioral Capacity Certificates for Quantized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37887)
*Arian Eamaz,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出行为容量证书（BCC），按量化模型的实际部署行为（权重、缩放、激活、KV缓存精度）计算复杂度，并给出三步部署工作流：前向筛选位宽、边缘认证可安全修改的权重、种群损失界验证。在多个开源模型上验证了各步骤，并发现相同缓存内存下键精度高于值精度更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接基于权重码的复杂度界无法区分行为不同的部署，且重复计费行为相同的权重码；如何根据实际部署行为（权重、缩放、激活与缓存规则）来刻画量化语言模型的复杂度，并提供可验证的部署工作流。

Method: BCC针对量化语言模型的部署行为建模。输入包括模型权重、量化缩放因子、激活量化规则和KV缓存精度规则。主要模块按处理顺序为：(1) 前向筛选：对每层候选位宽，通过前向传播扰动并统计参考预测保持频率，快速筛选出候选位宽组合，避免Hessian计算；(2) 边缘认证单元：在给定容差内识别可被剪枝或符号翻转而不改变部署行为的权重，通过独立探针在新文本上给出预测不变的概率界；(3) 种群损失界：基于聚合先验质量计算部署模型在完整解码器上的种群损失上界，并推导盈亏平衡定律以比较验证成本与量化节省。训练目标未明确，推理流程为：先执行前向筛选确定逐层位宽，再应用边缘认证进行安全剪枝/翻转，最后使用BCC界评估部署可靠性。实验在GPT-2、Qwen2.5、SmolLM2及OLMoE-1B-7B上进行，验证了各步骤的有效性并发现键精度高于值精度时缓存效果更优。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "质量（与Hessian引导选择可比）", "value": "可比", "setting": "前向筛选 vs Hessian引导的逐层位宽选择"}, {"metric": "预处理成本", "value": "更低", "setting": "前向筛选 vs Hessian引导选择"}, {"metric": "预测不变的概率界", "value": "独立探针给出界", "setting": "OLMoE-1B-7B 和 SmolLM2-1.7B 上，所有允许组合保持声明预测"}, {"metric": "种群损失界更紧", "value": "比压缩码路线更紧", "setting": "完整解码器的BCC界"}, {"metric": "更低的NLL和更高的预测一致率", "value": "键精度高于值精度时更优", "setting": "相等缓存内存下，GPT-2、Qwen2.5、SmolLM2"}, {"metric": "更紧的复杂度界", "value": "更紧", "setting": "GPT-2审计中"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出行为容量证书（BCC），按诱导相同有界损失的完整实现（权重、缩放、激活、缓存规则）的聚合先验质量来计费，而非直接按权重码计费。；给出当量化合并实现时共享质量降低复杂度惩罚的机制，并推导盈亏平衡定律以判断节省是否值得验证成本。；设计三步部署工作流：前向筛选、边缘认证单元、种群损失界。；前向筛选用于按候选扰动保持参考预测的频率来筛选逐层位宽，质量与Hessian引导选择相当但预处理成本更低。；边缘认证单元识别可在不改变部署行为的前提下被剪枝或符号翻转的权重，并通过独立探针给出新文本上预测不变的概率界。；BCC对完整解码器提供非空种群损失界，且比压缩码路线更紧。；实验发现在相同缓存内存下，给键更高精度比给值更高精度可带来更低的NLL和更高的预测一致率。

Abstract: Activation and key-value cache precision change what a quantized language model computes without altering its stored weights. Direct weight-code bounds, however, assign identical complexity to deployments that behave differently and charge separately for weight codes that behave identically. Behavioral Capacity Certificates (BCC) charge for behavior using the aggregate prior mass of complete implementations---weights, scales, activation and cache rules---that induce the same bounded loss. When quantization merges implementations, this shared mass lowers the complexity penalty, and a break-even law determines when the saving survives the cost of validating it. BCC supports a three-step deployment workflow, and our experiments verify each step. First, a forward-only screen shortlists per-layer bit-widths by how often candidate perturbations preserve the reference predictions, with quality comparable to Hessian-guided selection at lower preprocessing cost. Second, margin-certified cells identify weights that can be pruned or sign-flipped without changing the deployed behavior: every permitted combination preserves all declared predictions, and on OLMoE-1B-7B and SmolLM2-1.7B, independent probes bound the probability that any permitted combination changes a prediction on new text. Third, BCC bounds the population loss of the deployed model, nonvacuously for complete decoders and more tightly than the compressed-code route. At equal cache memory, giving keys higher precision than values yields lower NLL and higher prediction agreement on GPT-2, Qwen2.5, and SmolLM2, together with a tighter complexity bound in the GPT-2 audit.

</details>


### [126] [Delta-Matching: Closing the Final Gap of Native 8-bit Training for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37852)
*Haozhan Tang,Hao Kang,Han Cai,Song Han,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Delta-Matching通过恢复softmax梯度的零行和不变性，解决原生FP8注意力训练中前向-反向不一致导致的stale delta问题，在569M至5.29B参数规模上使FP8训练匹配BF16/FP32的损失与下游性能，无需架构改动或辅助输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生FP8注意力训练中前向-反向不一致导致的"stale delta"问题会扭曲训练动态，造成大模型（1.67B/5.29B）损失上升和下游性能退化，而小模型与短训练可能掩盖该问题。

Method: 论文针对Transformer注意力中的FP8训练数值路径。输入为token序列，注意力核心计算包括QK^T、softmax、与V相乘等矩阵乘法。在原生block-scaled FP8下，前向与反向使用不同的量化/缩放策略，导致反向传播中用于softmax梯度的delta并非由当前前向输出精确重算，而是使用过时或近似的stale delta，破坏了softmax梯度每行和为零的不变性，从而在训练中累积优化误差。Delta-Matching通过推导前向-反向不一致的来源，并构造一种匹配机制，在给定数值假设下证明其可恢复softmax梯度的零行和不变性；由此所有注意力核心矩阵乘法的前向与反向均可使用block-scaled FP8，无需改变模型架构、不需要更小的全局batch、也不需要额外的辅助前向输出。训练目标仍为标准的语言建模交叉熵，优化器与训练流程不变；推理路径未涉及低精度推理，但训练后模型可直接以常规精度或后续量化流程部署。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "训练损失差距", "value": "stale-delta hybrid在569M参数下损失差距适中，但在1.67B与5.29B下损失显著上升并出现下游性能退化", "setting": "stale-delta hybrid FP8注意力训练，对比BF16/FP32混合精度"}, {"metric": "训练损失与下游性能", "value": "Delta-Matching在测试的架构、规模与训练阶段上匹配BF16/FP32混合精度训练的损失与整体下游性能", "setting": "多架构、多规模（569M/1.67B/5.29B）、多训练阶段的原生block-scaled FP8训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 推导并实证前向-反向不一致产生stale delta的机制及其对训练动态的扭曲；提出Delta-Matching，在给定数值假设下证明其恢复softmax梯度的零行和（zero-row-sum）不变性；无需架构改动、更小全局batch或辅助前向输出，即可在注意力核心矩阵乘法的所有前向与反向中启用原生block-scaled FP8；系统评估QK normalization、NoPE、低学习率上下文扩展等措施仅能缓解或延迟退化，但不能消除

Abstract: Reliable FP8 attention remains a barrier to fully native 8-bit large language model training. We derive how forward-backward inconsistencies produce stale delta and empirically show how it distorts training dynamics. Our stale-delta hybrid runs show a modest loss gap at 569M parameters but substantial loss increases and downstream degradation at 1.67B and 5.29B. QK normalization, NoPE (no positional encoding), and lower-learning-rate context extension mitigate or delay degradation without eliminating it. This pattern suggests accumulated optimization error that smaller models and short runs can conceal. We propose Delta-Matching, proving that it restores the softmax gradient's zero-row-sum invariant under the stated numerical assumptions. It enables native block-scaled FP8 in every forward and backward attention-core matmul without architectural changes, smaller global batches, or auxiliary forward outputs. Across tested architectures, scales, and training stages, Delta-Matching matches BF16/FP32 mixed-precision training loss and overall downstream performance. We will release our implementation, trained models, and data recipes.

</details>


### [127] [Scaling Influence Functions in LLMs through Eigenbasis-Corrected One-Bit Gradient Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37842)
*Jaeseung Heo,J Rosser,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EOGP方法，通过EK-FAC降维、PCA压缩和1-bit量化，在极低存储预算下保持LLM影响力函数估计的准确性，实验显示在GPT-2和OLMo 2模型上优于存储开销更大的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）规模下，如何压缩训练梯度以在有限存储内保留对未来未知查询的准确影响力估计，同时减少重复影响力计算的开销。

Method: EOGP旨在压缩训练梯度以近似影响力函数。步骤：1）使用EK-FAC（一种Kronecker因子近似曲率）降低梯度维度，将高维梯度投影到低维子空间；2）在保留的子空间内应用PCA，从训练梯度中学习压缩方向，使表示逼近最优固定维度线性表示；3）对所得坐标进行1-bit量化，从而在固定的存储预算下保留更多坐标；4）在查询时，利用存储的压缩梯度进行影响力估计。该流程解决了全梯度存储不可行的问题，并通过对未来未知查询的泛化能力提升影响力估计的准确性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预测重训练结果的准确度", "value": "优于评估的压缩基线", "setting": "GPT-2模型，每样本存储为基线的1/16"}, {"metric": "影响力估计准确度", "value": "与基线竞争", "setting": "OLMo 2 SFT模型（1B至32B参数），每样本存储为基线的1/100以上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出EOGP方法，利用EK-FAC降低梯度维度，并在保留子空间内应用PCA学习压缩方向，最后对坐标进行1-bit量化。；通过最坏情况分析刻画了最优固定维度线性表示。；在固定存储预算下实现更准确的影响力估计。

Abstract: Influence functions estimate how individual training examples affect the behavior of large language models (LLMs). Analyzing how training data influence different behaviors of an LLM involves repeated influence computation. Reusing stored training gradients reduces the computational cost, but storing full gradients is prohibitively expensive at LLM scale. We study how to compress these gradients while preserving influence estimates for future queries that are unknown at storage time. Through a worst-case analysis, we characterize the optimal fixed-dimensional linear representation and propose eigenbasis-corrected one-bit gradient projection (EOGP) to approximate it at scale. Specifically, EOGP uses EK-FAC to reduce gradient dimensionality, then applies PCA within the retained subspace to learn compression directions from the training gradients. We then apply one-bit quantization to the resulting coordinates, allowing more coordinates to be retained within a fixed storage budget. On GPT-2, EOGP predicts retraining outcomes more accurately than the evaluated compression baselines while using one-sixteenth of their per-example storage. On OLMo 2 SFT models from 1B to 32B parameters, EOGP remains competitive with the baselines allocated over 100 times as much storage per example.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond Interaction Capacity: Estimator Scaling with Recursive Models for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37905)
*Shivang Chopra,Fotis Iliopoulos,Zsolt Kira,Gaurav Menghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文重新审视CTR预测中交互容量扩展的边际收益递减问题，提出estimator scaling新思路与参数高效递归模型RECAP。RECAP通过独立模型蒸馏、训练轨迹EMA和推理路径聚合三层机制获得估计器多样性，在多个标准benchmark上达到SOTA并落在有利的性能-参数量帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTR预测主要通过加深交叉网络或增强交叉算子来扩大单模型交互容量，但收益递减；如何在不过度增加部署成本的情况下利用多个相关估计器之间的非共享预测变异来继续提升性能。

Method: 论文提出RECAP模型，其将estimator scaling操作化于三个层次。输入为稀疏类别特征，经嵌入层表示后进入权重共享的递归骨干网络。骨干网络通过递归计算实现参数共享，在一个模型内产生多个推理路径（routes）。第一层多样性来自对独立训练的多个CTR模型进行蒸馏，将多个独立估计器的预测知识压缩进递归骨干；第二层多样性来自对训练轨迹上的模型权重进行指数移动平均（EMA），利用训练过程中不同时间点的权重作为不同估计器；第三层多样性来自推理阶段对递归骨干内部多个推理路径的输出进行聚合。最终预测由这些层次融合得到。理论分析指出，estimator scaling的收益受估计器之间非共享预测变异量控制，因此RECAP通过上述三种来源获取估计器多样性，同时以权重共享的递归结构避免维护多个完整模型，从而在部署成本不随估计器数量线性增长的前提下提升预测性能。训练目标未在摘要中详细披露，但流程包含独立模型蒸馏与EMA更新；推理时通过路径聚合产生最终CTR预估值。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "SOTA predictive performance", "value": "new state-of-the-art on standard benchmarks", "setting": "多个CTR预测标准benchmark（具体指标与数值未在摘要中披露）"}, {"metric": "performance-parameter Pareto frontier", "value": "favorable", "setting": "标准benchmark上RECAP位于有利的性能-参数量帕累托前沿"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出estimator scaling新扩展方向，用多个相关估计器而非仅扩大单一预测器来提升性能。；理论分析表明estimator scaling收益由估计器间非共享预测变异量决定，并指出朴素集成会导致部署成本随规模增长。；提出参数高效实现RECAP，在权重共享递归骨干上融合三类多样性：独立训练模型蒸馏、训练轨迹的指数移动平均、推理时路径聚合。

Abstract: Click-Through Rate prediction, a core task in recommendation and advertising systems, relies on modeling interactions among sparse categorical features. Explicit cross networks are a central paradigm for CTR prediction, and recent progress has largely come from increasing the interaction capacity of a single predictor through deeper cross networks and more expressive cross operators. We revisit whether continually increasing interaction capacity remains the most effective way to improve predictive performance, and find that its benefits quickly exhibit diminishing returns even as capacity continues to grow. This motivates a complementary scaling direction that we call estimator scaling, where additional resources are used to incorporate multiple related estimators rather than only enlarging a single predictor. Through theoretical analysis, we show that the gains from estimator scaling are governed by the amount of non-shared predictive variation available across estimators. However, exploiting this variation naively can be expensive: independently trained models provide substantial estimator diversity but require deployment cost to grow with ensemble size. This motivates a parameter-efficient realization of estimator scaling that can incorporate diversity from multiple estimator sources without maintaining multiple full models. Building on this view, we introduce RECursive Averaged Predictor (RECAP), a parameter-efficient recursive CTR model that operationalizes estimator scaling at three levels: distillation across independently trained models, exponential moving averaging over training trajectories, and aggregation over inference-time routes within a weight-shared recursive backbone. Experiments across multiple benchmarks establish new state-of-the-art predictive performance on standard benchmarks, while placing the RECAP on a favorable performance-parameter Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [129] [WUSH-KV: KV Cache Quantization with Data-Adaptive Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38121)
*Jiale Chen,Vage Egiazarian,Eldar Kurtić,Torsten Hoefler,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WUSH-KV 提出一种基于数据二阶统计量的 KV cache 低比特量化方法：分别对 key/value 构造自适应线性变换，value 变换可折叠进权重，key 变换在 RoPE 后应用，并在 QuEST INT 下给出近似最优性分析；集成 SGLang 后，2-bit 量化在困惑度和下游任务上与 OSCAR 变换相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV cache 的显存与带宽开销随上下文长度和 batch size 增长，限制了长上下文推理的效率；如何在极低比特（如 2-bit）下降低 KV cache 量化误差并保持端到端质量

Method: WUSH-KV 面向 Transformer 推理中的 KV cache 压缩。输入为校准数据，用于估计 key 与 value 激活的二阶统计量。方法核心是 WUSH：在矩阵乘积的两个因子（key/value 激活与对应权重/投影）上分别根据二阶统计量构造数据自适应的线性变换，使量化后的乘积误差最小化。论文为 key 和 value 构建独立的变换：value 的变换可折叠进模型权重，从而在推理时不增加额外计算；key 的变换在 RoPE 之后应用，以适配位置编码后的 key 分布。变换可与截断量化器组合；对 QuEST INT 量化器，论文在温和假设下证明 WUSH 变换近似最优。部署路径为将 WUSH-KV 集成进 SGLang，配以 OSCAR 风格的百分位截断仿射量化，在 2-bit 低比特下完成端到端评测。优化目标是从二阶统计意义下最小化量化对矩阵乘积的重建误差，从而降低逐层重建误差与端到端困惑度，并保持下游任务性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "逐层重建误差", "value": "降低（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "与其它被测 transform 对比"}, {"metric": "端到端困惑度（perplexity）", "value": "最低（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "与其它被测 transform 对比"}, {"metric": "2-bit 下下游任务表现", "value": "与 OSCAR transform 相当或更优", "setting": "所有评测模型与下游任务；集成于 SGLang，OSCAR 风格百分位截断仿射量化"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 WUSH-KV：利用矩阵乘积两因子二阶统计量构造数据自适应变换，用于低比特 KV cache 量化；对 key 与 value 分别构造独立变换，value 变换折叠进模型权重、key 变换在 RoPE 之后应用；在 QuEST INT 量化器下给出理论分析，证明 WUSH 变换在温和假设下近似最优；将 WUSH-KV 集成进 SGLang 推理引擎，采用 OSCAR 风格的百分位截断仿射量化进行端到端评测

Abstract: KV cache memory and bandwidth costs grow with context length and batch size, which limits efficient long-context inference. To address this bottleneck, we introduce WUSH-KV for low-bit KV-cache quantization. It adapts WUSH, which constructs a data-aware transform from the second-order statistics of both factors in a matrix product to reduce quantization error. WUSH-KV uses calibration data to construct separate key and value transforms, with the value transform folded into the model weights and the key transform applied after RoPE. The transforms can be paired with clipped quantizers. For one such quantizer, QuEST INT, we show that, under mild assumptions, the WUSH transform is near-optimal. With this quantizer, WUSH-KV reduces layerwise reconstruction error and achieves the lowest end-to-end perplexity among other tested transforms. For end-to-end evaluation, we integrate WUSH-KV into SGLang using OSCAR-style percentile-clipped affine quantization. At 2-bit, WUSH-KV performs comparably to or outperforms the OSCAR transform across all evaluated models and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [130] [LeapQuant: Efficient Linear Attention with Accurate Recurrent State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38166)
*Yi Pan,Haocheng Xi,Kan Zhu,Xingyang Li,Yibo Wu,Mayank Mishra,Hongtao Zhang,William X.Zheng,Baris Kasikci,Song Han,Kurt Keutzer,Rishabh Iyer,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeapQuant提出免训练的8比特线性注意力递归状态量化方案，通过per-window量化抑制误差累积、用高精度Compensator Tokens保留outlier，在Qwen/Kimi/GLM上实现与FP32相当的精度，并在B200/RTX 5090上取得内核级2.05–3.70倍、端到端1.47倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力模型将上下文压缩为固定大小的递归状态，但反复读写该状态成为长上下文推理的主要瓶颈；直接对状态做低比特量化会因rounding误差累积以及状态中outlier行/列的存在而显著损害模型质量。

Method: LeapQuant面向采用线性注意力（Gated DeltaNet、Kimi Delta Attention等）的混合LLM推理流程。这类模型把上下文压缩为固定大小的递归状态，推理时每个token都需要读取并更新该状态，成为长上下文的主要开销来源。LeapQuant在推理阶段对递归状态做8比特量化，但采用两个机制避免质量下降。第一是per-window quantization：把连续token切分为窗口，在窗口内不做状态量化，状态只以低比特形式被读取使用，同时把窗口内各token产生的高精度更新缓存在缓冲区中；到窗口结束（leap）时，将缓冲区中的高精度更新与当前状态合并，并只做一次量化，从而把量化误差的产生频率从每token一次降低到每窗口一次，抑制rounding误差随token累积。第二是Compensator Tokens：在量化前识别状态中数值最大的outlier行/列，把这些离群分量保留为少量高精度补偿token，参与与真实token相同的状态更新路径，从而在低比特主体中保留关键信息。随后对剩余部分先做smoothing再做量化，以减小单次量化引入的误差。整体是免训练方法，不需要修改模型权重或进行微调，推理时以低比特状态读取加高精度缓冲更新的方式计算窗口内输出，窗口边界处执行一次合并与重量化。实验在Qwen、Kimi、GLM多个模型族上验证，在NVIDIA B200、RTX PRO 6000、RTX 5090上达到内核级平均2.05–3.70倍加速、端到端1.47倍加速，精度与FP32基线相当。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: per-window quantization：跳过整个token窗口，仅在窗口末尾对状态量化一次，窗口内用固定低比特状态配合高精度缓冲的增量更新计算输出，从而抑制误差累积；Compensator Tokens：将状态中最大的outlier保留为少量高精度补偿token，与真实token共享更新路径；对剩余残差先做smoothing再量化，进一步降低每次量化的误差；免训练（training-free）方案，无需微调即可在8比特递归状态量化下达到近无损性能

Abstract: Recent LLMs increasingly adopt hybrid designs that replace standard attention with linear attention, such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN) and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA). Although they compress the context into a fixed-size recurrent state and substantially reduce the cost of long-context processing, repeatedly reading and updating that state remains a major inference bottleneck. Quantization offers a natural way to reduce this cost, but can significantly degrade model quality, due to the accumulation of rounding errors and the presence of outlier rows and columns in the state. To address these challenges, we propose LeapQuant, a training-free method that achieves near-lossless performance under 8-bit recurrent-state quantization. First, to mitigate error accumulation, we propose per-window quantization, which leaps over a window of tokens and quantizes the state only once at its end. Within a window, outputs are computed from the fixed low-bit state together with high-precision buffered updates. Second, to reduce the error introduced by each quantization, LeapQuant retains the state's largest outliers as a few high-precision Compensator Tokens, which share the update path of real tokens. We then smooth the remaining residual before quantization to further reduce the error. Comprehensive experiments across the Qwen, Kimi, and GLM model families show that LeapQuant substantially reduces memory and compute costs during inference. With accuracy comparable to the FP32 baseline, it achieves average speedups of 2.05--3.70$\times$ at the kernel level and 1.47$\times$ for end-to-end inference on NVIDIA B200, RTX PRO 6000, and RTX 5090 GPUs.

</details>


### [131] [Mira: Memory-Efficient MoE Inference Using Adaptive Caching and Predictive Expert Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38090)
*Sanjali Yadav,Bahar Asgari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mira提出面向单GPU内存受限MoE推理的算法-系统协同设计，通过提前两层的专家使用预测、HOT+STAGE两级缓存和定制专家参数压缩，实现主动预取与通信-计算重叠；报告相对SOTA最高5.71x吞吐、11.71x TTFT和3.84x beam search加速，但缺少公司、在线生产与详细资源披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE专家参数主导显存占用，而token级路由动态、不可预测且分布倾斜；现有offloading与缓存方案是反应式的，必须等待router输出后才移动专家，导致缓存利用率低、传输无法与计算充分重叠，难以在紧张VRAM预算下实现高容量MoE推理。

Method: Mira是面向单GPU内存受限场景的MoE推理算法-系统协同设计。输入为MoE模型请求的token序列，核心瓶颈在于专家参数体量大且token级路由动态倾斜。系统首先引入轻量级逐层预测器，基于当前层信息提前两个层预测后续专家使用情况，从而把专家搬运从反应式转为主动式：在router输出到达前即开始预取预测专家。预测结果进入两级GPU缓存HOT+STAGE：HOT层保留由token级路由遥测识别的高频专家，STAGE层暂存预测即将使用的专家，以兼顾复用与前瞻。为降低传输开销，Mira对专家参数采用定制压缩/量化格式，减少元数据并改善打包效率，论文称仅带来极小精度退化。整个系统实现为集成运行时，统一协调预测器、缓存替换策略和量化传输，使专家通信与GPU计算尽可能重叠，减少专家导致的停顿。推理路径上，请求按层推进：预测器提前发出预取，缓存管理决定HOT/STAGE驻留与换入换出，量化传输引擎完成专家参数搬运，计算在专家就绪后执行，从而在有限VRAM下支持更高容量MoE。论文未披露预测器具体结构、训练目标、损失函数、量化比特宽度和运行时调度细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出轻量级逐层预测器，提前两层预测专家使用情况，实现主动预取而非等待router输出的反应式搬运。；设计HOT+STAGE两级GPU缓存，由token级路由遥测驱动，保留高频专家并暂存预测专家。；为专家参数定制压缩/量化格式，减少元数据并提升打包效率，同时尽量降低精度损失。；实现全集成运行时，协调预测器、缓存策略与量化传输，以最大化通信与计算重叠。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are a compelling architecture for scaling model capacity, making them especially attractive for deployment on resource-constrained, single-GPU systems. However, this benefit is difficult to realize because expert parameters dominate memory, and token-level routing is dynamic, unpredictable, and skewed. Prior work using offloading and caching remains fundamentally reactive, as systems wait for router outputs before moving experts, leading to inefficient cache utilization and an inability to overlap transfers with compute under tight VRAM budgets. To address these challenges, we propose Mira, an algorithm-system co-design that enables high-capacity MoE inference on a single GPU. Mira shifts from a reactive to a proactive stance by coupling predictive expert management with a tailored quantization format. It introduces lightweight per-layer predictors that anticipate expert usage two layers ahead, enabling proactive prefetching. These predictions feed a two-tier HOT+STAGE GPU cache managed by token-level routing telemetry to retain frequently used experts while staging predicted ones. To minimize transfer overhead, Mira implements a custom compression for expert parameters, which reduces metadata and improves packing efficiency, while minimally degrading accuracy. Mira is implemented as a fully integrated runtime that coordinates predictors, caching policies, and quantized transfers to maximize overlap between communication and compute. Our experiments show that Mira reduces expert-induced stalls. Compared against state-of-the-art baselines, Mira achieves a 5.71x speedup in average throughput on a memory-constrained GPU. It accelerates Time-to-First-Token by 11.71x and achieves a 3.84$x average speedup in beam search inference, demonstrating its effectiveness across diverse inference scenarios.

</details>


### [132] [Probe-Space Preconditioning for Fast and Stable Zero-Order Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38095)
*Francois Chaubard,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Chris Ré*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 零阶优化训练大模型可节省约10倍显存，但收敛慢。本文通过大有效批量、多探针、少步数策略，以及1.5-SPSA对角预条件器，实现比MeZO和BP更好的收敛，并用系统优化实现在A100上训练OPT-30B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化（ZOO）训练大语言模型时收敛速度落后于反向传播，且其收敛问题未被充分解决，需要更少步数、更少训练计算达到或超越BP的效果。

Method: 论文面向零阶优化训练。输入为语言模型和训练数据，在推理模式（无梯度存储、无激活缓存、无优化器状态）下进行训练。基线1SPSA对每个批次产生多个随机扰动（探针），通过前向传播计算损失变化来估计梯度方向，并用大有效批量/多探针但少步数的策略优化计算效率。1.5-SPSA在此基础上额外增加一个干净前向传播，利用该前向传播的输出在探针空间计算廉价的逐维对角预条件子，从而对高曲率方向进行降权，提升收敛速率与最终收敛。训练目标遵循原始损失。工程上利用8-bit压缩随机生成器减少随机数内存，Triton融合解包/应用核函数加速探针的生成与参数更新，分布式并行扩展至OPT-30B规模。推理/服务路径未涉及。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "SST-2 accuracy", "value": "+3.1% (相比MeZO和BP)", "setting": "OPT-13B，仅70步 vs MeZO的100,000步"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 重分配训练计算预算：将更多计算花在大有效批量、更多扰动（probes）但更少步数上，使1SPSA以更少训练计算超越MeZO。；提出1.5-SPSA：在1SPSA基础上每步增加一次干净前向传播，在探针空间计算对角预条件器，通过降低高曲率方向权重改善收敛速率和最终收敛。；工程实现：8-bit压缩随机生成器、Triton融合解包/应用核函数和分布式并行，实现OPT-30B在商品级A100 GPU上的快速稳定原地训练。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) dominates deep learning but imposes a massive memory tax. For example, training OPT-30B with Adam requires $\approx$ 600GB of GPU memory (assuming batch size 8 and sequence length 2048). Alternatively, zero-order optimization (ZOO) trains in inference-mode (requiring only $\approx$ 60GB for the same model): no stored activations, no gradients, and no optimizer states. However, ZOO convergence has lagged behind BP. In this work, we evaluate two methods to close this gap. First, we show that reallocating training compute budget from many steps to large effective batch sizes with many perturbations (or probes) but fewer steps, allows 1SPSA (Spall, 1992) to outperform zero order methods like MeZO (Malladi et al., 2023) with less training compute. Next, we introduce 1.5-SPSA, adding a single "clean" forward-pass per step to 1SPSA to calculate a cheap diagonal preconditioner in probe-space, which improves convergence rate and convergence by down-weighting high curvature directions. Benchmarking on 6 post-training datasets on both Qwen3 and OPT model families, we show that 1.5-SPSA achieves State-of-the-Art results over previous ZOO solvers with much less optimization steps. For example, we train OPT-13B (for direct comparison to MeZO) and find 1.5-SPSA achieves +3.1% accuracy on SST-2 over both MeZO and BP in only 70 steps vs. MeZO's 100,000 steps. Finally, we combine an 8-bit-packing random generator, triton fused unpack/apply kernels, and distributed parallelism to achieve fast and stable training of models as large as OPT-30B in-place on commodity GPUs (e.g. A100).

</details>


### [133] [Alpha Diffusion Language Models: Factorization Alone Is Not the Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38066)
*Nikita Gushchin,Dmitry Baranchuk,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Alpha扩散语言模型（AlphaDLM），通过序列级alpha损失解决离散扩散语言模型中并行生成预测不一致的问题。该损失在alpha趋于0时恢复交叉熵，在alpha=1时具有联合模式最优。在TinyGSM训练后，GSM8K仅用4次模型评估达到34.6%准确率，并扩展到SDAR-1.7B在代码与数学基准上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散语言模型在减少去噪步数时预测不一致：标准交叉熵训练拟合条件token边际，而并行生成需要一致的联合预测。

Method: AlphaDLM基于离散扩散语言模型框架。输入为token序列，模型通过迭代去噪步骤并行预测多个token。标准训练使用交叉熵拟合条件token边际，导致并行生成时联合预测不一致。本文提出序列级alpha损失，该损失在alpha趋于0时退化为交叉熵，在alpha=1时具有联合模式最优。通过调整alpha，可以在保持多个有效完成的同时排除无效token组合。训练时在TinyGSM数据集上优化该目标，推理时使用固定步数（如4步）进行并行去噪生成。方法进一步扩展到SDAR-1.7B模型，在代码和数学基准上评估准确率与计算量的权衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "GSM8K准确率", "value": "34.6%", "setting": "在TinyGSM上训练，仅4次模型评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出序列级alpha损失，在alpha趋于0时恢复交叉熵，在alpha=1时具有联合模式最优；理论分析目标函数与因子分解如何共同决定拟合分布，并刻画中间alpha保留多个有效完成同时排除无效token组合的条件；在TinyGSM上训练，GSM8K仅4次模型评估达到34.6%准确率；将方法扩展到SDAR-1.7B并在代码与数学基准上评估

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models can generate multiple tokens in parallel, but reducing the number of denoising steps can lead to inconsistent predictions. Standard cross-entropy training fits conditional token marginals, whereas parallel generation requires consistent joint predictions. We introduce Alpha Diffusion Language Models (AlphaDLM), trained with a sequence-level alpha loss that recovers cross-entropy in the limit of vanishing alpha and has a joint-mode optimum at alpha one. Our analysis characterizes how the objective and factorization jointly determine the fitted distribution. We identify conditions under which intermediate alpha preserves multiple valid completions while excluding invalid token combinations. Trained on TinyGSM, our method achieves 34.6% accuracy on GSM8K with only four model evaluations. We further scale the method to SDAR-1.7B and evaluate it on code and mathematics benchmarks. These results show that changing the training objective can improve the accuracy-computation trade-off of factorized diffusion language models.

</details>


### [134] [Challenges and Solutions for Bandits in the Wild: Warm-Started Mixture Bandits for Cross-Cohort Slate Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37800)
*Serafima Lebedeva,Sumantrak Mukherjee,Ali Arshad Sadal,Ilias Ekşi,Rahul Sharma,Julia Mueller,Theresa Dombrowski,Jakob Karolus,Viktor Bengs,Eyke Hüllermeier,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CohortMix-TS，一种warm-started混合bandit，用于跨队列slate推荐。在校园游戏问答应用中进行了25天随机在线部署（713名用户），结果显示跨队列迁移改善早期推荐质量和用户级regret，库存感知slate构建有助于防止偏好物品过早耗尽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动用户群体中，如何从有限反馈快速学习用户偏好，并在用户目录有限且可能重复/耗尽的情况下持续提供有效推荐。

Method: CohortMix-TS从早期队列学习潜在用户组，利用可用元数据为新用户构建组信息先验。以这些固定先验为起点，随着每个用户的反馈逐渐个性化。会话slate结合Thompson采样与多样性和库存耗尽控制。系统通过模拟、半合成实验和25天随机在线部署进行评估。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "early-to-late change in correctness", "value": "treatment用户比随机推荐用户有更大的早期到晚期正确率变化（具体数值未披露）", "setting": "25天随机在线部署，713名注册参与者，校园游戏问答应用"}]}

Conclusion: CohortMix-TS：基于warm-start的混合bandit，从早期群体学习潜在用户组，并用可用元数据为新用户构建组信息先验，之后独立个性化；会话slate结合Thompson采样与多样性和库存耗尽控制；跨群体迁移与库存感知slate构建的框架

Abstract: Many recommender services repeatedly encounter cold-start cohorts, where new users arrive with little or no interaction history. This creates two challenges: learning user preferences quickly from limited feedback and sustaining useful recommendations when each user has a finite catalog that can become repetitive or depleted over time. We propose CohortMix-TS, a warm-started mixture bandit that learns latent user groups from earlier cohorts and uses available metadata to construct group-informed priors for new users. Starting from these fixed priors, the model personalizes independently as feedback from each user becomes available. Session slates combine Thompson sampling with diversity and inventory-depletion controls. We evaluate CohortMix-TS through simulation, semi-synthetic experiments, and a 25-day randomized in-the-wild deployment with 713 registered participants in a Campus Games quiz application. Our evaluations show that cross-cohort transfer improves early recommendation quality and user-level regret, while inventory-aware slate construction helps prevent premature exhaustion of preferred items. In the field deployment, treatment users also showed a larger early-to-late change in correctness than users receiving random recommendations. Together, these results show how warm-start transfer and inventory-aware recommendations can support personalization for short-lived, repeatedly cold-starting cohorts.

</details>


### [135] [Graph-Conditioned On-Policy Agent Distillation from Off-the-Shelf Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37522)
*Xiaohan Yi,Wen Luo,Yani Huang,Junfeng Zhan,Asher Qin,Peilin Zhao,Xi Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GC-OPD 通过图结构索引重复的教师执行并按共享状态检索证据，增强现成教师在多轮 agent 蒸馏中的评分上下文，在不优化教师的前提下显著提升 4B 学生在 ScienceWorld、ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮任务中，on-policy distillation 由于学生自生成轨迹的复合误差会偏离教师的有效监督范围，导致学生表现受限。

Method: GC-OPD 在标准 on-policy distillation 框架上增加了一个执行证据图。流程为：首先，对现成教师模型在任务上进行多次重复执行，将执行过程按共享状态进行索引，构成一个图结构，图中同时保留完整的成功历史和失败历史。学生模型在环境中生成自己的多轮轨迹（episode）后，GC-OPD 根据学生当前所在状态，从图中检索对应的当前状态参考或历史替代轨迹；随后把这些检索到的证据与学生自己的 hindsight 结合，形成增强的评分上下文，交给原始教师模型，对学生在原始轨迹中产生的 thought-action token 进行评分。该评分作为蒸馏信号更新学生模型。训练目标仍是让学生模仿教师的 token 级反馈，但教师评分时获得了执行证据的增强，从而缓解学生偏离教师有效监督范围的问题。推理或部署时，学生模型直接按常规多轮交互方式执行任务，不需要图检索或教师参与。整个方法不需要针对任务优化教师模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean success", "value": "从 24.70% 提升到 48.78%", "setting": "ScienceWorld，4B 学生模型，与 vanilla OPD 对比"}, {"metric": "mean success", "value": "从 53.36% 提升到 85.26%", "setting": "ALFWorld Unseen，与 vanilla OPD 对比"}, {"metric": "mean success", "value": "从 29.10% 提升到 37.65%", "setting": "WebShop，与 vanilla OPD 对比"}, {"metric": "mean success", "value": "GC-OPD 高于所有评估的 OPD 基线（最强 ScienceWorld 4B 基线为 46.66%）", "setting": "相同学生规模下，与使用 GRPO 训练教师的 OPD 基线在 ScienceWorld 和 ALFWorld 上对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用图结构索引重复的教师执行，按共享状态组织并保留完整的成功与失败历史；每个学生 episode 后检索当前状态参考或历史替代方案，并与学生 hindsight 结合，对原始 thought-action token 进行评分；在不针对任务优化教师的前提下，用现成教师提升多轮 agent 蒸馏效果

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains compact language agents with teacher feedback on student-generated trajectories. In multi-turn tasks, compounding errors can move students beyond the teacher's effective supervision. We introduce Graph-Conditioned On-Policy Agent Distillation (GC-OPD), which enriches an off-the-shelf teacher's scoring context with execution evidence. A graph indexes repeated teacher executions by shared states while preserving complete successful and failed histories. After each student episode, GC-OPD retrieves current-state references or historical alternatives and combines them with student hindsight to score the original thought-action tokens. Using the same original teachers, GC-OPD improves mean success over vanilla OPD from 24.70% to 48.78% on ScienceWorld (4B student), from 53.36% to 85.26% on ALFWorld Unseen, and from 29.10% to 37.65% on WebShop. At matched student sizes, it also achieves higher mean success than every evaluated OPD baseline using GRPO-trained teachers on ScienceWorld and ALFWorld; the strongest such ScienceWorld 4B baseline reaches 46.66%. GC-OPD requires no task-specific teacher optimization.

</details>


### [136] [Rethinking Soft Tokens for Parallel Decoding in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37391)
*Kodai Kawamura,Kenji Kawaguchi,Anji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散语言模型并行解码中token不一致问题，论文系统研究了软令牌反馈机制，指出常规构造与预训练嵌入空间的几何不匹配，提出免训练的几何感知软令牌构造，并在四个预训练模型和四个数学/代码基准上验证其优于标准并行解码和欧几里得软令牌基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在并行解码时可能生成个体合理但相互不一致的多个token，现有软令牌方法虽能保留预测不确定性，但其改善并行解码的机制尚未被系统研究，且常规软令牌构造与预训练嵌入空间存在几何不匹配。

Method: 论文在冻结的预训练扩散语言模型上研究软令牌反馈，不进行额外训练。输入为并行解码过程中上一步的预测分布，传统做法将不确定位置用连续嵌入（软令牌）表示并反馈给模型。作者发现这种常规软令牌构造与预训练嵌入空间存在几何不匹配，因此提出几何感知的软令牌构造：在免训练条件下，根据预训练嵌入空间的几何结构来构建软令牌输入，使其与模型已学习的表示空间对齐。该软令牌作为下一步去噪的输入，影响后续预测。通过分析软令牌反馈对后续预测的重塑作用，作者提供经验证据表明其改善并行解码的原因不仅是保留不确定性，还在于偏好连贯的token序列。方法在四个预训练扩散语言模型和四个数学与代码基准上进行了评估，与标准并行解码及免训练的欧几里得软令牌基线进行比较。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别出常规软令牌构造与预训练嵌入空间之间的几何不匹配；提出免训练、几何感知的软令牌构造方法；通过实证分析表明软令牌反馈不仅保留不确定性，还倾向于生成连贯的token序列，从而改善并行解码

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) enable parallel generation by predicting and committing multiple tokens at each denoising step, yet they can generate individually plausible but mutually inconsistent tokens. Recent work shows that \emph{soft tokens} can mitigate this issue by representing uncertain positions with continuous embeddings built from the model's predictive distribution at the previous decoding step. However, although soft tokens are commonly understood as preserving predictive uncertainty, how soft-token feedback improves parallel decoding has not been systematically examined. In this paper, we investigate this question in frozen pretrained DLMs to examine soft-token feedback without the effects of additional training. To construct soft-token inputs in a training-free setting, we identify a geometric mismatch between conventional soft-token construction and the pretrained embedding space. Based on this observation, we propose a training-free, geometry-aware construction of soft tokens. Our analysis of soft-token feedback suggests that uncertainty preservation alone does not fully explain how it reshapes subsequent predictions. To better explain how soft-token feedback improves parallel decoding, we provide empirical evidence that it favors coherent token sequences. Across four pretrained DLMs and four math and code benchmarks, our method outperforms standard parallel decoding and a training-free Euclidean soft-token baseline. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [137] [REVO: Rollout-Efficient Off-Policy Distillation via Variance-Guided Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37500)
*Yuxiao Yang,Shangzhe Li,Tianrun Yu,Kaixiang Zhao,Taylor W. Killian,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REVO reduces on-policy distillation rollout cost by reusing student rollouts across multiple learner updates, with stabilized prefix weighting, one-step resampling, and variance-guided token selection. It matches or exceeds OPD baselines with 50 vs 200 rollout iterations on reasoning benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation requires frequent student rollouts, which incurs substantial generation cost. REVO aims to reduce rollout cost by reusing each student rollout for multiple learner updates while addressing the policy mismatch introduced by off-policy reuse.

Method: REVO is an off-policy distillation framework for training language models with dense token-level teacher supervision. Inputs are student-generated trajectories and teacher log-probabilities. The core data flow reuses each student rollout for multiple learner updates instead of regenerating full trajectories every step. Two mismatch sources are explicitly addressed: prefix-level mismatch, mitigated by stabilized prefix weighting; and current-token mismatch, mitigated by one-step resampling from the current student. To prioritize informative token positions inside reused rollouts, REVO computes the variance of the student-teacher log-probability ratio, using it as a measure of remaining token-level learning signal to guide repeated optimization. Training uses these reweighted and selectively resampled token signals under an off-policy distillation objective. The stated inference or serving path is standard LM inference after distillation; no deployment details are disclosed. Experimental evaluation covers multiple student-teacher scales and both in-domain and cross-domain reasoning benchmarks.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "rollout iterations to match or exceed on-policy distillation baselines", "value": "50 rollout iterations vs 200 OPD iterations", "setting": "multiple student-teacher scales, in-domain and cross-domain reasoning benchmarks"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Off-policy distillation framework that reuses each student rollout for multi-step learner updates；Stabilized prefix weighting to address prefix-level policy mismatch；One-step resampling from the current student to address current-token policy mismatch；Variance of student-teacher log-probability ratio used to quantify remaining token-level learning signal and guide repeated optimization

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains language models using dense token-level teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, its reliance on frequently refreshed student rollouts often incurs substantial generation cost. We introduce REVO, an off-policy distillation framework that improves rollout efficiency by reusing each student rollout for multi-step learner updates. REVO addresses prefix-level and current-token policy mismatch through stabilized prefix weighting and one-step resampling from the current student, which enables repeated updates without regenerating full trajectories. To prioritize informative token positions within reused rollouts, REVO uses the variance of the student-teacher log-probability ratio to quantify the remaining token-level learning signal and guide repeated optimization. Across multiple student-teacher scales, REVO with only 50 rollout iterations matches or exceeds OPD baselines trained for 200 iterations on both in-domain and cross-domain reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [138] [$S^3$: Spectral Null-Space Swap Makes Reasoning Models Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37976)
*Hongbo Ma,Sansheng Cao,Jiajun Fan,Bangji Yang,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S^3 发现 Thinking 模型相对 Non-thinking 模型的权重差中位于零空间的分量是推理效率的关键，据此提出训练无关的模型组合方法，在 28 个评测环境、2B-30B 模型上平均减少 27.4% token 开销并提升 1.0 个百分点准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-Thought 推理模型虽然准确率高，但 token 开销过大；如何在无需重新训练的情况下提升推理效率并保持准确率。

Method: 论文针对成对的 Non-thinking 与 Thinking 检查点进行训练无关的组合。方法基于奇异值/投影分析：将 Non-thinking 模型的权重矩阵按主导奇异方向定义投影，其零空间补集对应 Thinking 模型引入的推理能力分量。S^3 在权重层面将 Non-thinking 检查点保留在其自身主导子空间，而将 Thinking 检查点仅用于零空间之外的部分，从而保留推理增益的同时减少冗余 token 生成。推理时直接使用组合后的检查点，无需额外训练或微调。论文还通过注意力熵分析说明保留分量导致更集中的注意力，并用简化优化分析模型论证零空间分量降低注意力熵，从而提升推理效率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理 token 开销", "value": "平均降低 27.4%", "setting": "对比完整 Thinking 模型，跨 28 个评测环境，2B-30B 密集与 MoE 架构"}, {"metric": "整体任务准确率", "value": "平均提升 1.0 个百分点", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "HMMT25 准确率", "value": "+8.3%", "setting": "同时获得 33.0% token 加速"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现 Thinking 模型与 Non-thinking 模型权重差中位于 Non-thinking 模型主导奇异方向零空间的分量对推理能力关键，移除该分量可提升效率而不损失 Thinking 后训练带来的准确率。；提出 Spectral Null-Space Swap (S^3)：训练无关地将 Non-thinking 检查点保留在其自身主导子空间，将 Thinking 检查点交换到零空间之外，实现效率与准确率兼顾。；在数学、多模态、音频推理等 28 个评测环境上验证并建立新的帕累托前沿，平均减少 27.4% 推理 token 开销并提升 1.0 个百分点整体准确率。

Abstract: LLMs trained with Chain-of-thought excel in reasoning capability, but often come with excessive token cost. We find that the core of reasoning capacity lies in the Thinking model's weight component within the null space of a projection defined by the corresponding Non-thinking model's dominant singular directions, and removing the subspace component can largely improve reasoning efficiency without hurting the accuracy gained during thinking-mode post-training. Unlike existing efforts that mostly operate within the dominant subspace, we are the first to unveil the critical role of the null space and harness it for model optimization. Motivated by this finding, we propose Spectral Null-Space Swap ($S^3$), a training-free composition of paired Non-thinking and Thinking checkpoints. Our method keeps the Non-thinking model inside its own dominant subspace and takes the Thinking checkpoint outside it, improving reasoning efficiency while maintaining accuracy. We extensively evaluate $S^3$ on 2B-30B dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures spanning 28 evaluation environments across mathematical, multimodal, and audio reasoning domains. $S^3$ establishes new empirical Pareto Frontiers among training-free model composition strategies: across all settings, it reduces inference token overhead by an average of 27.4% compared to full Thinking models while simultaneously improving overall task accuracy by 1.0 percentage point (e.g., yielding +8.3% accuracy on HMMT25 alongside a 33.0% token speedup). We further use attention entropy for explanation and find that the retained component produces more concentrated attention, and we use a simplified analytical model about optimization to demonstrate why null-space can effectively reduce attention entropy, thereby improving the efficiency of reasoning.

</details>


### [139] [Dr. OPD: Learning What to Follow for Optimal On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38025)
*Zhenyu Wang,Tianze Wang,Linjun Zhang,Yifan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Dr. OPD，将在线策略蒸馏建模为双层优化，通过动态加权 token 级教师监督最大化学生期望奖励，在数学与代码任务上强到弱蒸馏较 vanilla OPD 平均数学性能提升 9.7 分，并使学生超越更大教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线策略蒸馏（OPD）对所有 token 的教师监督信号同等对待，忽略了不同 token 的教师信号对学生最终表现影响差异巨大：部分信号能纠正关键推理错误，部分几乎不影响最终答案。

Method: Dr. OPD 的输入为学生模型生成的响应以及更强教师模型在这些 token 上的密集监督信号。整体架构包含两层：内层为学生策略在 token 级加权教师监督下进行 on-policy 蒸馏训练；外层为权重优化器，选择每个 token 的权重以最大化最终学生策略的期望奖励。训练流程采用迭代式求解：每一轮先根据当前学生策略与教师信号闭式更新 token 权重，再基于该加权 OPD 目标对学生的策略参数执行一步梯度更新。该过程交替进行，直到收敛。目标是最大化学生最终答案的期望奖励，而非简单地拟合教师分布。该架构通过差异化加权缓解了 vanilla OPD 对所有教师信号等权处理导致的关键推理错误未被充分纠正、无效信号占用梯度预算的问题。论文在数学与代码任务上验证了强到弱和同尺寸蒸馏，但未披露参数规模、训练硬件或推理延迟等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "average math performance", "value": "+9.7 points over vanilla OPD", "setting": "strong-to-weak distillation"}, {"metric": "average math performance", "value": "student surpasses its larger teacher", "setting": "strong-to-weak distillation"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 OPD 形式化为双层优化问题：内层学生按加权教师监督学习，外层权重以最大化学生期望奖励为目标。；提出高效迭代求解器，每轮闭式更新 token 权重、再对加权 OPD 目标执行一步梯度更新。；在正则条件下证明加权更新比 vanilla OPD 更新获得更高的期望奖励。；在数学与代码的强到弱、同尺寸蒸馏设置中一致超越基线；强到弱设置下平均数学性能较 vanilla OPD 提升 9.7 分，并使学生超越更大的教师模型。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own generated responses using dense, token-level supervision from a stronger teacher. Vanilla OPD treats all teacher signals equally, assuming that the teacher's supervision is equally important for every token. However, teacher signals at different tokens may have very different effects on the student's performance: some correct important reasoning errors, while others have little effect on the final answer. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Dr. OPD (OPD Done Right), which defines the optimal weighted OPD to maximize the student's performance. We formulate Dr. OPD as a bilevel optimization problem in which the student learns from weighted teacher supervision, while the weights are selected to maximize the expected reward of the resulting student. To solve Dr. OPD, we develop an efficient iterative solver that updates the token weights and student policy alternatively. At each round, it updates weights in closed form and then takes one gradient step on the resulting weighted OPD objective. Under regularity conditions, we show that this weighted update achieves a higher expected reward than a vanilla OPD update. Empirically, across strong-to-weak and same-size distillation on math and code, Dr. OPD consistently outperforms all evaluated baselines. In particular, in the strong-to-weak distillation setting, Dr. OPD improves average math performance by $9.7$ points over vanilla OPD, and enables the smaller student to surpass its larger teacher.

</details>


### [140] [On Trajectory-Aware Training for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37974)
*Manuel Madeira,Amitis Shidani,Alice Bizeul,Victor Turrisi,Louis Béthune,Bhavika Devnani,Dan Busbridge,Pierre Ablin,João Monteiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PUMBA，一种面向掩码扩散语言模型的轨迹感知训练框架。通过在策略诱导轨迹的连续步上训练、传递连续信息并利用时间反向传播联合优化，缩小训练与推理的条件错配。在 LLaDA-8B 的监督微调实验中，全画布生成匹配性能下最多减少 22% 的 NFE，块扩散生成最多减少 26% 的 NFE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型在训练时使用随机掩码序列，而推理时遵循由模型自身预测形成的轨迹，且每一步无法获取前一步的计算信息，导致训练与推理条件不匹配，影响生成质量与推理效率（函数评估次数 NFE）。

Method: PUMBA 面向掩码扩散语言模型，其核心流程为：输入一段被随机掩码的 token 序列，去噪器（denoiser）预测被掩码位置的概率分布。与标准 MDM 训练使用独立随机掩码不同，PUMBA 在策略诱导的轨迹上训练：采样阶段模型按自身策略逐步解掩码若干 token，形成一条多步轨迹；训练时则取出轨迹中连续两个（或多个）步骤的掩码状态，让去噪器在这些相邻步骤上学习。步骤之间不传递离散 token，而是传递连续信息（例如前一步的解掩码分布或嵌入），避免使用直通估计器等离散梯度近似带来的偏差。所有步骤的损失通过时间反向传播（BPTT）联合优化，使得早期步骤的表示能接收到后续步骤的梯度信号。理论上，BPTT 跨度越大，梯度估计越接近轨迹的全局目标，性能随之提升。推理时，模型以自回归方式逐步解掩码（全画布或块扩散），由于训练与推理的掩码条件更接近，且步骤间携带连续信息，模型能在更少的函数评估次数（NFE）下达到与标准微调相当的生成质量。该框架不改变基础模型架构，仅改变训练目标和信息流。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "NFE 减少比例（全画布生成）", "value": "最多 22% 更少", "setting": "在匹配性能下，与标准微调（两倍预算）相比，LLaDA-8B 监督微调，全画布生成"}, {"metric": "NFE 减少比例（块扩散生成）", "value": "最多 26% 更少", "setting": "在相同步数下，与标准微调相比，LLaDA-8B 监督微调，块扩散生成"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 统一框架 PUMBA，在策略诱导轨迹的连续步上训练去噪器，实现轨迹感知训练。；在步骤之间传递连续信息，替代离散梯度估计器（如直通估计器），减少承诺（commitment）造成的偏差。；通过时间反向传播（BPTT）联合优化多步去噪过程，并理论支持 BPTT 跨度越大性能越好。；在 LLaDA-8B 监督微调中验证方法，可同时用于全画布生成与块扩散生成，在匹配性能下减少 NFE。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate text by unmasking several tokens per step, but they are trained and sampled under different conditions. The model is trained on randomly masked sequences, whereas inference follows a trajectory shaped by the model's own predictions. Additionally, each step has no access to what the previous one computed. Recent methods narrow these limitations from separate angles, leaving open how these choices interact. We introduce PUMBA, a unified framework for trajectory-aware training that trains the denoiser on consecutive steps of policy-induced trajectories, passes information between steps, and optimizes them jointly by backpropagation through time. A controlled study of this design space shows that i) exact train--inference alignment fails due to local overfitting, whereas a looser alignment still brings training masks closer to those seen at inference; ii) passing continuous information outperforms discrete gradient estimators through the commitment at each step; and iii) performance improves as backpropagation through time spans more steps, which we support theoretically. Combined, these components match the best checkpoint of a same-size autoregressive model. Building on these findings, we scale PUMBA to supervised fine-tuning of LLaDA-8B, where it improves the trade-off between performance and number of function evaluations (NFEs) in both full-canvas and block diffusion generation. At matched performance, it needs up to 22% fewer NFEs than standard fine-tuning with twice the budget in full-canvas generation, and up to 26% fewer than standard fine-tuning for the same number of steps in block diffusion.

</details>


### [141] [Time-Anchored Diffusion Language Models: Latent-Space Caching for Fast Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37924)
*Joel Anto Paul,Litu Rout,Aditya Akella,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时间锚定扩散语言模型，通过潜空间缓存周期复用锚网络表示，在DiffusionGemma-26B上吞吐提升约49%-79%，预训练变体降低Transformer层计算最多38%，实测吞吐比ADLM高最多73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型反向去噪过程中，锚网络重复计算导致推理成本高，如何在不依赖监督重要token目标的情况下复用潜空间锚点以加速生成。

Method: TADM采用两阶段架构：一个相对昂贵的锚网络生成潜空间缓存状态，一个轻量级去噪网络在每个反向步骤中通过融合模块智能地结合缓存潜状态与当前状态。该设计将锚定解释为潜空间缓存：锚网络周期性评估，其缓存表示在多个反向步骤中复用。论文包含两种实例化：TADM:Post-train对预训练扩散语言模型进行时间锚定后训练；TADM:Pretraining在预训练期间学习时间锚点。输入为扩散过程中的token画布，锚点编码干净序列的持久属性如语义意图、全局结构或中间计划；随着token画布演变，隐藏表示会过时，但语义内容在邻近扩散时间仍有用，因此可跨步复用。训练目标为基于时间的自监督锚定，不依赖监督重要token目标。推理时，锚网络周期评估并缓存，去噪网络轻量融合缓存与当前状态，从而减少计算并提升吞吐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "吞吐量", "value": "提升约49%至79%", "setting": "在DiffusionGemma-26B上，于GSM8K、AIME26、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench-v6、HumanEval、MMLU-Pro等数学、代码和STEM基准"}, {"metric": "Transformer层计算量", "value": "相对标准单阶段DLM降低最多38%", "setting": "TADM:Pretraining"}, {"metric": "实测吞吐量", "value": "比ADLM高出最多73%", "setting": "TADM:Pretraining"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于时间的自监督锚定，学习并复用编码干净序列持久属性（语义意图、全局结构、中间计划）的潜空间锚点，无需监督重要token目标；将锚定解释为潜空间缓存：昂贵的锚网络周期性评估，其缓存表示在多个反向步骤中复用，轻量去噪网络通过融合模块结合缓存状态与当前状态；实例化TADM:Post-train对预训练DLM进行时间锚定，以及TADM:Pretraining在预训练中学习时间锚点

Abstract: Recent work on anchored diffusion language models improves denoising by shaping an intermediate latent space with supervised important-token targets. In this work, we introduce time-based (self-supervised) anchoring, which learns and reuses latent anchors without requiring such targets. Our key observation is that anchors encode persistent properties of the clean sequence, such as its semantic intent, global structure, or intermediate plan. Although their hidden representations become stale as the token canvas evolves, their semantic content remains useful across nearby diffusion times. This is implemented through a two-stage architecture consisting of a relatively expensive anchor network that generates the latent cache state and a lightweight denoising network that intelligently combines the cached latent state with the current state at each reverse step using a fusion module. This gives anchoring a latent-space caching interpretation: the anchor network is evaluated periodically, while its cached representation is reused across multiple reverse steps. We instantiate this framework as TADM:Post-train, which time-anchorizes pretrained DLMs, and TADM:Pretraining, which learns time-based anchors during pretraining. Applied to DiffusionGemma-26B, TADM:Post-train improves throughput by approximately 49% to 79% on several math, code, and STEM benchmarks (GSM8K, AIME26, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench-v6, HumanEval, MMLU-Pro). TADM:Pretraining reduces Transformer-layer computation by up to 38% relative to a standard single-stage DLM, achieves up to 73% higher measured throughput than ADLM.

</details>


### [142] [Learning Beyond What You Sample: Off-Policy-Aware Cross-Model Trajectory Exchange for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37868)
*Doohyuk Jang,Yoonsik Park,Gyouk Chu,Sihwan Park,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAFT 通过替换 RLVR 中的全失败组为 peer 模型的轨迹，并利用 off-policy 感知的加权与裁剪，在相同 rollout 预算下提升数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限 rollout 预算下，GRPO 等 RLVR 方法常遇到所有生成轨迹均失败（all-fail）的组，导致缺乏基于奖励的策略梯度信号，而增加 rollout 会提高成本。

Method: GRAFT 框架的工作流程如下：对于每个训练 prompt，当前模型首先进行一定 rollout 预算的采样，得到一组轨迹。如果该组轨迹全部失败（all-fail），则从 peer 模型（异质模型）的轨迹库中检索该 prompt 对应的轨迹，这些轨迹可能包含成功和失败的样本，且已由 peer 模型计算好 advantage。然后将这些 peer 轨迹替换原有的 all-fail 组，参与当前模型的策略梯度更新。为了缓解跨模型分布偏移，GRAFT 引入序列级兼容性权重（compatibility weighting），根据序列与当前模型策略的匹配程度对每条 peer 轨迹的整体贡献进行缩放；同时在 token 级使用重要性比率裁剪（importance ratio clipping），限制 off-policy 样本对梯度更新的影响。训练目标仍是最大化基于验证奖励的期望回报，但更新信号来自混合了 peer 轨迹的组。此外，peer 轨迹可以预先存储并复用，避免训练时的实时协同采样。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "模型级平均性能", "value": "相比 GRPO 平均提升 2.1 点，最高提升 4.5 点", "setting": "三个异质模型对，五个数学推理基准，每个模型相同 rollout 预算"}, {"metric": "模型级平均性能", "value": "相比 GRPO 平均提升 1.8 点", "setting": "使用存储的 peer 轨迹，无需同时协同训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 GRAFT（Gated Replacement of Answer-Failed groups with peer Trajectories）框架，用异质模型（peer）的成功/失败轨迹替换 all-fail 组。；引入 off-policy 感知的序列级兼容性加权与 token 级重要性比率裁剪，控制跨模型分布不匹配。；设计门控机制：仅当本模型组全失败时，才使用 peer 轨迹及其计算的 advantage 进行替换，避免过度 off-policy。；存储 peer 轨迹并复用，在无需同时协同训练的情况下仍能获得大部分增益。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) methods such as GRPO rely on successful self-generated trajectories, but finite rollout budgets can produce all-fail groups with no reward-based policy-gradient signal. While additional rollouts improve the chance of success at higher cost, successful trajectories missing from one model's rollouts may already have been discovered by another. Indeed, we observe that heterogeneous models often succeed on complementary prompts, creating opportunities for mutual learning without a designated stronger teacher. To exploit this complementarity, we propose GRAFT (Gated Replacement of Answer-Failed groups with peer Trajectories), an off-policy-aware framework that replaces all-fail groups with informative peer groups. GRAFT transfers both successful and unsuccessful peer responses with peer-computed advantages, while controlling cross-model mismatch through sequence-level compatibility weighting and token-level importance ratio clipping. Across three heterogeneous model pairs and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, GRAFT consistently improves both models over GRPO with the same per-model rollout budget, gaining 2.1 points on average and up to 4.5 points in model-level average performance. Stored peer trajectories preserve most of the gains, improving over GRPO by 1.8 points on average without simultaneous co-training.

</details>


### [143] [RLTL;DR: Self-improvement by Internalizing Self-generated Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37633)
*Michael Kirchhof,Eleonora Gualdoni,Andrew Szot,Khashayar Gatmiry,Aryo Lotfi,Abbas Kazerouni,Omar Attia,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Alexander Toshev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLTL;DR面向Pass@128=0的困难任务，让模型在每次失败后自写一条TL;DR洞察并条件化后续rollout，同时反向传播内化“任务→洞察”映射。Qwen 3.5 9B Thinking上GRPO基线Pass@1仅0-1%，RLTL;DR达14-31%（带洞察）与12-13%（无洞察评估）；仅4k条(task, insight)元组的SFTL;DR即可接近完整性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在困难任务上（Pass@128=0）没有成功轨迹、教师模型或示例解时，RLVR/GRPO因缺少正向信号而无法学习，如何让模型通过自生成反馈实现自我改进。

Method: 方法以Qwen 3.5 9B Thinking为策略模型。训练时，针对一个任务先采样一次rollout并交给验证器；若失败，则把验证器输出展示给策略，让策略写出一条TL;DR形式的自我反馈洞察。下一次rollout的上下文包含此前所有累积的洞察，如此顺序采样直到得到成功解或达到上限。成功轨迹与对应的洞察上下文用于强化学习（GRPO风格）优化，并对in-context洞察的生成路径进行反向传播，使模型学会直接从任务映射到洞察，而非仅在推理时依赖上下文中的洞察。评估时即使不提供任何洞察，模型也能凭借内化的映射达到12-13%的Pass@1。简化的SFTL;DR仅用(task, insight)元组做监督微调，不展示也不反向传播任何rollout，用4k条数据即可接近RLTL;DR的完整性能，说明关键机制是任务到洞察的内部化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Pass@1", "value": "0% to 1%", "setting": "标准GRPO训练Qwen 3.5 9B Thinking，在Pass@128=0的困难工具调用与代码数据集上"}, {"metric": "Pass@1", "value": "14-31%", "setting": "RLTL;DR训练时insight在上下文中"}, {"metric": "Pass@1", "value": "12-13%", "setting": "RLTL;DR评估时无insight在上下文中"}, {"metric": "Pass@1", "value": "接近RLTL;DR完整性能", "setting": "SFTL;DR仅在4k条(task, insight)元组上训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 失败后展示验证器输出并让策略自行撰写单条TL;DR洞察；将后续rollout条件化于此前所有洞察，顺序采样直到找到解；对in-context洞察进行反向传播，内化直接的“任务→洞察”映射；提出简化版SFTL;DR，仅用(task, insight)元组训练，验证内化机制是关键

Abstract: The common paradigm of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is to let agents make multiple attempts at a task, and optimize towards the successful ones. This becomes problematic in the realms of self-improvement, where tasks are so difficult that the agent has a low or even no chance of success, and where there are no teacher models or example solutions to distill from.
