In this paper, we introduce RLTL;DR. After each failed attempt, we show the policy the verifier outputs and let it write its own feedback, in the form of a single TL;DR insight. The next rollout is conditioned on all previous insights, and we sequentially sample rollouts until a solution is found. Moreover, we enable backpropagation on the in-context insights to internalize a direct task to insight mapping. On challenging tool-calling and coding datasets (filtered to Pass@128=0), standard GRPO training of a Qwen 3.5 9B Thinking policy stays flat at a Pass@1 of 0% to 1%. RLTL;DR breaks through this learning barrier, achieving a Pass@1 of 14-31% with insights in context during training and, crucially, 12-13% when no insight is in context at eval time. We identify that the key is the task to insight internalization. To study this further, we reduce our approach to SFTL;DR, training only on (task, insight) tuples, without showing or backpropagating on any rollouts. Training on only 4k of these tuples recovers almost the full performance of RLTL;DR and classical SFT on full rollouts. This demonstrates a promising compacted training paradigm of the form "on this sort of task, keep this sort of thing in mind", which we hope to inspire future research on.

</details>


### [144] [When Models Don't Manipulate Manifolds: The Geometry of a Comparison Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37680)
*Sai Sumedh R. Hindupur,Hadas Orgad,Thomas Fel,Demba Ba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct在数字比较任务中的内部计算进行机制可解释性分析，发现模型主要依赖线性表示而非流形结构，通过注意力与MLP实现比较，为理解LLM推理和干预提供了几何视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络在执行数字比较任务时是否以及如何依赖低维流形结构进行计算，以及线性表示与流形假设的关系。

Method: 论文分析Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct在数字比较任务中的计算几何。输入为两个待比较数字，模型首先将每个数字编码为向量表示，通过注意力机制和残差连接将两个表示相加，带入残差流中的共享空间；然后使用MLP神经元在共享空间的局部区域（对应输入数字的较小区间）上比较这对数字，并组合这些局部比较结果以确定最大值的位置。研究采用因果几何分析，揭示模型主要利用线性表示，尽管表示中存在弯曲几何结构。该机制同样适用于三个数字的比较。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 因果几何分析数字比较任务中Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的内部计算；发现模型主要使用数字的线性表示而非弯曲流形进行比�；揭示通过注意力与残差连接将两个数字表示相加到共享空间，再用MLP神经元在局部区域比较并组合得到最大值的机制；线性表示在比较三个数字时依然持续

Abstract: One of the current premises of mechanistic interpretability research is that detailed accounts of the geometry of neural network representations can tell us how models perform computations, and how to effectively intervene on them. While low dimensional manifolds have been observed for multiple concepts in the literature (e.g. numbers encoded on helices, days of the week on a circle, ...), with structure believed to reflect properties of data and tasks, the extent to which models rely on them for computation, and how they manipulate them, remains unclear. We characterize precisely the geometry of computation in a number-comparison task, as an abstraction of comparison for decision making, and how models utilize geometry in an elegant fashion to implement it. Specifically, we study the causal geometry of number comparison in Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, a capable and widely studied open-weight model, and find Qwen largely uses linear representations of numbers despite the presence of curved geometry. To compare two numbers, the model first encodes each number along a vector and adds the two representations using attention and the residual connection, bringing them into a shared space in the residual stream. Then, the model uses MLP neurons to compare the pair of numbers on local regions in this shared space, which correspond to smaller intervals of input numbers, and combines these to obtain the position of the maximum. In fact, this reliance on linear representations for comparison also persists when the model compares three numbers. Our findings demonstrate that the manifold hypothesis can co-exist with linear representations: while concepts that are ordered may have manifold structure in representations, the model may use an underlying linear structure of the concept in certain computations.

</details>


### [145] [Overcoming Scaling Limits in On-Policy Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37915)
*Md. Ismail Hossain,Humaira Kousar,Isidora Chara Tourni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过因子分析揭示在线自蒸馏中脚手架正确性比上下文正确性更重要，提出 OASIS：主要监督标签验证的在线策略轨迹，并用模型生成尝试替代参考解作为教师上下文，仅需最终答案标签。在 Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B 及多个数学推理基准上，OASIS 相比基座提升 3.2–3.8 分，且在 8B 规模上比 OPSD 高 3.05 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线自蒸馏（OPSD）对未验证的学生轨迹进行监督，导致教师可利用学生无法获得的信息，产生模仿差距；该差距随模型规模增大而缩小，但 OPSD 仍主要监督未验证轨迹，限制了其在模型规模扩大时的效果。

Method: OASIS 建立在在线自蒸馏（OPSD）框架之上。在标准 OPSD 中，学生模型根据输入问题采样轨迹，教师模型在特权上下文（通常为参考解）条件下对学生的采样轨迹提供监督信号，学生通过最小化与教师分布的差异进行训练。本文通过因子分析将脚手架正确性（轨迹是否正确）与上下文正确性（教师是否看到正确参考解）解耦，发现脚手架正确性对下游准确率影响更大。基于此，OASIS 保留 OPSD 的蒸馏目标，但修改监督数据的选择与教师上下文：主要监督由最终答案标签验证为正确的在线策略轨迹（verified by label on-policy trajectories），并将教师上下文从书面参考解替换为模型生成的未验证尝试。训练仅需最终答案标签，无需完整参考解。推理时学生模型独立生成答案，不依赖教师。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率提升", "value": "3.2–3.8 分", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B、4B、8B 在 AIME 2024、AIME 2025、HMMT 2025 上，OASIS 相比基座模型"}, {"metric": "OPSD 相比基座模型提升", "value": "1.7B 时 3.05 分；8B 时 0.14 分", "setting": "Qwen3 系列在 AIME 2024、AIME 2025、HMMT 2025 上"}, {"metric": "OASIS 相比 OPSD 提升", "value": "3.05 分", "setting": "8B 模型在 AIME 2024、AIME 2025、HMMT 2025 上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过因子分析分离脚手架正确性与上下文正确性的作用，发现脚手架正确性对下游准确率影响更大。；提出 OASIS 方法：保留 OPSD 目标，但主要监督由标签验证的在线策略轨迹，并用未验证的模型生成尝试替代书面参考解作为教师上下文。；OASIS 仅需最终答案标签，降低了对完整参考解的需求。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student to match a privileged teacher distribution along its own sampled trajectory. Standard OPSD applies this supervision to unverified student rollouts while conditioning the teacher on privileged context, typically a reference solution. We separate these roles in a factorial analysis and find that scaffold correctness has a stronger effect on downstream accuracy than context correctness. Unverified scaffolds create an imitation gap because the teacher can use information unavailable to the student. This gap shrinks with model scale, yet OPSD continues to supervise mostly unverified trajectories. In contrast, verified scaffolds remain effective even when the teacher is conditioned on the student's own unsuccessful rollout. Based on this finding, we introduce OASIS, which retains the OPSD objective but supervises mostly verified by label on-policy trajectories and replaces written solutions with unverified model-generated attempts as the teacher context. OASIS therefore requires only final-answer labels. Across Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025, OASIS improves over the base model by 3.2--3.8 points on average, while OPSD's gain falls from 3.05 points at 1.7B to 0.14 at 8B. At 8B, OASIS improves over OPSD by 3.05 points, showing that verified on-policy scaffolds preserve the effectiveness of self-distillation as models scale.

</details>


### [146] [Loss-Guided Pretraining Data Selection for Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37255)
*Yike Li,Shaoxu Song,Jianmin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向时间序列基础模型的静态预训练数据选择框架，用参考预测器对每个窗口打分，并保留每个数据集的中间难度区间。基于归一化平方损失控制梯度范数的理论，方法在多种TSFM架构上优于随机选择，甚至以更少窗口在全量预训练基础上改善MASE与CRPS，并展现跨尺度、跨架构的评分相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在包含数十亿观测的异构数据上预训练时，训练窗口通常被随机采样，未评估其是否提供有效学习信号，导致数据效率低下。

Method: 该框架为静态数据选择方法：输入为异构时间序列集合（数十亿观测），按源数据集划分候选预训练窗口；使用一个参考预测器（reference forecaster）对每个窗口计算参考损失分数（reference loss score），该分数基于归一化平方损失，并证明其控制局部雅可比条件下的单样本梯度范数，从而将预测损失与优化难度关联；随后在每个源数据集内分层选择，保留分数处于中间区间的窗口以平衡难度与多样性；选出的窗口用于预训练各类TSFM架构。实验显示跨尺度与跨架构的评分相关性强，说明小参考模型可为大目标模型选数据，前提是两者难度排序兼容。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MASE", "value": "相对随机选择有绝对提升；相对全量数据预训练亦有改善", "setting": "多种TSFM架构的预训练数据选择实验，保留更少候选预训练窗口"}, {"metric": "CRPS", "value": "相对全量数据预训练有改善", "setting": "多种TSFM架构的预训练数据选择实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从理论上建立归一化平方损失与单样本梯度范数之间的联系（在局部雅可比条件下），将预测损失与优化难度挂钩；提出基于参考预测器的参考损失评分（reference loss score）；采用数据集分层选择（dataset-stratified selection）以保持样本多样性；在每个源数据集内保留一个中间区间（intermediate interval）的窗口；发现跨尺度、跨架构的评分强相关，小参考模型可为大目标模型选数据

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are pretrained on heterogeneous collections containing billions of observations, yet their training windows are typically sampled without estimating whether they provide useful learning signal. We introduce a static data-selection framework that scores each window with a reference forecaster and retains an intermediate interval within every source dataset. Specifically, we connect forecasting loss to optimization difficulty by showing that normalized squared loss controls the per-sample gradient norm under a local Jacobian condition. We then define a reference loss score and apply dataset-stratified selection to preserve the diversity of samples. Across various TSFM architectures, our method outperforms random selection by an absolute margin and even improves both relative MASE and CRPS over full-data pretraining by retaining fewer candidate pretraining windows. Further analyses show strong cross-scale and cross-architecture score correlations, indicating that a small reference model can often select data for larger targets, provided that the reference and target share compatible difficulty orderings.

</details>


### [147] [Trident: Unifying Guarded Dispatch and Host Execution for PyTorch Triton Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37241)
*Jinjie Liu,Xiaoyan Liu,Shuhan Zhang,Wenjia Sun,Ruilin Yang,Chunlei Men,Yonghua Lin,Shaohua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Trident 是一个基于 Torch-MLIR 的 PyTorch Triton 编译器后端，通过 Specialization Cache Module 将 guard 特化、参数准备与宿主执行编译为单一可执行模块，消除缓存命中路径上的重复主机编排开销，在两个 LLM 上实现最高 1.47×（相对 eager）和 1.68×（相对 torch.compile）的端到端延迟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户自定义 Triton 内核在 PyTorch 中虽然能实现高性能 GPU 计算，但其端到端延迟常被主机侧编排主导，尤其是设备执行很短时；现有 torch.compile 生成的宿主包装器在每次调用时仍需经过运行时管理的特化查找、guard 求值和准备阶段，导致重复开销。

Method: Trident 是构建在 Torch-MLIR 之上的编译器后端，目标是在 PyTorch 图执行的特化缓存命中路径上消除重复主机侧开销。其输入是 PyTorch 计算图以及用户编写的 Triton 内核；系统在编译期将 guard 特化选择、参数与执行环境准备、多个特化的宿主执行统一编译进一个可执行模块，即 Specialization Cache Module（SCM）。运行时调用只需进入 SCM 一次：若当前调用的特化已存在于缓存中，执行全程停留在编译好的原生代码内，不再回到 Python 解释器；只有当遇到新的特化需要编译时，才返回 Python 侧处理。Trident 通过 Torch-MLIR 将 guard 求值与主机侧编排降低为原生代码，减少了运行时管理的特化查找与准备开销；同时，对于受支持的 ATen 算子，仍保留对优化运行时实现的调用，以维持算子级性能。最终推理路径为：Python 侧触发一次调用进入 SCM，SCM 内部完成特化匹配、参数与执行环境准备并直接执行宿主代码，必要时再回退到 Python 编译新特化。该设计针对设备执行时间短、主机编排占比高的工作负载，通过减少主机侧重复开销来降低模型级端到端延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "模型级端到端延迟加速比", "value": "最高 1.47×（相对 eager execution）", "setting": "在两个 LLM 上评估"}, {"metric": "模型级端到端延迟加速比", "value": "最高 1.68×（相对 torch.compile）", "setting": "在两个 LLM 上评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Specialization Cache Module (SCM)，将 guard 特化选择、参数与执行环境准备以及多个特化的宿主执行编译为单个可执行模块。；调用进入 SCM 后，若匹配已有特化则保持在编译代码中，仅在需要编译新特化时才返回 Python，从而消除缓存命中路径上的重复主机开销。；基于 Torch-MLIR 将 guard 和主机侧编排下沉为原生代码，同时保留对受支持 ATen 算子的优化运行时实现调用。

Abstract: User-written Triton kernels enable high-performance GPU computation within PyTorch, but their end-to-end latency can remain dominated by host-side orchestration, especially when device execution is short. Although this http URL can generate native host wrappers for captured graphs, each invocation still passes through runtime-managed specialization lookup, guard evaluation, and preparation before reaching the wrapper. We present Trident, a compiler backend that removes this recurring overhead from the specialization cache-hit path. Trident introduces the Specialization Cache Module (SCM), which compiles guarded specialization selection, argument and execution-environment preparation, and host execution for multiple specializations into a single executable module. An invocation enters the SCM once, remains in compiled code when a specialization matches, and returns to Python only when a new specialization must be compiled. Built on Torch-MLIR, Trident lowers guards and host-side orchestration to native code while retaining calls to optimized runtime implementations of supported ATen operators. Our evalu- ation on two LLMs shows that Trident achieves up to a 1.47x speedup in model-level end-to-end latency over eager execution and up to 1.68x over this http URL .

</details>


### [148] [UnlearningSoup: Is Repeated Tuning Necessary for Large Language Model Unlearning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37076)
*Puning Yang,Qizhou Wang,Junchi Yu,Bo Han,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究 LLM 遗忘中的超参数调优难题，发现不同遗忘运行模型共享评估性能盆地，据此提出 UnlearningSoup 框架，通过 EfficientSoup（二分搜索插值）和 PerformanceSoup（重加权 souping）两阶段策略，在多种数据集和模型上实现 2.4x-3.3x 的超参数选择效率提升并持续改进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 遗忘方法依赖基于训练的梯度更新（如梯度上升及其变体），遗忘与保留之间的权衡使超参数选择极为困难，需要反复调参才能得到较强模型，且跨模型与数据集迁移性差。

Method: UnlearningSoup 是一个用于 LLM 遗忘的统一权重空间 souping 框架，其核心洞察是不同遗忘运行得到的模型仍落在共享的评估性能盆地中，因此可以通过权重插值恢复更强模型，而无需反复调参。框架包含两个阶段对应的策略：早期阶段使用 EfficientSoup，通过二分搜索在权重插值路径上快速定位表现良好的模型，避免因重复调参而付出的高昂模型选择成本；后期阶段使用 PerformanceSoup，通过重加权 souping 有效释放剩余性能潜力。整个流程以已训练好的多个遗忘运行模型权重为输入，在权重空间内进行插值/重加权融合，训练目标为在遗忘与保留之间取得更好的权衡，推理时直接使用融合后的模型，无需额外训练。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "超参数选择效率提升", "value": "2.4x 到 3.3x", "setting": "在多种数据集和模型上的遗忘实验中，与需要重复调参的基线方法相比"}, {"metric": "遗忘性能", "value": "持续提升（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "跨不同设置的一致改进"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现不同遗忘运行得到的模型仍位于共享的评估性能盆地（shared evaluation-performance basin），即权重空间存在可利用的结构；提出 UnlearningSoup 统一框架，包含两种策略：EfficientSoup 使用基于二分搜索的插值快速在早期阶段找到表现良好的模型；PerformanceSoup 使用重加权 souping 在后期阶段高效释放剩余性能潜力，减少重复调参

Abstract: Large language models trained on vast corpora inherently risk memorizing harmful content that may later re-emerge in their outputs. To mitigate this issue, existing unlearning methods typically rely on training-based parameter updates, such as gradient ascent and its variants, to delete targeted content while preserving other knowledge. However, balancing the competing goals of forgetting and retention makes hyperparameter choices for these methods particularly difficult, often requiring repeated tuning to obtain a strong model that still leaves substantial room for improvement and transfers poorly across models and datasets. To address this challenge, we investigate whether unlearning runs exhibit exploitable structure in weight space, and observe that models from different runs still lie in a shared evaluation-performance basin. This suggests that stronger models may be recovered through an unlearning-tailored soup strategy, reducing the need for repeated tuning for further improvement or new settings. Motivated by this, we propose UnlearningSoup, a unified framework that provides two strategies: EfficientSoup uses binary-search-based interpolation to quickly discover a well-performing model in the early stage, where repeated tuning would otherwise make strong model selection costly. PerformanceSoup uses reweighted souping to efficiently unlock the remaining performance potential in the later stage, where repeated tuning becomes increasingly inefficient. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and models show that UnlearningSoup delivers 2.4x to 3.3x efficiency gains in hyperparameter selection, while consistently improving performance across settings.

</details>


### [149] [Interpolated Policy Distillation: A Controllable Continuum Between Off-Policy and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37170)
*Youxu Shi,Yifan Sun,Dacheng Yin,Haomiao Tang,Guangting Wang,Fengyun Rao,Jing Lyu,Dong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IPD将off-policy与on-policy蒸馏统一为词级分布插值的连续统，并用精确保持分布的投机解码加速采样；在文本与多模态推理基准上优于两端策略、两阶段组合及启发式片段交织方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在教师高质量但偏离学生分布的off-policy轨迹与学生可学习但含错误推理的on-policy轨迹之间取得可控平衡，并高效实现该中间策略的采样。

Method: IPD在自回归解码的每一步定义下一词分布为教师分布与学生分布的线性插值：p_interp = (1-λ) * p_student + λ * p_teacher，其中λ为插值系数。该插值在词级进行，直接控制轨迹向教师质量或学生可学习性倾斜的程度。为克服逐词查询教师带来的高成本，论文提出一种新的投机解码规则，在加速采样的同时精确保持插值后的下一词分布。从轨迹层面看，采样得到的序列会自然交织学生生成段与教师生成段，这种交织由精确实现的词级插值策略诱导，而非人工设计的切换规则。训练目标为在插值策略产生的轨迹上进行蒸馏/微调，从而兼顾轨迹质量与学生可学习性。推理时通过投机解码降低教师查询开销，使IPD在实际中可行。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理基准性能", "value": "IPD consistently outperforms both endpoint policies (SFT and OPD), their conventional two-stage combination (SFT-then-OPD), and recent heuristic segment-interleaving methods", "setting": "文本与多模态推理基准（具体数值未在摘要中披露）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将off-policy与on-policy蒸馏视为策略连续统，提出在每一步解码时对教师与学生下一词分布做显式线性插值（IPD），以系数直接控制轨迹质量与学生可学习性的权衡。；提出一种新的投机解码规则，在加速插值策略采样的同时精确保持插值后的下一词分布。；轨迹层面自然交织学生与教师生成的片段，该交织由精确实现的词级插值策略诱导，而非启发式切换规则。

Abstract: Off-policy and on-policy distillation have traditionally been formulated as separate paradigms, each favoring a different property of distillation trajectories. Teacher-generated (off-policy) traces are typically high-quality but lie far from the student's distribution, whereas student-generated (on-policy) rollouts are more learnable but often contain erroneous reasoning. We view these paradigms as the endpoints of a policy continuum and posit that a more effective rollout policy may lie in between. We introduce \textbf{Interpolated Policy Distillation (IPD)}, which defines the next-token distribution at every decoding step as an explicit linear interpolation between the student and teacher distributions. The interpolation operates at the distribution level, token by token, and its coefficient provides direct control over the balance between trajectory quality and student learnability. Naively sampling from this policy would require sequentially querying the teacher at every token and is thus expensive. To make IPD practical, we accelerate it with a new speculative-decoding rule while exactly preserving the interpolated next-token this http URL the trajectory level, the resulting rollouts naturally interleave student- and teacher-generated segments. Unlike recent heuristic segment-interleaving methods, however, this interleaving is induced by an exactly realized token-level interpolated policy rather than by hand-designed switching rules. Across text-only and multimodal reasoning benchmarks, IPD consistently outperforms both endpoint policies (SFT and OPD), their conventional two-stage combination (SFT-then-OPD), and recent heuristic segment-interleaving methods, demonstrating that token-level policy interpolation better balances trajectory quality and student learnability.

</details>


### [150] [Beam Search as Test-Time Self-Distillation via Counterfactual Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37041)
*Su Ee Tan,Xiaotong Ji,Rasul Tutunov,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Matthieu Zimmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将自蒸馏微调的思想迁移到推理阶段，用反事实上下文构造奖励，推导出吉布斯重加权最优策略，并通过束搜索近似全局重加权。在数学推理、代码生成和科学QA基准上平均优于采样、低温、束搜索和power sampling。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏微调（SDFT）依赖训练时的梯度更新和专家演示，无法在推理时应用；论文旨在将自蒸馏原理转化为无需参数更新、奖励模型或训练数据的测试时解码方法。

Method: 方法在解码时运行：输入为问题/提示和候选答案序列。构建两个反事实上下文，即固定文本模板，分别假设模型处于优秀推理与糟糕推理状态。对于候选答案，模型在这两种反事实条件下的对数几率比构成隐式奖励信号。基于该奖励，推导KL正则化最优策略，该策略等于对基模型分布的吉布斯重加权，即按奖励指数加权并重新归一化。由于该重加权依赖完整轨迹质量、不可逐token分解，论文使用束搜索在序列级别近似目标分布：束搜索维护多个候选轨迹，按重加权后的序列概率进行扩展和剪枝，最终从近似分布中采样输出。整个过程无需梯度更新、奖励模型或训练数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均性能", "value": "优于标准采样、低温、束搜索和power sampling基线", "setting": "MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA，多个模型规模"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出测试时自蒸馏，用反事实上下文（固定文本模板，分别假设优秀/糟糕推理）替代专家演示。；定义基于两个反事实条件下候选答案对数几率比的新奖励信号。；推导该奖励下KL正则化最优策略，得到对基分布的吉布斯重加权形式。；指出重加权是全局的，不能独立分解到每个token，并用束搜索近似目标分布。

Abstract: Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning (SDFT) enables a language model to act as its own teacher: by conditioning on a demonstration, the model produces an implicit reward via pointwise mutual information, which guides on-policy learning without external supervision. However, SDFT operates at training time: it requires gradient updates and access to expert demonstrations, making it inapplicable at inference. We propose test-time self-distillation, a decoding-time method that extracts a steering signal from the self-distillation framework without any parameter updates, reward models, or training data. Our key insight is that counterfactual contexts, i.e. fixed textual templates that hypothetically prime the model for excellent versus poor reasoning, can substitute for the demonstration. The log-odds ratio of a candidate answer under these two counterfactual conditions defines a new reward signal. We derive the optimal KL-regularized policy under this reward, which takes the form of a Gibbs reweighting of the base distribution. Crucially, this reweighting is global: it cannot be decomposed into independent per-token operations without ignoring future trajectory quality. We therefore approximate the target distribution via beam search. Experiments on mathematical reasoning (MATH500), code generation (HumanEval), and graduate-level science QA (GPQA) across multiple model scales show that test-time self-distillation improves over standard sampling, low temperature, beam search and power sampling baselines on average, demonstrating that the self-distillation principle can be operationalized at inference time.

</details>


### [151] [Authority Bias in Language Models: Source Deference and User Agreement Are Not Interchangeable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37616)
*Abhinav Rajeev Kumar,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了LLM对权威来源的过度服从与对用户的顺从是可分离的行为，单条‘已验证来源’注释可导致45-88%的正确回答被翻转。通过因果干预，移除来源方向可降低来源合规度65-80个百分点，且权威方向可跨任务迁移。研究呼吁对来源服从和用户同意分别进行评估，对RAG和工具增强系统的可靠性设计具有重要参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对权威来源的过度服从与其对用户顺从（sycophancy）是否是不同的行为机制，以及能否通过因果干预分别控制这两种偏差。

Method: 论文未公开模型内部架构细节，实验基于现有开源权重模型和闭源API。研究方法包括：1）构造带有‘已验证来源’注释的提示，评估模型对错误答案的合规翻转；2）通过因果干预（如激活向量方向移除）分离来源服从和用户同意的行为方向；3）从激活空间中拟合来源方向、用户方向和助手方向，并测试其选择性影响；4）拟合基于来源与用户线索的干预向量，在不改变提示文本的情况下调节合规行为；5）将权威方向迁移到其他任务（PIQA、SYCON）以评估泛化性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "错误来源合规翻转率", "value": "45%-88%", "setting": "8个模型（5个开源权重系列，3个闭源API），基线正确答案被单一已验证来源注释翻转"}, {"metric": "来源合规度降低幅度", "value": "65-80个百分点", "setting": "3个开源权重系列，移除拟合的来源方向"}, {"metric": "错误来源合规降低幅度", "value": "数十个百分点", "setting": "5个系列中的4个，移除在trivia上拟合的权威方向，迁移到其他任务无重新拟合"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 量化了单一已验证来源注释对错误答案的翻转效应：在8个模型中有7个出现45%-88%的基线正确响应被翻转；通过因果干预证明来源服从与用户同意在模型内部是可分离的：移除拟合的来源方向可降低来源合规度65-80个百分点，而移除用户方向影响较小；发现权威方向可从 trivia 任务迁移到 PIQA 和 SYCON 多轮对话，无需重新拟合，且对 MMLU-Pro 和 GSM8K 精度无明显影响；构建了基于来源与用户线索激活的干预向量，能在不改变提示文本的情况下双向调节合规行为

Abstract: Language models tend to agree with whatever a user asserts, and post-training increasingly targets this sycophancy so that models evaluate claims on their merits rather than deferring to the user. Yet the same models are far more compliant when a wrong answer is attributed to a verified source, which is how retrieval results, tool outputs, and grounded-search content often present information. We measure this gap across five open-weight families and three closed APIs. A single verified-source note endorsing a wrong answer flips 45-88% of baseline-correct responses in seven of eight models, and compliance rises with how authoritative the note sounds. Source deference and user agreement are not behaviorally interchangeable inside the model: on matched items with the same wrong answer, causal interventions can selectively suppress one without equally affecting the other. In three open-weight families, removing a fitted source direction lowers source compliance by 65-80 percentage points while removing a user or assistant direction has far smaller effects, and removing the user direction shows the reverse preference. A separately fitted intervention derived from source-versus-user cue activations moves compliance in both directions while leaving the prompt text unchanged. An authority direction fitted on trivia also transfers to PIQA and multi-turn SYCON dialogues without refitting, and removing it lowers wrong-source compliance by tens of percentage points in four of five families with no detected change in MMLU-Pro or GSM8K accuracy at our evaluation sizes. Source deference and user agreement therefore need separate evaluation.

</details>


### [152] [Hidden Reasoning Must Leak, but Need Not Be Readable: Fundamental Opportunities and Limits for Chain-of-Thought Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37312)
*Mohammadali Mohammadkhani,Madhava Krishna,Yash Sarrof,Michael Hahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 理论和实证表明，简单隐藏计算可在CoT中隐藏，但复杂任务必然泄露信息；然而泄露的信息可被加密，使得原则上不可读。为LLM推理监控提供信息论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型能否在链式思维（CoT）监控下进行隐藏计算而不在思考轨迹中泄露信息，以及这种泄露在信息论上的必然性和可读性限制。

Method: 论文未提供具体的端到端架构细节，主要理论分析基于信息论和密码学假设，实验部分涉及推理模型CoT监控的行为。未披露模型具体结构、训练目标或推理流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明超出与模型规模相关的任务难度阈值后，成功解决隐藏任务必然向CoT泄露关于隐藏输入的近线性信息量。；在合理密码学假设下，构造出单层Transformer可以加密其在线推理，使得任何多项式时间监控器无法提取隐藏计算的信息。；结合理论与实验，给出CoT监控的机会与局限的整体图景。

Abstract: Can reasoning models trick chain of thought (CoT) monitors and perform hidden computation without revealing it in their thinking traces? We show that the answer depends on the underlying task difficulty and the model size. Simple computations can be performed covertly; however, beyond a threshold depending on model size, successfully solving the task necessarily leaks a near-linear amount of information about the covert task input into the CoT. Therefore, sufficiently complex hidden computation always leaves an information-theoretic footprint. However, concerningly, this leakage need not be readable: Under plausible cryptographic assumptions, even a one-layer Transformer can encrypt its reasoning online so that no polynomial-time monitor can extract information about the hidden computation. Overall, our theoretical and empirical results provide a holistic view of both the opportunities and the limitations of CoT monitoring.

</details>


### [153] [Unlocking the Critic: Reward-Free Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37119)
*Hongyang Li,Xiao Li,Caesar Wu,Said Mammar,Grégoire Danoy,Pascal Bouvry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RFPO重新利用预训练冻结批评家作为奖励、基线和成功预测器，在无需外部奖励标签的情况下稳定LLM长程推理的强化学习后训练，并声称匹配监督PPO同时降低计算与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批评家（critic）在LLM强化学习后训练中常被移除或训练后丢弃，而长程推理任务回报延迟、生成成本高，需要更稳定、样本和计算高效的训练信号。

Method: RFPO围绕一个已校准且冻结的批评家构建后训练流程。输入为策略生成的轨迹（包括完整rollout与未完成的前缀），以及用于预训练批评家的数据。批评家经预训练后可估计给定状态或未完成前缀后最终成功的后验概率。在策略优化中，该批评家输出被三种方式复用：1）作为rollout级奖励信号，替代外部奖励标签或人工标注；2）作为价值基线，用于广义优势估计（GAE）；3）作为成功预测器，对未完成前缀给出密集的、基于结果推导的每前缀学习信号。为消除批评家预测中的长度偏差，对其去偏后的分数进行二值化，从而阻止策略利用长度偏差。训练目标为在稳定约束下最大化该奖励，约束策略更新步长小且方差低以维持收敛。推理或部署时，冻结批评家可继续用于预测成功概率；训练中因无需等待轨迹完成即可奖励，显著降低长程推理的生成等待与内存开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明批评家RL不稳定性主要是优化伪影，通过小且低方差的策略更新恢复稳定收敛；将预训练校准批评家重用于rollout级奖励、GAE价值基线及未完成前缀成功预测，实现无需外部奖励标签的策略优化；对去偏分数二值化以消除批评家的长度偏差，使RFPO在无标签情况下匹配监督PPO；支持未完成rollout即奖励，降低长程任务对完整轨迹等待的计算和内存开销

Abstract: Recent approaches to reinforcement learning (RL) post-training for large language models increasingly remove the critic to reduce training instability and memory overhead. Even where a critic is trained, it is discarded once training ends, although it has learned to predict outcomes. We revisit this trend and show that a pretrained critic's ability to predict future outcomes can make it a valuable asset for efficient long-horizon reasoning. First, we find that instability in critic-based RL for long chain-of-thought reasoning is largely an optimization artifact: keeping policy updates small and low in variance restores stable convergence. Second, a well-pretrained critic estimates the posterior probability of eventual success from later trajectory states and unfinished prefixes. Its predictions provide outcome-derived, dense, per-prefix learning signals that, during policy optimization, require neither completed rollouts, step-level annotations, nor external reward labels. Building on this insight, we introduce Reward-Free Policy Optimization (RFPO), which repurposes a single calibrated, frozen critic as a rollout-level reward, a value baseline for generalized advantage estimation, and a success forecaster for unfinished prefixes. We further show that binarizing the debiased score stops the policy from exploiting the critic's length bias. Binarized, RFPO matches supervised PPO without a single label in the training loop, while cutting compute and memory overhead. This makes RFPO well suited to long-horizon reasoning tasks, where outcomes arrive late and generation dominates cost: because rollouts can be rewarded before they finish, training no longer has to pay for waiting on every trajectory to complete. Our findings challenge the prevailing critic-free paradigm and establish critic-based, reward-free optimization as a scalable and computationally efficient path for LLM post-training.

</details>


### [154] [VACE: Validation-Gated Alternating Co-Evolution of Agent Models and Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37105)
*Jiexing Qi,Yu He,Jun Liu,Qichen Huang,Shaohua Hu,Zhan Dang,Guohua Chen,Rui Yang,Wen Jiang,Yang Liu,Tao Lyu,Fangming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VACE提出验证门控的交替协同进化框架，交替进行智能体强化学习与轨迹驱动的harness精炼，并仅在验证性能提升时采纳新harness。在Qwen3.5-9B上，OfficeQA达45.26%、AutomationBench达75.19%，分别超过仅权重RL 6.43和9.09个百分点；44个harness提案中有17个因降低验证性能被拒绝，凸显门控价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体的能力同时依赖模型权重与引导任务执行的harness，二者相互耦合：权重更新改变模型对harness的使用方式，harness更新又改变用于训练的轨迹。如何稳定地联合优化这两个耦合组件是核心问题。

Method: VACE以Qwen3.5-9B为基座模型，构建一个交替式协同进化训练框架。输入为智能体任务环境（OfficeQA、AutomationBench）及由harness定义的执行脚手架（引导智能体任务执行的结构与流程）。训练分为交替的两类阶段：其一是智能体强化学习阶段，模型权重在harness固定条件下通过收集的轨迹进行更新；其二是harness精炼阶段，复用上一轮RL阶段收集的轨迹提出harness修订候选。为避免耦合带来的退化，框架引入验证门控：在权重更新后的检查点上固定模型，分别用当前harness与候选harness评估验证集性能，仅当候选harness带来验证性能提升时才将其采纳并用于后续RL训练，否则丢弃。该门控解决了权重与harness相互变化导致轨迹分布漂移、harness更新可能损害训练信号的问题。推理或部署时使用最终权重与最终采纳的harness执行任务。论文报告在OfficeQA与AutomationBench上的测试表现，并对44个harness提案进行统计以说明门控过滤比例。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "OfficeQA test accuracy", "value": "45.26%（较仅权重RL提升6.43个百分点，较无门控交替提升4.59个百分点）", "setting": "Qwen3.5-9B，OfficeQA"}, {"metric": "AutomationBench mean partial-credit score", "value": "75.19%（较仅权重RL提升9.09个百分点，较无门控交替提升6.95个百分点）", "setting": "Qwen3.5-9B，AutomationBench"}, {"metric": "被拒绝的harness提案数", "value": "44个提案中17个降低验证性能并被拒绝", "setting": "VACE训练过程中的harness候选评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出VACE（Validation-Gated Alternating Co-Evolution）：交替进行智能体强化学习与轨迹驱动的harness精炼；每轮RL后复用收集的轨迹提出harness修订，并固定更新后的模型对现有harness与候选harness做验证评估；候选harness仅在验证性能提升时才用于后续训练，形成验证门控机制；通过44个harness提案的实证分析量化门控必要性：其中17个在更新后的检查点上降低验证性能并被拒绝

Abstract: Language model agents can be improved by updating their model weights or refining the harness that guides task execution. These components are coupled: weight updates change how the model uses the harness, while harness updates change the trajectories used for training. We propose VACE, Validation-Gated Alternating CoEvolution, which alternates agentic reinforcement learning with trajectory-driven harness refinement. After each RL stage, VACE reuses the collected trajectories to propose a harness revision and evaluates the incumbent and candidate with the updated model held fixed. The candidate guides subsequent training only if it improves validation performance. With Qwen3.5-9B, VACE achieves 45.26% test accuracy on OfficeQA and a mean partial-credit score of 75.19% on AutomationBench, exceeding weight-only RL by 6.43 and 9.09 percentage points and ungated alternation by 4.59 and 6.95 points, respectively. Across 44 harness proposals, 17 reduce validation performance at the updated checkpoint and are rejected before subsequent RL training, highlighting the importance of validation gating.

</details>


### [155] [Cool the Sampler, Not the Learner: Sampling Temperature Moves the Staleness Cliff of Importance-Corrected GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36953)
*Taiheng Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究生产级 RL 训练中采样器陈旧问题，发现重要性校正 GRPO 存在容忍悬崖。提出解耦冷却：采样温度降至 0.8 而学习器保持温度 1，从而在采样器长时间不刷新时稳定训练。实验表明冷却采样器每 192 步刷新可匹配未冷却每 96 步刷新的性能（0.857），而降低学习率则低 3-7 分。在 7B 模型上冷却使成功率从 68-81% 提升至 92-93%。冷却效果有窗口，温度与刷新间隔需联合调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产级 RL 训练中，采样器（sampler）可能落后于学习器（learner），导致分布失配；现有方法使用截断的重要性权重进行校正，但采样器不刷新能容忍多久尚不明确。论文研究重要性校正 GRPO 中采样陈旧度的容忍边界（staleness cliff），并探索通过调节采样温度来延长容忍窗口。

Method: 论文基于 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）训练框架。输入为数学推理问题（GSM8K 数据集），采样器（policy model）生成多个响应（组），学习器使用组内相对优势进行策略更新。采样器和学习器在异步设置下可不同步：采样器可能落后于学习器多个更新步，产生分布失配。校正机制使用截断的重要性权重（truncated importance weight）来调整学习器目标，以补偿采样器陈旧导致的分布偏移。论文的核心干预在采样端：解耦冷却使采样温度设为 0.8，而学习器、参考模型和重要性权重计算保持温度 1。行为概率从温度调节后的分布中记录，使得学习器目标中的重要性权重仍基于原始温度 1 的分布，从而不改变学习器优化目标。训练流程：采样器以温度 0.8 生成样本，学习器每步更新，重要性权重用记录的行为概率校正。采样器按固定间隔（如 96、144、192 步）刷新。推理时使用温度 1 的原始模型？论文未明确推理流程，只关注训练动态。目标函数为重要性校正的 GRPO 目标。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最终准确率（GSM8K）", "value": "0.857", "setting": "Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B，重要性校正 GRPO，每 192 步刷新采样器并冷却 vs 每 96 步刷新未冷却，相同更新预算"}, {"metric": "平均准确率（GSM8K）", "value": "0.79", "setting": "Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B，冷却采样器每 192 步刷新，平均训练期间准确率"}, {"metric": "最终准确率（GSM8K）", "value": "92-93%", "setting": "Qwen2.5-Math-7B，间隔 192 冷却采样器，两个数据种子，未冷却则在首次刷新前退化（68-81%）"}, {"metric": "最终准确率（GSM8K）", "value": "68-81%", "setting": "Qwen2.5-Math-7B，间隔 192 未冷却采样器，两个数据种子，在首次刷新前退化"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现重要性校正 GRPO 中采样器刷新的'悬崖'现象：在每 192 次更新刷新一次时，训练在第 180 步后在所有三个数据种子上严重退化。；提出解耦冷却（Decoupled Cooling）：采样时使用温度 0.8，而学习器、参考模型和重要性权重保持温度 1，行为概率从退火分布记录，使学习器目标不变。；证明冷却采样器可在相同更新预算下，让每 192 步刷新匹配未冷却每 96 步刷新的最终性能（均为 0.857），而降低学习率至安全值则低 3-7 个百分点。；揭示冷却效果的适用窗口：在安全间隔三倍时或高失配 MATH 设置下，冷却只能延迟退化不能阻止；更强冷却无益；没有重要性校正时冷却会崩溃。；提出采样温度和刷新间隔应联合选择，温度是陈旧容忍度的控制变量。

Abstract: Production RL for language models lets the sampler fall behind the learner and repairs the resulting mismatch with a truncated importance weight. We ask how long the sampler can go without a refresh under that correction, and find a cliff: on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and GSM8K, importance-corrected GRPO refreshed every 192 updates learns well for 180 steps and then degrades severely in all three data seeds before the refresh arrives. Published remedies for staleness act on the update; we act on the sampler instead. Decoupled cooling draws samples at temperature 0.8 while the learner, the reference model and the importance weights stay at temperature 1, with the behaviour probability recorded from the tempered distribution, so the learner's objective is unchanged. All corresponding cooled runs are stable, and the longer interval keeps what the short one delivered: at the same update budget, a cooled sampler refreshed every 192 steps matches an uncooled sampler refreshed every 96 at the end of training (0.857 for both) and averaged over it (0.79), whereas lowering the learning rate to a safe value ends 3-7 points lower. On Qwen2.5-Math-7B the degradation points at interval 192 predict that an interval of 144 is fatal without cooling and survivable with it; on two data seeds the uncooled runs degrade before their first refresh and the cooled runs pass it and end at 92-93% against 68-81%, with one cooled run degrading transiently late in the second cycle. The benefit has a window: at three times the safe interval and in a high-mismatch MATH setting cooling delays degradation without preventing it, stronger cooling is not better, and cooling without the correction collapses. Sampling temperature is a control on staleness tolerance, and temperature and refresh interval should be chosen together.

</details>


### [156] [Trajectory Soup: Pushing the Compute-Scaling Frontier of LLM Mid-training via Diverse Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37169)
*Zhehao Huang,Changxin Tian,Qingyuan Yang,Kunlong Chen,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Xiaolin Huang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Trajectory Soup：把 mid-training 算力分配到多个从共享检查点分叉的独立训练分支，经验证集选择检查点后做轨迹内与轨迹间平均，合并为单一模型。实验表明在匹配预算下优于最强单轨迹平均，且随预算扩展持续提升，优势在相同 post-training 后仍保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mid-training 阶段存在串行算力饱和天花板：对单次训练继续增加串行算力回报递减，甚至损害部分能力，需要重新思考如何将算力预算分配到一次或多次优化中。

Method: 论文以预训练后的 LLM 检查点为起点，在 mid-training 阶段将总算力预算分配给多个从共享检查点分叉的独立训练分支。每个分支采用不同的受控训练配方（学习率调度等），从而探索参数空间的不同区域。各分支训练完成后，基于验证集指标选择每个分支中最强的检查点。随后进行两级平均：轨迹内平均（同一分支内多个检查点的平均）和轨迹间平均（不同分支所选检查点之间的平均），最终合并为单一模型。论文给出局部偏差-方差分析来解释两级平均的作用：轨迹内平均降低方差但受限于单一轨迹；轨迹间平均利用分支间兼容多样性进一步消除残余误差；而检查点选择会引入偏差，从而限制可有效合并的检查点数量。合并后的模型可接入相同的 post-training 流程。推理路径与标准 LLM 无异，合并模型为单一模型，不增加推理时的多分支开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "聚合下游性能", "value": "优于最强单轨迹平均（在匹配预算下）", "setting": "跨模型规模、学习率调度、token 预算和轨迹数的实验"}, {"metric": "聚合下游性能", "value": "随预算扩展持续提升，优势在相同 post-training 流程后仍保持", "setting": "匹配预算下的 mid-training 算力扩展实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现从共享检查点在不同受控配方下分叉出的分支会到达参数空间中可测量的不同区域，形成串行延长单次训练无法提供的“兼容多样性”。；提出 Trajectory Soup：将 mid-training 预算分配到多个独立分支，通过验证集选择最强检查点，再进行轨迹内与轨迹间平均，合并为单一模型。；通过局部偏差-方差分析分离两种平均层级，表明轨迹间平均能消除轨迹内平均无法触及的残余误差，而检查点选择引入的偏差会限制可合并检查点数量。

Abstract: Mid-training equips pretrained large language models with specialized and reasoning capabilities, but the returns of this stage are bounded since additional serial compute yields little further downstream improvement and can even degrade some capabilities, which places a practical ceiling on how much compute mid-training absorbs. We revisit how this compute should be allocated to a single run or multiple similar optimizations. We find that branches forked from a shared checkpoint under various controlled recipe reaches measurably different regions of parameter space, and establish a form of compatible diversity that extending one run cannot supply. Therefore, we introduce Trajectory Soup, which distributes a mid-training budget over several independent branches, and consolidates strongest checkpoints selected on validation through intra- and inter-trajectory averaging into a single model. A local bias and variance analysis separates the two averaging levels, showing that inter-trajectory averaging removes residual error beyond the reach of averaging within a trajectory, while checkpoint selection carries a bias that bounds how many checkpoints are worth merging. Across model scales, learning-rate schedules, token budgets, and trajectory counts, Trajectory Soup improves aggregate downstream performance over the strongest single-trajectory average under matched budgets and keeps improving as budgets expand, with the advantage preserved after an identical post-training pipeline. These results position trajectory allocation and merging as a practical way to extend the compute-scaling frontier of mid-training beyond serial saturation.

</details>


### [157] [Where the Model Changes Its Mind: Hindsight-Divergence Localization for Efficient Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36864)
*Fanchao Chen,Hengyu Fu,Shivaram Venkataraman,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HDL 通过事后似然变化定位策略会重新考虑的分支点，用少量根轨迹加分支续写填充 RLVR 训练组，在匹配组大小与训练步数下最多减少 2.5 倍生成 token、加速 1.8 倍 rollout，并在数学、代码和智能体任务上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组相对 RLVR 方法独立采样完整轨迹成本高昂，且没有在关键决策位置显式探索决策空间。

Method: HDL 在 RLVR 训练流程中重新设计 rollout 生成。首先，对每个任务生成少量完整的根轨迹（root trajectories）。然后，利用事后诱导的 token 对数似然变化来识别策略会重新考虑的关键决策位置，即分支点。在每个选定的分支点处，保持原始任务上下文，采样生成若干续写（continuations），以填充每个训练组。每条续写复用其根前缀，策略更新仅通过新生成的后缀进行，从而避免完整轨迹的重复生成成本。训练目标仍为组相对策略优化（如 GRPO），但组内样本由根轨迹和分支续写混合构成，使额外探索与学习集中在分支之后的决策上。推理/服务流程未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "生成 token 数", "value": "匹配组大小与训练步数下，相比 GRPO 最多减少 2.5 倍", "setting": "三种模型、数学/代码/智能体任务"}, {"metric": "rollout 墙钟时间", "value": "最多加速 1.8 倍", "setting": "三种模型、数学/代码/智能体任务"}, {"metric": "任务性能", "value": "在三个领域均有提升，智能体任务最多提升 12.5 分", "setting": "相比 GRPO 在匹配生成预算下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用事后诱导的 token 对数似然变化定位分支点（Hindsight-Divergence Localization）；生成少量完整根轨迹，并从选定位置在原始任务上下文下填充续写以构成训练组；每条续写复用根前缀且仅通过新生成后缀贡献策略更新，降低生成成本并聚焦分支后决策的学习

Abstract: Group-relative methods for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) learn from differences in rollout outcomes. Independently sampling complete trajectories is costly and does not explicitly explore the decision space at critical positions. Feedback on a completed trajectory can reveal which earlier choices the policy reconsiders, suggesting where to sample alternative continuations. We introduce Hindsight-Divergence Localization (HDL), which uses hindsight-induced changes in token log-likelihoods to select branch points. HDL generates a small number of complete root trajectories and fills each training group with continuations from the selected positions under the original task context. Each continuation reuses its root prefix and contributes policy updates only through its newly generated suffix, reducing generation cost while focusing additional exploration and learning on decisions after branching. Experiments with three models across math, code, and agent tasks show gains in both rollout efficiency and task performance. Compared with GRPO at matched group sizes and training steps, HDL yields up to a 2.5$\times$ reduction in generated tokens and a 1.8$\times$ speedup in rollout wall-clock time. Despite this reduced generation budget, HDL improves performance across all three domains, with gains of up to 12.5 points on agent tasks.

</details>


### [158] [Towards Better Training Signal: Advantage Clipped Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36816)
*Ruichuan Huang,Jinghan Liu,Congliang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ACPO 通过裁剪重要性采样比率与优势的乘积来稳定 LLM 强化学习后训练，并建立与策略镜像下降梯度裁剪的理论联系，在数学推理基准上以 Qwen3-8B 取得 4-6 个百分点提升，训练效率优于 PPO 和 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PPO/GRPO 等方法通过裁剪重要性采样（IS）比率来稳定 LLM 强化学习训练，但训练稳定性和梯度估计实际上主要由 IS 比率与优势的乘积决定，仅裁剪 IS 比率不足以稳定梯度。

Method: ACPO 针对 LLM 强化学习后训练中的策略优化环节。在标准 on-policy RL 流程中，策略模型生成推理轨迹，用重要性采样（IS）比率修正 off-policy 数据带来的分布偏移，并乘以优势函数估计策略梯度。PPO/GRPO 通常裁剪 IS 比率以稳定训练。ACPO 的核心改动是在梯度估计中裁剪 IS 比率与优势的乘积，而非仅裁剪 IS 比率，从而直接控制梯度的幅度，得到更稳定的梯度估计。理论方面，论文将 ACPO 与策略镜像下降（PMD）中的梯度裁剪建立联系，并在标准 RL 设定下证明裁剪 PMD 的收敛性。实验在数学推理基准上进行，使用 Qwen3-8B 配合 PPO 类训练框架，比较 ACPO 与 PPO、GRPO 的准确率和训练效率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "4-6 个百分点", "setting": "标准数学推理基准，Qwen3-8B+PPO"}, {"metric": "训练效率", "value": "优于 PPO 和 GRPO", "setting": "数学推理基准训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 ACPO 算法，裁剪 IS 比率与优势的乘积，以获得更稳定的梯度估计；建立 ACPO 与策略镜像下降（PMD）中梯度裁剪的联系；证明标准 RL 设定下裁剪 PMD 的收敛性

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a cornerstone for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs), but the need for on-policy data substantially limits training efficiency. Reusing off-policy data through importance sampling (IS) can improve efficiency but introduce considerable instability. Hence, algorithms such as PPO and GRPO widely adopt IS-ratio clipping to stabilize training. However, training stability and gradient estimate are mainly determined by the product of IS ratio and advantage. To further stabilize training, we propose ACPO, which clips the product of the IS ratio and the advantage, leading to more stable gradient estimates. We also establish a connection between ACPO and gradient clipping in policy mirror descent (PMD), which is a standard technique to stabilize optimization process, and prove the convergence of clipped-PMD under the standard RL setting. Experiments on widely used mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that ACPO consistently outperforms PPO and GRPO in both accuracy and training efficiency, delivering 4-6 percentage points gains on standard math benchmarks, with Qwen3-8B+PPO. Hence, ACPO is a practical and effective alternative to conventional IS-ratio clipping for RL post-training of LLMs.

</details>


### [159] [CorrGRPO: Correlation-Normalized GRPO for Multi-Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36820)
*Wenbin Hu,Huihao Jing,Haochen Shi,Yuxuan Liu,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CorrGRPO 针对 GRPO 多奖励归一化被大尺度相关奖励主导的问题，把成对协方差归一化为 Pearson 相关系数，在代码生成、工具调用与智能体安全上以 0.5B–8B 模型取得改进，并开源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO 在多奖励场景下将各奖励求和后按组内标准差归一化，导致大方差/大尺度且相关的奖励主导归一化项，压制小尺度奖励的信号，使优势估计失衡。

Method: CorrGRPO 建立在 GRPO 之上。输入为同一提示的多条 rollout 及其多个奖励分量。GRPO 先将各奖励分量求和得到总奖励，按组内均值中心化，再除以总奖励的组内标准差得到优势；该方差等于所有成对奖励协方差之和，因此大尺度且相关的奖励会放大归一化分母，压缩优势幅度。CorrGRPO 保留中心化后的总奖励不变，但把归一化中的成对协方差替换为 Pearson 相关系数，使不同尺度的奖励在相关性归一化中获得均衡影响，避免大尺度奖励主导。训练目标仍为策略梯度式优化，但优势幅度由奖励相关性结构决定。论文在代码生成、工具调用与智能体安全三类多奖励任务上，用 0.5B–8B 模型与 GRPO 及其他变体比较，报告三个领域均有提升，并开源代码。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 CorrGRPO，将成对协方差归一化为 Pearson 相关系数，使归一化不被大尺度奖励组件主导；保持中心化后的总奖励不变，仅调整归一化机制，从而让优势幅度自适应奖励相关性；在代码生成、工具调用、智能体安全三个多奖励领域验证，覆盖 0.5B 至 8B 参数模型

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely used to train reasoning language models, where it computes advantages by centering and normalizing rewards across rollouts of the same prompt. For multiple rewards, GRPO sums the reward components and normalizes the total reward by its within-group standard deviation. The corresponding variance equals the sum of all pairwise reward covariances. For a fixed centered reward, larger aggregate covariance produces smaller advantages, and vice versa, allowing update magnitudes to adapt to reward dependence. However, correlated rewards with large scales can dominate this normalization and suppress signals from smaller-scale rewards. We propose Correlation-Normalized GRPO (CorrGRPO), which normalizes pairwise covariances into Pearson correlation coefficients. CorrGRPO keeps the centered total reward unchanged while balancing the influence of differently scaled rewards on the correlation-based normalization. This allows advantage magnitudes to adapt to reward correlations without the normalization being dominated by large-scale reward components. We compare CorrGRPO with GRPO and other variants on code generation, tool calling, and agent security, using models ranging from 0.5B to 8B parameters. These tasks all involve multiple rewards that can improve together or present tradeoffs. Results show improvements across three domains, including code generation, tool calling, and agent security. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [160] [EasyPPO: Stabilizing the Critic Is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36802)
*Xuanyi Zhou,Qiuyang Mang,Huanzhi Mao,Dacheng Li,Wenhao Chai,Mayank Mishra,Yichuan Wang,Karthik Narasimhan,Alvin Cheung,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EasyPPO通过改进PPO中critic的训练稳定性，解决截断轨迹过滤导致的奖励偏移和异构回报噪声问题。在代码、数学推理和多轮搜索任务上均优于PPO、VAPO和HL-Gauss PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO中critic的不稳定性导致LLM强化学习训练不稳定，具体包括截断轨迹过滤导致的奖励偏移和异构回报噪声导致的critic更新偏差。

Method: EasyPPO在标准PPO基础上改进critic训练。输入为LLM生成的rollout轨迹（包括完整和截断），用于actor和critic更新。主要模块：1）Actor-only overlong filtering：在计算策略梯度时过滤掉截断的轨迹，但critic训练时保留完整和截断的轨迹，从而避免策略目标变为条件于完成的奖励，防止截断率上升而条件奖励改善的偏差。2）Noise-normalized critic regression：对每个提示，计算其采样回报的标准差，并用其倒数对critic回归损失加权，从而平衡高噪声提示和低噪声提示对critic更新的贡献，避免高方差提示主导有限批次内的更新。3）Critic mini-batch缩小：使用适度较小的critic mini-batch，使得在梯度裁剪时异常值的影响局限于更少的rollout。训练目标为PPO的标准裁剪目标加上改进的critic回归损失。推理时，使用训练好的actor生成输出，critic仅训练时使用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最佳验证分数相对提升", "value": "14.89%", "setting": "FrontierCS连续奖励代码任务，对比PPO"}, {"metric": "最佳验证分数相对提升", "value": "2.28%", "setting": "AIME24二元奖励数学推理，对比PPO"}, {"metric": "最佳验证分数相对提升", "value": "9.47%", "setting": "Search-R1多轮搜索，对比PPO"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Actor-only overlong filtering：critic在完整和截断的rollout上训练，避免策略目标偏移。；Noise-normalized critic regression：按每个提示采样回报标准差的倒数加权critic损失，平衡不同提示的噪声贡献。；Moderately smaller critic mini-batches：限制异常值在梯度裁剪时的影响范围。

Abstract: A key strength of Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) is its learned critic, which uses historical trajectories collected during reinforcement learning to estimate expected returns and reduce policy-gradient variance. However, we find that the critic is also a major source of instability in reinforcement learning for large language models (LLMs). We identify two critic failure modes that destabilize PPO. First, filtering truncated rollouts from both actor and critic shifts the policy objective to reward conditioned on completion, allowing truncation to increase even as conditional reward improves. Second, heterogeneous return noise can cause high-variance prompts to dominate critic updates in finite batches. We introduce EasyPPO to address these failures. Actor-only overlong filtering trains the critic on returns from both completed and truncated rollouts. Noise-normalized critic regression weights each prompt's critic loss by the inverse standard deviation of its sampled returns, balancing noise contributions across prompts. Moderately smaller critic mini-batches confine outlier influence to fewer rollouts during gradient clipping. Across continuous-reward coding on FrontierCS, binary-reward mathematical reasoning on AIME24, and multi-turn search on Search-R1, EasyPPO remains stable throughout the full training horizon and consistently outperforms vanilla PPO, VAPO, and HL-Gauss PPO. Its best validation scores show relative gains of 14.89%, 2.28%, and 9.47% over PPO, respectively.

</details>


### [161] [RESCUE: Repairing Language Model Errors to Sparse Circuits via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36813)
*Chuanpu Liu,Miao Yu,Yikai Cai,Yuanhe Zhang,Zhenhong Zhou,Li Sun,Zuming Jiang,Yufei Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RESCUE提出通过强化学习细化掩码，定位LLM中与错误相关的稀疏电路，并用剪枝与微调修复错误。在数学推理上将准确率从6.0%提升至75.5%，医疗QA从0%提升至81%，电路密度仅约1.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路研究侧重保留功能或解释安全性，较少探索任务失败背后的错误内部计算机制；且在多步推理和长文本生成中，早期偏差导致前缀漂移，使基于SFT的掩码优化忽略生成时错误涉及的电路。

Method: RESCUE是一个定位并修复LLM错误关联稀疏电路的框架。输入为模型在特定任务上的失败样本与监督参考，输出为修复后的模型。流程包括：首先通过掩码优化定位与错误相关的电路；由于多步推理和长文本生成中早期偏差导致前缀漂移，单纯SFT掩码优化会忽略生成时错误电路，因此使用强化学习，以多次掩码模型的rollout为反馈，细化掩码使其与观察到的任务失败更相关；最后引入剪枝技术精确微调错误电路，将错误定位转化为稀疏且有针对性的模型更新。训练目标为在保持其他能力的同时修复目标任务失败。实验在数学推理和医疗QA两个异构修复集上验证，分别定位到1.40%和1.44%密度的错误电路，并显著提升修复集准确率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "从6.0%提升到75.5%", "setting": "数学推理域，修复集，定位到1.40%密度的数学错误电路后修复"}, {"metric": "repair-set accuracy", "value": "从0%提升到81%", "setting": "医疗QA域，修复集，定位到1.44%密度的紧凑电路后修复"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将电路分析从能力扩展到错误，假设失败源于错误内部计算，可通过针对性参数调整修复；RESCUE框架：定位错误关联电路并进行手术式修复；使用强化学习与多次掩码模型rollout优化掩码，提高其与任务失败的相关性；引入剪枝技术并精确微调错误电路，将错误定位转化为稀疏目标模型更新

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong general capabilities that mechanistic interpretability has attributed to sparse computational circuits. However, existing circuit studies emphasize preserving functionality or explaining safety, leaving the mechanisms underlying failures across a broader range of tasks largely unexplored. Extending circuit analysis from abilities to errors, we explore the perspective that such failures may likewise arise from erroneous internal computations and that targeted tuning of the corresponding parameters can correct such errors while largely preserving other capabilities. Motivated by this insight, we introduce RESCUE (Reasoning-Error Sparse-Circuit Uncovering and Editing), a framework that localizes error-associated circuits and surgically repairs them for performance enhancement. General tasks typically involve multi-step reasoning and long-form generation, where early deviations can cause prefixes to drift from supervised references, leading SFT-based mask optimization to overlook circuits involved in generation-time errors. RESCUE therefore refines these masks through reinforcement learning with multiple masked-model rollouts, improving their relevance to observed task failures. Finally, RESCUE introduces a pruning technique and precisely fine-tunes error circuits to correct task failures, thereby translating error localization into a sparse and targeted model update. We validate RESCUE on heterogeneous repair sets across two domains: (1) mathematical reasoning, identifying a math error circuit of 1.40% density whose repair raises accuracy from 6.0% to 75.5%; and (2) medical QA, where a similarly compact 1.44% circuit improves repair-set accuracy from 0% to 81%. Our code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [162] [Group-Marginalized Self-Rewarding RL Drives Zero-Label Self-Evolving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36750)
*Yiming Wang,Yikang Liu,Qingyuan Tian,Xingyu Chen,Zhuosheng Zhang,Zhaopeng Tu,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GMAE 针对自奖励 RL 中奖励依赖单一随机组上下文的问题，将不同组上下文下的奖励实现聚合为响应级分布并估计期望优势，在 8 个基准与 4 个基座模型上验证了性能、跨域泛化、稳定学习和低额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自奖励强化学习中基于 rollout 组的集成式奖励构造，只依赖单一随机组上下文实现，奖励表示受组内其他响应的随机采样影响，可能遗漏期望奖励信号并给出不可靠的策略优化指导。

Method: 论文提出 GMAE 用于自奖励 RL 训练流程。输入为 LLM 在给定 prompt 下采样生成的一组 rollout 响应（rollout group）。传统集成式自奖励方法基于该组的其余响应构造奖励参考，并据此为每个响应分配奖励；其缺陷是响应获得的奖励表示依赖于随机采样的组上下文。GMAE 将同一响应在不同可能组上下文实现下的奖励聚合为响应级分布，再基于该分布估计期望优势（expected advantage），以此替代单一上下文实现下的优势估计，从而为策略优化提供噪声更低、更接近期望奖励的信号。训练目标遵循自奖励 RL 的策略优化范式，使用估计出的期望优势更新策略，无需人工标签。实验在 8 个基准与 4 个基座模型上验证性能与跨域泛化，并报告学习稳定性、低额外成本和跨训练数据集及 RL 骨干的适用性。具体模块交互、推理与服务路径、算法公式细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Group-Marginalized Advantage Estimation (GMAE)：将响应在不同可能组上下文下的奖励实现聚合为响应级分布，并估计期望优势；降低对单次随机组采样上下文的依赖，提供更稳定、更可靠的自奖励信号；跨 8 个基准与 4 个基座模型验证强性能与跨域泛化，并报告低额外开销与跨训练数据集、RL 骨干的适用性

Abstract: Self-rewarding reinforcement learning (RL) enables large language models (LLMs) to self-evolve without human labels. Existing ensemble-based methods construct reward references from rollout groups and assign rewards accordingly. However, a response's reward representation also depends on its randomly sampled group context, i.e., the other responses in its group. Using only one group-context realization may miss desired reward signals and provide unreliable guidance for policy optimization. To address this issue, we propose Group-Marginalized Advantage Estimation (GMAE), which aggregates reward realizations across possible contexts into a response-level distribution and estimates expected advantages. Experiments across eight benchmarks and four base models demonstrate strong performance and cross-domain generalization. GMAE also exhibits stable learning, low extra cost, and good applicability across training datasets and RL backbones.

</details>


### [163] [QuantMLA: Function-Aligned Dual-Path Quantization for Low-Bit MLA KV Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36760)
*Zunhai Su,Yuxuan Sun,Jianchao Tan,Tao Zhang,Ruihan Hu,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuantMLA提出面向MLA KV缓存的双路径低比特量化框架，通过离线可融合的路径特定变换和函数对齐目标，首次实现内容与RoPE缓存联合INT4，并支持内容INT2，配合原生低比特内核在128K上下文压缩3.59倍，服务吞吐提升5.168倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA缓存仍随上下文长度和批量线性增长，现有量化方法未区分其内容路径与RoPE路径的量化误差，尤其RoPE路径误差会被放大，导致低位量化精度下降严重。

Method: QuantMLA针对MLA的KVCache（内容cache和RoPE key cache）进行低比特量化。首先建立双路径量化误差模型，分别分析内容路径和RoPE路径误差对注意力输出的影响，发现RoPE路径误差放大显著。基于此推导路径特定的变换空间，变换可离线融合进模型权重，推理时无额外开销。在该空间内学习路径特定变换，内容路径采用注意力输出重建目标，同时捕捉匹配和聚合误差；RoPE路径采用位置QK重建目标，保留RoPE引起的注意力logits分量，并给出输出失真的理论界。量化方案支持内容cache和RoPE cache联合INT4，或内容cache INT2、RoPE key cache INT4。推理时使用原生低比特MLA注意力内核，将解包与反量化融合进注意力计算，物理缓存布局在128K上下文下实现3.59倍压缩。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "压缩率", "value": "3.59x", "setting": "128K上下文，物理缓存布局"}, {"metric": "whole-job输出吞吐", "value": "5.168x", "setting": "缓存压力服务负载，相比BF16"}]}

Conclusion: 系统建模MLA双路径量化误差对注意力输出失真的不同影响，解释RoPE路径误差放大现象；推导路径特定的变换空间，保持全精度计算且可离线融合到模型参数，消除在线变换开销；设计函数对齐目标：内容路径用注意力输出重建，RoPE路径用位置QK重建并给出输出失真理论界；首次实现内容缓存与RoPE缓存的联合INT4缓存，内容缓存可进一步压至INT2且保持竞争力；开发原生低比特MLA注意力内核，将解包和反量化直接集成到注意力计算中

Abstract: Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA) enables expressive multi-head attention with compact caches for its content and decoupled RoPE paths, yet cache memory still scales linearly with context length and batch size. In this work, we establish a systematic model of MLA's dual-path quantization errors, characterizing their distinct effects on attention-output distortion and explaining the pronounced amplification of RoPE-path errors. Guided by this analysis, we introduce QuantMLA, a function-aligned framework for low-bit dual-path quantization. We derive path-specific transformation spaces that preserve full-precision computation while remaining fully fusible into model parameters offline, eliminating online transformation overhead. Within these spaces, QuantMLA learns path-specific transformations with function-aligned objectives: attention-output reconstruction captures the content path's coupled matching and aggregation errors, while positional QK reconstruction preserves the RoPE-induced component of the attention logits and admits a theoretical bound on output distortion. Across four MLA model families, QuantMLA enables, to our knowledge, the first reported joint INT4 caching of the content and RoPE caches with minimal accuracy degradation. Further compressing the content cache to INT2 while retaining the RoPE key cache at INT4 maintains competitive performance on challenging reasoning and code benchmarks. We develop a native low-bit MLA attention kernel that integrates unpacking and dequantization directly into attention computation. The physical cache layout provides 3.59x compression at 128K context, while a cache-pressure serving workload achieves 5.168x higher whole-job output throughput than BF16. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [164] [Efficient Offline Learning of Ranking Policies via Top-$k$ Policy Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36740)
*Ren Kishimoto,Koichi Tanaka,Haruka Kiyohara,Yusuke Narita,Yasuo Yamamoto,Nobuyuki Shimizu,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: R-POD提出一种新的排序策略离线学习方法，通过将排序策略分解为Top-k选择和底部排序两阶段，结合策略梯度和回归方法的优点，在保证无偏性的同时降低方差，适用于电商和新闻等推荐场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略学习（OPL）方法在推荐排序场景中面临动作空间巨大导致的高方差（策略梯度法）或高偏差（回归法）问题。

Method: R-POD将排序策略分解为两个阶段：第一阶段是Top-k选择策略，负责从全部物品中选出Top-k物品集合；第二阶段是底部排序策略，在给定Top-k物品的条件下，对剩余物品进行排序。训练时，第一阶段使用新的策略梯度估计器，该估计器仅对Top-k动作应用重要性加权，避免了对整个排列空间的重要性加权，从而显著降低方差；第二阶段使用回归方法拟合期望奖励，避免高方差。论文证明在条件成对正确性条件下，第一阶段策略梯度估计器是无偏的。推理时，先通过第一阶段选出Top-k物品，再由第二阶段对剩余物品排序，最终生成完整推荐列表。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出R-POD方法，将排序策略分解为Top-k选择的第一阶段策略和剩余底部的第二阶段策略；第一阶段使用新的策略梯度估计器并仅对Top-k动作应用重要性加权，大幅降低方差；在条件成对正确性条件下证明了第一阶段策略梯度估计器的无偏性

Abstract: Many recommender systems such as for e-commerce and news platforms aim to provide users with rankings they are likely to interact with. Off-Policy Learning (OPL) of ranking policies enables us to learn new ranking policies using only historical logged data. However, ranking settings make OPL remarkably challenging because their action spaces consist of permutations of unique items, being extremely large. Existing methods primarily use either policy- or regression-based approaches. The policy-based approach, which typically uses importance-weighted policy gradients, can suffer from high variance due to large action spaces. The regression-based approach, on the other hand, estimates the expected reward using conventional machine learning methods, avoiding variance issues but potentially suffering from severe bias. To circumvent these issues of existing methods, we propose a new OPL method for ranking, named Ranking Policy Optimization via Top-$k$ Policy Decomposition (R-POD), which combines the policy- and regression-based approaches in an effective fashion. Specifically, R-POD decomposes a ranking policy into a first-stage policy for selecting top-$k$ actions and a second-stage policy for choosing the bottom actions given the top-$k$ actions. It learns the first-stage policy using a new policy gradient estimator and the second-stage policy via the regression-based approach. This method can substantially reduce variance, since it applies importance weighting only to the top-$k$ actions. We also demonstrate that our policy-gradient estimator for the first-stage policy is unbiased under a conditional pairwise correctness condition, which only requires that the expected reward differences of pairs of rankings sharing the same top-$k$ actions can be estimated correctly.

</details>


### [165] [Backpropagated Output Momentum: Relocating Optimizer History from Parameters to Task Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36738)
*Yuchen Li,Zongqi Fan,Nguyen H. Tran,Ken-Tye Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BOM 提出将优化器动量从参数空间迁移到任务输出空间存储，并通过反向传播重投影回参数空间。作为即插即用组件，它减少了49.7-99.8%的参数形状优化器状态，平均降低4.0%配对步时间，并在语言和视觉微调中平均提升1.42分验证性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化器动量以参数大小的移动平均存储，导致历史状态内存开销大，且将过去梯度信号固定在原参数坐标中。

Method: BOM 将优化器的一阶动量历史从参数空间迁移至模型输出（任务）空间。前向传播后，在输出端计算预测误差（如监督损失的梯度或残差），维护一个紧凑的移动平均作为历史信号。每次参数更新前，通过当前网络的反向传播将该输出空间的动量重投影回参数空间，从而获得当前坐标下的参数动量估计。该估计可替换自适应优化器中第一矩的更新项，同时保留当前批次的监督梯度用于二阶矩或其他组件。训练目标仍为原监督损失，不改变梯度计算，仅改变一阶动量的存储与重建方式。推断/服务流程与原始网络一致，不引入额外推理模块。该设计在保持优化性能的同时显著降低优化器状态内存并轻微改善步时。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "参数形状优化器状态减少", "value": "49.7-99.8%", "setting": "三种组合方式下"}, {"metric": "配对步时间", "value": "平均降低4.0%", "setting": "三种语言骨干网络"}, {"metric": "平均验证性能", "value": "+1.42点", "setting": "语言与视觉微调五任务主要对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将动量历史从参数空间迁移到任务输出空间，存储紧凑的预测误差移动平均；每一步通过当前网络反向传播重新投影该输出历史，获得参数空间动量；兼容多种自适应优化器，可替换其第一矩组件，并兼容已有压缩状态的优化器；保留当前监督梯度不变，仅改变一阶动量估计方式

Abstract: Optimizer momentum is usually stored as a parameter-sized moving average of past gradients, which makes history costly and fixes each past signal in the coordinates in which it was computed. We introduce Backpropagated Output Momentum (BOM), which instead stores a compact moving average of prediction errors at the model output and reprojects that history through the current network at every step. A batch-level analysis characterizes the information retained and omitted by this relocation, while the implementation preserves the current supervised gradient and can replace the first-moment component of several adaptive optimizers. As a plug-in for momentum-based optimizers, including ones that already compress their state, BOM reduces parameter-shaped optimizer state by 49.7-99.8% in three compositions and, averaged over three language backbones, paired step time by 4.0%. It also improves mean validation performance across language and vision fine-tuning, by 1.42 points in the primary five-task comparison. Language and vision pretraining studies, together with matched mechanism controls, further test the construction across output spaces and model scales.

</details>


### [166] [Normalize-Then-Precondition: A Hierarchical Approach to Marginal Scale and Interaction Geometry for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36692)
*Zixuan Gong,Zeyu Gan,Jiaye Teng,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Normalize-Then-Precondition 分层优化框架与 NormPre 优化器，将边际尺度与交互几何解耦处理，NormPre-G/L 分别用 Newton-Schulz 与随机素描实现可扩展谱预条件，在 GPT-2、LLaMA、Qwen3 预训练中匹配预算下优于 AdamW/Muon/MANO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 等矩阵优化器在 full-Gram 表示中同时耦合了边际尺度（marginal scale）与方向性交互几何（interaction geometry），缺乏对两类几何信息的分层组织，限制了优化效率与可扩展性。

Method: NormPre 是训练期的优化器而非网络结构。其端到端流程为：输入为参数矩阵的梯度更新；(1) 利用梯度的对角 Gram 信息构造边际归一化（marginally normalized）更新，使各边际尺度先被统一；(2) 在该归一化更新的方向性交互几何上施加谱预条件，依据谱范数最速下降原理逼近最优方向更新。两种实现：NormPre-G 采用全局谱预条件，用 Newton-Schulz 迭代近似矩阵符号/谱变换，避免昂贵的 SVD 以支撑大规模训练；NormPre-L 采用正则化公式先做主模态（leading mode）选择，再用随机素描近似主导交互特征子空间，实现局部化谱预条件。理论侧对简化版本给出 O(T^{-1/2}) 收敛率。实验侧在 GPT-2 Small、LLaMA 与 Qwen3 预训练中与 AdamW、Muon、MANO 在相同训练预算下对比，并给出额外效率与谱分析以刻画两变体的性能-效率权衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预训练损失/下游性能", "value": "在匹配训练预算下一致优于 AdamW、Muon 和 MANO（具体数值未在摘要披露）", "setting": "GPT-2 Small、LLaMA、Qwen3 的广泛预训练实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Normalize-Then-Precondition 分层优化框架：先用对角 Gram 信息构造边际归一化更新，再对方向性交互几何做谱预条件；NormPre-G：基于谱范数最速下降，使用 Newton-Schulz 迭代近似全局谱预条件以支持大规模训练；NormPre-L：基于正则化公式与主模态选择的局部化谱预条件，使用随机素描（randomized sketching）近似主交互特征子空间；对简化版 NormPre 建立 O(T^{-1/2}) 收敛保证；在 GPT-2 Small、LLaMA、Qwen3 预训练上以匹配训练预算超越 AdamW、Muon 与 MANO

Abstract: Matrix optimizers have emerged as a promising direction, with Muon standing out as a prominent design. Revisiting Muon through its full-Gram representation, we observe that it jointly processes marginal-scale and interaction information. This opens an alternative way to organize geometric information hierarchically, motivating the Normalize-Then-Precondition framework. Specifically, it first uses diagonal-Gram information to construct a marginally normalized update, then applies spectral preconditioning to its directional interaction geometry. Building on this framework, we develop NormPre with NormPre-G and NormPre-L adopting global and localized spectral preconditioning, grounded in spectral-norm steepest descent and a regularized formulation followed by leading mode selection, respectively. To enable large-scale training, NormPre-G uses Newton-Schulz iterations and NormPre-L employs randomized sketching to approximate the leading interaction eigenspace. Theoretically, we establish $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ convergence guarantees for simplified versions of NormPre. Across extensive pretraining experiments on GPT-2 Small, LLaMA and Qwen3, both variants consistently outperform AdamW, Muon and MANO under matched training budgets. Further efficiency and spectral analyses reveal the complementary strengths of two variants and characterize their performance-efficiency trade-off. We open-source our code through a GitHub repository at this https URL .

</details>


### [167] [Where Root Cause Analysis Fails: A Retrieval-Reranking Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36686)
*Hada Melino Muhammad,Luan Pham,Laure Barrière,Sachin Shetty,Leonardo Pulga,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出现有RCA评估的top@k指标混淆检索失败与重排失败，通过检索-重排分解审计四个基准，并提出多信号检索+LLM重排的两阶段流水线，在六个基准套件上无需因果图和标注数据即达到或超过最佳基线top@1，最高提升12点；在保证候选包含真实根因时加入系统描述可使重排器领先7-18点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA评估使用top@k准确率会混淆两类失败：检索失败（真实根因未被召回）和重排失败（真实根因被召回但排名过低）。论文旨在通过检索-重排分解暴露该盲点，并构建无需因果图和标注数据的两阶段流水线提升根因排序。

Method: 论文提出两阶段根因分析流水线：第一阶段为多信号检索器，从数百个传感器信号中生成候选根因集合，旨在提高真实根因的召回率，避免检索失败；第二阶段为LLM重排器，对检索到的候选重新排序，利用系统描述文档等上下文信息提升真实根因的排名，减少重排失败。训练目标或优化细节未在摘要中披露。推理时先检索后重排，无需因果图或标注数据。该架构直接针对检索与重排的失败模式进行分离优化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "top@1 accuracy", "value": "匹配或超过最佳基线，最高+12点", "setting": "六个基准套件，固定配置两阶段流水线，无因果图和标注数据"}, {"metric": "top@1 accuracy", "value": "+7至+18点", "setting": "所有方法使用相同保证包含真实根因的候选池，加入简短系统描述文档"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出检索-重排分解，将RCA性能拆分为检索失败与重排失败，并审计四个基准暴露top@k盲点；发现复杂故障基准上统计基线误排真实根因79-100%，图方法未明显优于最佳统计基线；简单基准检索近乎解决98-100%；构建多信号检索器+LLM重排器的两阶段流水线，无需因果图或标注数据，在六个基准套件上匹配或超过最佳基线top@1（最高+12点）；当所有方法使用相同保证包含真实根因的候选池时，加入简短系统描述文档可使LLM重排器在每个基准上领先最佳基线+7至+18点

Abstract: Identifying the root cause of an anomaly among hundreds of sensors is critical for preventing safety incidents and costly downtime in complex monitored systems. Existing studies evaluate root cause analysis (RCA) methods using top@k accuracy. We show that this metric has a fundamental blind spot: it conflates two failure modes, retrieval failure, where the true cause is never considered, and reranking failure, where it is considered but ranked too low. In this work, we introduce a retrieval-reranking decomposition and audit four well-known benchmarks to expose this blind spot. Our experiments show that, on benchmarks with complex faults, statistical baselines mis-rank the true cause 79-100% of the time, and graph-based methods never clearly beat the best statistical baseline, whether their causal graphs are learned on short fault windows, on retrieved candidate pools guaranteed to contain the cause, or on multi-day normal-operation data. Meanwhile, on simple benchmarks where faults manifest significantly at their origin, retrieval is nearly solved (98-100%). Guided by the decomposition, we build a two-stage pipeline combining a multi-signal retriever with an LLM reranker that, as one fixed configuration, matches or exceeds the best baseline's top@1 accuracy on all six benchmark suites (by up to +12 points), with no causal graph or labeled data required. When all methods rank the same retrieved candidates with the true cause guaranteed present, adding a short system-description document lets the reranker lead the best baseline by +7 to +18 points on every benchmark. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [168] [Human-inspired, Task-Dimension-Guided Exploration for Efficient Learning in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36672)
*Fanyu Zhu,Jiahui An,Ni Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TDGE是一种受人类维度引导探索启发的模型无关算法，通过自动构建任务-维度-特征-物品层次结构并采用自顶向下探索，提升高维推荐场景的探索效率与冷启动适应性，在多个推荐数据集上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维决策空间中推荐/决策系统的探索效率低下，且难以快速适应冷启动场景；人类能够通过探测候选特征维度、识别奖励相关维度并限制有效决策空间来高效导航大空间，论文旨在模拟这一机制。

Method: TDGE构建一个自动生成的任务-维度-特征-物品层次结构。输入为决策/推荐任务及候选物品集合，系统首先在顶层从任务相关特征维度中进行选择，随后在选定的维度内识别信息性特征，最后基于这些选定特征推荐具体物品。该自顶向下过程将高维动作空间限制到有效低维决策空间，从而提升探索效率与冷启动适应能力。训练目标或优化细节未在摘要中披露。实验在MovieLens-20M、Amazon等推荐数据集上进行，通过聚类方法、层次深度等消融实验和推荐轨迹可视化分析增益来源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "探索效率与冷启动适应性", "value": "substantially improves over baseline algorithms", "setting": "MovieLens-20M、Amazon推荐数据集上的实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出TDGE（任务-维度-引导探索）算法，模型无关且自动构建任务-维度-特征-物品层次结构；采用自顶向下的探索策略：先选择任务相关特征维度，再在维度内识别信息性特征，最后基于选定特征推荐具体物品；将人类维度引导探索行为形式化为推荐/决策算法，并在推荐数据集上验证探索效率和冷启动适应性的提升；通过聚类方法、层次深度等消融实验和推荐轨迹可视化，证明层次结构与语义特征空间探索是增益来源

Abstract: Efficient exploration in high-dimensional decision spaces remains a central challenge for decision-making systems. Humans, in contrast, can navigate large decision spaces with remarkable efficiency. Recent behavioral studies suggest that humans reduce dimensionality in large decision spaces by probing candidate feature dimensions, identifying reward-relevant ones, and restricting the effective decision space. Inspired by this mechanism, we propose TDGE (Task-Dimension-Guided Exploration), a human-inspired, model-agnostic algorithm with an automatically constructed task-dimension--feature--item hierarchy. TDGE follows a top-down exploration strategy: it first selects task-relevant feature dimensions, then identifies informative features within those dimensions, and finally recommends concrete items based on the selected features. Experiments on MovieLens-20M, this http URL , and Amazon recommendation datasets show that TDGE substantially improves exploration efficiency and cold-start adaptation over baseline algorithms. Comparisons with other structured algorithms and ablation studies attribute these gains to TDGE's hierarchical structure and semantic feature-space exploration, with robust results across clustering methods and hierarchy depths. Recommendation-trajectory visualizations also show exploration patterns similar to human dimension-guided behavior.

</details>


### [169] [AutoLoCo: Communication Efficient Distributed LLM Training via Adaptive Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36662)
*Pengyu He,Yan Zhang,Ruien Li,Guangwen Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoLoCo提出一种自适应同步框架，通过训练统计量动态调整本地更新步数，并用累积内层学习率校正外层优化器的动量与学习率，在通信受限实验中将通信频率较DiLoCo降低27%且保持训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模跨数据中心LLM预训练中频繁同步成为瓶颈；现有局部更新方法在训练前固定本地优化器步数间隔，但最优间隔随训练阶段变化，导致通信效率与训练性能难以兼顾。

Method: AutoLoCo是一个自适应分布式LLM训练框架，目标是在跨多数据中心的预训练中减少通信。其基本流程沿用局部更新（local update）范式：每个worker在同步间隔内执行若干次本地优化器步（inner steps），然后进行一次外层同步（outer update）。与固定间隔方法不同，AutoLoCo在训练过程中根据标量训练统计量动态调整每个同步周期内的本地步数（local interval）。改变内层步数会使外层优化器所期望的更新尺度与实际的累积内层更新之间出现不匹配，尤其影响动量和学习率；为此，AutoLoCo对外层优化器进行校正，利用累积的内层学习率来修正动量与学习率的更新，从而在自适应改变间隔时仍保持优化轨迹与训练性能。整个框架不依赖对模型的额外约束，仅需训练过程中已有的标量统计量即可在线决定间隔，因此能在通信受限条件下降低同步频率。论文未披露具体的模型规模、硬件、数据量或训练时长。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "通信频率降低比例", "value": "27%", "setting": "通信受限的分布式LLM训练实验，相对DiLoCo，训练性能保持"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 利用标量训练统计量自适应调整本地更新间隔，使同步频率随训练状态动态变化。；针对改变内层步数导致外层优化器与动量/学习率不匹配的问题，提出用累积内层学习率对外层更新进行校正。；在通信受限设置下实现比DiLoCo减少27%通信频率并保持训练性能。

Abstract: The pre-training of Large Language Models (LLMs) is increasingly conducted across multiple data centers. As training scales to a larger number of accelerators, the fraction of time spent on computation decreases, while the fraction spent on communication increases. Therefore, frequent synchronization becomes a growing bottleneck. Local update methods reduce this cost by allowing workers to perform several optimizer steps between synchronizations. Most local update methods set the number of local optimizer steps between synchronizations before training and keep this interval fixed throughout the run. However, the best interval can change during the entire train process. If the interval and optimizer are adapted to the current training state, the communication frequency is reduced while maintaining the training performance. In this work, we introduce AutoLoCo, an adaptive training framework to reduce communication in LLM training. It adapts the local interval using scalar training statistics and corrects each outer update. Our method is motivated by two observations: 1) the appropriate local interval varies across training stages, and 2) changing the number of inner steps per interval creates a mismatch with an unchanged outer optimizer, requiring a correction to the outer update. We optimize this mismatch by correction of the outer optimizer for the momentum and the learning rate using the accumulated inner learning rate. Our experiments under communication constraints demonstrate that AutoLoCo reduces communication frequency by 27% relative to DiLoCo while maintaining training performance.

</details>


### [170] [Constitutional adapters: Inference-time interventions for misalignment and misuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36657)
*Adam S. Lowet,Mark Kurzeja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出宪法适配器（CAs），通过从合成语料中蒸馏低秩适配器和引导向量，实现推理时干预，有效防御越狱和多轮攻击，且支持零样本迁移和推理时缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何从合成语料中蒸馏出符合宪法原则的轻量对象（低秩适配器和引导向量），并在推理时干预模型行为，以防御对齐攻击和滥用，同时保持良性合规。

Method: 论文提出 Constitutional Adapters (CAs) 作为推理时干预机制。首先，从合成的宪法一致性语料中训练轻量对象——低秩适配器 (LoRA) 和引导向量 (steering vectors)。通过从宪法训练的对象中减去控制训练的对象，得到 CAs，这些适配器在推理时被添加到基础模型的特定层，以调整其激活值。CAs 可以在基础模型上训练，然后零样本迁移到后训练检查点。在推理时，可以缩放 CAs 的强度，从而在防御能力和良性请求的合规性之间进行权衡。该架构不改变模型权重，而是通过外部干预影响生成行为。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 训练宪法一致的轻量对象（低秩适配器和引导向量）用于推理时干预；通过对比控制训练和宪法训练的对象，得到‘宪法适配器’（CAs），进一步增强防御效果；支持零样本迁移到后训练检查点，并可在推理时缩放以调节防御与良性合规的权衡

Abstract: Training models to act in accordance with an explicitly defined set of principles, or "constitution," has shown promise as a robust and transparent mechanism for AI alignment. However, the generality and flexibility of such methods remain unclear. Here, we show that constitution-consistent behavior can be distilled from synthetic corpora into lightweight objects (low-rank adapters and steering vectors). Despite never seeing a harmful request or jailbreak during training, such objects increase jailbreak defense success and measured alignment -- particularly at long context lengths and against multi-turn attacks, where they outperform both prompted and steered baselines. Subtracting control-trained from constitution-trained objects further accentuates these effects, yielding defenses we call "constitutional adapters" (CAs). CAs can be trained on a base model, transferred zero-shot to its post-trained checkpoint, and scaled at inference time to predictably trade off defense for benign compliance. Taken together, these results recommend CAs as a lightweight, portable, and tunable lever for mitigating misalignment and misuse in API deployments.

</details>


### [171] [Inducing Process Supervision from Outcome-Only Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36641)
*Shengda Fan,Xin Cong,Zhong Zhang,Haotian Chen,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIPS提出一种仅使用结果标签的强化学习框架来训练生成式过程奖励模型(PRM)：模型先输出思维链再给出步骤级与结果级标签，奖励仅基于结果是否与真值一致，借此在无显式过程监督下强化步骤级验证能力。TIPS-Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507仅用3.2K结果标注轨迹即在ProcessBench达到85.2 F1，超过评估中的已训练PRM及GPT-5.4-Instruct、Claude-4.7-Opus等强提示判别器，但仍落后于o1-mini。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练强过程奖励模型(PRM)成本高：人工逐步标注难以规模化，Monte Carlo估计计算昂贵且可能偏离步骤内在正确性。

Method: TIPS采用生成式PRM架构：给定问题与待验证的推理步骤序列，模型自回归生成一段思维链(CoT)，随后输出每个步骤的步骤级标签（正确/错误）以及一个最终结果标签。训练时只使用结果标签与真实答案是否一致作为奖励信号，不提供任何人工或Monte Carlo过程监督；对同一输入采样一组生成响应，计算组相对优势(group-relative advantage)，用该优势对整个生成响应（包含CoT、步骤标签和结果标签）做策略优化。直觉是：当检查中间步骤有助于判断最终结果时，更准确的步骤验证会带来更正确的最终结果判断，从而获得更高奖励，因此仅结果级RL即可反向强化步骤级验证能力。推理/使用时，模型直接生成步骤级判断，作为PRM提供步骤级反馈，可服务于后训练过滤与测试时推理的验证与重排序。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "ProcessBench F1", "value": "85.2", "setting": "TIPS-Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507，仅使用3.2K结果标注轨迹训练；超过所有对比的已训练PRM以及GPT-5.4-Instruct、Claude-4.7-Opus等强提示判别器，但仍低于o1-mini"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出TIPS(Thinking-Induced Process Supervision)纯结果标签的强化学习框架，用于训练生成式PRM；模型先生成思维链(CoT)，再输出步骤级标签与最终结果标签，奖励仅取决于结果标签是否与真实答案一致；利用组相对优势(group-relative advantage)优化整个生成响应，使结果级RL间接强化步骤级验证能力，无需显式过程监督

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) have become a key component for LLMs, as their step-level feedback supports both post-training and test-time reasoning. However, training strong PRMs remains costly: human step annotation is difficult to scale, while Monte Carlo estimation is computationally expensive and can drift from the intrinsic correctness of steps. To get effective PRMs at low cost, we introduce TIPS (Thinking-Induced Process Supervision), an outcome-only reinforcement learning (RL) framework for training generative PRMs. In TIPS, the model generates a chain-of-thought (CoT) followed by step-level labels and an outcome label. The reward depends solely on whether the predicted outcome matches the ground truth, and the resulting group-relative advantage is used to optimize the entire generated response. Intuitively, when checking intermediate steps helps determine the outcome, more accurate checks can lead to better outcome judgments and higher rewards. Outcome-only RL can therefore reinforce step-level verification without explicit process supervision. We validate the effectiveness of TIPS across math and agent benchmarks and four backbone families. Notably, TIPS-Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 reaches 85.2 F1 on ProcessBench with only 3.2K outcome-labeled trajectories, surpassing all evaluated trained PRMs and strong prompt-only judges such as GPT-5.4-Instruct and Claude-4.7-Opus, while still trailing o1-mini. Code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [172] [Act First, Reason Later: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents via Reference-Conditioned Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36608)
*Zubin Zheng,Jiahao Wu,Shaofeng Zhang,Zhirui Zhang,Yew-Soon Ong,Shengcai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ActFirst-OPD 提出“先行动、后推理”的多轮 agent 蒸馏框架，通过参考条件逆动力学快速执行动作、偏差时回退自主预测，并异步生成完整响应用于 token 级教师监督。在 ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld 上相比 Vanilla OPD 分别取得平均 2.3×、1.8×、4.9× 训练加速，并在 9 个 benchmark-model 设置中的 8 个匹配或超过基线成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 think-then-act 的 on-policy distillation 在每轮动作前先生成长推理，导致环境交互延迟高、经验收集慢；直接生成动作虽可降低延迟，但会降低 rollout 质量和蒸馏效果。

Method: ActFirst-OPD 将多轮 agent 训练分为三个耦合阶段。第一，学生智能体在环境中执行动作时，不先生成完整 think-then-act 文本，而是以当前交互上下文和一条参考下一观察为条件，通过 reference-conditioned inverse dynamics 直接推断并执行动作，从而快速获得环境转移。第二，当实际下一观察与参考轨迹的偏差超过设定阈值时，学生切换为自主 next-action prediction，避免因偏离参考轨迹而导致 rollout 质量下降。第三，收集到的交互上下文被异步送入生成过程，学生模型在这些上下文上生成完整的 think-then-act 响应，教师模型对这些完整响应提供 token 级监督，用于 on-policy distillation 更新学生参数。训练目标与标准 OPD 一致，即最小化学生生成响应与教师分布之间的 token 级蒸馏损失，但 rollout 与监督生成解耦，使环境交互不被长推理阻塞。推理时，学生可先执行动作推进环境，再按需生成完整推理链。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "wall-clock training speedup", "value": "平均 2.3×", "setting": "相比 Vanilla OPD，在 ALFWorld 环境"}, {"metric": "wall-clock training speedup", "value": "平均 1.8×", "setting": "相比 Vanilla OPD，在 WebShop 环境"}, {"metric": "wall-clock training speedup", "value": "平均 4.9×", "setting": "相比 Vanilla OPD，在 ScienceWorld 环境"}, {"metric": "mean task success rate", "value": "匹配或超过所有对比 OPD 基线，在 9 个 benchmark-model 设置中的 8 个", "setting": "Qwen3 0.6B、1.7B、4B 学生模型在 ALFWorld、WebShop、ScienceWorld 上的任务成功率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: ActFirst-OPD 框架：将环境交互与完整响应生成解耦，先在环境执行动作，再异步生成 think-then-act 完整响应用于 token 级教师监督。；Reference-conditioned inverse dynamics：学生模型利用当前交互上下文和参考下一观察，通过逆动力学推断并执行动作，从而快速推进环境。；偏差回退机制：当实际转移偏离参考轨迹时，切换为自主 next-action prediction，保持 rollout 质量。；异步监督生成：在收集到的交互上下文上异步生成完整推理-动作响应，以支持 token 级 OPD 蒸馏而不阻塞交互。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains multi-turn language agents with dense teacher supervision on student-generated responses.
