However, standard think-then-act rollouts require lengthy reasoning before each short action, delaying environment transitions and experience collection. Generating actions directly reduces this delay but can degrade rollout quality. To address this, we propose ActFirst-OPD, an act-first, reason-later training framework that decouples environment interaction from full-response generation. The student infers and executes actions through reference-conditioned inverse dynamics using its current interaction context and a reference next observation, and switches to autonomous next-action prediction when the resulting transition deviates from the reference trajectory. From the collected interaction contexts, the student asynchronously generates full think-then-act responses for token-level teacher supervision. Experiments across 0.6B-, 1.7B-, and 4B-parameter Qwen3 students show that ActFirst-OPD achieves average wall-clock training speedups of $2.3\times$ on ALFWorld, $1.8\times$ on WebShop, and $4.9\times$ on ScienceWorld over Vanilla OPD. It matches or exceeds all compared OPD baselines in mean task success rate across eight of nine benchmark-model settings. These results demonstrate that reasoning need not block acting during multi-turn agent distillation.

</details>


### [173] [Learned Reporting Preferences in RLVR Can Conflict with the Current Request](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36587)
*Yupeng Chang,Wenxuan Zhang,Yuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过交叉训练与评估设计，证明RLVR在提升约定匹配准确率的同时，可能显著降低模型对当前请求中其他格式约定的遵循度，并将“学到的报告偏好”“当前请求遵循度”“提交答案正确性”区分为三个独立的后训练结果。实验基于Qwen2.5-7B与GSM8K，包含多随机种子、控制干预与人工校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR采用约定匹配（convention-matched）的奖励/评估方式，可能强化特定的报告格式约定，从而损害模型对当前请求中其他格式要求的遵循能力；论文试图分离并度量“学到的报告偏好”“当前请求遵循度”与“提交答案正确性”三个不同的后训练结果。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是围绕已有LLM（Qwen2.5-7B）的后训练行为展开实验。整体流程为：以同一初始策略为共享参照，分别在两种报告约定（如boxed格式与hash格式）下进行RLVR训练，得到两个匹配的策略；随后将两个策略分别置于两种当前请求（要求boxed或hash格式）下评估，构成交叉设计，以分离“训练约定偏好”与“当前请求遵循度”。训练目标为带可验证奖励的强化学习，奖励信号与特定报告约定匹配。评估维度包括：响应是否包含所请求的payload、是否保留训练约定、以及提交答案的正确性。此外，通过仅改变监督目标中的最终答案标记进行受控干预，验证偏好是否可学习；并引入独立人工校准，比较约定敏感评分器与提交答案正确性两种度量下的增益差异。推理阶段未涉及新的部署系统或加速优化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "包含所请求hash格式payload的响应比例变化", "value": "在五个训练种子中的四个下降35.33–74.37个百分点；第五个种子提升2.50个百分点", "setting": "GSM8K数据集，Qwen2.5-7B，boxed-format RLVR训练，初始基线95.45%"}, {"metric": "省略所请求payload的响应中保留训练boxed约定的比例", "value": "几乎每一个省略所请求payload的响应都保留训练boxed约定", "setting": "四个性能下降的训练种子，GSM8K，Qwen2.5-7B"}, {"metric": "固定改写下对同一四个种子的性能退化复现", "value": "相同四个种子在两种固定改写请求下均出现退化", "setting": "GSM8K，Qwen2.5-7B，固定paraphrase请求"}, {"metric": "仅改变最终答案标记后模型偏好报告约定的逆转", "value": "三个种子中均出现偏好逆转", "setting": "监督目标微调，最终答案标记控制实验"}, {"metric": "约定敏感评分器增益超过提交答案正确性增益", "value": "在三种设置中约定敏感评分器增益均超过提交答案正确性对应增益", "setting": "三种独立人工校准设置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 交叉实验设计：在两种报告约定下分别训练匹配的策略，并在两种当前请求下评估每个策略，以同一初始策略作为共享参照，从而揭示单一约定训练对另一种请求遵循度的影响。；控制变量干预：仅改变监督目标中的最终答案标记，即可逆转模型偏好的报告约定，提供该偏好可学习的受控证据。；独立人工校准：在三种设置中引入人工校准，区分约定敏感评分器下的增益与模型实际提交答案正确性的增益。；提出将“当前请求遵循度”与“约定匹配任务准确率”并列评估，作为后训练行为的补充评价维度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a prominent approach for improving language-model performance on reasoning tasks using automatically checked answers. Yet convention-matched evaluation cannot reveal whether reinforcing one reporting convention reduces adherence to a different request that the initial policy already follows. To test this, we train matched policies under two reporting conventions and evaluate each policy under both current requests, using the same initial policy as a shared reference. We complement this crossed design with controlled interventions and independent human calibration. On GSM8K, boxed-format RLVR reduces the fraction of Qwen2.5-7B responses containing the requested hash-format payload by 35.33--74.37 percentage points relative to a 95.45% initial baseline in four of five training seeds; the fifth improves by 2.50 points. In the four deteriorating runs, almost every response that omits the requested payload instead retains the trained boxed convention, and the same four seeds deteriorate under two fixed paraphrases. Changing only the final-answer marker in supervised targets reverses which reporting convention the model prefers across three seeds, providing controlled evidence that this preference is learnable. Across three settings with independent human calibration, gains under a convention-sensitive scorer exceed the corresponding gains in committed-answer correctness, i.e., the correctness of the answer the model actually commits to. Together, these results separate three distinct post-training outcomes: learned reporting preference, current-request adherence, and committed-answer correctness. They show that convention-matched accuracy alone does not fully characterize post-training behavior and motivate evaluating current-request adherence alongside convention-matched task accuracy.

</details>


### [174] [Interactive-Policy Distillation with Bidirectional Propose-and-Verify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36546)
*Shutong Wu,Xiwen Chen,Brendan Rappazzo,Daiheng Zhang,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IPD提出一种交互式策略蒸馏方法：学生与教师通过双向propose-and-verify状态机交替充当proposer与verifier，生成来源混合轨迹，并按token来源施加不同监督，缓解on-policy蒸馏中的teacher unanchoring问题。论文还给出师生双模型共置、分离KV cache的融合推理引擎。在数学推理任务上，将Qwen3-30B-A3B蒸馏到Qwen3-1.7B-Base时，IPD较OPD平均mean@8与best@8提升+3.28；仅用约1/4训练样本和步数即超过OPD整集一个epoch。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy distillation（OPD）存在teacher unanchoring问题：学生自生成轨迹会漂移到教师几乎不会访问的状态，导致教师提供的token级监督不可靠。论文要解决如何在学生rollout中自适应引入教师干预，同时保持对学生自身分布的训练，并让交错的师生双模型rollout在推理服务上更高效。

Method: IPD的输入是学生模型在推理任务上的自生成rollout。核心流程由双向propose-and-verify状态机控制：学生与教师交替交换proposer和verifier角色，教师在必要时自适应干预学生的生成，从而产生来源混合的轨迹（部分token由学生提出、部分token由教师提出或验证）。生成完成后，训练阶段根据每个token的来源应用source-split loss，对学生来源token和教师来源token施加不同监督，使得学生既能在自身分布上学习，也能接收教师的高质量纠正，并统一了on-policy与off-policy蒸馏。为了支撑这一交错双模型rollout，作者设计了专用融合推理引擎：在单个serving实例中同时托管教师与学生模型，为二者维护独立的KV cache，并将状态机实例化为调度模型，负责在双模型之间分发、收集和处理请求，从而降低交错推理的开销。推理/部署路径是同一融合实例按状态机在师生模型间切换执行，训练目标是结合混合来源监督的蒸馏损失。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "benchmark-averaged mean@8 accuracy", "value": "+3.28", "setting": "将Qwen3-30B-A3B蒸馏到Qwen3-1.7B-Base，IPD相比OPD"}, {"metric": "benchmark-averaged best@8 accuracy", "value": "+3.28", "setting": "将Qwen3-30B-A3B蒸馏到Qwen3-1.7B-Base，IPD相比OPD"}, {"metric": "训练样本与训练步数", "value": "约1/4", "setting": "IPD仅消耗约1/4训练样本和步数即可超过在完整训练集上训练一个epoch的OPD"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Interactive-Policy Distillation（IPD）：在学生rollout中自适应引入教师干预，使学生与教师交替充当proposer和verifier的双向propose-and-verify状态机，协作生成混合来源轨迹。；source-split loss：根据每个token的来源（学生或教师）应用不同监督，使IPD统一on-policy与off-policy蒸馏范式。；专用融合推理引擎：将教师和学生两个模型共置在同一个serving实例中，使用分离的KV cache，并以状态机实例化模型来分发、收集和处理请求，以提升交错双模型rollout效率。；对loss变体以及takeover / handback配置进行实验分析，并验证IPD在不同训练数据上的鲁棒性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model on its self-generated trajectories with dense token-level teacher feedback. However, naive OPD may suffer from teacher unanchoring, where the student's reasoning trajectory drifts far from the teacher, causing the teacher to be queried on states it would hardly visit and thus provide unreliable supervision. We propose Interactive-Policy Distillation (IPD), which applies adaptive teacher intervention to the student rollout. Under a bidirectional propose-and-verify state machine, the student and teacher alternately exchange their roles as proposer and verifier, and collaboratively generate mixed-source trajectories. Then different supervisions are applied according to the source of each token. This bidirectional propose-and-verify mechanism and the source-split loss make IPD not only a more performant distillation method, but also a unified bridge between on-policy and off-policy paradigms. To make the interleaved dual-model rollouts more efficient, we also design a dedicated fused inference engine that co-hosts both models in one serving instance with separate KV caches and instantiates the state machine model to distribute, collect, and process requests. On math reasoning tasks and across multiple teacher-student model pairs, student models trained with IPD not only outperform those trained with OPD, but also demonstrate higher data efficiency. Specifically, when distilling Qwen3-30B-A3B into Qwen3-1.7B-Base, IPD brings a +3.28 mean@8 and a +3.28 best@8 benchmark-averaged accuracy improvement compared with OPD. Besides, IPD only consumes about 1/4 of the training examples and steps to outperform OPD trained on the whole training dataset for one epoch. We also investigate the impact of different loss variants and takeover / handback configurations, and demonstrate the robustness of IPD on different training data.

</details>


### [175] [From Checkpoint Variation to Selection Gains in Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36569)
*Yupeng Chang,Wenxuan Zhang,Yuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究SFT中检查点选择问题，通过受控实验证明增加验证数据可提升选择效果，且基于生成的选择规则优于NLL选择，但相对于最终检查点的增益尚不明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在监督微调中，如何从多个训练检查点中选出一个最优检查点，以及验证数据量、选择规则（如生成精度）与最终检查点基线之间的增益是否可分离测量。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是聚焦于监督微调（SFT）流程中的检查点选择环节。其方法论可概括为：在固定的训练轨迹、候选检查点和独立测试项下，变化验证集预算，测量三种增益——增加验证数据带来的改进、相对NLL选择的增益、以及相对最终检查点的增益。选择规则基于生成精度（如生成答案的准确性）和检查点之间的一致性（如多个检查点输出是否一致）。训练目标仍为标准的SFT损失（如交叉熵），推理流程为直接使用选定的检查点进行生成任务。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "独立测试准确率", "value": "验证预算从32增加到305-313个例子后，生成精度选择提升0.32 pp，检查点一致性选择提升0.29 pp，95%配置自助CI分别为[0.10, 0.56]和[0.11, 0.50]。", "setting": "60个数学SFT轨迹，19种配置"}, {"metric": "相对于NLL选择的增益", "value": "在完整验证预算下，生成精度选择比匹配的NLL选择高0.71 pp，检查点一致性选择高0.85 pp。", "setting": "60个数学SFT轨迹，19种配置"}, {"metric": "跨域复制中的准确率提升", "value": "验证预算从32增至1,024个问题，生成精度选择提升0.87 pp，检查点一致性选择提升0.27 pp。", "setting": "12个新训练的常识推理轨迹"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将检查点选择建模为有限信息决策问题，区分三个经验性声明：增加验证数据是否提升选择、选择规则是否优于NLL选择、是否优于保留最终检查点。；提出基于生成精度（generated-accuracy）和检查点一致性（checkpoint agreement）的选择规则。；通过60个数学SFT轨迹和19种配置的受控实验，以及12个常识推理轨迹的跨域复制，量化了不同验证预算下的增益。

Abstract: Checkpoint selection is a routine decision in supervised fine-tuning (SFT): training produces multiple checkpoints, but only one is retained. Yet fixed-budget comparisons do not by themselves distinguish three empirical claims: whether more validation data improve checkpoint selection, whether a selection rule outperforms validation-loss selection, and whether it improves over simply retaining the final checkpoint. We therefore treat checkpoint selection as a finite-information decision problem. Holding completed training trajectories, candidate checkpoints, and independent test items fixed, we vary the validation budget and separately measure improvement from additional validation data, gain over negative log-likelihood (NLL) selection, and gain over the final checkpoint. Across 60 mathematical SFT trajectories and 19 configurations, increasing the validation budget from 32 to 305-313 examples raises independent-test accuracy by 0.32 percentage points (pp) for generated-accuracy selection and 0.29 pp for checkpoint agreement, with 95% configuration-bootstrap CIs of [0.10, 0.56] and [0.11, 0.50], respectively. At the full validation budget, the two generation-based rules outperform matched NLL selection by 0.71 and 0.85 pp, respectively, while their gains over the final checkpoint remain unresolved. A cross-domain replication on 12 newly trained Commonsense trajectories shows the same qualitative separation: increasing the validation budget from 32 to 1,024 questions improves generated-accuracy and checkpoint-agreement selection by 0.87 and 0.27 pp, while gains over the final checkpoint again remain unresolved. Together, these results show that benefiting from more validation data, outperforming NLL selection, and outperforming the final checkpoint are distinct empirical claims that require separate evidence.

</details>


### [176] [Triadic Linear Attention: Three-Dimensional Recurrent States for Long-Context Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36529)
*Oliver Sieberling,Bharat Runwal,David Jin,Ryan Chin,Rameswar Panda,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三阶线性注意力，将递归状态从矩阵扩展为三阶张量，通过键、第二键和值的三重外积写入并用两个查询收缩读取，以极低参数代价大幅增加状态容量，兼容Gated DeltaNet和scalar-gated线性注意力，在长上下文建模和召回任务上优于其他状态扩展方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力通过外积将向量隐藏状态扩展为矩阵状态，但状态容量受限于二阶结构，在长上下文语言建模和召回任务上性能受限。论文研究如何进一步扩展递归状态容量，同时保持参数效率、数据依赖遗忘能力和并行训练可行性。

Method: 三阶线性注意力将历史上下文压缩到固定大小的三阶张量状态中。具体地，使用键向量k1、第二键向量k2和值向量v做三阶外积，写入张量状态S（维度为d_k1 × d_k2 × d_v）。读取时使用两个查询向量q1和q2分别沿两个键轴与张量状态收缩，得到输出。这一过程可看作线性注意力的推广：普通线性注意力使用二阶外积和单个查询收缩，而三阶方法将状态容量提升至三阶，且通过仅添加两个投影（将输入映射到第二键和对应查询）控制参数增长。该方法支持数据依赖的遗忘机制（如门控）和delta规则，使其能够在线性时间内进行递归推理，并可通过chunkwise并行训练高效训练。该架构可直接替换Gated DeltaNet或scalar-gated linear attention中的线性注意力层，在长上下文语言建模和召回任务上取得提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出三阶（3D）张量递归状态，通过键、第二键和值的三重外积写入状态，并用两个查询分别收缩键轴进行读取，将状态容量从矩阵级扩展到张量级。；每个E维第二键仅需增加两个投影即可带来E倍状态大小增长，参数效率显著。；方法兼容数据依赖遗忘、delta规则和chunkwise并行训练，可无缝集成到Gated DeltaNet和scalar-gated linear attention中。；在长上下文语言建模和召回基准上，性能优于其他扩大状态的替代方案，验证了三阶状态的有效性。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks (RNNs) compress the historical context into a memory state of fixed size, thus allowing for constant-time inference. The memory state size is a crucial factor in their performance, as exemplified by the strong performance and resurgence of linear attention, which extends the vector-valued hidden states of ordinary RNNs to matrix-valued hidden states. Crucially, linear attention does so in a parameter-efficient way, in particular by using an outer product of the key and value vectors to write to the matrix-valued hidden state. We generalize this construction and propose triadic linear attention, which writes the triadic outer product of a key, a second key, and a value, into a third-order (i.e., 3D) tensor state, and reads from it by contracting both key axes with two queries. An $E$-dimensional second key thus yields an $E$-fold increase in state size while adding only two projections. Triadic linear attention is compatible with data-dependent forgetting, the delta rule, and chunkwise-parallel training. Applied to Gated DeltaNet and scalar-gated linear attention, triadic linear attention substantially improves long-context language modeling and recall, outperforming alternatives that enlarge the state.

</details>


### [177] [Adapting Context Compression for Long-Horizon Agents with Counterfactual Continuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36526)
*Guanghui Min,Liang Wu,Mingjia Shi,Yinhan He,Mayank Darbari,Liangjie Hong,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 PAIR，一种基于反事实续接的干预式提示适配方法，用于优化长时程智能体的上下文压缩模板。通过隔离单个压缩事件的影响并修订提示，PAIR 在多个基准上取得了压缩方法中最强的跨运行可靠性，使压缩执行接近无压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体在交互历史增长时需要进行上下文压缩，而压缩质量最终由下游执行决定，现有提示适配方法通过比较全上下文与压缩后的轨迹来推断压缩错误，无法隔离单个压缩事件且受智能体随机性干扰。

Method: PAIR 以结构化压缩提示模板作为输入，该模板固定用于指导智能体在长时程交互中压缩上下文。在智能体执行过程中，PAIR 通过匹配的反事实续接进行干预式演练：对于每一个压缩事件，从同一智能体状态出发，分别在有压缩和无压缩条件下继续执行，从而隔离该次压缩对后续任务执行的影响。PAIR 识别出显著降低后续执行可靠性的单个压缩，诊断其具体效应，并据此修订固定压缩模板中的相关部分。修订后的模板被重新用于智能体的上下文压缩，而下游智能体本身不做任何修改。该流程迭代进行，逐步优化压缩提示，最终在不改动智能体的前提下使压缩后的执行表现接近甚至偶尔超过无压缩基线。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现压缩首先降低可靠性而非可解性，严重退化集中在孤立的压缩事件上；提出匹配的反事实续接方法，从同一智能体状态比较有无压缩的执行结果以隔离单个压缩的影响；提出 PAIR（Prompt Adaptation using Interventional Rollouts），识别导致后续执行退化的单个压缩、诊断其影响，并修订固定压缩模板的相关部分

Abstract: Long-horizon agents require context compression to manage growing interaction histories. Compression quality, however, is ultimately determined by downstream execution. Existing prompt-adaptation methods infer compression errors by comparing full-context and compressed trajectories. Such comparisons cannot isolate individual compressions and are confounded by agent stochasticity. We first find that compression degrades reliability before solvability. Using matched counterfactual continuations that compare execution from the same agent state with versus without compression, we further show that severe degradation concentrates at isolated compression events. Motivated by this finding, we propose PAIR (Prompt Adaptation using Interventional Rollouts) for adapting structured compression prompts. PAIR identifies individual compressions that degrade subsequent execution, diagnoses their effects, and revises the relevant sections of a fixed compression template. PAIR achieves the strongest cross-run reliability among compressed methods in every main benchmark-scope combination, consistently exceeding the competing prompt-adaptation baseline. Without modifying the downstream agent, PAIR brings compressed execution close to the no-compression baseline and sometimes numerically exceeds it.

</details>


### [178] [AdaptArena: Evaluating Test-Time Personalization of Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36488)
*Dongchan Shin,Xing Han Lù,Jiaqi Deng,Jay Gala,Tomás Vergara Browne,Jaewon Moon,Fengyuan Liu,Alexandre Drouin,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Lacoste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaptArena是一个评估Web Agent测试时个性化能力的基准，通过隐式偏好推断和检索历史用户轨迹来衡量个性化效果。实验显示oracle agent成功率82.92%，而实际LLM agent仅15.62%，指出偏好推断与动作执行是两大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web Agent难以应对用户意图描述不充分、偏好异构的场景，缺乏评估测试时个性化能力的基准，且隐式偏好推断与下游动作执行之间存在显著鸿沟。

Method: AdaptArena包含480个任务，分为单偏好和双偏好场景。每个任务需要系统从历史用户轨迹中检索出最相关的一条（隐式编码目标偏好），然后基于该轨迹推断偏好并执行Web导航动作。AdaptiveAgent是一个基于检索的框架：给定当前任务和用户的历史轨迹库，首先进行检索以找到最相关的轨迹，利用该轨迹推断用户偏好，然后指导Web Agent执行任务。评估时对比了oracle agent（直接获得真实偏好）与使用该框架的LLM agent。具体模型架构、训练目标、检索机制细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "success rate", "value": "82.92%", "setting": "oracle agent with ground-truth preferences on AdaptArena"}, {"metric": "success rate", "value": "15.62%", "setting": "best LLM agent using AdaptiveAgent framework on AdaptArena"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出AdaptArena基准：480个任务，覆盖单偏好和双偏好场景，要求检索并利用最相关的历史用户轨迹来推断目标偏好。；引入AdaptiveAgent：一种基于检索的标准化评估框架，用于隐式偏好推断，支持一致的评估流程。；实验揭示关键瓶颈：oracle agent成功率达82.92%，而基于该框架的LLM agent最高仅15.62%，且正确推断偏好是任务成功的必要非充分条件，执行失败仍是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong performance on complex web navigation tasks, yet they remain brittle in real-world settings where user intentions are underspecified and preferences are heterogeneous. In practice, users rarely provide explicit profiles, requiring agents to infer latent preferences from implicit signals. Despite its importance for deployment, this problem setting is largely underexplored in existing benchmarks. To address this gap, we introduce AdaptArena, a benchmark for evaluating test-time personalization of web agents via implicit preference inference. AdaptArena consists of 480 tasks, featuring both single-preference and double-preference scenarios. Each evaluation task must be solved by retrieving and leveraging the most relevant historical user trajectory that implicitly encodes the target preference. In addition, we introduce AdaptiveAgent, a retrieval-based framework for standardized evaluation of implicit preference inference. Experiments reveal a substantial performance gap: while oracle agents with access to ground-truth preferences achieve an 82.92% success rate, the evaluated LLM agents using our framework reach at most 15.62%. Furthermore, we find that correctly inferring user preferences is necessary but not sufficient for task success, as execution failures in downstream web interactions remain a significant bottleneck even when agents align with the target preference. These findings highlight implicit preference inference and robust action grounding as key challenges for deploying reliable, user-facing web agents. We release our code: this https URL

</details>


### [179] [The Teacher Is a Direction, Not a Destination: Extrapolating RL-Induced Representation Residuals in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36484)
*Hao Li,MeiJia Chen,Weijie Ren,Donghan Li,Zijun Tian,Jingchun Huang,Naibo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出RIDE，在表示空间外推RL诱导的残差，以稳定on-policy蒸馏并让学生超越RL教师。在四个base/RL-teacher对上，RIDE是唯一均值达到或超过RL教师的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在on-policy distillation中，输出空间外推依赖采样token的对数概率比，语言模型头会各向异性地衰减隐藏状态变化，且采样噪声被外推放大，导致训练不稳定；如何更稳定地让学生超越RL教师。

Method: RIDE是一种on-policy蒸馏方法，在训练时对每层、每个token位置计算RL教师与其pre-RL checkpoint的隐藏状态残差，即RL诱导的表示变化方向。然后以学生当前轨迹为条件，将学生的隐藏状态回归到沿该残差方向超出教师的偏移目标（目标 = 教师隐藏状态 + α·残差，α为外推系数）。该回归在数学上等价于在二次惩罚（以教师为中心）下最大化一个由残差定义的线性方向奖励，从而在表示空间沿RL诱导方向移动学生，同时限制其偏离教师，避免输出空间外推中log-prob比带来的噪声放大和语言模型头的各向异性衰减。训练目标为回归损失，推理路径与标准LM一致。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean performance across four base/RL-teacher pairs", "value": "approach or exceeds the RL-trained teacher", "setting": "四个base/RL-teacher对，覆盖不同规模、架构、预训练谱系"}, {"metric": "comparison with output-space extrapolation", "value": "consistently outperforms", "setting": "当教师接近其base checkpoint时，输出空间外推会退化学生"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 观察到RL会相对base checkpoint在每层产生内部表示偏移，且方向可测。；提出RIDE，在每层和每个token位置计算教师与其pre-RL checkpoint的残差，并将学生隐藏状态回归到沿残差方向超出教师的偏移目标上。；证明该回归等价于在二次惩罚下最大化由残差定义的线性方向奖励，显式控制学生沿RL诱导方向移动并限制偏离教师。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student to match the teacher's next-token distributions on the student's own trajectories and has yielded substantial empirical gains. Generalized variants allow the student to surpass the teacher by extrapolating an implicit reward in output space. The language-model head, however, attenuates this change anisotropically: much of the change encoded in the teacher's hidden states reaches the logits at a small fraction of its weight, and the sampled-token log-probability ratios on which output-space extrapolation relies inject noise that the extrapolation amplifies, making training unstable. We observe that reinforcement learning (RL) shifts a model's internal representations relative to its base checkpoint, and that the direction of this shift can be measured at every layer. Motivated by this observation, we propose RIDE (RL-Induced Direction Extrapolation), which extrapolates the RL-induced change directly in representation space: at every layer and token position, RIDE computes the residual between the teacher and its pre-RL checkpoint and regresses the student's hidden states toward targets displaced beyond the teacher along this residual. Conditioned on a sampled trajectory, this regression is equivalent to maximizing a linear directional reward defined by the residual under a quadratic penalty centered at the teacher, which makes explicit how the objective moves the student along the RL-induced direction while limiting its deviation from the teacher. Across four base/RL-teacher pairs spanning different scales, architectures, and pre-training lineages, RIDE approaches or exceeds the RL-trained teacher on every pair and is the only method whose mean does so, and it consistently outperforms output-space extrapolation, which degrades the student whenever the teacher is close to its base. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [180] [Reward-rate Policy Gradient for Efficient Machine Learning Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36393)
*Muhang Tian,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Reward-rate Policy Gradient（RPG），把连续时间 RL 与 SMDP 思想引入 agentic RL，通过 off-policy 估计奖励速率并按动作耗时计费，使 MLE agent 在固定时间预算内获得更高奖励；在 Qwen3.5-4B 的自我改进循环下于 MLE-Bench 与 NanoGPT 分别取得 19.2% 和 85.7% 的奖励提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RL 假设每个动作消耗固定单位时间，但在 MLE 等 agentic 任务中动作耗时差异很大，导致在固定时间预算下优化累积奖励不等价于最大化单位时间收益。

Method: 论文方法 RPG 以 Semi-Markov Decision Process 为建模基础：状态为 agent 当前所处环境状态，动作为数据加载、特征工程、模型训练等 MLE 操作，每个动作带有可变持续时间。RPG 首先从 off-policy 样本估计长期单位时间奖励（reward rate），随后在策略梯度更新中按每个动作实际消耗的时间以该 rate 进行惩罚或计费，从而直接优化奖励速率而非累积奖励。理论上在 bandit 设定下证明 RPG 能近似最优 reward rate，并避免已有方法需要对策略空间枚举的问题。训练流程上，RPG 被嵌入小语言模型 Qwen3.5-4B 的自我改进循环中，在固定时间预算内与环境交互并更新策略；推理/执行时 agent 通过外部环境执行 MLE 动作并接收反馈，最终在 MLE-Bench 与 NanoGPT 任务上评估固定时间预算下的表现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "固定时间预算内的奖励提升", "value": "19.2%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-4B 在 MLE-Bench 上，与 vanilla RL 对比"}, {"metric": "固定时间预算内的奖励提升", "value": "85.7%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-4B 在 NanoGPT 上，与 vanilla RL 对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从连续时间 RL 与 Semi-Markov Decision Process (SMDP) 出发提出 Reward-rate Policy Gradient (RPG)；用 off-policy 样本估计 reward rate，并按每个动作消耗的时间以该 rate 进行计费；在 bandit 设定下理论分析 RPG 可近似最优 reward rate，并避免对策略空间枚举；将 RPG 应用于小语言模型（Qwen3.5-4B）的自我改进循环，在固定时间预算下优于 vanilla RL

Abstract: Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) techniques focus on maximizing expected cumulative reward, where each action assumes to take a constant unit of time. However, this assumption does not hold for agentic RL tasks such as machine learning engineering (MLE) agents, where actions involve data loading, feature engineering, and model training that take variable durations. Efficiency matters in modern agentic RL where actions are costly. To address this limitation, we adapt from continuous-time RL and Semi-Markov Decision Process (SMDP) formulation and propose Reward-rate Policy Gradient (RPG), where we focus on optimizing the reward rate -- the long-term reward per unit of time. RPG estimates the reward rate from off-policy samples, then charges each action for the time it consumes at that rate. We first conduct theoretical analysis in the bandit setting to establish that RPG approximates the optimal reward rate and empirically demonstrate it outperforms baselines while avoiding enumeration over the policy space, a known issue for an existing method. We then further apply RPG on a small language model (Qwen3.5-4B) with self-improvement loops and empirically show it obtains higher rewards within a fixed time budget than vanilla RL on MLE-Bench and NanoGPT, with a 19.2% and 85.7% margin, respectively. Our method provides a practical solution for optimizing performance under wait time considerations in modern agentic RL tasks, where actions interact with external environments and cost time.

</details>


### [181] [When Trees Are Not Enough: Learning Mixed-Topology Feature Graphs with Adaptive Graph Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36294)
*Xiaozuo Shen,Yifei Cai,Tian Tan,Rui Ning,Chunsheng Xin,Hongyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AG-SAE将每个特征的完整父集作为原子结构假设，学习LLM激活中多亲混合拓扑特征图，并以此结构作为可微损失指导SAE训练，通过拓扑引导精炼缓解特征吸收。在玩具模型、真实LLM激活关系可靠性和因果干预实验中优于结构化SAE与后验图基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化稀疏自编码器只能表达单亲树/森林，无法可靠恢复特征间的多亲混合拓扑关系；后验图方法虽允许多亲但不指导特征学习，也无法保证关系恢复的可靠性。

Method: AG-SAE以LLM激活为输入，目标是学习可解释的稀疏特征字典并同时恢复特征间的混合拓扑关系。主要流程为：(1) 结构假设：对每个特征，将其完整父集视为一个原子结构假设，候选包括空集、单亲、多亲、子集和替代解释；通过竞争与必要性检验选择零个、一个或多个父节点，要求每个子特征在父集之外仍有贡献，从而拒绝冗余或伪关系。(2) 拓扑诱导：在所有特征上诱导出混合拓扑图，图结构随后定义可微结构损失。(3) 结构引导训练：该可微损失与SAE常规重构目标联合优化，使字典学习过程中受到关系结构约束。(4) 拓扑引导精炼：利用学习到的结构暴露的持久重构差异初始化新特征，缓解特征吸收。(5) 自洽循环：从修订后的字典重新诱导图，重新评估每个特征的完整父集，迭代直至字典与图自洽。推理时，AG-SAE输出稀疏特征字典及其诱导的混合拓扑图，可用于特征组织、关系查询和因果干预。该架构解决树/森林表达力不足、后验图不指导学习、关系恢复不可靠、特征吸收等问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "精确混合拓扑恢复", "value": "在受控玩具模型中实现精确恢复", "setting": "受控玩具模型"}, {"metric": "关系可靠性与语义有效性", "value": "优于结构化SAE与后验图基线", "setting": "真实LLM激活"}, {"metric": "特征级因果干预强度", "value": "强于传统SAE特征", "setting": "真实LLM激活上的因果干预实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将每个特征的完整父集视为原子结构假设，通过证据选择零个、一个或多个父节点，并竞争完整父集对null/子集/替代解释进行必要性检验。；用诱导出的混合拓扑定义可微结构损失，引导SAE训练，使恢复的结构成为无监督训练信号。；拓扑引导精炼：利用持久重建差异初始化新特征，缓解特征吸收。；字典-图自洽循环：从修订字典重新诱导完整图，反复评估父集，闭合一致性问题。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) expose interpretable features in large language model activations, yet existing structured SAEs impose single-parent trees or forests, while post-hoc graphs permit multiple parents but neither guide feature learning nor ensure reliable relation recovery. We introduce the Adaptive Graph Sparse Autoencoder (AG-SAE), a structure-guided training paradigm that treats each feature's complete parent set as an atomic structural hypothesis and lets evidence select zero, one, or multiple parents. By competing complete parent sets against null, subset, and alternative explanations, AG-SAE identifies jointly necessary multi-parent relations while rejecting redundant or spurious alternatives and verifying that each child contributes beyond its parents. The induced topology over SAE features then defines a differentiable structural loss that guides SAE training, while topology-guided refinement mitigates feature absorption and uses persistent reconstruction gaps exposed by the learned structure to initialize new features. The entire graph is then induced again from the revised dictionary by reassessing every feature's complete parent set, closing the dictionary-graph self-consistency cycle. Experiments demonstrate exact mixed-topology recovery in a controlled toy model, greater relational reliability and semantic validity than structured and post-hoc baselines on real LLM activations, and stronger feature-level causal interventions than conventional SAE features. AG-SAE thereby turns recovered mixed-topology feature structure into an unsupervised training signal that improves the dictionary, enables reliable feature organization beyond the topological limitations of trees, and exhibits stronger causal control beyond reconstruction.

</details>


### [182] [MoRE: Scaling mixture of experts with hardware-aware low-rank routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36301)
*Honam Wong,Surbhi Goel,Enric Boix-Adserà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoRE通过低秩分解MoE路由器并设计融合Triton内核，将路由成本从Θ(Mh)降至O((h+M)r)，在匹配FLOPs下可支持更多专家，并在电话簿记忆与知识Q&A基准上取得提升，推理能力匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE路由器的线性计算成本Θ(Mh)在专家数量M很大时成为瓶颈，限制了更多更小专家的可扩展性。

Method: MoRE将标准MoE层中的线性路由器权重矩阵进行低秩分解，秩为r。输入token经隐藏层表示后，通过两个低秩矩阵相乘得到路由logits，路由成本从Θ(Mh)降至O((h+M)r)。在训练时，路由器以低秩形式参数化；推理时，设计融合Triton内核，避免在HBM上进行昂贵的中间内存读写，从而在wall-clock时间上实现理论增益。模型保持固定数量的活跃专家，但通过低秩路由可扩展更多专家。目标函数与标准MoE一致，包括负载均衡损失，并通过理论证明和合成任务验证了低秩路由的表达性与负载均衡性质。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "专家数量", "value": "在匹配活跃FLOPs下，可支持Θ(h/r)倍更多的专家", "setting": "理论分析"}, {"metric": "记忆化性能", "value": "在电话簿任务上提升", "setting": "合成任务实验"}, {"metric": "知识密集型Q&A基准", "value": "预训练后性能提升", "setting": "Q&A基准"}, {"metric": "推理能力", "value": "匹配原有水平", "setting": "推理基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出MoRE（Mixture of Rank-reduced-routed Experts），将路由权重矩阵分解为秩r，使路由成本降至O((h+M)r)。；证明当活跃专家数固定时，秩对数于M即可保证路由表达性，且该条件在精度因子下是必要的。；证明在高斯记忆化模型中低秩路由可保持负载均衡，并通过合成电话簿任务验证低秩不损害记忆能力。；设计融合Triton内核以避免HBM上的昂贵内存操作，实现wall-clock时间收益。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers are central to frontier language models, and recent architectures push toward more and smaller experts. In this regime, the standard linear router becomes a bottleneck: with $M$ experts and hidden dimension $h$, its per-token cost $\Theta(Mh)$ dominates the MoE layer once $M$ is large. We introduce MoRE (Mixture of Rank-reduced-routed Experts), which factorizes the router weight matrix at rank $r$ and reduces the routing cost to $O((h + M)r)$. We prove that rank logarithmic in $M$ suffices for routing expressivity when the number of active experts is fixed, and is necessary up to precision factors. We also prove that logarithmic rank preserves load balance in a Gaussian memorization model, and training on a synthetic phonebook task shows that low rank does not hurt memorization. At matched active FLOPs, the factorization allows a factor of $\Theta(h/r)$ more experts. To realize this gain in wall-clock time, we design a fused Triton kernel at inference that avoids expensive memory operations on HBM. Empirically, MoRE improves memorization on the phonebook task and performance on knowledge-intensive Q\&A benchmarks after pretraining, while matching reasoning ability. Code available at this https URL .

</details>


### [183] [Periodic Weak Spots: Phase Sensitivity from Chunked KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36322)
*Xingyu Zhu,Ziheng Cheng,Ang Lv,Jing Liu,Lexing Ying,Yiyuan Ma,Xin Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示分块 KV-cache 压缩带来的“相位敏感”现象：同一信息在不同相位（token 相对压缩窗口边界的位置）下检索性能差异最高达 40 个百分点。作者通过多组从零预训练的 Transformer 复现该现象，用因果干预发现注意力组件的相位特化，并用理想化检索模型分析梯度流如何导致这种特化。结论是评估分块压缩模型必须跨相位测量，平均分可能掩盖系统性位置失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块 KV-cache 压缩引入了新的位置坐标——相位（token 相对压缩窗口边界的位置），导致同一信息在不同相位下检索难度系统性不同；平均基准分数会掩盖这种周期性弱点，因此需要按相位评估压缩模型。

Method: 论文针对采用分块 KV-cache 压缩的长上下文推理架构：标准注意力把每个 token 的 KV 写入缓存，而分块压缩以固定 stride 将连续 token 窗口压缩为更少的缓存条目，从而降低显存与注意力开销。该压缩引入了一个新的位置坐标“相位”，即 token 相对于压缩窗口边界的位置。论文的架构分析流程为：(1) 在大型开放权重、使用分块 KV-cache 压缩的模型上测量长上下文检索准确率随相位的变化，发现同一信息在不同相位下可检索性差异最高达 40 个百分点；(2) 从零预训练一族 Transformer，覆盖多种 KV 压缩设计，在受控条件下复现相位敏感现象；(3) 在这些模型上做因果干预的机制分析，考察各注意力组件对不同源相位信息检索的贡献，发现注意力组件存在相位特化，即不同组件不对称地服务于不同相位的检索；(4) 构建理想化检索模型，分析梯度流动力学如何可能选择出尖锐的相位特化。推理时模型仍按分块压缩的 KV 缓存进行注意力计算，而相位决定了 token 相对压缩窗口边界的位置，进而影响其被检索的难易。论文强调评估需跨压缩相位进行，而非仅看平均基准。文中未披露具体参数量、层数、压缩 stride 或训练细节，故不在此补充。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "长上下文检索准确率跨相位差异", "value": "最高 40 个百分点", "setting": "大型开放权重且使用分块 KV-cache 压缩的模型，长上下文检索任务，按相位对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出并定义 phase sensitivity（相位敏感）现象：分块 KV-cache 压缩下检索性能随相位周期性变化，长上下文检索精度跨相位差异最高达 40 个百分点。；从零预训练一族 Transformer 覆盖多种 KV 压缩设计，复现相位敏感现象，说明该问题不依赖特定压缩方案或开源权重。；通过因果干预的机制分析揭示相位特化：不同注意力组件对检索不同源相位的信息贡献不对称。；分析理想化检索模型，说明梯度流动力学可能倾向于形成尖锐的相位特化。；提出评估方法论建议：对使用分块 KV-cache 压缩的模型必须跨压缩相位测量，高平均准确率可能与系统性位置失败共存。

Abstract: Chunked KV-cache compression reduces the memory and attention costs of long-context inference by compressing windows of consecutive tokens into fewer cache entries at a fixed stride. Such compression also introduces a new positional coordinate: a token's phase, or its position relative to compression-window boundaries. We uncover a systematic asymmetry in models using such compression: the same information can be easy to retrieve at one phase and difficult at another. We call this periodic variation in retrieval performance phase sensitivity. In large open-weight models with such compression, long-context retrieval accuracy can differ by up to 40 percentage points across phases, revealing periodic weak spots that average benchmark scores can conceal. To investigate this behavior, we pretrain a family of transformers from scratch across multiple KV-compression designs, reproducing phase sensitivity across the variants. Mechanistic analysis using causal interventions in these models reveals phase specialization: different attention components contribute asymmetrically to retrieving information at different source phases. We further analyze idealized retrieval models, showing how gradient flow dynamics may favor sharp phase specialization. Evaluating models with chunked KV-cache compression thus requires measuring across compression phases: high average accuracy can coexist with systematic positional failures.

</details>


### [184] [The Universal Classifier for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36302)
*Ben Finkelshtein,André Linhares,Petar Veličković,Bryan Perozzi,Mikhail Galkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出通用分类器（UC），通过将异构特征和标签映射到三维潜在张量，并用相似度分类统一节点、边、图级任务，使单一预训练模型能零样本迁移到未见图上的多种预测任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图机器学习领域缺乏统一的特征与结构表示，现有所谓的图基础模型通常局限于节点级预测或需要固定特征维度，无法为全谱系图学习任务提供任务无关的骨干网络。

Method: 输入为图数据，包含任意维度的节点特征和任意类别的标签。模型首先将异构特征和标签提升为三维潜在张量，从而在统一的潜在空间中表示不同模态和图元素。核心模块是一个基于相似度的分类器，通过最大化潜在空间中元素之间的相似度，将节点级、边级和图级预测任务统一为同一个分类目标。训练目标是最小化相似度分类损失，使得模型学习到独立于具体输入模式的可迁移特征表示。在推理阶段，单一预训练模型可直接应用于未见图上的节点分类、节点回归和链接预测，无需针对特定任务调整特征维度或类别数。整个流程消除了以往方法对固定特征维度和任务类型的依赖，实现了任务无关的图学习骨干网络。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过将异构特征和标签提升为三维潜在张量，将节点、边、图级预测统一重述为潜空间中的相似度最大化分类目标，支持任意特征和类别基数。；提出统一的相似度分类目标，使单一预训练模型能够泛化到未见图的节点分类、节点回归和链接预测任务。；架构支持抽象掉特定输入模式，学习与输入模式无关的可迁移特征。

Abstract: While foundation models have revolutionized natural language processing and computer vision by leveraging universal vocabularies, Graph Machine Learning (GML) remains fractured due to the absence of a unified feature and structural representation across diverse domains. Existing works claiming to be Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) are typically restricted to node-level predictions or require fixed feature dimensions, failing to provide a truly task-agnostic backbone for the full spectrum of graph learning applications. In this paper, we introduce the Universal Classifier (UC), which supports arbitrary feature and class cardinalities, unifying node-, edge-, and graph-level objectives under a single similarity-based classification objective. The UC reformulates all node-, edge-, and graph-level prediction tasks as maximizing similarity in the latent space: by lifting heterogeneous features and labels into 3D latent tensors, the model learns transferable features independent of specific input schemas. This architecture allows a single pre-trained model to generalize to node classification, node regression, and link prediction across unseen graphs with varying feature semantics. Experiments show strong zero-shot transfer performance across node-, link-, and graph-level tasks.

</details>


### [185] [Fractional State Space Transition for Long Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36314)
*Ivan Kobyzev,Abbas Ghaddar,Ali Nasiri-Sarvi,Lifeng Shang,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FRAC 提出分数阶选择性状态空间模型，用幂律长记忆替代指数遗忘，通过有限状态对数间隔指数模式之和实现高效循环模块，支持并行训练与有界状态解码。1.3B 参数语言建模实验表明长上下文性能优于 SOTA SSM 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 ODE 的状态空间模型具有指数遗忘特性，难以在长时序范围内保留信息，限制了长上下文建模能力。

Method: FRAC 是一种选择性状态空间模型，其动力学从分数阶微分方程导出，目标记忆核为重尾幂律形式。为便于实际实现，FRAC 用有限个对数间隔的指数模式之和逼近该重尾核，将分数阶记忆转化为一个循环模块。该模块在训练和预填充阶段支持并行计算，在自回归解码阶段保持有界状态，从而兼顾长上下文建模能力与推理效率。实验部分包括 1.3B 参数的语言建模任务，在长上下文性能上持续优于现有 SSM 基线，短上下文性能保持竞争力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "长上下文语言建模性能", "value": "一致优于 state-of-the-art SSM 基线", "setting": "1.3B 参数语言建模，长上下文评估"}, {"metric": "短上下文性能", "value": "与基线保持竞争力", "setting": "1.3B 参数语言建模，短上下文评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 FRAC，一种基于分数阶动力学的选择性 SSM 架构，用幂律长记忆替代指数衰减；通过有限状态、对数间隔的指数模式之和逼近重尾目标核，使分数阶动力学可高效实现；构造出支持并行训练和预填充、同时保持有界状态自回归解码的高效循环模块

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs) compress sequence history into a bounded recurrent state, making the resulting memory law a central architectural choice for long-context performance. Most modern SSMs rely on ODE-based dynamics that lead to exponential forgetting, limiting their ability to retain information over broad temporal ranges. We introduce FRAC, a selective SSM architecture derived from fractional dynamics that replaces this exponential decay with power-law long memory. To make fractional dynamics practical, FRAC approximates the heavy-tailed target kernel with a finite-state, log-spaced sum of exponential modes. This construction turns fractional memory into an efficient recurrent module with parallel training and prefill, while retaining bounded-state autoregressive decoding. Extensive experiments, including 1.3B-parameter language modeling, demonstrate that FRAC consistently improves long-context performance over state-of-the-art SSM baselines while staying competitive on short-context. These results show that fractional dynamics provide a practical and effective prior for long-context SSMs.

</details>


### [186] [Understanding LLM Parameter Update Sparsity through the Lens of Fisher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36262)
*Yufan Zhang,Sagnik Mukherjee,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从对角 Fisher 信息的角度统一解释 LLM 后训练中参数更新稀疏的现象，理论证明小 Fisher 导致小期望梯度，并在 RL 和 on-policy 蒸馏中验证 Fisher 可识别梯度集中位置、固定稀疏掩码能保留大部分训练收益，同时揭示 on-policy 训练中高概率 token 的参数敏感度相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为什么 LLM 后训练中参数更新会集中在少数坐标上？不同后训练范式（RL、on-policy distillation、近策略数据上的 SFT）中重复出现的稀疏更新模式是否有共同解释？

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是围绕 LLM 后训练中的参数更新进行分析。方法上，计算对角 Fisher 信息矩阵，即模型输出分布对每个参数的敏感度，该度量不依赖于特定奖励或教师信号。理论部分建立小对角 Fisher 与多种训练目标下小期望梯度之间的联系，从而统一解释稀疏梯度更新。实验部分在强化学习（RL）和 on-policy 蒸馏（OPD）两种后训练范式下，比较 Fisher 与梯度集中位置，并检验基于初始 Fisher 选取的固定稀疏掩码在训练后保留的性能提升比例。最后通过分析 on-policy 训练中高概率下一个 token 的参数敏感度分布，解释为何 Fisher 较低。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出用对角 Fisher 信息作为统一视角解释后训练中的参数更新稀疏性，Fisher 独立于具体奖励或教师信号。；理论证明小的对角 Fisher 导致多种训练目标下期望梯度较小，为稀疏梯度更新提供共同解释。；在 RL 和 on-policy distillation 中实验验证 Fisher 能识别梯度集中的位置，且基于初始 Fisher 的固定稀疏掩码能保留大部分完整训练带来的提升。；揭示 on-policy 训练中低 Fisher 的机制：高概率下一个 token 往往具有相似的参数敏感度。

Abstract: Recent studies have observed that parameter changes during language-model post-training can be concentrated in a small subset of coordinates. This phenomenon has been reported in reinforcement learning, on-policy distillation, and supervised fine-tuning on near-policy data. Its recurrence across different post-training paradigms suggests shared structure in training dynamics. In this paper, we examine this pattern through the diagonal model Fisher, which measures the sensitivity of the model's output distribution to individual parameters and is independent of any particular reward or teacher signal. Theoretically, we show that small diagonal Fisher leads to small expected gradients across a range of training objectives, providing a common explanation for sparse gradient updates. Empirically, we test this connection in RL and OPD. We find that Fisher identifies where gradients are concentrated, and fixed sparse masks selected from the initial Fisher retain a large proportion of the improvement from full training. Finally, we investigate the mechanisms underlying low Fisher in on-policy training. Our results show that high-probability next tokens tend to have similar parameter sensitivities, contributing to low Fisher. Together, these results establish the diagonal model Fisher as a unifying perspective linking update sparsity to on-policy training dynamics in LLM post-training.

</details>


### [187] [In-Context Learning Amplifies a Latent Symbolic Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36265)
*Melissa Wessel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对三个模型系列进行因果分析，揭示 in-context learning 中抽象、归纳、检索三阶段符号电路在少量示例下即可检测并发挥作用，并通过 activation patching 和 function vector 注入实现显著准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能从少量上下文示例中学习抽象规则，但其内部三阶段符号推理电路（抽象、归纳、检索）在示例数量增加时如何被激活尚不清楚。

Method: 论文未提出新的训练架构，而是对现有三类模型系列进行机制层面的因果分析。输入为 in-context 示例序列（从 0-shot 到 10-shot），模型内部被刻画为三阶段符号推理电路：抽象阶段从示例中提取抽象规则，归纳阶段跨示例整合规则，检索阶段调用已学习的规则表征。作者通过逐 head 因果贡献度量电路各组件在 shot 数量变化时的功能权重；通过 cross-shot activation patching 将一个 shot 设置下的激活移植到另一 shot 设置，验证电路激活的可迁移性；通过缩放并注入 function vector 在 0-shot 条件下替代归纳阶段，测试对下游检索阶段的依赖。实验目标是证明抽象规则跟随的基础设施在没有任何示例时已存在于权重中，而 in-context 示例、function vector 及相关干预向同一潜在电路提供输入。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "per-head 因果贡献增长", "value": "最高 8 倍（1-shot 到 10-shot）", "setting": "三个模型系列，按 shot 数量分析"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "1% 提升到 56%", "setting": "0-shot，cross-shot activation patching"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "17% 提升到 88%", "setting": "1-shot，cross-shot activation patching"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "挽救至最高 86%", "setting": "0-shot，function vector 缩放注入"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 追踪跨 shot 数量的三阶段符号推理电路（abstraction、induction、retrieval），并发现其在模型达到高准确率之前就可检测且具备功能；按 head 的因果贡献分析显示从 1-shot 到 10-shot 贡献最高增长 8 倍；cross-shot activation patching 在 0-shot 将准确率从 1% 提升到 56%，在 1-shot 从 17% 提升到 88%；function vector 缩放注入在 0-shot 将准确率挽救至最高 86%，主要替代归纳阶段但依赖完好的下游检索阶段

Abstract: Large language models can learn abstract rules from just a few in-context examples, but how their internal mechanisms activate as examples accumulate is not well understood. We trace a three-stage symbolic reasoning circuit (abstraction, induction, retrieval) across shot counts in three model families and find it is detectable and functional well before the model achieves high accuracy. Per-head causal contribution grows up to 8x from 1- to 10-shot, and cross-shot activation patching raises accuracy from 1% to 56% at 0-shot and 17% to 88% at 1-shot. Function vectors scaled and injected at 0-shot rescue accuracy up to 86%, largely substituting for the induction stage but depending critically on an intact downstream retrieval stage. The infrastructure for abstract rule-following is present in the weights before any demonstrations; in-context examples, function vectors, and related interventions appear to supply input to the same latent circuit.

</details>


### [188] [CyFA: Linear Sequence Modeling with Relative-Time-Partitioned Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36259)
*Yixiao Chen,Shuojin Yang,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CyFA提出带相对时间分区记忆的线性RNN，通过学习时钟控制key/value状态的循环传输，将历史关联组织到不同时间槽位，并推导出等价的双标量衰减线性注意力递推以支持高效chunk-wise训练。在400M–1.4B预训练实验中提升召回密集型任务表现，同时保持有竞争力的语言建模性能，且核心算子执行时间仅为KDA的一半左右。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性RNN的固定大小循环状态需要容纳所有历史key-value关联，现有遗忘机制与Delta Rule更新虽能减少干扰，但更早的关联仍难以检索。

Method: CyFA是一种带有相对时间分区记忆的线性RNN。输入序列的每一步，key和value状态联合接受一个由学习到的时钟控制的循环传输（cyclic transport），随后当前key-value对被写入age-zero槽位。这种相对时间分区机制将存储的关联按相对时间槽位组织，使早期关联更易检索。作者进一步推导出向绝对时钟坐标的精确变换，将CyFA等价表达为两个带标量衰减的线性注意力递推，该形式支持chunk-wise并行训练。训练目标为语言建模，在400M–1.4B参数规模下进行预训练，并与匹配循环状态大小的基线对比。推理时保持常数内存解码，且核心算子执行时间低于KDA。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "FDA", "value": "42.60 vs. 26.07", "setting": "400M预训练模型，与KDA对比"}, {"metric": "前向核心算子执行时间", "value": "KDA的46.7%", "setting": "400M实验，匹配循环状态大小"}, {"metric": "反向核心算子执行时间", "value": "KDA的48.3%", "setting": "400M实验，匹配循环状态大小"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出CyFA（Cyclic Flow Attention），一种具有相对时间分区记忆的线性RNN，用学习到的时钟控制对key和value状态的循环传输，将存储的关联组织到不同相对时间槽位。；推导出向绝对时钟坐标的精确变换，将CyFA表达为两个带标量衰减的线性注意力递推，从而实现高效的chunk-wise训练。

Abstract: Linear RNNs offer linear-time sequence processing and constant-memory decoding, but their fixed-size recurrent states must accommodate all past key--value associations. Existing forgetting mechanisms and Delta Rule updates reduce interference by selectively clearing or correcting the state, yet earlier associations can still become difficult to retrieve. We introduce CyFA (Cyclic Flow Attention), a Linear RNN with relative-time-partitioned memory. At each step, a learned clock controls the cyclic transport applied jointly to the key and value states before the current key--value pair enters the age-zero slot, thereby organizing stored associations across relative-time slots. We further derive an exact change to absolute-clock coordinates that expresses CyFA as two scalar-decay linear attention recurrences and enables efficient chunk-wise training. Across 400M--1.4B pretraining experiments with matched recurrent-state sizes, CyFA improves recall-intensive performance while maintaining competitive language modeling and high computational efficiency. At 400M, CyFA outperforms KDA on FDA (42.60 vs. 26.07) while requiring only 46.7% and 48.3% of KDA's forward and backward core-operator execution times, respectively. Our code is publicly available at \href{ this https URL }{this https URL}.

</details>


### [189] [BASE: Batch-Aware Selection of Experts Using Predicted Removal Error for Efficient MoE Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36222)
*Ali Abbasi,Justin Shi,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BASE 针对 MoE 批量解码时多请求活跃专家集膨胀的问题，用校准阶段训练的线性预测器估计每个 token 上专家的移除误差，并据此在批量层面选择专家，配合自定义 GPU kernel 实现高效 serving；在 Qwen3-30B-A3B 等三种 MoE 架构上无需重训练即改善质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 稀疏激活在批量解码场景下无法直接转化为效率收益：不同请求选择不同专家，批量合并后的活跃专家集可覆盖大部分专家池，导致需搬运的专家权重显著增加；现有逐 token 保留决策和基于路由排名/校准统计的批量感知方法均未直接与移除专家造成的输出误差挂钩，也无法刻画专家贡献在推理时随 token 的变化。

Method: BASE 面向 MoE 模型批量解码的服务流程：输入为批量并发请求的 token 序列，经 transformer 层到达 MoE 层；MoE 层中 router 为每个 token 产生专家偏好。与逐 token 独立保留不同，BASE 在批量层面协调专家使用：先评估每个候选专家若被移除会对该 token 的 MoE 层输出造成多大误差。该移除代价无法在 serving 时直接计算，因此在校准阶段采集数据，训练一个轻量线性预测器，输入 token 相关特征、输出该 token 上专家移除代价的估计。推理时，先由自定义 GPU kernel 批量预测代价，再据此选出一组既能覆盖批量请求、又不超过专家预算的专家集合，只将这些专家的权重从 HBM 载入片上 SRAM 并执行计算。整个方法为后处理式专家选择，不修改或重训练原模型权重，目标是降低每步解码的专家权重搬运量，在专家预算约束下尽可能保留 MoE 层输出质量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "在同等吞吐、较紧专家预算下，比最强基线提升 29.5 个百分点", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B，同等吞吐、紧专家预算"}, {"metric": "推理速度", "value": "在较高专家预算下比 dense 推理快 60%，准确率差距在 0.4 个百分点以内", "setting": "Qwen3-30B-A3B，较高专家预算"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 以专家移除对 MoE 层输出造成的误差变化作为专家排序准则，替代路由排名或校准统计。；在校准阶段训练轻量线性预测器，在 serving 时对每个输入 token 估计专家移除代价。；为代价预测与专家选择开发自定义 GPU kernel。；在无需重训练的前提下，于三种 MoE 架构上改善质量-效率权衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly expensive to serve. In large-scale serving systems, autoregressive decoding is often bottlenecked by transferring model weights from accelerator high-bandwidth memory into on-chip SRAM. Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models reduce computation by activating only a small subset of experts per token, but this sparsity does not translate directly to batched decoding. Different requests select different experts; therefore, the combined active set across many concurrent requests can span a substantial fraction of the expert pool and require significantly more expert weights to be transferred. Most expert-reduction techniques make retention decisions independently for each token and therefore do not address this batch-level expansion. More recently, batch-aware methods have attempted to coordinate expert use across concurrent requests and reuse experts already fetched for the batch. Yet their selection criteria are based primarily on router rankings or expert statistics collected during calibration. Consequently, these criteria are not directly tied to the output error caused by dropping an expert, nor do they capture how an expert's contribution changes across tokens at inference time. We instead rank experts according to how much their removal would change the MoE-layer output. To apply this criterion during serving, we train a lightweight linear predictor during calibration that estimates the expert removal cost for each incoming token, and develop custom GPU kernels for cost prediction and expert selection. Across three MoE architectures, BASE improves the quality-efficiency tradeoff without retraining. On Qwen3-30B-A3B, it improves average accuracy by 29.5 points over the strongest baseline at comparable throughput under a tight expert budget. At a higher expert budget, it is 60% faster than dense inference while remaining within 0.4 accuracy points.

</details>


### [190] [Draft in Parallel, Condition Through Depth: Adjacent Causal Injection for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36173)
*Haohui Zhang,Keyu Chen,Haocheng Sun,Weibo Gu,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DSpine 通过在草稿模型每层注入前驱特征实现深度展开的因果条件，提升并行投机解码的接受长度，在 SGLang 中取得 23.3% 吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行投机草稿生成中，独立 token 选择导致续写不一致，缩短可接受前缀。现有方法将条件解码放在骨干网络之后，限制了前驱信息向后继的流动。

Method: DSpine 是一种用于投机解码的并行草稿模型。输入为上下文 token 序列，所有草稿位置在单次骨干前向中并行计算。骨干网络每一层都包含门控相邻注入模块：每个后继位置接收其直接前驱在当前层预测特征的加权版本，从而将因果条件链沿网络深度传播。此外，模型使用由目标模型输出嵌入构建的统一转移空间，将逐层注入特征与前驱条件解码映射到同一空间。训练时，逐层输出嵌入监督强制浅层即产生可恢复的预测特征。推理时，融合算子和转移缓存支持 SGLang 内的高效并行执行，最终输出候选 token 序列供目标模型验证。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "七个基准平均接受长度", "value": "从 DFlash 的 3.77 提升至 4.82（+27.8%）", "setting": "Qwen3-8B，温度 0，七个数学、代码、对话基准"}, {"metric": "七基准平均接受长度最优", "value": "在两种温度下均取得最长接受长度", "setting": "Qwen3-4B 与 Qwen3-8B"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "SGLang 服务吞吐", "value": "比 DFlash 平均提升 23.3%", "setting": "SGLang 服务测试"}]}

Conclusion: 提出 DSpine：在每层通过门控相邻注入将每个前驱的预测特征写入其后继，使因果条件链随网络深度展开，同时所有位置并行更新。；构建统一转移空间，基于目标模型输出嵌入统一逐层注入与前驱条件解码。；逐层输出嵌入监督促进浅层预测特征的形成。；融合算子与转移缓存，在 SGLang 内高效并行执行。

Abstract: Parallel speculative drafting generates multiple candidates in one backbone pass, but independent token selection can produce inconsistent continuations that shorten the accepted prefix. Existing methods mostly leave conditional decoding to a lightweight module after the backbone, which limits the flow of predecessor information to successors. Our analysis of DFlash shows that early positions already form recoverable predictions in shallow layers, and that accurate adjacent predecessors help successors more when they enter earlier. We therefore propose DSpine, a drafter with causal conditioning injection throughout the backbone: at every layer, gated adjacent injection writes each predecessor's predicted feature into its successor, so the causal conditioning chain unfolds over network depth while all positions update in parallel. A unified transfer space built from the target model's output embeddings unifies layer-wise injection with predecessor-conditioned decoding, and layer-wise output-embedding supervision promotes the formation of predicted features in shallow layers. Fused kernels and a transition cache execute both efficiently in parallel within SGLang. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DSpine achieves the longest acceptance length at both temperatures on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B. At temperature zero on Qwen3-8B, it raises the seven-benchmark mean from DFlash's 3.77 to 4.82 (+27.8%); in SGLang serving tests, it delivers 23.3% higher throughput than DFlash on average.

</details>


### [191] [Dyad: Extending Large Language Models with Native Typed Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36116)
*Yundaichuan Zhan,Weishi Wang,Wenbiao Liu,Daniel Dahlmeier,Chengwei Qin,Juncheng Li,Fredrik D. Johansson,Zhongqi Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dyad extends pretrained LLMs with a native typed decision-making module: an environment-conditioned action encoder that embeds candidate actions in parallel and scores them against the LLM's internal state to output a distribution over typed actions. With the LLM frozen, training only the action encoder yields consistent gains across four unseen environments. Joint optimization of both components outperforms conventional RL post-training across diverse interactive tasks and model scales, including a 3.80% average absolute gain on ALFWorld with a 9B model, while improving general knowledge, reasoning, and coding.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Building more capable general-purpose LLM agents by enabling efficient, reusable, and generalizable decision-making over large, environment-specific action spaces.

Method: Dyad augments a pretrained LLM with an environment-conditioned action encoder. Inputs consist of the evolving interaction state (processed by the LLM) and a set of candidate action descriptions (processed in parallel by the action encoder). The action encoder embeds each action description into a representation that captures environment-specific action semantics. These action embeddings are then scored against the LLM's internal state representation to produce a distribution over typed actions. This factorized design introduces an inductive bias for learning reusable representations and keeps action scoring efficient even as the action space grows. Training is driven by environment interaction in two complementary RL settings: (1) with the LLM frozen, training only the action encoder enables modular adaptation without modifying any LLM parameters; (2) jointly optimizing both components yields better performance across diverse interactive tasks and model scales. The architecture is evaluated on ALFWorld and other unseen environments, showing consistent gains and improved general knowledge, reasoning, and coding.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "average absolute gain", "value": "3.80%", "setting": "ALFWorld, 9B model, joint optimization vs. conventional RL post-training"}, {"metric": "general knowledge, reasoning, and coding", "value": "improved", "setting": "joint optimization across diverse interactive tasks and model scales"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Environment-conditioned action encoder that embeds each candidate action description in parallel；Scoring action embeddings against the LLM's internal state to yield a distribution over typed actions；Factorized decision-making into evolving interaction state and environment-specific action semantics；Two complementary RL settings: frozen LLM with trainable action encoder (modular adaptation) and joint optimization of LLM and encoder；Efficient action scoring that scales with action space growth

Abstract: We study how to build more capable general-purpose agents by extending large language models (LLMs) with native typed decision-making. We introduce Dyad, an architecture that augments a pretrained LLM with an environment-conditioned action encoder that embeds each candidate action description in parallel, then scores these embeddings against the LLM's internal state to yield a distribution over typed actions. By factorizing decision-making into representations of the evolving interaction state and environment-specific action semantics, Dyad introduces an inductive bias for learning reusable representations while keeping action scoring efficient even as the action space grows. We investigate two complementary reinforcement learning settings driven by environment interaction. With the LLM frozen, training the action encoder alone achieves consistent gains across four unseen environments, enabling modular adaptation without modifying any LLM parameters. Jointly optimizing both components outperforms conventional RL post-training across diverse interactive tasks and model scales, including a 3.80% average absolute gain on ALFWorld with a 9B model, while improving general knowledge, reasoning, and coding.

</details>


### [192] [ThinQuant: Scalable Rotation Learning for Weight and Activation Quantization of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36120)
*Mehdi Makni,Ryan Lucas,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ThinQuant 提出基于激活凸包的数据选择与精确降维，将 LLM 低比特量化中的旋转学习归约为 Stiefel 流形上的薄矩阵优化，并用 ADMM 高效求解。Llama-3-70B 上 W4A4KV4 校准不到12分钟、困惑度5.63（优于DartQuant的7.55/111分钟），并可在单张H200上校准 Llama-3.1-405B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于旋转的LLM低比特（W4A4/KV4）量化校准方法（如SpinQuant梯度法、DartQuant无梯度法）计算开销过大，难以扩展到数百亿至千亿参数的最大模型架构。

Method: ThinQuant 针对 LLM 低比特权值与激活量化中的旋转矩阵学习问题，提出端到端的校准流程。输入是校准数据（模型激活）以及待学习的旋转矩阵；首先通过一个数据选择过程，利用激活凸包的几何结构挑选出少量但信息量最大的校准激活，使得所需校准点数比现有旋转方法少数个数量级。由于所选激活仅张成 r 维子空间（r<d）而非整个 d 维空间，原 d×d 正交旋转优化被证明可精确归约为 Stiefel 流形上的 d×r 矩阵优化，从而大幅降低变量维度和计算量。该归约问题用 ADMM 算法迭代求解，每次迭代只进行 thin 矩阵更新，避免对大矩阵做昂贵运算。优化结束后得到旋转矩阵，用于平滑激活中的离群值，再结合低比特量化（W4A4、KV4）应用于模型权重与激活。推理路径即使用学习得到的旋转对激活做变换后再量化，全部流程可在单卡上完成对 Llama-3-70B 与 Llama-3.1-405B 的校准。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "WikiText-2 困惑度", "value": "5.63", "setting": "Llama-3-70B, W4A4KV4 量化，旋转校准时间<12分钟"}, {"metric": "WikiText-2 困惑度", "value": "7.55", "setting": "Llama-3-70B, W4A4KV4，DartQuant 基线，需111分钟"}, {"metric": "WikiText-2 困惑度", "value": "2.97", "setting": "Llama-3.1-405B, W4A4 量化，单张H200 GPU，旋转校准约2小时"}, {"metric": "WikiText-2 困惑度", "value": "3.48", "setting": "Llama-3.1-405B, W4A4，GPTAQ+QuaRoT 基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于激活凸包几何结构的数据选择过程，可把校准激活数量减少数个数量级；证明在所选校准集下激活仅张成 r 维子空间（r<d），从而将 d×d 旋转优化等价归约为 Stiefel 流形上的 d×r 矩阵优化；使用高效 ADMM 算法求解该归约问题，每一步仅需 thin matrix 更新（ThinQuant）；实现单卡H200上完成 Llama-3.1-405B 的旋转校准（约2小时）

Abstract: Learned rotations play an important role in enabling low-bit weight and activation quantization of large language models by smoothing outliers in the activation distribution. State-of-the-art approaches include gradient-based procedures such as SpinQuant and computationally friendlier gradient-free approaches such as DartQuant, but both remain hard to scale to the largest architectures. To address the computational bottlenecks in gradient-free rotation learning, we introduce two ideas for efficiency, (i) a data selection procedure which reduces the required number of calibration data points, and (ii) an exact reduction of the associated optimization on this reduced calibration set. Our data selection procedure exploits the geometric structure of the convex hull of the activations. Using this idea, we show that a carefully selected calibration set with several orders of magnitude fewer activations than state-of-the-art rotation-based methods can match their performance in low-bit quantization settings. Under this extreme data efficiency, the selected activations span an $r$-dimensional subspace with $r<d$, making optimization over a $d\times d$ rotation equivalent to optimizing a $d\times r$ matrix on the Stiefel manifold. We solve this reduced problem using an efficient ADMM algorithm that iteratively employs thin matrix updates at every step, hence the name ThinQuant. For Llama-3-70B with W4A4KV4 quantization, ThinQuant completes the entire rotation calibration in under 12 minutes and achieves a WikiText-2 perplexity of 5.63, compared with 7.55 for DartQuant, which requires 111 minutes. Unlike SpinQuant and DartQuant, ThinQuant also scales to Llama-3.1-405B on a single H200 GPU, completing rotation calibration in just over 2 hours and achieving WikiText-2 perplexity of 2.97 at W4A4, compared with 3.48 for GPTAQ+QuaRoT.

</details>


### [193] [Koa-action: Fast and Consistent Structured Decision Making with Generative LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36115)
*Shenghong Dai,Shiva Kumar Pentyala,Yingchi Liu,Shubham Mehrotra,Suman Banerjee,James Zhu,Bin Bi,Sitaram Asur,Phil Mui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Koa-action把分类等原子决策转为单token约束生成，通过原子标签token和监督微调实现低延迟解码；在生产意图路由基准达到85.5%准确率，中位延迟约0.5秒，较前沿模型最高约7.5倍加速，并支持多模态与多标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM提示与约束解码输出冗长、需逐token生成，难以满足工业低延迟分类；编码器模型虽快但缺乏任务灵活性。

Method: Koa-action将低延迟原子动作建模为约束生成：输入为文本及多模态输入，输出为单个原子标签token，从而把分类、语义端点检测、布尔检查和评分约简为确定性单步解码。训练采用监督微调，模型学习生成原子标签token。推理时进行单步约束解码，输出单token而非多token序列，从而显著降低逐token生成开销；框架还支持多标签输出。该设计在保持生成式LLM任务灵活性的同时获得接近编码器模型的低延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "85.5%", "setting": "生产意图路由基准"}, {"metric": "median latency", "value": "约0.5秒", "setting": "相同服务条件"}, {"metric": "relative median speedup", "value": "最高约7.5倍", "setting": "相比前沿模型，相同服务条件"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将原子动作（分类、语义端点检测、布尔检查、评分）形式化为单token输出的约束生成；引入原子标签token，通过监督微调把分类约简为确定性单步解码问题；在生产意图路由基准上实现低延迟且与前沿模型竞争准确率，并支持多模态输入和多标签输出

Abstract: Industry applications often demand low-latency classification, yet current large language model (LLM) approaches remain poorly suited for latency-critical applications. Existing prompting and constrained decoding produce verbose, multi-token outputs that require expensive token-by-token generation, while encoder-based models achieve faster inference but sacrifice task flexibility. We propose Koa-action, a framework for low-latency atomic actions -- fast, single-step decisions such as classification, semantic endpointing, Boolean checks, and scoring -- formulated as constrained generation with single-token outputs. By introducing atomic label tokens and applying supervised fine-tuning, our method reduces classification to a deterministic one-step decoding problem. Across standard benchmarks, Koa-action delivers competitive accuracy with consistently low and stable latency. On a production intent-routing benchmark, Koa-action reaches 85.5% accuracy -- competitive with the strongest frontier models (Claude-4.8-Opus, Gemini-Pro-3.1) and ahead of GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5-Pro -- while answering in about half a second, several-fold faster than every frontier model (up to ~7.5x at the median) under identical serving conditions. Against the dedicated single-token system Jev/TypeSafe, Koa-action is competitive on accuracy and faster at the median, while also handling multimodal inputs and multi-label outputs that single-label text systems do not.

</details>


### [194] [ABC: Advantage-Based Control Variates for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36058)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Florent Draye,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对RLVR中actor-critic方法价值函数偏差问题，提出基于优势的控制变量ABC，并与DAE结合形成样本高效的actor-critic算法，在数学推理任务上以更少在线步数达到GRPO竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RLVR中，critic-free方法（如GRPO）简单有效但缺少价值函数，而actor-critic方法的价值函数近似误差会通过TD误差等优势估计器引入偏差，如何设计低偏差且样本高效的优势估计方法。

Method: 论文提出ABC（Advantage-Based Control Variates）方法，从优势-价值表述重新审视轨迹级控制变量。ABC利用价值函数构造控制变量以减少策略梯度估计的方差，并揭示其协方差结构与DAE（Direct Advantage Estimation）中的回报分解相关。随后将ABC与DAE结合成单一actor-critic算法：critic在离线收集的轨迹上预训练，然后在在线学习阶段进行适应。在数学推理任务上评估，表明ABC能用更少的在线优化步数达到与GRPO竞争的性能。训练目标结合策略梯度与价值函数学习，整体流程为离线预训练critic后在线微调。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Advantage-Based Control Variates (ABC)轨迹级控制变量，通过优势-价值形式重新审视控制变量；揭示ABC的协方差结构与Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE)中回报分解的紧密联系；将ABC与DAE结合为单一actor-critic算法，在离线到在线RLVR设置中验证，critic先离线训练后在线微调

Abstract: Recent progress in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has highlighted the effectiveness of simple critic-free policy-gradient methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). In contrast, actor-critic methods rely on learned value functions whose approximation error can introduce bias through commonly used advantage estimators such as temporal-difference error. Motivated by this observation, we revisit trajectory-level control variates through an advantage-value formulation, which we call Advantage-Based Control Variates (ABC). This formulation reveals that the covariance structure is closely related to the return decomposition used in Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE). Finally, we combine ABC with DAE into a single actor-critic algorithm and evaluate it in an offline-to-online RLVR setting, where the critic is first trained on previously collected trajectories and adapted during online learning. On mathematical reasoning tasks, ABC achieves performance competitive with GRPO using substantially fewer online optimization steps.

</details>


### [195] [GEM-KMeans: Memory-Efficient and Accurate Clustering on Massive Scale with GPU Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36074)
*Peng Xu,Nihar Koganti,Volodymyr Kindratenko,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-KMeans 提出一种内存高效的 GPU 加速聚类方法，通过融合梯度、非负投影和充分统计量到矩阵乘 epilogue，仅需保留单个因子数组，大幅降低 HBM 占用，并在大规模合成和真实数据集上验证了聚类的准确性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非负低秩（NLR）矩阵分解用于 K-means 时，需要多个大规模因子缓冲区及大量数据搬移，导致 GPU 实现受内存带宽严重限制，难以在保持统计精度的同时扩展至海量数据。

Method: GEM-KMeans 在传统非负低秩矩阵分解（NLR）的基础上重构了 K-means 的求解流程：输入为大规模数据矩阵，目标是学习一个非负低秩因子，使得其对应的聚类结构与 K-means 等价。核心架构包含三个主要模块，并融合至单一的 GPU 矩阵乘法内核中：1）梯度更新模块：在每次迭代中计算 NLR 目标函数关于因子的梯度；2）非负投影模块：对因子施加非负约束，保证物理解释；3）充分统计量计算模块：用于谱归一化（保持数值稳定性）和迭代移动量（如动量或步长调整）。传统实现需要保留三个与因子同规模的大数组，而本方案通过将这三个操作融合为矩阵乘法的 epilogue，仅需在 HBM 中物化一个因子数组以及用于 tile 规约的小数组，极大降低了内存占用和数据搬移。训练目标为最小化 NLR 损失，并采用推导出的谱归一化光滑性边界来指导步长和优化，保证收敛性。推理/聚类流程为：给定数据，执行迭代优化直至收敛，随后从学到的低秩因子中提取聚类分配。该设计主要解决了大规模聚类中 GPU 内存瓶颈和带宽限制问题，在保持与精确求解相似统计精度的同时，实现了数据依赖的运行时间。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出一种谱归一化且数学等价的 NLR 公式，将梯度更新、非负投影以及用于归一化和迭代移动的充分统计量融合到矩阵乘法尾部（epilogue）。；设计 IO 感知的 GPU 实现，仅保留单个因子数组及小规模 tile-reduction 数组，显著降低 HBM 内存占用。；推导了聚类目标函数的显式内存开销与谱归一化光滑性边界，为优化提供理论依据。

Abstract: Memory-efficient scaling on clustering problems without sacrificing statistical accuracy is of central interest for large-scale data analysis and machine learning problems. Nonnegative low-rank (NLR) matrix factorization for $K$-means is a scalable clustering method, which connects to semidefinite relaxations with optimal average-case exact recovery guarantees. However, a direct GPU implementation of NLR requires multiple large factor-sized buffers and substantial data movements that are essentially memory-bound. In this paper, we introduce GEM-KMeans, a spectrally normalized yet mathematically equivalent NLR formulation that fuses the gradient update, nonnegative projection, and sufficient statistics for normalization and iterate movement into a matrix-multiplication epilogue. Instead of retaining three massive factor-sized arrays, our IO-aware GPU implementation materializes only one single factor with small tile-reduction arrays as additional storage in the High Bandwidth Memory (HBM). We derive explicit memory costs and spectrally normalized smoothness bounds for optimizing the clustering objective function. Accurate clustering is demonstrated at massive scales on synthetic and real datasets, where performance gains of GEM-KMeans over existing GPU-accelerated Lloyd's algorithms involve data-dependent runtime tradeoffs.

</details>


### [196] [Improving scalable oversight with co-trained monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36049)
*Joseph H. Rudoler,Kevin Tan,Benedict Tessler,Timothy Kong,Enric Boix Adserà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过协同训练monitor避免worker规避监控，提出有监督理论表征（Littlestone维数）与基于测试时蒸馏的自监督方法，并在代码安全任务中实证自适应monitor优于固定monitor。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在worker-monitor监督范式中，若worker针对固定monitor训练，可能学会规避监控。核心问题是能否通过协同训练monitor来避免此类监控规避，并同时探索有监督与自监督方法。

Method: 论文提出的协同训练框架包含一个worker和一个monitor。在有监督设置中，worker和monitor同时训练，monitor试图检测worker的异常行为，而worker可能学习规避监控。理论分析表明，当monitor函数类具有有限Littlestone维数时，监控能以消失的错误率和查询率实现。在自监督设置中，采用测试时蒸馏：monitor首先利用额外的测试时计算生成标签（例如通过多数投票），然后使用这些标签训练一个标准计算策略（monitor策略）。该过程在自适应worker分布下具有有限样本锐化保证，即monitor的裁决会收敛到其初始众数裁决。推理时，标准计算策略快速给出监控裁决，而测试时蒸馏仅在训练阶段使用额外计算。实验在代码安全场景中验证，worker被对抗性训练以欺骗monitor，比较自适应monitor与固定monitor的规避脆弱性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "监控性能对比（未披露具体指标）", "value": "自适应monitor比固定monitor更能跟上不断演变的worker策略，固定monitor更易被规避", "setting": "代码安全任务，worker对抗性训练以欺骗monitor"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在有监督设置下证明：监控能以消失的错误率和查询率实现，当且仅当可能的monitor函数类具有有限Littlestone维数，将此问题与对抗在线学习文献相连接。；提出基于测试时蒸馏的自监督协同训练过程：monitor利用额外测试时计算生成训练标签，再用这些标签训练其标准计算策略。；针对多数投票标签，在自适应worker分布且满足动作覆盖条件下，给出有限样本锐化保证，表明monitor的裁决收敛于其初始众数裁决。；在代码安全场景中压力测试有监督协同训练，worker被对抗性训练以欺骗monitor，观察monitor适应与worker规避的动态。

Abstract: Worker-monitor setups are a promising approach to AI oversight, but training workers against fixed monitors can incentivize monitor evasion. We study whether this failure mode can be avoided by co-training the monitor alongside the worker, and explore both supervised and self-supervised approaches. In the supervised setting, we prove a characterization: monitoring is possible with vanishing error and query rates exactly when the class of possible monitor functions has finite Littlestone dimension. This connects worker monitoring with an established literature on adversarial online learning. For self-supervision, we propose a co-training procedure based on test-time distillation: the monitor uses additional test-time compute to generate training labels, then trains its standard-compute policy on those labels. For majority-vote labels, we give a finite-sample sharpening guarantee under adaptive worker distributions with action coverage, that shows that the monitor's verdicts converge to its initial modal verdicts. We stress-test the former in code-security settings where the worker is trained adversarially to fool the monitor. Our results suggest that adaptive monitors are better at keeping pace with evolving worker strategies, while fixed monitors are more vulnerable to evasion.

</details>


### [197] [Learn Now, Use Next, Trust Later: Prequential Test-Time Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35911)
*Tong Zhao,Reed Li,Yuyang Hu,Yutao Zhu,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi,Yu Lu,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StepLearn 提出一种非参数测试时学习框架，将即时可用的假设与经过前瞻验证的持久知识分离，在不更新模型参数的情况下提升 LLM Agent 在 WebArena-Lite 和 ALFWorld 上的成功率，并优于最强基线 EvoTest。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在 LLM Agent 部署过程中，将即时交互经验快速转化为可指导下一步决策的规则，同时避免将仅适用于局部情节的不可靠规则持久化传播，从而平衡即时利用与长期信任。

Method: StepLearn 是一个非参数框架，在模型参数固定不变的前提下，通过外部知识库实现测试时学习。其处理流程为：Agent 在交互中产生转移（transition），框架从中筛选出信息量大的转移，将其转化为假设（hypothesis），这些假设立即可用于指导下一步决策，以缩短知识获取与利用的延迟。为了跨情节复用，假设需要在源情节之外的新观察中进行前瞻验证（prospective validation），即检查假设预测的效果是否与后续实际观察一致；只有得到充分支持的假设才会被加入持久知识库，供未来情节使用。整个过程分离了“即时使用”与“持久信任”，所有更新发生在外部的、可操作的知识库中，不修改任何 LLM 参数。推理时，Agent 结合当前上下文与已验证的持久知识库进行决策。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均成功率", "value": "59.9%（WebArena-Lite）, 84.0%（ALFWorld）", "setting": "GPT-5-mini，5 轮实验"}, {"metric": "平均成功率", "value": "57.8%（WebArena-Lite）, 88.1%（ALFWorld）", "setting": "Qwen3.5-35B-A3B，5 轮实验"}, {"metric": "相对最强基线 EvoTest 的提升", "value": "2.2-12.7 个百分点", "setting": "四种设置（两个数据集 × 两个模型）的比较"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出非参数测试时学习框架 StepLearn，将知识获取与信任验证解耦：从信息量大的转移中生成假设以即时指导下一步，同时要求跨情节的前瞻验证后才可持久复用。；采用前瞻验证机制：假设在源情节之外的新观察中被检验，仅足够支持的假设成为持久指导，防止误用偶然或错误归因的规则。；所有模型参数保持固定，仅更新外部知识库，实现无参数更新的持续适应。

Abstract: Adapting large language model agents during deployment requires not only retaining past experience, but also turning new observations into timely guidance. Many test-time learning methods, however, acquire knowledge from completed episodes. Feedback from an ongoing interaction may therefore not be distilled into knowledge soon enough to help the next decision. Acquiring knowledge at the granularity of individual transitions could reduce this delay, but raises a separate challenge: a rule that is useful within one episode may not be reliable enough to guide future episodes. Waiting for validation can forfeit immediate benefits, whereas unrestricted reuse can propagate accidental or misattributed guidance. We introduce StepLearn, a nonparametric framework that separates immediate use from persistent trust. It turns informative transitions into hypotheses that can guide the next step, while requiring prospective validation before reuse across episodes. Their predicted effects are checked against subsequent observations outside the source episodes, and only sufficiently supported hypotheses become available for persistent guidance. This process updates external knowledge while keeping all model parameters fixed. Over five rounds on WebArena-Lite and ALFWorld, StepLearn achieves average success rates of 59.9% and 84.0% with GPT-5-mini, and 57.8% and 88.1% with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, respectively. It outperforms EvoTest, the strongest baseline, by 2.2-12.7 percentage points across the four settings. Learning dynamics further shows that these gains are not restricted to the final repetition, with advantages already present on first task attempts in most settings.

</details>


### [198] [Mara Chain: Rethinking Failure as a Stepping Stone for AI System Auto-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35855)
*Yubin Lyu,Fu Li,Jiawei Fei,Yang Zhao,Weixing Mei,Yinan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mara Chain 把优化过程中被拒绝的候选配置保留下来并迭代精炼，结合固定链深与 Pareto Top-N 选择限制候选池，在 AppWorld 技能优化、TerminalBench 2.1 harness 优化和 MuSiQue 检索流水线优化上以更少 rollout 取得更高任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 propose-evaluate-select 优化方法会丢弃未通过验收的候选配置，导致后续提案重复进入相同的失败模式，浪费 rollout；如何将失败候选转化为后续优化的有效信息。

Method: Mara Chain 是一种面向已部署 AI 系统 artifact（提示、技能、harness、代码、检索流水线）的精炼式优化流程。输入是当前系统配置与可评估的任务环境；流程以 propose-evaluate-select 为基线，但改变被拒绝候选的处理方式：不再丢弃拒绝候选，而是将其保留为精炼链的起点，并用前序尝试累积的证据对其进行迭代精炼。每条精炼链深度固定，避免无限扩展；精炼产生的候选通过 Pareto 过滤与 Top-N 选择来限制候选池规模。评估在任务环境中执行，任务性能与 rollout 消耗共同决定保留哪些候选。最终选择满足验收标准的配置部署回系统。该方法在 AppWorld 技能优化、TerminalBench 2.1 harness 优化和 MuSiQue 检索流水线优化上验证：相比 GEPA、ACE、SkillOpt-Lite 在 AppWorld 上相对性能提升最高 20.5%，并用比 GEPA 少 65.5% 的 rollout 达到目标分数；在 TerminalBench 2.1 上 pass rate 分别比 AHE、Meta-Harness 高 20.2 和 22.5 个百分点；在 MuSiQue 上 test nDCG@10 和 Recall@10 比人工检索流水线高 0.104 和 0.131。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "relative performance gain over GEPA, ACE, SkillOpt-Lite", "value": "up to 20.5%", "setting": "AppWorld skill optimization"}, {"metric": "rollout reduction to reach target score vs GEPA", "value": "65.5% fewer rollouts", "setting": "AppWorld skill optimization"}, {"metric": "pass rate improvement over AHE", "value": "+20.2 percentage points", "setting": "TerminalBench 2.1 harness optimization"}, {"metric": "pass rate improvement over Meta-Harness", "value": "+22.5 percentage points", "setting": "TerminalBench 2.1 harness optimization"}, {"metric": "test nDCG@10 improvement over hand-written retrieval pipeline", "value": "+0.104", "setting": "MuSiQue retrieval-pipeline optimization"}, {"metric": "test Recall@10 improvement over hand-written retrieval pipeline", "value": "+0.131", "setting": "MuSiQue retrieval-pipeline optimization"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将拒绝候选保留为可迭代精炼的起点，而非直接丢弃；利用先前尝试累积的证据对拒绝候选进行迭代精炼；对每条精炼链设置固定深度以控制探索范围；使用 Pareto 过滤的 Top-N 选择限制候选池规模；在技能、harness、检索流水线三类 artifact 优化任务上统一验证

Abstract: Optimizing deployed AI systems increasingly amounts to editing prompts, skills, harnesses, and code rather than model weights. Existing approaches commonly optimize these artifacts through propose-evaluate-select procedures, where candidate configurations are evaluated and only those meeting an acceptance criterion are selected. Yet our analysis shows that discarded candidates often contain information critical for subsequent optimization. Discarding them causes later proposals to revisit the same failure modes. We introduce Mara Chain, a refinement procedure that turns rejected candidates into stepping stones. Rather than discarding a rejected candidate, Mara Chain retains and iteratively refines it using evidence accumulated across preceding attempts. The procedure limits each refinement chain to a fixed depth and applies Pareto-filtered Top-N selection to bound the candidate pool. Across AppWorld skill optimization, TerminalBench 2.1 harness optimization, and MuSiQue retrieval-pipeline optimization, Mara Chain delivers greater task-performance gains with fewer rollouts. It outperforms GEPA, ACE, and SkillOpt-Lite by up to 20.5% in relative performance on AppWorld, reaching the target score with 65.5% fewer rollouts than GEPA. It improves the pass rate by 20.2 and 22.5 percentage points over AHE and Meta-Harness on TerminalBench 2.1, respectively, and improves MuSiQue test nDCG@10 and Recall@10 by 0.104 and 0.131 over a hand-written retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [199] [HeadGuard: Selective Head Protection for Low-Bit VLM KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35800)
*Nenad Banfic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HeadGuard 通过在低比特 KV-cache 量化时选择性保护约 1/8 的敏感 KV 头为 BF16，在 8 个 VLM 上恢复被量化损失的精度，最弱基量化器在 2 bits 下六任务判别式平均准确率从 0.436 提升到 0.580，方法可组合且不替换底层量化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特 KV-cache 量化能节省存储，但会显著损害视觉语言模型（VLM）精度，需要在保持低存储开销的同时尽量恢复精度。

Method: HeadGuard 是一个可组合的头保护层，叠加在任意基础 KV-cache 量化器之上。其流程为：先离线计算两类敏感性分数——图像敏感性（反映各物理 KV 头对图像 token 的重要性）与输出敏感性（反映对最终输出的影响）——据此为每个模型选出固定的高精度头掩码，约保护 1/8 的物理 KV 头。推理时，被保护头的图像 key 和可选的 value 以 BF16 存储，未被保护的头仍由基础量化器进行低比特量化；该掩码在推理中固定不变，不引入额外的在线计算路径。论文通过模拟量化评估，在 8 个 VLM、3 种基础量化器、8 个基准（6 个判别式、2 个生成式）上验证精度恢复，并比较了 keys-only 保护与同时保护 value 的存储-精度权衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "六任务判别式平均准确率", "value": "0.436 → 0.580", "setting": "2 bits，最弱基量化器，8 个模型的六任务判别式平均"}, {"metric": "均值准确率增益", "value": "在三种量化器上均保持", "setting": "两种校准数据集，8 个 VLM、8 个基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 HeadGuard 可组合的头保护方法，在基础 KV-cache 量化器上叠加固定的高精度掩码；离线使用图像敏感性和输出敏感性分数选择物理 KV 头，主实验保护约 1/8 的头，其图像 key（可选 value）保留 BF16；验证仅保护 key 在更低建模存储成本下仍能保留大部分精度恢复，并在多种量化器与校准数据集上泛化

Abstract: Low-bit key-value (KV) cache quantization saves storage but can sharply degrade vision-language model (VLM) accuracy. We introduce HeadGuard, a composable head-protection method that augments a base KV-cache quantizer with a fixed high-precision mask. Image-sensitivity and output-sensitivity scores select physical KV heads offline, with approximately 1/8 protected in the main experiments; their image keys and optionally values remain in bfloat16 (BF16), while the base quantizes unprotected image entries. Across eight VLMs, three base quantizers, and eight benchmarks (six discriminative and two generative), HeadGuard recovers a substantial fraction of lost accuracy on weaker quantizers, with the strongest gains for Qwen and InternVL. At 2 bits, the six-task discriminative mean over eight models rises from 0.436 to 0.580 on the weakest base; protection can also improve generated answers and caption fidelity to BF16 outputs. Mean accuracy gains persist across all three quantizers with both tested calibration datasets. Keys-only protection retains substantial recovery at lower modeled storage cost. Evaluated through simulated quantization, HeadGuard offers a composable way to improve low-bit VLM accuracy without replacing the underlying quantizer.

</details>


### [200] [Learning from the Gap Between Pass@K and Pass@1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35793)
*Xuan Liu,Jingbin Qian,Haosheng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GapFT 通过按源模型的单样本结果筛选 Pass@K 与 Pass@1 之间的差距样本进行后训练，在匹配预算下提升单样本解码准确率，并可将测试时扩展的部分收益吸收到模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定预算下，如何选择训练样本以吸收搜索/多样本解码所暴露的行为到单样本解码策略中，避免重复策略已掌握的行为。

Method: GapFT 是一种训练数据选择与微调方法，不改变模型架构或训练目标。流程：给定源检查点，对每个问题先做单样本解码（Pass@1）得到首次解码正确与否的答案；对单样本失败的问题再做最多 K 次采样，判断是否在 K 个样本内恢复（Pass@K）。训练集由“单样本失败但 K 样本内恢复”的问题构成，即 Pass@K 与 Pass@1 之间的差距。在匹配训练样本数、处理 token 数和优化器步数的预算下，用恢复的失败样本填充预算，保持与基线相同的目标函数，从而将搜索暴露的可恢复失败行为吸收进单样本策略。评估时仅进行一次解码，并用精确分解把提升归因于对恢复失败和遗漏失败的修正，同时监控首次解码成功问题上的退步。该设计让训练预算集中在策略尚未掌握的可迁移失败模式上，而非重复已掌握的行为。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Pass@1", "value": "提升 14.4 分", "setting": "LogiQA 2.0，Llama-3.1-8B，相对源模型"}, {"metric": "Pass@1", "value": "提升 13.9 分", "setting": "ReClor，Llama-3.1-8B，相对源模型"}, {"metric": "Pass@1", "value": "优于预算匹配的 uniform verified RFT（相同学习率）", "setting": "LogiQA 2.0 与 ReClor，Llama-3.1-8B"}, {"metric": "Pass@1", "value": "匹配在全量验证池上微调的效果，仅用三分之一数据", "setting": "Llama-3.1-8B"}, {"metric": "单次解码", "value": "匹配源模型经验证器选择的 Pass@4 准确率", "setting": "Llama-3.1-8B"}, {"metric": "Pass@1", "value": "在两个逻辑任务上相对 uniform RFT 保持正向增益", "setting": "Qwen2.5-7B，三随机种子复现"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: GapFT：按源检查点的单样本结果选择训练证据，针对 Pass@K 与 Pass@1 之间的差距（单样本失败但 K 样本内恢复的问题）进行微调。；在匹配样本数、处理 token 和优化器步数的条件下，用恢复的失败填充预算，并用精确分解区分对恢复失败、遗漏失败的修正与首次解码成功上的退步。；分析可用收益与可迁移失败支持之间的关系，并用随机对照归因收益来源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trained with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR). An exact verifier can also support test-time scaling by selecting a passing response from multiple samples, while other deployments use beam search, adaptive sampling, or tools. We study single-sample decoding, where each query receives one response without search, to ask whether search-exposed behavior can be absorbed into the model. Existing verified-response post-training recipes do not generally distinguish problems already solved on the first decode from failures recovered within K samples. Under a fixed budget, this can spend examples repeating behavior the deployed policy already has. We introduce GapFT, which selects training evidence by the source checkpoint's single-sample outcome and fine-tunes on the Pass@K-Pass@1 gap: problems the policy fails on one sample but solves within K samples. We match training examples, processed tokens, and optimizer steps while keeping the objective unchanged. GapFT fills the matched budget with recovered failures and uses an exact decomposition to distinguish corrections of recovered and missed failures from regressions on first-decode successes. On LogiQA 2.0 and ReClor with Llama-3.1-8B, GapFT improves Pass@1 by 14.4 and 13.9 points over the source model, outperforms budget-matched uniform verified RFT at the same learning rate, and matches fine-tuning on the full verified pool using one third of the data. A single decode matches the source model's verifier-selected Pass@4 accuracy. A randomized control attributes gains to covering distinct failures, and our analysis relates available gains to transferable failure support. A three-seed Qwen2.5-7B replication retains positive gains over uniform RFT on both logic tasks.

</details>
